# 451: Chapter 448: Steven Requests an Audience

[Shocking! Team Magma and Team Aqua's actions in the Hoenn Region are actually for...]

With this eye-catching title, D started editing the detailed content of the post below.

The situation over in the Hoenn Region isn't complicated, and with Team Magma and Team Aqua opposing each other, it makes it relatively easy for him to muddy the waters.

Moreover, the Rotom in his Rotom Phone can provide a perfect signal blocking function for the phone, preventing others from locating him via network signals.

And in the Pokemon World, there's no need to use real names online. In this case, no matter what he says online, no one will find him.

Thinking of this, D's hands quickly started typing on the keyboard.

[Just an outsider, talking about the recent fuss on the net regarding Team Aqua's regular members provoking the Hoenn League, making a rational guess about Team Magma and Team Aqua's rampage and their goals in the Hoenn Region.

As everyone knows, the Hoenn Region has a very ancient history of legends. I have checked the relevant materials, and according to the records in ancient ruins and artifacts, world-destroying events once occurred in the Hoenn Region.

The legendary Pokemon Groudon, known as the Earth God, and Kyogre, known as the Sea God.

One created the land, and the other created the sea. Thousands of years ago, they awoke once to fight a great war to expand their territories.

We won't dwell on the battle's results.

What I mainly want to talk about here is that Team Magma and Team Aqua, roaming the Hoenn Region, just happen to correspond to these two legendary Pokemon.

Legend has it that Groudon leaves magma veins in its path, which solidify into land, while Kyogre sends down torrential rains from the sky to engulf the land to expand the sea.

Magma for Groudon, Aqua for Kyogre.

Is it possible that these two organizations aim for Groudon and Kyogre?

And everything they're doing in the Hoenn Region is all to awaken or capture these two legendary Pokemon.

If this speculation is accurate, then we can move on to the next prediction.

According to my investigation, Groudon and Kyogre's appearance is said to cause weather changes. Groudon will make the temperature rise, causing intense sunlight, while Kyogre will cause torrential rains to fall from the sky.

If Team Magma and Team Aqua want to find where they're located, then the next target for these two organizations may be the Weather Institute in the Hoenn Region.

As for Groudon, the Earth God, it is on land. As long as its location is confirmed, it can be found directly.

And Kyogre, the Sea God, must be asleep in the sea. To see it, a submarine capable of withstanding deep-sea pressure is needed.

So next, Team Aqua probably will target the Stern's Shipyard in Slateport City.

As far as I know, it has the best shipbuilding technology in the entire Hoenn Region.

Hopefully, the evil plans of those two organizations will fail.

If Groudon and Kyogre are caught by them, the two organizations are likely to erupt in a great battle, possibly affecting the entire Hoenn Region.

If this really happens, there might be no choice but to go to the Sky Pillar to ask the Sky God Rayquaza to come out and stop them, just as recorded in the legends from thousands of years ago.

Alright, that'll be it for today's speculation. If you have any thoughts, you can leave a comment to let me know!]

After finishing the edits, D clicked send, and the next second, the post was uploaded online and then automatically pushed to potentially interested individuals via the Pokemon Forum's algorithm.

After doing this, D switched away to watch Lono's live stream.

Meanwhile, in the Hoenn Region, a glasses-wearing man who loves collecting historical legends suddenly saw the post while surfing online.

The man, named Jinhei, is a member of an association of legend enthusiasts, a place where a large group of people who enjoy researching local historical legends gather.

"Shocking, Team Magma and Team Aqua's actions in the Hoenn Region are actually for... huh? What a strange post."

Jinhei's eyes were immediately drawn to the title.

He couldn't help but click in, wanting to see what was so shocking.

But the next second, Jinhei saw something in the post that shocked him.

"The legendary Earth God Groudon and Sea God Kyogre?!"

"Magma for Groudon, Aqua for Kyogre... Could it be that Team Magma and Team Aqua are trying to recruit these two legendary Pokemon?"

"Holy crap!"

After the poster pointed it out, Jinhei suddenly realized this was very possible!

After all, those two organizations have been active in the Hoenn Region and haven't caused any major incidents, nor have they posed a tricky problem for the Hoenn League so far.

All along, many thought Team Magma and Team Aqua were against the Hoenn League, but it now seems they might be after those two legendary Pokemon!

This speculation is incredibly bold, involving legendary Pokemon Groudon and Kyogre. Jinhei hurriedly shared this post in the group chat of the legend enthusiasts' association.

Soon, the group chat burst into a quick discussion over the issue.

Some logged onto the forum to search for the post and leave comments under it, asking the poster if they knew any more information.

Word spread from one to ten, ten to a hundred.

This matter soon reached the ears of a Miss Jenny who was investigating Team Magma and Team Aqua.

After reading the post, Miss Jenny immediately reported the relevant information to the Hoenn League.

Quickly, in his mansion in Mossdeep City, while attending to Beldum, Steven saw the post online.

Steven Stone, the Hoenn Region's Champion and also the young master of Devon Company, is admired for his handsome and charming appearance and strong capabilities, having many fans in the Hoenn Region.

At this moment, a look of surprise appeared on Steven's handsome face.

Soon after, the Champion fell into deep thought.

Team Magma and Team Aqua, Groudon and Kyogre.

There seem to be some secretive connections between these.

No, it can't be called secretive anymore. The Hoenn League's interrogation of that Team Aqua member last time already showed some signs.

Were those two organizations aiming to awaken those two super ancient Pokemon?

Regarding this outcome, maybe because of previous speculations in his heart, Steven didn't feel too surprised.

What surprised him was how accurately the poster predicted Team Magma and Team Aqua's routes of action.

Several days prior, Steven had already heard from his father, Mr. Stone, about the Devon Company's package being stolen.

That package contained critical parts for the Submarine Explorer 1, with Devon Company and Stern's Shipyard in Slateport City pursuing a collaborative project to explore the ocean.

They plan to jointly create a more advanced submarine for deep-sea exploration.

Initially, Steven didn't quite understand why those people would steal the components of the Submarine Explorer 1.

But now, it makes sense.

Thinking of this, Steven clicked on the poster's profile page and sent a message using his account.

[Hello, I am the current Champion of the Hoenn Region, Steven Stone. I would like to discuss your post; could we meet?]

*

At the same time, in Team Magma and Team Aqua's bases.

Maxie and Archie received reports from their subordinates and saw the post online.

Maxie calmly read through the entire post, a solemn expression on his face.

This person... is there a mole in Team Magma? Why do they know their plans so well?

"Boss, what should we do?" a subordinate asked.

Maxie adjusted his glasses with a calm voice, "Activate the Alpha Plan."

"Yes!"

On the other side, in Team Aqua's base.

Archie stared at his phone, cursing under his breath:

"Damn it, who the hell is this? Is there a mole among us?"

The members of Team Aqua shook their heads repeatedly.

"It's not me, Boss. You've been so good to us; how could we possibly betray Team Aqua!"

"If I find out who this guy is, stopping Team Aqua's actions, I'll make sure to teach him a lesson!" Archie snorted coldly.

"What should we do now, Boss?"

"The plan goes on as usual. I've received word that the Submarine Explorer 1 will soon be completed; when it's ready, we'll just sneak in and steal it... Hmph, no one can stop us!"

---

---

---

---

# 452: Chapter 449: Drago-Ride Service

"Beep beep!"

"You have a new private message, please click to view!"

Three days later, just as D was steaming Grassy Seed for his Gogoat at home, his Rotom Phone suddenly received a notification.

D opened the Pokemon Forum to check it, and then saw a series of private messages from fellow users.

After a quick glance, there were people wanting to discuss the legends of Groudon and Kyogre with him, some asking about follow-up stories, and even a League Investigator seeking more information.

D even saw what seemed like threatening messages from possible members of Team Aqua and Team Magma.

A user named [Fire Fire Fire] said: Brat, I advise you to be sensible and delete this post quickly, or else don't blame us for being rude!

D replied with a question mark.

Soon, another message came through.

[Fire Fire Fire: If you've got the guts, send me your address. You're looking for trouble, and I'm going to show you what happens to those who obstruct our plans!]

This time, D decided to employ a strategy of sowing discord.

He pretended to be from Team Rocket and replied in Giovanni's tone:

[Hmph, overestimating yourself, this world ultimately belongs to us, Team Rocket. Little pawns like Team Magma and Team Aqua, let the Hoenn League deal with you once and for all!]

Then the other side began to break character and started cursing.

The gist was that Team Rocket isn't even worthy of carrying Team Magma's shoes, and Team Magma is all for the world, or something like that.

They even said Team Rocket is nothing, they've never even heard of it.

D: "..."

Seeing this, he directly replied:

[Team Rocket has been around for decades, while Team Magma only recently emerged these past few years. A mere rookie force dares to challenge Team Rocket. Come to Celadon Street if you have the guts; you won't get away unscathed!]

Then he added the user to the blacklist, making them unable to reply or comment, leaving them just to stew in frustration.

Celadon Street is a famous street in Celadon City, located in the Kanto Region and a base for Team Rocket.

If a lackey from Team Magma went there to provoke, the scene would surely be entertaining.

Continuing to scroll down, D suddenly noticed a familiar name.

[Steven]

Huh? Steven? Could it be the Steven he knows?

With curiosity, D clicked into the user's profile and saw the League's verified badge.

This indicated that he was indeed Steven Stone, the current Champion of the Hoenn League.

[Hello, I am the current Champion of the Hoenn Region, Steven Stone. Regarding your post, I would like to discuss it with you. May we meet?]

Steven wanted to meet him?

D was slightly taken aback, but then quickly realized.

It was obvious that Steven wanted to discuss matters concerning Team Magma and Team Aqua with him.

After a brief thought, D replied: [Hello, I'm sorry, it's inconvenient for me to meet right now. If you want to know something, let's chat online!]

And soon, Steven sent a reply.

He didn't insist on meeting up but instead asked: [Are you with the International Police?]

International Police? A handsome face flashed in D's mind.

But unfortunately, he was not.

[Stranger 1: No, I'm just an ordinary person.]

Meanwhile, in the Hoenn Region, Mossdeep City.

Steven looked at the reply on his screen and fell into contemplation.

Not wanting to reveal his identity, or is he afraid of retaliation from Team Magma and Team Aqua?

He clicked into the other user's profile, finding it was a newly registered account named Stranger 1, clearly to deliberately hide their identity.

Given that, he didn't ask further.

With this in mind, Steven switched to a different question, getting to the point.

[Steven: Is all the content in that post purely your speculation?]

[Stranger 1: Yes, it's my deduction based on the current situation in the Hoenn Region. Whether you believe it or not is up to you.]

[Steven: To tell you the truth, your speculation is quite accurate. Currently, there are signs of activity from Team Magma and Team Aqua all over Hoenn. Traces have been detected at Slateport City's shipyard and the Weather Institute left by suspicious individuals.]

[Steven: Before this year, these two organizations were generally perceived by the public as the Nature Conservation Association and the Ocean Conservation Association until they recently revealed their true colors.]

Nature Conservation Association and Ocean Conservation Association?

Reading Steven's message, D was momentarily at a loss for words.

If only Maxie and Archie weren't so extreme, perhaps it could really have been that way.

For Team Magma aims to expand the landmass, increasing human activity zones to link human development closely to the world's overall happiness.

Meanwhile, Team Aqua's goal is to expand the oceans, reclaiming habitats for the ocean Pokemon lost to human greed.

They believe the ocean is the source of life for all creatures, planning to create an ideal home for Pokemon by expanding the oceans.

But it's too extreme; both Maxie and Archie are too extreme.

D stared thoughtfully at the message Steven had sent on the screen.

Steven said suspicious activity had been detected at Stern's Shipyard and the Weather Institute in Slateport City.

This also means that Team Magma and Team Aqua are about to begin their actions.

If they manage to get the Submarine Explorer 1 and use the Weather Institute to pinpoint the locations of the two Super Ancient Pokemon.

Then soon, Groudon and Kyogre will be awakened.

Seeing no response for a long time, Steven asked.

[Are you still there?]

D snapped back to reality, thought carefully, and then told Steven everything.

[Things are just as I predicted. If I'm not mistaken, Team Magma and Team Aqua are about to start moving.

Their goal is indeed those two Legendary Pokemon. Based on the previous activities of these two organizations, Team Magma seems intent on expanding the land mass, while Team Aqua plans to increase the ocean's size.

It just so happens that Groudon and Kyogre have the power to do this, so they need to catch them.

Next, Team Magma and Team Aqua's targets might be the Weather Institute and some shipyard.

This is all I know, and from here on, it's up to your Hoenn League.]

D typed all this out in one go, then exhaled deeply.

This way, the Hoenn League can be on alert.

If they can stop those two organizations' plans, Groudon and Kyogre won't wake from their slumber, and the Hoenn Region can continue its stability for several more years.

If they can't stop them, then coastal cities in the Hoenn Region will have to prepare for evacuation in advance.

Steven carefully read through Stranger No. 1's response and then thanked him earnestly.

He then prepared to get in touch with the Hoenn League, report this information, and dispatch some competent Trainers to guard the Weather Institute and Stern's Shipyard in Slateport City.

As for verifying the authenticity of these suspicions...

Steven felt there was no need. If it doesn't happen, great, but if it does, their preparations will come in handy.

"Phew."

Steven put away the Rotom Phone, but just then, another private message came through.

Steven clicked to view it and was surprised by its contents.

The message was still from Stranger No. 1, and its content was completely unexpected.

[Stranger No. 1: If Groudon and Kyogre really do awaken, and the Hoenn League can't handle them, you can contact me. Maybe I can help.]

Steven was slightly taken aback.

Originally, he thought the other party was just a regular person afraid of Team Magma and Team Aqua's revenge.

But now...

*

D put down his phone, exhaling deeply; now everything should be fine!

He didn't know the specific time of Groudon's and Kyogre's awakening, so he couldn't go watch the events unfold in the Hoenn Region beforehand.

But as long as he's linked with Steven, maybe he'll have the chance to witness the spectacle.

Moreover, Kyogre had once severely injured his Dragonite, and as Dragonite's Trainer, he had to help his Pokemon regain its dignity.

With the cards he has now, he's capable of dealing with Groudon and Kyogre even if they team up.

Not to mention Koraidon for now.

A single phone call could summon Giratina and Calyrex.

Even in the worst-case scenario, Dragonite could use Driptrip Dragon Service.

Using the Dragon Scale gifted by the shiny Rayquaza to summon it over.

Considering the number of Poffins and Pokeblocks, Rayquaza would surely be happy to give Kyogre a lesson for Dragonite.

Thinking about it, D suddenly realized something.

He had unknowingly become a Legendary Beast Guy.

If he had a few more Legendary Beasts at home, he could even form a Legendary Beast team and participate in the World Championships and the Eight Great Masters.

Extreme Koraidon, peak double horse Calyrex, Giratina, Mewtwo...

What a fearsome lineup.

It's even superior to Tobias, the Legendary Beast Guy.

But D could only think about this secretly, because not every Legendary Pokemon likes to battle.

Although given Giratina's character, it's likely happy to join the excitement.

Calyrex would surely want to help him with this favor.

But now, Mewtwo, eager for a peaceful life, might not.

After all, Mewtwo is now like Calyrex, turned into a Pokemon farmer who loves peaceful farming in the World Tree...

Never mind, let's not think about it anymore!

Today is [Role Model Day].

D needs to take his three Gogoats to the World Tree to visit the Skiddo that Mewtwo rescued, who had once been harmed by humanity.

---

---

---

---

# 453: Chapter 450: Gogoat and Skiddo

D had previously heard Mewtwo talk about the past encounters of that Skiddo.

A year ago, Mewtwo was aimlessly traveling the world with the Cloned Pokemon.

During one of their stops to rest, they saw a remote house out in the wilderness.

The house's owner was a middle-aged robust man with a menacing face, covered with a beard.

He used a Houndoom to capture many wild Pokemon, forcing them to work for him.

Since Skiddo was a male Pokemon and couldn't produce milk like a female goat, its job was to help the man pull heavy loads and carry stones.

During that time, the man always sat behind, accompanied by Houndoom, holding a bramble-covered whip to supervise the Pokemon at work.

The Pokemon he forcibly captured were toiling away in agony.

Whenever Skiddo got too tired and wanted to rest, the man would whip it from behind, forcing it to get up and continue working.

In the end, Skiddo was left battered and bruised.

It was at that time that Mewtwo passed by that place.

Finding the Pokemon tormented by the human, Mewtwo, who was kind-hearted, could not stand to see such things happen.

Thus, Mewtwo intervened and saved Skiddo and the other Pokemon, giving the man a lesson and erasing his memory.

From then on, Skiddo joined Mewtwo's team, and Mewtwo willingly took on the responsibility of protecting them.

Because of this, Skiddo became extremely fearful of humans and so very cautious.

On the journey to the World Tree, D narrated word by word Skiddo's past encounters to his three Gogoat at home.

"...So, today's mission is to visit that poor Skiddo at the World Tree and play with it, understood?"

As D spoke, he adjusted the position of the box of Grassy Seed he was holding.

These were freshly steamed Grassy Seed made by him, ready to be taken to the World Tree and given to Skiddo to eat.

The Gogoat had never experienced anything like that.

Since their birth, they had been inhabiting the grassy area around Casseroya Lake.

Later on, D's arrival brought the three of them back to the ranch.

On the ranch, they received the best care, with an endless supply of Grassy Seed and a shelter to protect them from the wind and rain.

The Gogoat often felt they were the happiest Gogoat in the world.

Compared to them, that Skiddo seemed so pitiable...

"Baa!"

One of the Gogoat, with resolute eyes, bleated, assuring that the task of comforting Skiddo could safely be entrusted to it!

The second Gogoat nodded in succession: "Baa!" (We will help it move beyond those terrifying days!)

The third Gogoat looked left and right and hurriedly nodded along with its two companions: "Baa!" (Me too!)

Witnessing this, D nodded in satisfaction.

"Mm, good, just like that!"

In the midst of the casual conversation, D and the three Gogoat had already passed through the Reversed World, arriving at the massive Sky Island where the World Tree was located.

He took the Gogoat on a bubble elevator all the way to the top level.

Here, the Explorer Team Guild was operating in an orderly manner.

The Pokemon living in the World Tree were coming in and going out of various buildings, fully enjoying life in this place.

D led the Gogoat along the paths, with Pokemon they passed by greeting them along the way.

D smiled and responded.

When they arrived at the café, D saw his bunch of Eevee there.

When they saw him approach, the Eevee trio, led by Flareon, eagerly waved their paws, loudly greeting.

"Vee!"

D stepped forward and poked its little head, humming as he asked, "You haven't been home all day, have you turned into wild Eevee?"

Ever since the Explorer Team Guild was built, the Pokemon from the ranch frequently came to play here.

From the nine Eevee to Alcremie and Garganacl, even Ogerpon and Gholdengo often came over.

At first, everything was fine, but later on, these Pokemon became so playful they began to neglect home.

Because of this, D jokingly referred to them as wild Pokemon.

However, hearing this, Flareon's eyes began to wander, looking a bit guilty.

"Vee..."

The eyes of the other nearby Pokemon also started to dart around.

"Alright! Play to your hearts' content!" D stood up, speaking to the Eevee in front of him with some feeling: "After all, this place was built for you to enjoy."

"Vee!"

Upon hearing this, a gleam appeared in the eyes of Eevee, looking extremely happy.

D chuckled twice, as the Eevee came to play at the World Tree, they wouldn't be at home bothering him, allowing him to relax a lot more.

Glaceon and Umbreon, with their independent personalities, were fine, but Flareon, Sylveon, and the others were too clingy.

Sometimes, while he sat on the couch playing on his phone, several Eevee would cluster around him.

Pressing against his side or on top of him to gain his attention often left him breathless, covered in Eevee's fur.

Thinking of this, D stood up and told the Eevee: "You all keep playing, I'm going to take the Gogoat to find Skiddo."

---

"Eevee!"

Seeing this, Leafeon quickly pointed out Skiddo's location to D.

It had just seen Skiddo enjoying the breeze over by the mill!

"The mill area? Alright, got it." D reached out and patted Leafeon's head, then bid farewell to the Eevees: "I'm heading off now. Remember to come home tonight—I have delicious treats for you."

At the mention of delicious treats, the Eevees' eyes lit up instantly, and they responded in unison: "Eevee!"

Following Leafeon's directions, D headed to the mill, and indeed, he spotted Skiddo there.

At that moment, Skiddo stood alone by the riverside, gazing down at the clear water—which reflected its own image.

"Skiddo!" D called out.

The Skiddo promptly turned its head.

D advanced with the Gogoats and introduced them: "These are the Gogoats I've told you about. Today, I've brought them to see you and brought some treats as well."

With that, D placed a basket of Grassy Seeds on the grass.

Skiddo twitched its nose, clearly drawn by the aroma of the Grassy Seeds.

Witnessing this, the three Gogoats happily exclaimed:

"Baa!" (Eat, eat!)

"Baa!" (These Grassy Seeds are made by D—the most delicious thing in the world!)

"Baa!" (Indeed!)

Faced with such enthusiastic Gogoats, Skiddo seemed a bit reserved.

D thought maybe his presence as a human was reminding Skiddo of past injustices by humans.

So he turned away to inspect the vast farmland planted by the Pokemon themselves.

The Gogoats also mistook Skiddo's hesitance as fear of D.

They reassured: "Baa!" (Don't be afraid, D is a good person!)

"Baa!" (Indeed, even though he didn't hatch us, he always looks after us!)

Skiddo listened, looking puzzled.

Curiously, it asked: "Baa?" (Is D your master?)

Master? One Gogoat shook its head: "Baa!" (No, D is our Trainer!)

Seeing the opportunity, the earnest Gogoats started sharing their story with Skiddo.

A long time ago, they were weak Skiddo living by a great lake.

One day, a human arrived, engaged them in fierce battle, and captured them with Poke Balls.

That human was D.

Since that day, they had been living on this pasture.

To make them feel at home, D built them a shelter from wind and rain and daily crafted delicious Grassy Seeds from fresh, rich grass.

Gradually, under D's care, they grew and evolved into formidable Gogoats.

Furthermore, they managed a Trial.

Many humans came to challenge them, but they defeated them all. So far, only two humans have passed the Trial.

Listening to the tale of the Gogoats, Skiddo couldn't help but show longing.

It could sense from the Gogoats' tone, they truly liked D.

That human is so wonderful!

Reflecting on its own experiences, Skiddo further asked: "Baa?" (Did D ever use a whip on you?)

The question puzzled the three Gogoats.

"Baa?" (A whip? No way! Why would D use a whip on us?)

Skiddo thought carefully: "Baa?" (Because you didn't finish your work?)

The Gogoats shook their heads repeatedly.

What a joke! At the pasture, all they do is eat, sleep, sunbathe, and use Grass Energy to tend to the crops—there was nothing else to do.

Seeing D doesn't whip Pokemon, Skiddo felt quite envious.

One Gogoat stood firmly before Skiddo and said: "Baa!" (Therefore, come train!)

Skiddo looked puzzled.

Gogoat continued: "Baa!" (So you can become strong like us and protect yourself and other Pokemon!)

The moment it spoke, a terrifying wave of green energy surged from Gogoat's body.

The energy surged like giant waves raging in the ocean, sweeping across the surroundings.

In almost the blink of an eye, the entire world seemed to transform into a green ocean.

The grasses, formed from pure energy, swayed gently with the breeze, appearing dreamy and surreal.

Upon seeing this scene, Skiddo froze in place.

"Baa... "

Staring at the Gogoat before it, radiating a rich grass energy, Skiddo was completely bewildered.

So impressive!

However, in the next second, Skiddo witnessed something even more astonishing.

The three Gogoats suddenly bowed slightly.

A rich grass energy began to quickly gather from their mouths, eventually transforming into a massive Grass Sacred Sword.

The Gogoats held these swords with their mouths, then their bodies swiftly dashed forward.

With a loud boom, a large rock in the distance was split in half and then shattered apart.

Skiddo: (*ﾟﾛﾟ)!!

Truly amazing!

Skiddo's eyes were full of admiration for these three senior Gogoats.

If it could become as powerful, would it be able to protect itself and its companions from human persecution? Skiddo couldn't help but wonder.

Scenes from the past emerged in its mind.

In the desolate mountains, a human who called themselves a Pokemon Hunter wielded a long whip, cursing them and harshly striking them as they worked.

"Damn it! Hurry up! You useless wastes, since you can't fetch a good price, you better work hard for me!"

"Whack!"

"Whack!"

The whip fell relentlessly.

Some companions collapsed on the ground from exhaustion.

Some Pokemon tried to resist, but were pinned down by that Houndoom.

The other Pokemon trembled in fear.

The man mocked the resisting Pokemon, "Humph, overestimating your abilities."

They had no choice but to keep working.

Yet Skiddo had already been beaten many times, its body covered in wounds, and it could barely endure such intense labor.

The man noticed it had stopped, cursing as he walked over: "Damn it, slacking off again!"

Skiddo was paralyzed with fear, unable to move in the face of the high-held whip, and could only shiver in place.

It was at that moment.

Mewtwo appeared.

In the stance of a protector, it stood before Skiddo, unleashing a terrifying telekinesis that sent the human and Houndoom flying away.

If Mewtwo hadn't saved it in time then, it might have lost its life from exhaustion.

It didn't want to be like that anymore.

If it had powerful strength at that time, it wondered, could it have protected the other hurt Pokemon?

Skiddo's gaze gradually became resolute.

It looked up at the three strong Gogoats before it and pleaded, "Baa!" (Senior Gogoats, please teach me!)

The Gogoats also looked down at the little Skiddo.

One of the Gogoats used Vine Whip to bring over a bunch of Grassy Seed made by D, placing them in front of Skiddo.

"Baa!" (Eat them! Then we will start guiding you to learn our special move, Grass Sacred Sword!)

"Baa?!" (Grass Sacred Sword?)

Hearing this unfamiliar move name, Skiddo was startled.

The Gogoat nodded, "Baa!" (That's right! D helped us name it!)

Skiddo instantly understood.

It turned its gaze to the basket of Grassy Seed in front of it and began to feast.

It too wanted to become as strong as the senior Gogoats!

If so, it could protect itself and other hurt Pokemon!

---

---

---

---

# 454: Chapter 451: Giratina's Competitive Nature

"How is life by the World Tree?"

D, who was checking the growth of crops between the furrows, casually greeted Mewtwo when he encountered it.

Upon hearing this, Mewtwo's expression remained as calm as water, and it gently nodded.

Immediately, a deep voice rang in D's mind.

"Everything is fine."

Yes, everything is fine, thought Mewtwo.

This World Tree perfectly met its expectations.

Remote location, tranquil and peaceful, no outsiders to disturb, only Pokémon living here.

More importantly, it no longer needed to go out specifically to find food for the Pokémon following it like before.

Thinking of this, Mewtwo couldn't help but recall a few days ago when some of the Pokémon it had invited brought it Berries they had grown themselves.

Those cloned Pokémon were so pure and kind, they knew how to express gratitude.

Even though Mewtwo didn't need those Berries, it still accepted the heartfelt gift.

It liked this place.

"That's good." D smiled and said, "I thought you might not be used to living here. Do you have any plans for the future? Like leaving here and seeing the world."

Mewtwo remained silent.

After a while, it gently shook its head, "No, it's unnecessary now."

"Alright then!" D nodded upon hearing this, "In that case, feel free to settle down here! If there's anything you want to know, you can also use the Rotom Phone to learn more about the world we live in."

"Thank you." Mewtwo expressed its gratitude because the Rotom Phone was a gift from D, and this place was cultivated by him.

As a Pokémon that possessed both human and Pokémon genes, perhaps not wanting to owe a favor, after a moment of silence, Mewtwo added:

"If you ever need any help in the future, you can find me."

Hearing this, D was slightly taken aback.

But looking at Mewtwo's expression, he quickly understood its meaning.

Mewtwo was thanking him in this way!

"Alright, okay." D didn't stand on ceremony with Mewtwo and agreed with a smile.

Seeing this, Mewtwo also breathed a slight sigh of relief.

Otherwise, it didn't know how it would continue living here.

At this moment, D suddenly saw the ground beneath his feet turn into a wide expanse of Grassy Terrain.

He turned around to see, and sure enough, saw the three Gogoat using Grassy Terrain over there.

And the Skiddo was vigorously eating the Grassy Seed in the basket.

What were they doing?

D and Mewtwo were drawn over.

"What are you doing?" D asked.

The Skiddo swallowed the Grassy Seed in its mouth and explained, "Meeh!" (The Gogoat seniors are instructing me to practice Grass Sacred Sword!)

Having eaten a few Translation Nuts Cake ahead of time, D naturally understood what the Skiddo meant.

He looked in surprise at his three Gogoat: "Grass Sacred Sword?"

The Gogoat quickly nodded: "Meeh!" (That way it can protect itself and other Pokémon!)

"Meeh!" (That's right! I want to become as strong as the Gogoat seniors!)

The Skiddo said with determination and then lowered its head and continued to eat the Grassy Seed with effort.

D: "..."

He turned his gaze to his three Gogoat and then looked at the Skiddo in front of him.

Could it be reading Keldeo's script?

Doesn't that mean his three Gogoat have become Sacred Swordsmen?

It's just a pity there's no Kyurem here for it to challenge... Otherwise, it could really recreate Keldeo's movie plot, awakening its own Sacred Sword in a moment of crisis.

Mewtwo, witnessing everything on the side, fell into contemplation.

Protection?

D took a deep breath and said, "Alright, in that case, from today, I'll leave the Skiddo's training to you three."

The natural energy and grassy aura by the World Tree were abundant, making the Skiddo's training achieve twice the result with half the effort.

"Meeh!" The Gogoat immediately expressed they had no problem with this.

*

In the evening, D returned to the ranch. After handing over the produce that needed to be sold today to Zack, he went to the kitchen to start preparing dinner for the Pokémon.

Many of the Pokémon on the ranch leaned more toward sweet dishes than meat.

Like Alcremie, Eevee, Pawmot, Calyrex, Indeedee siblings, and so on.

So tonight, following the recipes from the dessert recipe book, D baked many sweet treats.

For example, Tiramisu, Portuguese egg tarts, caramel pudding, and apple pie.

Tiramisu is a dessert with a taste of coffee liqueur, with mascarpone cheese as the main ingredient, replacing the sponge cake of traditional desserts with ladyfingers, and then adding coffee, cocoa powder, and other materials, and refrigerating it.

In the afternoon, D had already made one to put in the refrigerator, and it would be ready to serve when the Pokémon returned.

Besides Tiramisu, there was caramel pudding made from rich cream and thin, crispy caramel, apple pie with a filling of sweet apple, mixed with cinnamon and lemon juice, and so on.

The sky outside grew darker.

It was at this time that the Eevee who had spent the day playing by the World Tree returned.

---

As soon as they entered the house, they rushed towards the kitchen.

"Vee!"

The leading Flareon caught a whiff of the sweet aroma and loudly asked D what delicious treats he made for them.

Watching the rushing Eevees, D responded with a smile, "I've made lots of desserts you all love. Sister, could you help me take them out from the fridge?"

The sister Indeedee guarding nearby nodded cheerfully.

It went forward to take out the tiramisu and caramel pudding.

The Eevees twitched their little noses, gathering around the desserts, their eyes sparkling with surprise and delight.

"A bunch of little gluttons!"

D glanced at the effects of those desserts and found that most of them improved Pokemon happiness and affection, so he didn't give it much thought.

Sister Indeedee took a dining knife to the side to help them cut the desserts.

The sweets-loving Alcremie and Pawmo also ran over.

Only a few meat-loving Pokemon were left staring longingly at the scene.

"Raaagh..."

"Rraa..."

"Woo..."

"Mew..."

Finally, Koraidon couldn't stand it anymore and directly surrounded the kitchen, along with Cyclizar, Flutter Mane, Dragonite, and a Shiny Dratini, looking longingly at D.

D was feeding a slice of apple pie to the Slugma under the stove. Seeing Koraidon and the others' pitiful looks, he couldn't help but laugh:

"Are you that hungry?"

"Raaagh!" Koraidon shook its big head, indicating it wasn't hungry but wanted to be cared for by D like the Eevees were.

"...The Eevees are still babies. You guys are big enough to start your own lives." D was speechless.

"Raaagh..." Koraidon drooped its head, looking very forlorn.

Cyclizar perked up: "Rraa!"

It's not big yet; it's not even three years old!

The recently hatched Dratini nodded vigorously.

The Dragonite, over a dozen years old, threw away its pride, pretending to be a baby Dragonite, and hugged D, acting spoiled.

"Woo~"

Flutter Mane pondered deeply.

How old was it again? Hmm, that was indeed a question.

Suddenly hugged by Dragonite, D accidentally dropped the spatula from his hand onto the floor, making him break out in a cold sweat.

He started pushing Dragonite's back to shoo them out: "All of you, get out; with you causing trouble here, I can't cook!"

After driving a group of Pokemon to the living room, D returned to the kitchen and started cooking some meat.

Koraidon and the others expressed their sadness over this.

But they soon perked up as the familiar scent of meat wafted from the kitchen!

Seeing this, Sister Indeedee couldn't help but cover her mouth and chuckle.

Brother Indeedee, meanwhile, was using a vacuum cleaner to clean up the hair shed by the Eevees on the floor.

Summer had arrived, causing the thick fur coat the Eevees grew for winter to start thinning, resulting in a large amount of shedding each day.

When D emerged from the kitchen with the ready dishes, he saw the scene of Pokemon eagerly waiting for a meal in the house.

He called out, and several Pokemon rushed over happily, indulging in today's dinner.

The Eevees, fully sated and satisfied, were lazily scattered across the living room.

Some were huddled on the same sofa, others sat alone on the floor, licking their fur, while others lounged leisurely on the rocking chair by the fireplace, gently swaying their tails.

Looking at this beautiful scene, D felt a sense of deep contentment in his heart.

He couldn't resist reaching out to pat Cyclizar on the head and said to them:

"Eat up, eat up, there's more in the kitchen if it's not enough!"

Cyclizar responded happily: "Rraa!"

D had eaten a bit while cooking, so he wasn't very hungry. He had just been warmed up by the heat from Slugma in the kitchen, so he decided to go outside for some fresh air.

The summer night's temperature wasn't as scorching as during the day, and the breeze from Glaseado Mountain was refreshingly cool.

D took out his Rotom Phone to check Poke Communications and replied to a message from Lono.

When he exited the chat interface, he saw that in the Legendary Beast chat group, Giratina was chatting with Mewtwo again.

[Giratina: You should set up a Pokemon Forum account! Compete with me to see who gets more followers!]

[Mewtwo: ...Not interested.]

D: "..."

If Mewtwo did set up a Pokemon Forum account and became a mega influencer, Team Rocket members would probably all set it as a special follow.

But come to think of it, what kind of hobby was this for Giratina?

Does it want to compare who's more popular with other Legendary Beasts?

D remembered when Calyrex's followers surpassed Giratina's, it kept asking Rotom to take and upload countless photos to its personal page.

All sorts of angles and distances, you name it.

Such a peculiar sense of competitiveness...

And it wasn't the first time Giratina had invited Mewtwo.

Yet Mewtwo had always shown little interest in Giratina's invitations from the beginning to the end.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but chime in.

[Wing King: Giratina, I'll give you two Rotom Phones. How about you give them to Dialga and Palkia?]

Soon, Giratina responded.

[Giratina: No way! Those two guys can't join the group!]

D was puzzled.

[Wing King: Why not?]

[Giratina: Hmph, no reason, just no.]

D: "..."

---

Logically speaking, the relationship between the Sinnoh Three Gods shouldn't be as tense as that between Groudon and Kyogre, who fight every time they meet, right?

Maybe they just don't see eye to eye?

Anyway, he doesn't have the chance to connect with Dialga and Palkia; so if they aren't invited, they aren't invited!

But what about Arceus? D couldn't help but wonder. Is it possible to pull Arceus into the group?

In the movie "Arceus and the Jewel of Life," Arceus regained its sanity in the end.

Anyway, he promised Giratina that he'd join in the fun later and help get back at Arceus.

From Arceus's perspective, it should only see them as Trainers preventing the insane Arceus from destroying the world, just like Ash.

Once Arceus regains sanity, he can then send Arceus a Rotom Phone, claiming it's to better understand the world.

Just not sure if Arceus would accept the phone...

However, according to the game's background setting, Arceus in this world seems to be only a clone.

The true Creator God Arceus observes quietly from outside all cosmos, watching human and Pokemon development.

Compared to the clone Arceus, the true Arceus is quite fashionable.

It even developed its own Arceus Phone and gave it to those crossing over to the Hisuin Region.

One could say it's a Creator God keeping up with the times.

*

League Year 200, April 28.

On this day, Lono received D's invitation and once again came from Levincia City to the ranch.

The event started because D posted photos of the desserts he made on his social circle.

Lono commented, saying they look great, and she would love to try them!

The hospitable D replied: When you next visit the ranch, I'll use them to welcome you.

So the next day, Lono arrived at the ranch.

Seeing Lono come over so quickly, D was quite puzzled.

"Isn't it busy at Levincia Gym?"

Lono suddenly felt a bit guilty: "It's okay lately; taking a day off occasionally isn't a big deal."

Hearing this, D nodded. If he recalls correctly, in some anime, Gym Leaders also stop challenges occasionally due to personal matters.

Sometimes they even need Trainers to help them finish tasks before returning to accept challenges.

That makes sense!

Lono spent a delightful day at the ranch.

Only at dusk did she leave the ranch.

D watched as Dragonite flew Lono into the sky, recalling her praise of his cooking; he felt quite pleased.

For this lively and cheerful Gym Leader, D really welcomes her frequent visits to the ranch.

In the Pokemon World, D doesn't have many human friends: Geeta, the two professors, Alder, Leon, and some townsfolk.

But they don't meet often, only occasionally chatting online.

Whenever Lono visits, the ranch becomes lively, Pokemons find new friends, and D gets a chance to talk human talk.

"Phew..."

Watching Dragonite's silhouette gradually recede, D let out a long sigh.

But speaking of which, is this similar to Mikuri and Steven's relationship?

In the anime, these two are very good friends.

He chuckled and turned back, taking Koraidon and others home.

In the living room, the big brother Indeedee was cleaning up.

The little sister Indeedee smiled softly as she looked at D.

Noticing the little sister Indeedee's expression, D raised his eyebrows in surprise and asked, "Hmm? Do you have something to say?"

Speaking casually, D grabbed a piece of Translation Nuts Cake and sipped the red tea on the coffee table.

The little sister Indeedee nodded slightly, replying with a playful tone: "Love? Love!" (Lono really likes you - will she become the future mistress of the ranch?)

"Pfft——" Upon hearing this, D spat out the tea due to the shock, making him choke.

"Cough, cough, cough, cough, cough——"

The little sister Indeedee quickly stepped forward to pat his back.

After a while, D finally felt better and immediately asked, "What did you just say?"

The little sister Indeedee blinked and repeated: "Love!" (Brother and I both sensed it, Lono really likes you!)

Being Pokemons that draw energy from emotions, the Indeedee siblings are very sensitive to human feelings.

Upon hearing this, D was utterly shocked.

Lono likes him? Impossible, right?

He completely treated Lono as a friend!

Furthermore, Lono looks quite youthful; sometimes he even sees her as a junior like Ash and Lillie.

The little sister Indeedee showed confusion: "Love? Love!" (But we both sensed it - Lono has a fantastic impression of you!)

Beside him, Koraidon chimed in.

"Wagga!" (That's right!)

This left D feeling quite surprised.

He cast a surprised glance at Koraidon: "You like Lono?"

Nearby, Cyclizar also curiously looked at Koraidon.

Koraidon pretended to play dumb: "Wagga..." (She's very nice...)

The little sister Indeedee nodded in agreement: "Love～" (That's right, I can feel it - Lono is a very kind human～)

D: "..."

He suddenly felt a headache: "You must be mistaken; okay, this matter is over, don't mention it again!"

He didn't even know how to face Lono now.

Seeing D like this, the little sister Indeedee had no choice but to temporarily skip the topic: "Love." (Alright.)

D wanted to say more, but quickly his attention was diverted by another matter.

Because Steven messaged him again.

---

---

---

---

# 455: Chapter 452: Teddiursa's [Pickup], Found a Treasure!

[Steven: You're right, Team Magma and Team Aqua indeed made a move on the Weather Institute and Stern's Shipyard.]

Seeing this message, D first sat down on the sofa, then replied back.

[Stranger #1: What was the outcome?]

[Steven: Since we were prepared in advance, we replaced the Submarine Explorer 1 at Stern's Shipyard and hid the real submarine elsewhere, but things didn't go well at the Weather Institute.]

[Steven: Members of Team Magma and Team Aqua had infiltrated the Weather Institute disguised as staff. When the Hoenn League Trainers were guarding outside the institute, they took action, and now the information related to the Super Ancient Pokemon has been stolen.]

Seeing this message, D silently thought "As expected."

The Hoenn League indeed had information on Groudon and Kyogre.

Whether in games or animations, this has been indirectly hinted to players and viewers to some extent.

In fact, the Hoenn League is constantly monitoring Groudon and Kyogre, and once they show any signs of movement, the Hoenn League can prepare in advance.

Thinking of this, D questioned Steven.

[Stranger #1: What do you think their next move will be?]

Steven did not reply immediately.

He stared at the information on the screen, unable to help but fall into deep thought.

This Stranger #1 knows so much about the history of the Hoenn Region, it's clear he has done special research.

The Hoenn League did not hide the legends of Groudon and Kyogre, and the Hoenn Region is home to many Ancient Ruins.

It's not difficult to gather information about the Super Ancient Pokemon from these places, and perhaps Team Magma and Team Aqua learned about Groudon and Kyogre from these sources.

But this Stranger #1 is unaware of one point.

Long, long ago, aside from the legends of the Super Ancient Pokemon, two powerful Treasure Pearls containing Super Ancient Energy also passed down through the ages.

They are the Red Orb and the Blue Orb.

It's said that whoever obtains them can control Groudon, the Earth God, and Kyogre, the Sea God.

These two significant treasures are now kept at Mt. Pyre, guarded by Ghost Elite Phoebe's grandparents.

Moving forward, those two organizations are likely to target Mt. Pyre!

Thinking of this, Steven's heart tightened.

He quickly opened Poke Communications, contacting Ghost Elite Phoebe from the Hoenn Region, urging her to rush to Mt. Pyre as soon as possible to guard the important treasures, and that he would be heading there afterward as well.

Steven walked out of the house, went outside, and dialed a number on his phone.

Soon, a private plane marked with the Devon Company emblem slowly landed in front of Steven.

Steven boarded the plane and then contacted Stranger #1.

[Steven: I guessed their next plan, and I'm heading there now.]

Seeing this reply, D raised an eyebrow. Steven must be talking about the two Orbs on Mt. Pyre, right?

The Red Orb and the Blue Orb, through them one can awaken Groudon and Kyogre.

And the Super Ancient Energy within the Orbs can trigger Primal Reversion, restoring those two Pokemon to their original forms.

[Stranger #1: Wishing you a successful operation.]

D replied.

From Steven's side, there were no more messages.

D was not in a hurry; he made a cup of black tea and patiently waited in the living room.

Time ticked by, and during this period, D received a thank-you text from Lono.

In the text, Lono said she was very grateful to Dragonite for taking her home and had sent a small gift back to the ranch with Dragonite.

Looking at the photo of the Sunny Day Doll smiling, D couldn't help but smile.

Once Dragonite returned to the ranch, he hung the Sunny Day Doll under the eaves with the other Pokemon in the ranch.

At 10 p.m., there was finally news from Steven's side.

[Steven: Everything went smoothly; we successfully thwarted Team Magma and Team Aqua's plan, thanks to a few Pokemon that suddenly appeared.]

Seeing this response, D was slightly taken aback.

[Stranger #1: What Pokemon?]

[Steven: It was a Teddiursa, a Legendary Pokemon Zapdos, and another Pokemon that looks like a cat with pink fur and blue eyes.]

D: "?"

Isn't that the Heroic Expedition Team?

Meanwhile, in the Hoenn Region, at Mt. Pyre.

Watching those three Pokemon turn away, Steven was greatly relieved.

The situation had been extremely dangerous just now.

When he and Phoebe arrived here, Team Magma and Team Aqua were engaged in a fight, seemingly to snatch the two Orbs and to prevent the other side from obtaining them.

But when they arrived, Team Magma and Team Aqua decisively chose to join forces and even took Phoebe's grandparents hostage.

But luckily, at the critical moment, those three peculiar Pokemon passed by. The pink Pokemon used Psychic to rescue the two elderly hostages.

Taking advantage of the situation, they launched an attack, capturing all the Team Magma and Team Aqua members.

"Where on earth did those three Pokemon come from?"

With the crisis averted, Ghost Elite Phoebe curiously watched the departing backs of those three Pokemon.

What's more interesting, the Zapdos had a satchel hanging around its neck.

---

Steven still maintained the demeanor of a Devon nobleman; he shook his head and said, "No idea, but we really have those three to thank for stepping in today."

"Interesting." A light flashed in Phoebe's eyes.

Three Pokemon of different species and different Types actually came together?

Judging from how well-trained they looked, they didn't seem like Normal wild Pokemon.

Thinking of the shoulder bag hanging around that Zapdos's neck, Phoebe wondered if there was some Human Trainer who had specially trained them.

Beside her, Steven withdrew his gaze. Calmly looking at the houses around them that had been damaged by the Pokemon battle, he said:

"We can't keep the two Treasure Pearls here anymore."

Just now, in the middle of the fight, those two Treasure Pearls had been tossed into the grass by members of Team Magma and Team Aqua.

Steven turned around, intending to search nearby and then send them to the Hoenn League Headquarters for safekeeping.

However, very soon, he noticed something.

The patch of grass where the two Treasure Pearls had been lying just a moment ago was now completely empty.

Steven couldn't help being stunned.

Where were the Treasure Pearls?

Phoebe noticed this as well. She hurried forward and started rummaging through the grass, but after searching for a long time, she still didn't see a trace of the Treasure Pearls.

This made her expression grow dark.

A bad guess surfaced in Phoebe's heart: "Don't tell me Team Magma and Team Aqua took them?"

"Impossible." Steven analyzed calmly.

All the Team Magma and Team Aqua members present had been captured in one swoop; right now they were being escorted into police cars by the arriving Inspectors, so there was no way they could be secretly hiding the Treasure Pearls.

Which raised the question: where on earth had those two Treasure Pearls gone?

*

High up in the sky, the proud figure of Zapdos was streaking through the clouds at incredible speed.

The originally pure-white clouds were, under the influence of Zapdos, all turning into thunderclouds brewing with lightning.

A crash of thunder echoed through the night sky; people on the ground all raised their heads to look up, but all they could see was a flash of Spark.

The Hoenn Region hadn't been very peaceful lately, had it!

People who already had some advance understanding of the current state of affairs hurried their pace on the way home.

Mew and Teddiursa were sitting on Zapdos's back.

In their hands, each was holding a beautiful-looking Treasure Pearl—one crimson red, one indigo blue.

Even more interesting, they could glow!

These two Treasure Pearls had just been picked up by Teddiursa in the grass.

When they passed by there, Teddiursa had immediately spotted those two beautiful Treasure Pearls casually tossed on the ground.

The Pickup instinct hidden in its body let it recognize at a glance that these were two treasures!

So, while Mew was using Helping Hand to assist that old man, Teddiursa ran into the grass and picked up those two Treasure Pearls that nobody wanted.

Right now, Teddiursa was excitedly cradling the Treasure Pearls, thinking how pretty they were! If it gave them to D, D would definitely like them!

So, once the Heroic Expedition Team found a place to rest, Teddiursa handed the Treasure Pearls to Zapdos, asking it to deliver them back to the ranch.

*

Meanwhile, D, who had been about to Sleep, received another message from Steven.

[Steven: Change of plans, the two important items Team Magma and Team Aqua were trying to snatch have disappeared!]

Seeing this message, D instantly perked up.

He immediately caught the meaning in Steven's words.

Those two important items should be the Blue Orb and Red Orb.

[Stranger No.1: What happened?]

[Steven: I'm not sure either.]

He then told him everything that had happened today.

All the Team Magma and Team Aqua members had been arrested by the International Police, but those two items were nowhere to be found.

They had searched every member from head to toe and still hadn't found them.

The two important Treasure Pearls had vanished as if into thin air.

Reading these messages from Steven, D's first reaction was: no way, right?

Could it be that people from Team Magma and Team Aqua had used Teleport to move those two Treasure Pearls?

He immediately proposed this idea to Steven.

[Stranger No.1: Could it be a Psychic Type Pokemon used Teleport to transfer them somewhere else?]

[Steven: That should be impossible, because among the Pokemon Team Magma and Team Aqua sent out, there were no Psychic Type Pokemon; they were almost all Mightyena, Carvanha, and Sharpedo.]

Now that was a bit strange…

D couldn't help falling into deep thought.

But it was already very late, and Steven didn't ask any more questions.

[Steven: Thank you so much for your help. I won't bother you anymore tonight; tomorrow I'll continue investigating.]

[Stranger No.1: Okay.]

Exiting the Pokemon Forum, D began mulling over where those two Treasure Pearls had gone, but after thinking for a long time, he still couldn't figure it out.

However, D soon learned the answer to this question.

The next morning, Zapdos, with its shoulder bag, landed in the middle of the ranch.

Seeing this, D knew the Heroic Expedition Team had something else to send him.

He reached into the shoulder bag with practiced ease.

Very quickly, his hand brushed against two round objects.

D took them out, and the moment he clearly saw what he was holding, he froze on the spot.

[Red Orb: A Treasure Pearl that emits a red glow. It is said to have deep ties with the legends of the Hoenn Region.]

[Blue Orb: A Treasure Pearl that emits a blue glow. It is said to have deep ties with the legends of the Hoenn Region.]

---

"Whoa!"

Seeing these two things, D couldn't help but burst out in a curse.

No wonder Steven couldn't find the two Treasure Pearls; turns out they were picked up by the Heroic Expedition Team!

At this moment, D also received a call from Mew and Teddiursa.

He pressed the button to connect.

The little faces of Teddiursa and Mew appeared on the screen.

Through the Rotom Phone screen, Teddiursa saw the Treasure Pearls in D's hand.

It happily asked, "These are the treasures I found on the grass, D, do you like them?"

D: "..."

As he thought!

No wonder Steven didn't find them; it was because Teddiursa triggered its Pickup ability!

Seeing D not speaking, Teddiursa's little face was full of confusion.

It seemed a bit sad: "D doesn't like them?"

D snapped back to reality, shaking his head: "That's not the reason."

It's actually quite a coincidence; while he was contacting Steven, the Heroic Expedition Team showed up to help him.

But the problem is, you can't just pick up someone else's stuff!

D said with a headache: "Did you guys rescue an elderly couple on a mountaintop yesterday?"

Hearing this, Teddiursa and Mew were filled with shock.

"How do you know that?" their words were automatically translated by the Rotom Phone.

D: "...because I heard it from someone else. The person you guys met informed me, and although he didn't recognize Mew and Zapdos, I could tell it was you from his description."

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa and Mew suddenly realized.

So that's how it was!

"Then why doesn't D like them?" Teddiursa asked in confusion.

D explained: "Because they are very important to the people there; they are not unowned."

Teddiursa was shocked.

It thought they were unclaimed!

D had taught it not to take other people's things, and Teddiursa always remembered it.

But now, it made a mistake again...

By this time, Steven was probably already anxiously searching for the two Treasure Pearls, right?

Holding the two Treasure Pearls, D thought for a moment.

There's no need to hide his identity anymore.

He took out his phone and sent the message about having the Treasure Pearls to Steven.

Steven quickly replied.

[Steven: What?]

[Stranger No.1: Uh, this is a misunderstanding, let's add each other as treasure friends!]

With that, D sent his treasure friend account over.

He soon received a friend request from Steven.

Accepting it, D concisely explained the situation.

[Wing King: That Teddiursa from yesterday is my Pokemon, its ability is Pickup, and it thought the Treasure Pearls it found on the ground were unclaimed, so it picked them up.]

On the other side of the screen, Steven looked at the message sent by the other side and couldn't help falling into silence.

Indeed, such behavior matches the Pickup ability of a Pokemon.

However, Steven still seemed puzzled.

Were all three of those Pokemon yesterday owned by someone named [Wing King]?

How did they happen to pass by and offer help?

Could it be that the helper was near Mt. Pyre at the time?

Was all of this part of his plan?

But since Stranger No. 1 took the initiative to tell him this, it means the other side harbors no ill intent.

At that moment, Steven saw more messages from the other side.

[Wing King: Hello, let's have a formal introduction! I'm D, currently living in the Paldea Region, and I've traveled through the Hoenn Region before, really interested in its history and legends.]

Steven was astonished.

[Steven: You're saying you're in the Paldea Region now?]

The other side seemed to anticipate his curiosity and provided an explanation on their own.

[Wing King: Yes, Zapdos brought the two Treasure Pearls to the Paldea Region; long story short, it's a misunderstanding, send me your location, and I'll have my Pokemon deliver the Treasure Pearls to you.]

Steven still had many questions, but the most crucial thing now was the two Treasure Pearls.

He sent his location over.

D glanced at it and forwarded it to Mew and Teddiursa, then entrusted Zapdos to deliver the Treasure Pearls back.

Zapdos had no objections; it nodded and left.

[Wing King: Alright, my Pokemon have set off; they should reach you by the afternoon.]

Seeing this message, Steven hesitated to speak.

After a while, he expressed the question on his mind to the other side.

[Steven: Who are you?]

That Legendary Pokemon Zapdos isn't something an average Trainer can capture.

Moreover, based on their words, the three Pokemon seem to be traveling in the Hoenn Region without a Trainer with them...

Coupled with the mysterious aura vaguely revealed by the other side in recent days, Steven's curiosity and desire for knowledge peaked at that moment.

And finally, the reply came from that side.

Steven focused intently.

[Wing King: Me? I'm a Rancher.]

Steven: ?

---

---

---

---

# 456: Chapter 453: Mewtwo's Happiness, An Unexpected Visitor

Hoenn Region, Sootopolis City.

The gentle patter of rain fell from the sky, passed through the volcano's crater, and landed on the stone streets at the town center.

The Sootopolis Gym is situated within this city inside the volcano.

It is said that long ago, an underwater volcano erupted, then emerged above the sea.

The crater in the center of the land formed a lake due to accumulated rainwater.

Soon, people discovered the climate here was very warm, suitable for living, and thus, Sootopolis City was built inside the crater.

To find the two lost Treasure Pearls, Steven specially came to this city to seek the help of his friend Wallace.

However, it was at this time that he received information about the two Treasure Pearls.

At this moment, inside Wallace's private residence.

Staring at the reply on the screen, Steven fell into a long silence.

"A rancher?" Wallace, who had witnessed everything, spoke with surprise in his voice, showing an intrigued expression on his face: "It's quite an interesting matter."

Steven: "..."

Indeed! He never expected that the Treasure Pearls would travel overnight from the Hoenn Region all the way to the distant Paldea Region, a hint of coincidence in the absurdity.

Those three Pokemon, Teddiursa, Zapdos, and that pink little cat Pokemon.

Last night, Steven specifically looked up relevant information and finally found that the Pokemon might very well be the Mythical Pokemon Mew.

That mysterious stranger number 1 not only captured Zapdos, but also captured Mew, such an extremely rare Pokemon.

Steven found it hard to imagine that the person who accomplished these feats was a rancher.

It's not that he looked down upon the profession of a rancher, but because in his impression, no rancher...

Could ever capture Pokemon as rare as Mew and Zapdos.

"In that case, you don't have to worry anymore." Wallace displayed an elegant smile: "In any case, Team Magma and Team Aqua would never think that the two Treasure Pearls have already been transferred to the Paldea Region."

Upon mentioning this matter, Steven couldn't help but feel relieved.

During this period, he had researched many ancient texts and materials, knowing full well how terrifying a disaster the awakening of the two Super Ancient Pokemon would bring to the Hoenn Region.

Such an event had also occurred a thousand years ago.

At that time, if it weren't for the Sky God Rayquaza appearing from the clouds and stopping their conflict, the entire Hoenn Region might have been destroyed.

After that, humans living in the Hoenn Region built the Sky Pillar for Rayquaza.

Thinking of this, Steven replied: [I'll leave it to you then.]

And quickly came a reply from the other side.

[Wing King: No problem, once things settle, you can head directly to D Ranch in Porto Marinada Town, Paldea Region to pick them up.]

[Steven: Alright.]

Seeing the reply from the other side, Wallace chuckled softly: "D Ranch... It's getting more and more intriguing! I'd really like to see with my own eyes what the rancher who captured Legendary Pokemon is like."

Steven calmly thought: "Let's wait until all matters in the Hoenn Region are settled first."

Even though the Treasure Pearls are gone, Team Magma and Team Aqua have now obtained the precise locations of Groudon and Kyogre.

They might very well abandon using the Treasure Pearls to awaken them and instead use forceful means to awaken the two Super Ancient Pokemon.

After pondering for a moment, Steven used Poke Communications to hold a remote video conference with all the Gym Leaders in the Hoenn Region.

Once everyone was gathered, this Hoenn Champion sternly commanded:

"Next, I will head to Groudon's resting place to stop Team Magma's plans, while Wallace will go to the deep sea to capture Team Aqua.

If we fail, I ask you all to organize citizens to evacuate early.

The Weather Institute will monitor the movements of the two Super Ancient Pokemon, and if they awaken, we'll need your assistance then."

"Understood!"

"Got it!"

"Understood!"

"..."

Each Gym Leader responded, stating they would fulfill the duties of a Gym Leader.

A short video conference came to an end.

Steven then turned his gaze to his friend, nodding towards him: "I'll leave the Kyogre matter to you."

Wallace maintained a graceful smile on his face: "Understood, our Champion, sir~"

Steven: "..."

Everyone in the Hoenn Region began to take action.

Meanwhile, at the ranch.

D was chatting with Teddiursa about Mewtwo and those Cloned Pokemon.

"Don't worry! Mewtwo and the others have settled down at the World Tree, and the Explorer Team Guild and other facilities are completed. You'll be amazed the next time you come back!" D said with a smile.

Upon hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa, who were a bit worried, were immediately relieved.

"Does Mewtwo like the World Tree?" Mew asked.

"Probably likes it?" D recalled Mewtwo's behavior in recent days.

He initially wanted to tell this to Mew but suddenly remembered something, snapped his fingers, and said: "Oh, right, Mewtwo also has a Rotom Phone now, I'll send you its number so you can add it as a friend."

Saying that, D switched to the background, shared Mewtwo's Poke account.

Hearing this news, Mew and Teddiursa both showed delighted expressions.

They couldn't wait to add Mewtwo as a friend!

---

Before hanging up, Teddiursa glanced again at the two beautiful Treasure Pearls in D's hand.

They belonged to someone else; even if D liked them, he wouldn't seize them by force...

Teddiursa thought carefully and then patted its chest, promising: "D, next time I'll definitely find even prettier Treasure Pearls to give to you!"

D: "..."

He had no doubts about Teddiursa's treasure-hunting abilities; with Pickup ability + Super Luck + Ho-oh's Divine Blessing, even if it someday brought back an Origin Creation Plate of Arceus, it wouldn't be surprising.

But he was still moved by Teddiursa's sentiment.

D nodded, saying: "Hmm, I believe in you."

Teddiursa's eyes brightened immediately, a happy expression appeared on its face, and it nodded vigorously: "Hmm!"

D took a deep breath and began saying goodbye to the Heroic Expedition Team:

"Alright, I won't disturb you any longer. I need to go replenish supplies for Zapdos now. Do you have anything you'd like to eat? I'll prepare it for you, then have Zapdos bring it over."

Teddiursa wanted honey pudding and honey candy.

Mew wanted Pokeblock and Poffin.

D nodded: "Alright, no problem; I'll go make them now!"

After hanging up the phone, D asked Zapdos if it wanted anything to eat.

However, the proud Zapdos raised its head arrogantly, indicating it wasn't hungry.

The next moment, D heard the rumbling sound from Zapdos' stomach.

D: "..."

Zapdos bristled.

So D pretended he hadn't seen anything, turned around, and went back to his room to start making delicious food for Mew and Teddiursa.

Meanwhile, Mewtwo on the World Tree suddenly received a friend request.

It clicked open, rather surprised. When it saw the avatar of Teddiursa, Mew, and Zapdos, Mewtwo understood, then accepted the friend request.

The next second, a video call came through.

Mewtwo pressed to answer.

Immediately, the faces of Teddiursa and Mew appeared on the screen.

The two Pokemons excitedly greeted it.

"Mewtwo! You've reached the World Tree, haven't you? Do you like it there?" Mew asked happily.

Seeing Mew's adorable form floating to and fro on the screen, Mewtwo's heart softened.

It nodded lightly, replying: "Yes, here is the paradise we've been searching for all along."

"That's great!" Mew nodded excitedly: "Once we finish the commission here, we'll return, and then I'll take you to play at the secret base of the Heroic Expedition Team!"

Mewtwo: "Okay."

It paused and then asked: "Are you still traveling around like before?"

Mew responded with a nod: "That's right! There are still many Pokemons in this world that need help!"

Upon hearing this, Mewtwo felt a bit perplexed and puzzled: "Why do you want to help other Pokemons?"

Is it because doing so brings a sense of fulfillment?

Or is it to receive thanks and gifts from those Pokemons?

Yet Mew's answer surprised it.

"Because they need help!"

What? Mewtwo was slightly taken aback, was it just because those Pokemons needed help?

At this moment, Teddiursa beside added: "That's right! We want to make friends with everyone and invite them to the World Tree, so everyone can live together forever!"

"Playing together on the grassland every day, picking Berries together, sleeping in tree holes together, never feeling lonely and isolated, just imagining it is so blissful!"

"Blissful..." Mewtwo repeated these two words softly.

What is bliss? And what does bliss feel like?

Old worries ended, and new worries came.

After finishing the communication with Mew and Teddiursa, Mewtwo opened the search engine on the Rotom Phone, and typed in human words.

—What is bliss?

Soon, relevant answers popped up on the webpage.

[Bliss is a longer-term sense of life satisfaction, a joy that arises when individual needs are met, and a stable mood hoping to maintain the current state. It's a state of mind, an experience, and even a lifestyle.]

Satisfaction with life... hoping to maintain the current stable mood...

Is it satisfied with this current peaceful life? Mewtwo asked itself.

Mewtwo's mind conjured up images of the life on the World Tree in recent days.

Collaborating with Cloned Pokemons to cultivate fields, plant crops, harvest Berries, sleeping together on rocks...

Here, no humans want to harm them, no Team Rocket pursuing them, and no fear of hunger, war.

At this moment, perhaps it is blissful, Mewtwo thought.

*

Approaching noon, D finished making the food Mew and Teddiursa wanted.

He packed the honey pudding, soft honey candy, Poffin, and Pokeblock into Zapdos' spatial satchel around its neck.

He stopped only when the space inside was stuffed full.

In addition, D specially prepared a pack of Pokeblock catering to Electric Type Pokemon flavors for Zapdos.

Finally, D checked the Poke Balls inside the spatial satchel, discovering that recently Mew and Bear-type Pokemon hadn't used them to invite new companions to the World Tree.

Perhaps because they've been busy searching for Jirachi's wishes?

D put things in place, then stroked the slightly prickly feathers of Zapdos, saying:

---

"Everything is ready. Besides the food that Mew and Teddiursa want, I also prepared a pack of Pokeblocks for you. I didn't know what flavor you like, so I made them in a general flavor for Electric Type Pokemon."

Zapdos looked sharply at D standing in front of it.

Upon hearing that this human had specially prepared Pokeblocks for it, a hint of emotion flickered in Zapdos's heart.

It's impossible to say it wasn't moved.

But as a proud and sky-soaring Zapdos, it would never easily express its feelings.

Therefore, Zapdos turned its head and let out a sharp cry, signaling that it was leaving.

"Hmm, go ahead!" D said.

Zapdos glanced back, then flapped its wings and soared into the sky.

In almost the blink of an eye, it disappeared from D's sight.

Watching Zapdos's figure, D couldn't help but think of Mew and Teddiursa.

Unknowingly, the Heroic Expedition Team had traveled to many places.

It's quite moving when you think about it. Who would have imagined that at the beginning, Teddiursa was just a Pokemon that was good at using its cute appearance to trick farmers out of honey?

And now, it has become a kind-hearted Pokemon filled with a sense of justice.

D let out a sigh of admiration, just about to turn around and take Koraidon home to enjoy the air conditioning.

But at this moment, Koraidon seemed to have discovered something, turned its head, and looked in a particular direction.

"Arghiss?"

"What's wrong?"

Seeing Koraidon's reaction, D followed its gaze.

Then, he saw a mysterious Pokemon's figure within a patch of grass.

It was a Pokemon that resembled a small cat.

It had a light green body, with deep green patterns resembling overlapping leaves on its face, and similar leafy fur like a collar on its chest.

Once D got a good look, he was completely stunned.

Is that... Sprigatito?

Sprigatito, the Grass Type Starter Pokemon from the Paldea Region.

Huh? Did a wild Sprigatito wander into the ranch?

D's spirit was instantly lifted.

But soon, he noticed something was off.

The Sprigatito had a small bell, just like those from the Pleasant Goat series, hanging around its neck, clearly put there by someone.

"Not a wild Sprigatito?" D murmured.

The Sprigatito didn't seem afraid as it approached him and Koraidon.

When it got close enough, Sprigatito lifted its head to look at Koraidon, then at D.

"Meowha?"

D blinked in surprise, then took out his Rotom Phone and opened the translation feature.

"Could you repeat that?"

Sprigatito looked at the Rotom Phone and repeated, "Meowha?"

The Rotom Phone translated: "[Is this the Pokemon Tavern?]"

Huh? A Sprigatito looking for the Pokemon Tavern?

D was slightly taken aback and then asked, "Did you come here for something specific?"

Sprigatito: "Meowha!" (I heard you can help Pokemon here!)

"That's right." D nodded, looking at the Sprigatito. "Do you want me to help you find your Trainer?"

The Sprigatito, with the bell around its neck, might have gotten lost from its Trainer and heard about the Tavern from other Pokemon, leading it here to seek help. D imagined this poor Sprigatito's predicament in his mind.

However, in the next second, Sprigatito huffed and turned its head away.

"Meowha!" (That's not it!)

It had run away deliberately because its Trainer was just too biased, neglecting it after capturing new Pokemon!

Sprigatito was angry, so it decided to run away from home. After hearing there was a tavern here that could help Pokemon with problems, it came straight over.

Hearing Sprigatito's reply, D was quite surprised.

Not here to have him help find its Trainer, so what did it come for?

Thinking this, he asked directly.

Sprigatito: "Meowha?" (I want to know how to make my Trainer only like me as their Pokemon?)

D was momentarily stunned.

Seeing the human's reaction, Sprigatito thought he seemed a bit foolish.

So it proceeded to recount its entire story.

"Meowha, meowha!" (Yuki is just too much, catching new Pokemon and then ignoring me, only caring for those annoying Pokemon! I ran away from home for days, and he hasn't even noticed!)

D instantly understood.

"So, you want your Trainer to only like you as their Pokemon?"

If he remembered correctly, Sprigatito as a Pokemon tend to have a lonely nature, full of possessiveness toward their Trainers, and get easily jealous.

Sprigatito nodded affirmatively, looking at him expectantly: "Meowha!" (That's right! Can you help? If you can help me, I'll give you my most treasured item!)

D: "..."

He felt like saying don't bother; he was just like that person named Yuki.
