Sure enough, he's not the only one in this world facing the problem of having too many Pokemon getting jealous of each other.

---

---

---

---

# 457: Chapter 454: The Disguise Plan

"So, your request is to make sure the Trainer only knows about one Pokemon of yours, is that right?"

In the living room, D sat on the sofa alongside Koraidon, looking across at Sprigatito and asked, unsure.

Sprigatito leisurely licked its paw and nodded, "Meow-ha!" (That's right!)

Witnessing this scene, Koraidon beside him was a bit speechless.

"Ah-gah-sizzle..." (I feel you asked the wrong person...)

It thought to itself that D hadn't found the answer to this problem yet.

D: "..."

Indeed, what Koraidon said was completely correct.

He often encountered situations where Pokemons got jealous of each other!

Sprigatito looked at the big Pokemon next to it, "Meow-ha?" (What?)

"Nothing, ha ha!" D hurriedly jested, covering Koraidon's mouth.

Sprigatito's little face immediately filled with suspicion.

"Meow-ha...?" (Can you really help here?)

"Even I'm not sure, I can only try to think of a few solutions for you." D said.

Sprigatito looked at D, "Meow-ha?" (What solutions?)

D began to help it come up with ideas.

"For instance, you need to be proactive, bravely express your feelings to the Trainer, or offer your Helping Hand when he needs it. Maybe this way, he'll find you to be a smart and thoughtful Pokemon and like you more." D suggested.

But speaking of which, Sprigatito is known among all Pokemons for its mercurial temperament and jealousy.

If the Trainer favors other Pokemons besides it, then Sprigatito is very likely to throw a tantrum.

Then it'll sulk, ignore the Trainer, refuse to follow orders; in more serious cases, it might even run away from home.

Hearing this, Sprigatito showed a pensive expression on its face.

"Meow-ha?" (Will the Trainer only like me as the only Pokemon then?) it asked.

Koraidon nearby looked full of words yet stopped short.

D couldn't guarantee: "Not necessarily, but your Trainer might like you more, far beyond other Pokemons."

Sprigatito thought seriously for a while, then spoke: "Meow-ha!" (Got it! I'm going to try it, and if it succeeds, I'll send you my treasure!)

Having said this, without waiting for D's response, Sprigatito jumped off the sofa, ran outside, and swiftly vanished into the grass.

D slightly sighed in his heart.

Fortunately, he didn't have Pokemons as possessive as Sprigatito at home.

"Ah-gah-sizzle..."

Watching Sprigatito go off, Koraidon pondered for a while.

It turned its head towards D and suddenly asked, "Ah-gah-sizzle?"

Through the Translation Nuts Cake, D immediately understood what Koraidon was asking.

It was asking: Who is D's favorite Pokemon?

D: "..."

He displayed a polite smile: "I equally like all of you."

However, this wasn't the answer Koraidon was looking for.

"Ah-gah-sizzle..." It pushed further, "Ah-gah-sizzle?" (Which Pokemon does D like more then?)

"Equally liked." D still didn't change his answer.

Seeing this, Koraidon changed its line of questioning.

"Ah-gah-sizzle?" (Does D like Cyclizar?)

"Of course." Hearing Koraidon's mention of Cyclizar, although a bit surprised, D explained: "For a Human Trainer, the first Pokemon in life is always meaningful, and Cyclizar was the first Pokemon I encountered here."

"Before you came to the ranch, it was Cyclizar that accompanied me in work. During that time, the ranch was poor, and I had no money. Cyclizar didn't disdain me, squishing with me in a shabby little log cabin every night to Sleep."

Koraidon instantly caught the meaning in D's words.

It didn't know what to feel at the moment.

If you talk about jealousy, it had been through such a thing before because it was Cyclizar.

If it didn't feel anything, that's not possible either, for it is now both Cyclizar and isn't Cyclizar.

Koraidon felt both happy and jealous.

Happy that Cyclizar is immensely significant to D.

Jealous that the Cyclizar D talked about isn't the present one but the one from the past.

Borrowing words from Slowking, it felt quite complicated.

Watching Koraidon daydream, D continued:

"However, each of you Pokemon to me is different, just like in human society within a family, there are fathers, mothers, and children. Every person herein is essential for the family, it's everyone together that defines a family."

"Ah-gah-sizzle... What a complex matter."

Koraidon only felt its head spinning, drooped its head, and eventually decided to give up thinking, lie down on the soft grass basking in the sun, watching white clouds float across the sky.

As for D, he turned to the kitchen and began to prepare today's lunch for the Pokemons.

Slugma, living in the heating pipes, obediently crawled over, ready to help D cook and boil rice.

Seeing Slugma approach automatically, D crouched down, feeding it Berry and Pokeblock.

Slugma was in a great mood, couldn't help but use Lava Plume to express its happiness at the moment.

"Boom boom!"

Two streams of white smoke plumed out from the chimney, sending a few wild Squawkabilly resting on the rooftop fleeing.

---

"Eat, eat!" D said as he used a hook to pull out the scorched Berry core from Slugma's body.

Looking at the slightly narrow ventilation duct and then thinking of the vast World Tree, D asked:

"By the way, Slugma, do you want to go live over at the World Tree?"

Team Magma immediately stopped eating the Berries and looked up, puzzled: "Maku?"

"Because I think this place is too small. You can only move around in the ducts every day, just thinking about it feels uncomfortable." D explained.

Initially, it was alright, but now that the World Tree has grown, everyone is living there, and only Slugma remains in this place, which seems a bit unfair to it.

Moreover, with air conditioning and a fireplace at home, Slugma's heating isn't really needed during winter.

However, upon hearing this, Slugma quickly shook its head.

"Maku!"

Seeing this, D was a bit surprised: "You want to stay in this place?"

Sluga happily nodded: "Maku!"

"Why? There's a lot more space to move around by the World Tree, and there are many Pokemon to befriend there," D asked.

Team Magma: "Maku!"

At this moment, the Phone Rotom automatically helped with translation.

"Slugma says [Because D and Pawmot are here, it wants to stay here too] Roto!"

"Maku～" Slugma happily nodded, indicating it was correct.

After a brief moment of surprise, D quickly understood what Slugma meant.

He no longer mentioned living at the World Tree to Slugma, but instead smiled and said:

"Alright, in that case, let's continue living here together!"

"Maku～"

The flame on Slugma's body burned even stronger.

D stood up and began to focus on cooking.

He set the Rotom Phone to airplane mode, letting it float by, playing the Paldea Region's midday news.

Just then, a message from Lono came through.

[Lono: Hello! Hehe, have you eaten yet?]

Seeing Lono's name, D's heart skipped a beat, immediately recalling the words his sister Indeedee had said before.

He pondered while replying.

[Wing King: I'm in the middle of it, what's up? Suddenly contacting me at this time?]

[Lono: Well... mainly, I wanted to ask if you're available in June. There will be a super luxurious summer festival in Levincia City, and since you have always been hosting me, which makes me feel a bit guilty, I'd like to invite you and Koraidon to come over for a day!]

Invite him and Koraidon to Levincia City for the summer festival?

If his sister Indeedee hadn't told him about that, perhaps he wouldn't have felt anything.

But now D suddenly had a premonition that maybe what Indeedee said was true?

Does Lono have a crush on him?

"No, it shouldn't be possible."

D subconsciously denied it in his mind; if it was a misunderstanding, it would be awkward and he'd end up looking like a clueless guy.

After contemplating for a moment, D decided to act as if nothing had happened and continue interacting as usual.

He calculated the timing and initially wanted to agree, but after thinking carefully, he might not be available in June.

Because by then, the World Tree would be fully mature, and he would need to help move it to the sky with Giratina and Mewtwo.

Moreover, according to the information provided by Steven, a major event might be happening in the Hoenn Region within those two months, so he needed to stay at the ranch to see if Steven needed any help.

With that in mind, D replied.

[Wing King: June will be quite busy at the ranch—harvesting crops and preparing seeds for the autumn season afterward.]

[Lono: Ah... that's a pity. There's going to be a big fireworks display in Levincia City, and the Levincia Gym will be closed for a day. I really wanted to invite you and Koraidon to come and watch!]

Fireworks display?

D raised an eyebrow.

[Wing King: There's also a fireworks display here in Porto Marinada Town that night. People gather by the seaside to watch the fireworks and spend the last day of summer together. By the way, is this considered a traditional festival in the Paldea Region?]

[Lono: Yes, it should be! To my knowledge, many coastal cities hold such parties. Will you bring Koraidon to participate?]

[Wing King: Of course, but the town's fireworks festival might not be as lively as in the big city.]

[Lono: I envy you! The beach in Levincia City is always packed, and last year I couldn't even get in with Bellibolt and the others. This year, I don't even want to go, ah! I'm probably going to have another boring holiday at home!]

Seeing this message, D couldn't help but laugh. He could almost imagine the look on Lono's face as she said this.

Thinking about it, he replied:

[Wing King: Porto Marinada Town isn't as crowded, so if you're bored alone in Levincia, you can come join us here.]

Lono seemed pleasantly surprised.

[Lono: Eh? Really?]

[Wing King: Of course, the more the merrier.]

Lono answered very straightforwardly.

[Lono: Great! We'll definitely come to join!]

D replied with a simple "Okay."

After that, Lono got busy and, after a brief goodbye, ended the chat.

Meanwhile, D began to think about how he should assist Steven if needed when the time comes.

If Steven reached out to him, it meant the situation in the Hoenn Region had become something even the Champion couldn't stop.

By then, only Legendary Pokemon acting might resolve it.

Now in the internet age, with such a big event, surely some brave reporters and daring Trainers would come to report on the situation.

D wasn't keen on revealing his true identity.

If he was caught on camera, it might spread across the internet overnight, and some Trainers who had seen him and challenged his Trials might recognize him.

If Calyrex and Giratina acted, then everyone would know those Pokemon are at D Ranch, with him.

The ranch would then be overrun by enthusiastic people.

After a brief contemplation, D had an idea.

To maintain the peace and tranquility of the ranch, he needed to disguise himself a bit.

This way, they can continue their calm life without being disturbed and prevent any ill-intentioned thoughts.

As for the method...

He had an example of a Pokemon at home that could serve as a reference.

Like Ogerpon, wearing a mask or hood to cover the face.

That way, with a mask on, he would be the Masked Man saving Hoenn from disaster.

Without the mask, he'd be just an ordinary Rancher.

Aside from some insiders, no one would be able to connect the two identities.

Much like in the anime with Blaziken Mask Meyer and Professor Kukui.

Meyer is the father of Clemont and Bonnie.

This man, who loves his children, is a store owner by day.

But secretly, he disguises himself as Blaziken Mask to fight against Team Flare's attacks.

Similarly, Professor Kukui is a Teacher and a Professor at the Pokemon School.

But secretly, he turns into the Royal Masked Man, engaging in passionate Pokemon battles at the Royal Dome.

Decided, this is how he would act outside!

D decided to find someone to help custom-make a mask for disguise.

Or not, he didn't really need an outsider.

Mewtwo, maxed out with handcrafting skill points, would do just fine.

So after finishing lunch, D contacted Mewtwo through the Phone Rotom and shared his plan via video call.

After listening, Mewtwo fell silent for a moment and then, after a while, responded:

"Okay, but I'll need materials."

When building the castle on New Island, Mewtwo had tried concentrating Psychic power to create objects from thoughts.

But for some reason, perhaps insufficient power or something else, it failed.

Ultimately, it had to use the materials from Team Rocket's research facility to build that high-tech castle.

With years of learning, Mewtwo had a basic understanding of human affairs.

Compared to a complex castle, using Psychic power to craft a mask seemed much simpler.

---

---

---

---

# 458: Chapter 455: The Runaway Sprigatito

As soon as he decided to make the mask, D went onto a shopping app and ordered the materials needed to make the mask that same afternoon.

When the delivery Pelipper arrived at the ranch, it was already three days later.

That day was May 2nd, 200 in the League calendar.

The Pelipper had set off from Cabo Poco Town, wobbling its way down to the ranch.

This pace would be considered slow even for a normal delivery.

Under normal circumstances, with the speed of a Pokemon like Pelipper, it should take about a day to get from Cabo Poco Town to Porto Marinada Town.

The delivery Pelipper that landed at the ranch looked a bit disheveled, dirty all over.

D was somewhat surprised,

and after some inquiries, he learned that the Pelipper had been attacked by wild Pokemon on its way and had struggled hard to safeguard this delivery.

Pelipper looked rather shaken, deciding it would never take the back roads again.

D: "..."

He took the package from Pelipper and then brought out some potion to treat the injuries on its body.

Expressing its deep gratitude, Pelipper then set off on its return journey.

D, on the other hand, took the mask-making materials to the World Tree and found Mewtwo.

He handed the package containing the materials over to Mewtwo.

Mewtwo took a glance at it.

The next second, its deep voice resonated within D's mind: "What shape do you want it to be?"

What shape to make...

The first thing D thought of was Meyer's Blaziken Mask.

It was a mask designed with Blaziken as the original model, including a cape, tights, and other clothing besides the mask.

Then, why not make it based on a Pokemon.

The only question was which Pokemon to use as a model?

D glanced at Koraidon beside him, who had come with him to the World Tree.

"No, it won't work. Koraidon would be too obvious." D shook his head instinctively.

Because, so far, in this world, there are only two Koraidon, one at the Area Zero Research Institute and the other is right here with him.

Even the Pokedex doesn't have the ecological details of Koraidon recorded yet.

Then, what about Cyclizar?

In the Paldea Region, Cyclizar is a widely known Pokemon and a crucial mode of transportation for its people.

Since everyone has Cyclizar, wearing a Cyclizar Mask wouldn't make him stand out so much.

Thinking of this, D had his answer in mind.

"Cyclizar it is!"

Mewtwo, having seen Cyclizar before, calmly nodded at these words, then, with a small mental movement, the materials for the mask began to levitate under its Telekinesis.

Then, a miraculous scene unfolded.

The materials, under Mewtwo's control, started transforming at a speed visible to the naked eye.

Ultimately, it turned into a mask shaped like Cyclizar, with black and dark green as the dominant colors.

"Done." Mewtwo said.

D glanced at it and was quite satisfied, then thanked Mewtwo, and departed with Koraidon.

With this, he wouldn't have to worry about revealing his identity anymore!

From now on, with the mask, he is the Cyclizar Mask, without it just an ordinary rancher.

D and Koraidon left the Reversed World.

But as soon as they returned to the ranch, D spotted a familiar figure sitting on the grass.

It was that Sprigatito from not long ago!

D munched on a few Translation Nuts Cake stored in his pocket and went up to ask, "So, did my advice work last time?"

Sprigatito puffed up its cheeks and relayed to D and Koraidon what had happened after it went back last time.

"Meow-ha!" (It's so infuriating! What I did had no effect at all, and Xiaodong still cares for other Pokemon! Not to mention that while I was away from home, he caught another new Pokemon! He doesn't care about me at all!)

D: "..."

If that's the case, that trainer is overstepping.

He stood on the grass, frowning, and asked, "So, did you run away from home again today?"

Sprigatito responded angrily, "Meow-ha!" (I'm never going back!)

Goodness!

D asked in surprise, "Could it be you want to abandon your trainer?"

He had seen trainers abandon Pokemon, but never Pokemon abandon their trainers!

In this world, trainers discarding Pokemon is not unheard of.

Like Shinji from the anime, he only captures Pokemon with high potential and strength, releasing them if they fail to meet his standards.

Even a Pokemon like Charmander could be abandoned.

But a captured Pokemon abandoning its trainer, that's a rarity indeed.

Sprigatito was evidently heated, it replied loudly, "Meow-ha!" (Exactly!)

D was about to say something, but just then, a boy approximately ten years old suddenly appeared at the entrance of the ranch.

Sprigatito, with its keen sense of smell, caught the boy's scent, and without hesitation turned and bolted into the grass, disappearing quickly.

Since Sprigatito was completely green and nearly blended into the grass, the boy didn't notice it and turned his attention to D standing there.

"Uh, excuse me, have you seen a Sprigatito around here?"

D: "..."

He glanced around, only seeing Butterfree, Combee, Cyclizar and a few other Pokemon—but no Sprigatito in sight.

---

Looking at the boy, he seemed to be the Trainer of the Sprigatito.

D noticed the anxious expression on the other's face and, after a brief thought, replied:

"Sprigatito? I did see one around here. It was still nearby this morning. I saw it drinking water and eating Berries by the river. Are you its Trainer?"

When he heard that Sprigatito had been in the area, the boy's eyes lit up, and he visibly breathed a sigh of relief.

"Yes, I am its Trainer. It's just that recently, I don't know why, Sprigatito often sneaks out, and takes several days to come back…"

At this point, the boy looked troubled.

It was hard not to think that this kid was really clueless; his Pokemon was about to abandon him, and he still didn't realize it.

Although it was most likely that Sprigatito was just speaking out of anger.

"Don't worry yet, maybe that Sprigatito will come back here later, because there aren't any other homes in the area. It might come around when it gets hungry. Would you like to wait here for a bit?" D asked.

"Oh? Can I?" The boy was very grateful: "Thank you so much, Mr. Rancher, I'm Kodong from Cabo Poco Town! Currently traveling around the Paldea Region with my Pokemon!"

D smiled slightly.

He handed the Cyclizar Mask to Koraidon to keep it for him, then invited Kodong into the living room and poured him a glass of water.

Kodong had followed Sprigatito's tracks all the way here, and in his rush, he had forgotten to drink water.

He picked up the glass and gulped it down, then said gratefully, "Sorry to bother you!"

"No need to be so polite." D asked, "If it's convenient, could you tell me what's going on with that Sprigatito?"

Meanwhile, the Sprigatito that had sneaked away just now, tiptoed to the door, hid under the eaves, and perked its ears to eavesdrop on the conversation inside.

A few Pokemon in the ranch noticed it, looking over in confusion.

"Ah, of course!" Kodong nodded and then detailed his situation with Sprigatito.

"This is my first year traveling. I received a starter Pokemon, Sprigatito, from the principal of the Paldea Academy. Later, we attended classes together, ate together, and participated in outdoor activities together. Everything was fine then."

"But later on, for some reason, Sprigatito suddenly started throwing tantrums and then took advantage of my inattention to run away from home. So far, Sprigatito has run away five times."

D: "..."

Sprigatito is known for its strong possessiveness as a Pokemon.

He nodded thoughtfully and asked, "When did Sprigatito first start throwing tantrums with you?"

Upon hearing this, Kodong was slightly stunned and then fell into reminiscing.

"Probably about three months ago! At that time, I had just become a Trainer and was eager to head into the wild after leaving Paldea Academy to capture a second Pokemon."

"Back then, on the grassland outside of Mesagoza City, I used a Poke Ball to capture a Bounsweet."

"Oh, yes! It was after that, that Sprigatito started having tantrums!"

This answer was within D's expectations, and he asked again, "And then what happened?"

Kodong thought for a moment and continued, "After that… I captured a Hoppip, Shinx, Rookidee, Marill, and Psyduck."

D incredulously asked, "All in one day?"

Kodong quickly shook his head, "No, it was within a week!"

While saying this, he looked very excited, "I've been looking forward to this day for a very long time, becoming a Trainer and capturing all kinds of Pokemon!"

D: "..."

"I think I might know why Sprigatito keeps running away." He said.

Kodong was stunned, "Oh?"

"A Sprigatito's possessive nature towards its Trainer is very strong. They don't like sharing their Trainer with other Pokemon."

D looked at the boy in front of him and continued to ask:

"If I'm not mistaken, after capturing those Pokemon, you made sandwiches for them and petted them, right?"

"Yes." Kodong nodded, because doing so could quickly build a relationship and become friends with the captured Pokemon…

At this point, Kodong paused, as if he suddenly realized something, and incredulously asked:

"Could it be that Sprigatito ran away because I captured so many Pokemon?"

D nodded affirmatively.

"Pokemon have very rich emotions just like us humans. They'll be happy when their Trainer praises them, and feel sad and upset when their Trainer neglects them." He said.

Upon hearing this, Kodong showed a pensive expression on his face.

"Sometimes, Trainers need to learn to restrain themselves, because there's only one Trainer, and once too many Pokemon are captured, it's very hard for a single person to take care of them all."

"In this situation, there will definitely be Pokemon that get overlooked, just like Sprigatito."

"So, Sprigatito chose to run away because it felt neglected…" Kodong muttered.

Sprigatito has already run away five times, staying out longer each time.

If this continues, if he keeps capturing more Pokemon, would there come a day when Sprigatito completely gives up on him and never comes back?

At the thought of this, Kodong suddenly felt very scared.

---

Scenes of his past moments with Sprigatito floated into his mind.

At their first meeting, he immediately chose the adorable Sprigatito over Fuecoco and Quaxly.

They attended classes together at Paldea Academy, ran on the playground until they were exhausted, and then headed to the cafeteria to enjoy meals together. At night, Sprigatito would even accompany him to sleep in the dormitory.

D had noticed the curious Pokemon outside the door and knew well that Sprigatito must be eavesdropping there right now.

He exchanged a glance with Koraidon outside, then turned to the boy in front of him, who seemed downcast, and asked:

"Is that Sprigatito very important to you?"

"Of course, it's very important!" Xiaodong answered without hesitation.

Outside, Sprigatito's heart suddenly trembled upon hearing this.

"Meow-ha..."

"If you saw Sprigatito again, what would you like to say to it?" D asked again.

Guided by D, Xiaodong continued:

"I would apologize. I've been neglecting it lately, and from now on, I promise to care more about Sprigatito's feelings, never letting it feel sad again!"

D smiled and nodded, looking towards the door, he said, "Did you hear that, Sprigatito."

"Eh?!" Xiaodong was stunned, he looked at D, then quickly turned his head in the direction of D's gaze.

Then, he saw his Sprigatito awkwardly walk in from outside.

"Sprigatito!" Seeing Sprigatito, Xiaodong was thrilled. He stood up and ran to Sprigatito, then hugged it tightly, "I'm sorry, I've been neglecting you all this while! I won't do that again!"

Seeing its Trainer apologizing, Sprigatito looked at him blankly, then responded, "Meow-ha!"

*

"In short, that's what happened."

D briefly explained to the now calm Xiaodong everything that happened after Sprigatito ran to the ranch.

"So that's how it is..." Xiaodong murmured, then earnestly thanked D, "Thank you very much for taking care of Sprigatito, Mr. D!"

"No need to thank me." D waved his hand dismissively, he looked at Sprigatito, and then said to Xiaodong, "Sprigatito has something to say to you, do you need me to help translate?"

"What?"

D smiled and said, "It says it really likes you and wants to stay with you always."

Upon hearing this, Xiaodong was stunned on the spot.

"So, don't ever neglect Sprigatito's thoughts again."

D turned around, waved his hand, and said, "Goodbye, and have a great journey!"

On the small country road outside the ranch, Xiaodong and Sprigatito stood there, staring at the gradually receding figure.

Only after a long time did they avert their gaze.

"Sprigatito, let's continue our journey too!" Xiaodong said.

Sprigatito happily nodded, "Meow-ha!"

The silhouette of the boy and his cat gradually disappeared at the end of the road.

Meanwhile, D glanced at the rounded object Koraidon handed him, slightly taken aback.

"What is this?"

"Ah-gah-ss." (It's something Sprigatito left behind.) Koraidon answered.

Upon hearing this, D immediately recalled the treasure Sprigatito had mentioned earlier.

He focused his gaze, and detailed information about the rounded object emerged from his mind.

[Miracle Seeds: A special gift from Sprigatito filled with its gratitude, sure to grow into a unique crop if planted.]

---

---

---

---

# 459: Chapter 456: Breeding Treehouse and Dragonite

"Thank the seed... it will surely grow into a special crop..."

Looking at the seed in front of him, D couldn't help but feel very surprised.

"Ah-ga-sii?"

Koraidon tilted its head to the side, looking somewhat confused.

D glanced at it, then put away the seed and said, "Anyway, as long as it's planted, we'll find out."

Taking advantage of the time with nothing to do, D fetched a shovel, dug a hole in the pasture, planted the seed, and covered it back with soil.

Finally, D called Calyrex over.

Calyrex casually waved its hand, and a rich blue light surged from the soft soil.

The light filtered through the cracks in the soil, as if something mysterious was hidden below.

In the light, the seed began to change at a speed visible to the naked eye, transforming from its original seed form into a tender sapling.

The sapling kept growing and eventually became a tree over five meters tall, with a sturdy trunk.

At first glance, it seemed no different from ordinary trees.

But looking closely, D discovered a strange tree hole on the trunk.

The entrance to the tree hole was round, and the internal space was neither too big nor too small, as if it were dug out by a Greedent Pokemon using its claws.

The walls and ground inside the tree hole were polished smooth, not at all like the roughness D had imagined.

This thing was the special crop into which the seed had transformed!

[Breeding Tree House: A special crop mutated from a particular seed, its internal space perfectly simulating a natural environment.

If a Pokemon Egg is placed inside, the Breeding Tree House will absorb nutrients from the earth and nature to nurture it, ensuring a steady incubation.

Pokemon hatched from the Breeding Tree House will have significantly enhanced health and strength at birth, greatly boosting their potential and understanding.]

"Huh?"

Upon seeing the detailed information of this tree, D was stunned on the spot.

Breeding Tree House...

Perfectly simulating the natural environment, providing energy and nutrients to Pokemon Eggs, isn't this essentially an automatic incubation chamber?!

And what's more astounding is that Pokemon hatched from this tree will have their potential and understanding greatly enhanced.

What does that mean?

In game terms, it means the Pokemon born from the Breeding Tree House are all top-tier Pokemon!

High understanding means they can easily learn new moves.

Exceptional potential means their future path can extend very far.

This tree, with its innate breeding sanctuary body, is perfect for opening a Breeding Tree House!

If he opened a Pokemon Breeding House, wouldn't he earn tons of money?

"Unfortunately, this tree has no effect on Pokemon that have already hatched." D murmured.

If only it had appeared earlier, Eevee Eggs and Dragonite Eggs from Geeta could all have been placed inside to be incubated by the tree.

D felt quite regretful, yet it didn't matter.

With so many Pokemon in the pasture and on the World Tree, eventually new sparks will be kindled between the Pokemon, leading to the laying of eggs.

By then, this tree can be put to good use.

However, that day seemed far down the road.

D felt an itch of anticipation, eager to witness the magical effects of this tree.

He looked at Koraidon beside him.

It seems Paradox Pokemon in the game can't lay eggs, and Koraidon hasn't shown romantic interest in other Pokemon.

Noticing D's gaze, Koraidon was a bit puzzled: "...Ah-ga-sii?"

"Nothing." D narrowed his eyes, turning to find his Dragonite in the pasture.

He remembered Dragonite once had an affection for another Dragonite.

And that Dragonite had come along to live on the World Tree with Dragon Island.

If his Dragonite was inclined, D would indeed encourage it to pursue love.

Because of his immense curiosity.

Wondering about the potential height that could be reached by the offspring of a Dragonite that ate a Storm Cluster and reached Champion Level, coupled with incubation in the Breeding Tree House.

With this in mind, D found his Dragonite, ready to probe its thoughts.

Dragonite was there, following Rotom's orders, packing Poffins and Pokeblocks into the bag.

Seeing D coming, it happily greeted him.

"Aw-woo～"

"How have you been feeling lately?" D asked.

These days, Dragonite occasionally visited the Land of Dragons on the World Tree, dropping by Dragonite Island to see old companions.

Now, traveling from the pasture to Dragonite Island, with Dragonite's speed, only takes three minutes for a round trip, very convenient.

"Aw-woo!" Dragonite expressed its happiness right away, but quickly, it looked at D with a puzzled expression, seemingly questioning why he asked: "Aw-woo?"

"Ahem." D approached Dragonite, whispering: "I remember the Dragonite you used to like is also on Dragonite Island, right? Have you been going there lately to see it?"

"Aw-woo!" Dragonite quickly shook its head.

It was going to Dragonite Island clearly to deliver Berries and Poffins to the Dratini and Dragonair there!

Because it too had been cared for by the adult Dragonites on Dragonite Island, now with time and excess Poffins, it rationally took care of the little dragons.

Every time it went over, the Dratini and Dragonair would cling to it, asking for stories about the outside world.

---

The Dragonite would then tell them about its happy life on the ranch and its delivery experiences.

After hearing these stories, the little dragons all said that when they grew up, they also wanted to go out and deliver goods.

D: "..."

Oh no, it seems that the little Dratini and Dragonair on Dragonite Island are being led astray by Dragonite.

"Aowu?" Dragonite was a bit puzzled.

D finally snapped back to reality and said, "Oh, so that's how it is! I thought you went to visit that Dragonite!"

Dragonite: "Aowu..."

"Never mind, go ahead with your work!"

He realized that his own Dragonite seemed to have lost that kind of thought.

Well, in that case, let's leave it at that!

It's best to leave everything to Dragonite's own decision.

Thinking of this, D reached out to stroke Dragonite's head.

Dragonite affectionately hugged back, then continued packing Poffins and Pokeblocks.

Seeing this, D asked, "Are you going to Dragonite Island?"

Dragonite happily nodded: "Aowu!"

It had previously promised the Dragonair and Dratini there to bring them delicious Poffins.

D pondered for a moment, then found the Shiny Dratini sunbathing alone on the grass and handed it to Dragonite.

"Take it to play on Dragonite Island for a while!" D said.

There's only one Dratini on the ranch now, compared to here, Dragonite Island is more suitable for the growth of Dratini.

Because there are many Dratini and Dragonair there to play with, and adult Dragonites to take care of them.

Dragonite looked at this Dratini and readily agreed.

"Aowu～"

The Dratini looked fearfully at D, its small face full of panic and unease, with tears welling up in its big eyes.

"Wu?" Did D not want it anymore?

D was slightly taken aback upon seeing this scene.

The Dragon Power allowed him to quickly sense Dratini's thoughts.

D shook his head helplessly and said, "No, I just want you to go play by the World Tree for a while, there are lots of Dratini like you over there."

Hearing that D didn't want to abandon it, Dratini finally relaxed a bit.

It nervously asked, "Wu?"

Can it return to the ranch later?

D smiled and nodded: "Of course, you can come back with Dragonite when it returns."

Dratini's worries finally subsided.

"See you later then!"

D waved at them.

After that, Dragonite happily took Dratini away.

They passed through the lake, entered the Reversed World, and flew to the World Tree.

Finally, they took the World Tree elevator and smoothly arrived at the layer where Dragonite Island is located.

Dragonite held Dratini with its claws, landed in front of the lake at the center of Dragonite Island, and happily greeted everyone.

"Aowu!"

The Dratini and Dragonair in the lake swam over at the sight.

Dragonite took out Poffins and Pokeblocks from its bag to share with them.

Seeing these delicious foods brought by Dragonite, the Dratini and Dragonair were overjoyed.

The Shining Dratini on the side curiously observed these unfamiliar Dratini.

Seeing this, Dragonite placed it into the lake as well, and then introduced it to the other Dratini: "Aowu aowu!"

The Dratini and Dragonair looked at this unfamiliar Dratini.

"Wu?"

"Aowu."

"Wu～"

"Aowu～"

Soon, they all smiled warmly, inviting the Shining Dratini to join them in eating Poffins, and once they finished, they'd go practice catching fish in the seawater together!

These warm and friendly Dratini dispersed Shining Dratini's inner worries.

It blinked at these companions, happily agreeing.

"Wu!"

At this time, a few Dragonites were drawn over by the aroma of Poffins and Pokeblocks.

They stared with big eyes at Dragonite's parcel.

Dragonite, noticing their gaze, immediately covered its bag tightly.

"Aowu!" (If you want to eat, go get a job!)

Upon hearing this, the wild Dragonites blinked.

This wasn't the first time they heard the word "job".

Even before, they heard the term from other working Dragonites.

Those Dragonites left Dragon Island every morning, ventured to a human ranch, and only returned by evening.

And often, when they returned to Dragon Island, they always brought back a large box of delicious Poffins.

They had received a few pieces before.

The Poffins were so delicious that they couldn't forget the taste, waiting every afternoon on Dragon Island for the working Dragonites to return and hoping to get a piece or two from them.

At first, it was fine; the working Dragonites would share with them.

But later, they stopped sharing, saying, "We worked hard to earn this, and giving it to you would leave us hungry!"

Thinking of this, the wild Dragonites looked at Dragonite and asked, "Aowu?" (Can we also work on the ranch to earn Poffins like you?)

Dragonite nodded: "Aowu!" (That's right!)

Hearing this, the wild Dragonites looked at each other and made up their minds.

So, five minutes later, dozens of Dragonites flew out of the Reversed World.

D, who was weeding in the fields, only noticed the scene after Cyclizar alerted him.

He saw those Dragonites flying into the air, looking around, and finally setting their sights on him.

A few seconds later, more than ten Dragonites landed beside the fields.

---

D: "?"

The leading Dragonite said confidently: "Roar!"

The Rotom Phone next to D automatically flew out and translated for him: "It says [We want to work too] Rotom!"

D: "..."

Wow, the Dragonites are all mobilized.

D had plenty of experience with situations like this.

After confirming the Dragonites' intentions, he contacted his own Dragonite using the Rotom Phone to quickly come over and train these Dragonites.

Then, D equipped each of them with a Phone Rotom to get them familiar with their work partners.

*

Dragonite Delivery Company welcomed twelve new employees.

The next morning, a large group of Dragonites, carrying satchels and Rotom Phones, flew out of the ranch in waves, heading to various parts of Paldea to deliver goods to customers.

D stood in the ranch watching this astonishing scene, unable to hold back a sigh.

There were only a few dozen Dragonites on Dragonite Island, and now most of them were probably working in his ranch.

According to the Dragonites, even the young Dratini and Dragonair were looking forward to growing up and working on the ranch to enjoy a happy life eating Poffins.

Too exaggerated...

Those Poffins are too enticing for the Dragonites, right?

As the number of Dragonite employees increased, new problems kept cropping up.

The more Dragonites there were, the more Poffins were needed.

With over thirty Dragonites, D and his Indeedee sister needed to make more than forty boxes of Poffins every day.

Because not only do the Dragonites need to eat, but other Pokemon on the ranch also need Poffins.

As a result, even with the help of his Indeedee sister, D was struggling to keep up.

After careful consideration, D decided to make only a portion of the Poffins himself every day, and order the rest online.

As a basic snack in the Pokemon World, Poffins were very cheap; you could buy a big box for five hundred League coins.

This way, he wouldn't have to worry about not being able to pay salaries.

With this in mind, D started looking for a dessert shop with good skills online.

Finally, he signed a contract with a shop called "Bulbasaur Seed Dessert Shop," to order a batch of Poffins every afternoon.

The shop was located nearby in Cascarrafa City.

Their usual delivery method was Pelipper Express, but D thought Pelipper was a bit slow, so he decided to pick up the orders himself.

Every afternoon at three, he would dispatch a Dragonite to pick up the ordered Poffins, and then distribute them to the Dragonites when they got off work at five.

This way, Dragonite Delivery could continue to operate and there was no worry of a strike due to unpaid salaries.

Once everything was resolved, D let out a sigh of relief.

However, things turned out a bit differently than he imagined.

At five in the afternoon, after finishing work early, the Dragonites at the ranch received their wages on time.

Seeing the box full of Poffins, the dozen or so newly joined Dragonite employees felt incredibly excited.

The work here is so easy!

They only needed to spend at most half an hour delivering a trip, and the rest of the time could be spent playing, sleeping, and sunbathing on the ranch.

When they were in the wild, they often flew over the ocean, flying for even longer than their current workday combined.

Compared to back then, this job is ridiculously easy!

At this moment, the Dragonites deeply regretted not coming to work on the ranch sooner, missing out on so many days of Poffins!

---

# 460: Chapter 457: Legendary Beasts Drawn to the World Tree

"Awoo~"

Dragonite couldn't wait to return to Dragonite Island and immediately began enjoying the fruits of its labor for the day right on the spot.

As soon as the Poffin entered, the crispy and soft texture filled the entire mouth, with the rich Berry aroma perfectly blending with the sweet crust, tasting sweet without being greasy.

This is the taste!

The eyes of the dozen or so newly employed Dragonite employees lit up instantly.

They simply sat down on the grass, basking in the sunset, happily enjoying these Poffins with blissful expressions.

Seeing the actions of these Dragonites, the other veteran Dragonite employees looked on in surprise, standing aside to watch.

Soon, some working Dragonite felt quite speechless.

They ignored these guys, held their Poffin Boxes, flew into the Reversed World, and prepared to return to Dragonite Island to rest.

Dragonite looked rather disdainfully at these newly hired working Dragonite.

D stood aside observing for a while and then said, "By the way, Dragonite, you should try some too and see if there's any change in flavor."

Hearing this, Dragonite picked up a Poffin and took a bite, slightly widened its eyes, then showed a puzzled expression, "Awoo?"

Weren't today's Pokemon made by D and his sister Indeedee?

D noticed Dragonite's confusion and explained, "I bought them from a store that specializes in Poffins. Are you still used to the taste?"

Dragonite tasted it carefully. Although it preferred D's homemade Poffins, these were also delicious.

"Awoo!" Dragonite nodded its large head.

Seeing this, D breathed a sigh of relief; he was worried Dragonite wouldn't get used to them.

Now it seems they're able to accept it, which makes things much easier for him and his sister Indeedee.

However, continually ordering from the store isn't a long-term solution...

If only there were Pokemon skilled in making Poffins.

If there were, D could move the specialized Poffin cooking equipment to the World Tree and have them help with baking.

This way, the Pokemon at the World Tree could also make their own Poffins.

Thinking of this, images of several Pokemon flashed across D's mind.

He once asked the Heroic Expedition Team to help with invitations, but so far, Mew and Teddiursa haven't encountered those Pokemon yet.

D did consider capturing a few more Indeedee for the ranch, but wild Indeedee are hard to come by.

The two he has were purely due to luck, having met an old gentleman who was helping Indeedee find future owners.

"Forget it, such matters can't be rushed."

After a brief contemplation, D decided to put this matter on hold for now.

Because there's another very important task awaiting him next.

League Year 200, May 3rd.

Calyrex's energy had recovered, and today was again a day to accelerate the growth of the World Tree.

Early in the morning, D took the Pokemon from the ranch to the Reversed World, to the Sky Island where the World Tree was located.

Looking up at the giant tree before him, both D and Calyrex felt incredibly excited inside.

This was their second-to-last time nurturing the World Tree.

Just one more time next month, and this World Tree would be fully mature!

Having experienced this so many times, Calyrex was thoroughly familiar with how to nurture the World Tree.

Without speaking, it gently raised its hand.

A thick glow poured into the World Tree like a tide.

In the next second, an unprecedented green tide erupted from the World Tree, transforming into rings of green energy ripples, undulating outward.

The Pokemon inside the World Tree seemed to sense something, raising their heads to look at the sky.

Mewtwo's form flickered and then appeared atop the World Tree.

In the misty green glow, Mewtwo lowered its head, its gaze falling on D and his group of Pokemon.

"Buzz!"

Alongside a strange buzzing sound, the entire Reversed World underwent cataclysmic changes.

Green ripples enveloped all the surrounding Sky Islands.

Where the light passed, the once desolate and lifeless Sky Islands immediately grew lush greenery.

In that instant, the entire Reversed World gained a trace of vitality.

Surfing the internet, Giratina sensed this anomaly and was instantly startled awake.

It lifted its massive dragon head and flapped its wings, flying over to land beside D and the many Pokemon.

Giratina's eyes widened, showing an incredulous expression.

Because it could clearly feel the changes happening in its Reversed World.

Compared to Giratina, D remained much more composed.

This was a sign of the World Tree nearing maturity, its power beginning to affect this world.

And the first to be impacted was Giratina's Reversed World.

Looking towards the distant vibrant Sky Islands, D felt incredibly curious.

What will the future Reversed World turn into?

It's really something to look forward to!

In the unnoticed places, the green energy transmitted through the entrance connecting the Reversed World and the real world, rippling outward like waves on water, spreading in all directions.

In no time, it enveloped the entire planet, detected by some ancient beings.

Johto Region.

A magnificent rainbow spanned the sky, and at the rainbow's end, a sacred and beautiful Pokemon flew towards the distance.

---

That is the Legendary Pokemon known to bring happiness to people, Ho-oh.

Suddenly, a breeze filled with the scent of nature blew towards it.

Feeling the rich scent of nature and vitality within it, Ho-oh was momentarily stunned.

A hint of surprise and doubt flashed in its fiery eyes, then it looked around, trying to identify the source of the energy.

But the wind vanished in an instant, as if it was all just an illusion.

*

Within the ozone layer.

Rayquaza, who was gnawing on Minior's shell, suddenly stopped.

It turned its head to look around but found nothing.

A hint of confusion flashed through Rayquaza's eyes, and it loosened its claws, its dark figure moving like lightning, sweeping towards the distance, trying to search for the source of the energy.

*

Kalos Region.

Deep within a mysterious cave.

A Pokemon that looked like a cobra, with a black and green intertwined body, suddenly opened its pale eyes.

This was Zygarde, the legendary Pokemon monitoring the ecosystem.

It is the guardian of the ecosystem, but unlike other legendary Pokemon active outside, Zygarde, known as Z, divides itself into "Cells" and "Cores" and spreads them across the land.

Through these cells and cores, it monitors whether the world's ecosystem has any abnormalities.

Once a threat to the ecosystem appears, Zygarde will emerge and unleash its hidden power, mercilessly destroying all adversaries.

Currently, the ecosystem on this land has started to become precarious.

In the wild forests, there are negative energy cores shaped like giant plants everywhere.

And the culprit causing all of this, damaging the region's order, is precisely the highly dangerous species known as "humans".

But now... Zygarde suddenly detected a faint yet noticeable aura.

Was that a warning released by nature and the ecosystem?

Zygarde looked puzzled.

Even though the aura was weak, it could still feel the direction it came from.

Not in Kalos Land, but in a faraway place.

After a brief silence, Zygarde made a decision.

It contacted the "Cells" monitoring the ecosystem in the outer forest, commanding them to investigate in that direction.

*

Hoenn Region.

Inside a massive volcano, in this Land of Life's End filled with high temperatures and magma, a large Pokemon with a red shell, sleeping in the lava, suddenly opened its eyes.

In the depths of the sea, within a concealed cave in a pitch-black underwater canyon, a blue fish-type Pokemon sleeping in the water also opened its eyes.

Groudon and Kyogre looked around in confusion.

Moments ago, they distinctly felt a pure natural energy.

But that energy disappeared in an instant, and when they tried to sense the direction it came from, they could no longer feel it.

Was it an illusion?

*

Beyond the entire cosmos.

In a vast, endless void.

The Creator God, silently observing the development of humans and Pokemon in these worlds, suddenly noticed an anomaly from one of them.

A light appeared in the eyes of this great being.

Subsequently, everything within that world appeared completely before its eyes.

A human named D, Calyrex, the World Tree grown, Explorer Team Guild, Heroic Expedition Team, Mew, and Teddiursa...

"Explorer Team..." Arceus murmured, remembering that in the world it created, there was no such entity as an Explorer Team.

It had created many worlds, some with only humans, others with only Pokemon.

And the Explorer Team was a concept that originated in worlds populated only by Pokemon.

Yet, in those worlds where humans and Pokemon interacted and developed together, it was impossible for an Explorer Team composed solely of Pokemon to emerge.

Was it due to that human?

Causing this world to develop things that shouldn't exist.

Thinking of this, a curious expression appeared in Arceus' eyes.

"Interesting."

Two words slowly came from its mouth.

Could that human and those Pokemon, by meeting, create so many intriguing things?

Even the cold and vicious Giratina could become friends with him.

Arceus postponed observing other worlds and began to focus intently on this one.

It wanted to see what other interesting things the human named D could bring to it.

*

Reversed World.

With the end of Calyrex's cultivation this time, the anomaly on the World Tree also gradually subsided, the green light slowly disappearing.

In the blink of an eye, this big tree returned to its original appearance.

D looked up, and the detailed information of the World Tree appeared in his mind.

[World Tree (Mature Stage): A seedling that absorbed a large amount of the Power of Grass. Due to Gogoat, it experienced an anomaly during growth, with abundant life force brewing within, finally breaking through the ground. It is said to be able to support an entire world, nurturing all things with natural energy. It is still 9854 years away from full maturity.]

Seeing this number, D felt delighted.

In that case, if it happens again next month, the World Tree will fully mature!

---

It's still quite incredible to talk about.

From a hundred thousand years to nine thousand years, they only used two years.

And all of this, thanks to Calyrex.

If it weren't for Calyrex's power, he wouldn't have lived to see the World Tree grow.

"I'm counting on you for today next month!"

D looked at Giratina beside him, his eyes bright as he spoke to it.

Giratina was in a very good mood at the moment, because this tree had just changed its Reversed World, causing many flowers and plants to grow on those Sky Islands, making the place full of life.

It grinned with a fierce smile and said boldly, "Don't worry, leave it to me!"

D smiled, "You should come to live on the World Tree too then!"

Giratina had already planned on doing that.

Even if D didn't say anything, it would go over there.

Although it can't leave the Reversed World for long, it can live within the shadow of the World Tree.

All it needs to do is poke its head out and it can move through the shadow to the World Tree.

Thinking of this, Giratina grinned and said, "Great!"

After the World Tree was cultivated, D bid farewell to Giratina, and with a group of Pokemon left the Reversed World, returning to the ranch.

Today was a Sunny Day, the temperature in the northwest region of Paldea had reached thirty-seven degrees, very hot.

The Eevees had just run off to play on the World Tree, and the Dratini went to Dragonite Island, leaving only D, Koraidon, Alcremie, and the others at home.

D glanced around, Koraidon was lying on the carpet yawning, Alcremie was chatting on the windowsill with Pawmot, Cyclizar was watching TV on the sofa, and the Indeedee siblings were playing the 3ds that D bought for them.

"What are you playing?" D was very curious, what game could engross the siblings so much.

"Eh-yay!"

Indeedee sister turned her head happily, showing the screen of the game console to D.

On the screen were two Pokemon, a Charmander and an Eevee, seemingly exploring a maze.

To make sure D could understand her, Indeedee sister handed him a Translation Nuts Cake and then happily said:

"Eh-yay!" (We're playing Pokemon Rescue Team! This Charmander is brother, this Eevee is me, my brother and I are teaming up to explore the maze!)

"Rescue Team?" D was slightly surprised.

He switched the game to the background and then saw the name of the game—"Mystery Dungeon: Pokemon Rescue Team," the game company was Game Freak.

D widened his eyes, wow, how can there be GF in the Pokemon World too?

Indeedee sister looked at D expectantly and asked, "Eh-yay?" (D, do you want to play with us?)

D hesitated a bit, "Can this support multiplayer online?"

Her brother, who excelled at researching game consoles, nodded: "Eh-yay." (Up to six players online.)

"Alright, you guys play first, I'll go order a 3ds, once the game console arrives, we can team up and play." D said while using his Rotom Phone to place an online order.

A 3ds costs around eighteen thousand League Coins, not too pricey.

Hearing this made the siblings extremely happy.

Indeedee sister: "Eh-yay!" (That's great!)

Her brother's eyes also filled with determination, he was definitely going to lead D through the hardest maze!

He had cleared that maze a long time ago, but Indeedee sister was a bit clumsy, she couldn't pass it, so she kept pestering him to play together.

Eventually, he had no choice but to agree.

---

# 461: Chapter 458: Game Day, Wishing Star

The next afternoon, D's online order of a 3ds arrived.

After signing for it, he started teaming up with the Indeedee siblings to play a game.

"Pokemon Mystery Dungeon Rescue Team," this native Pokemon World game is also part of the rescue team series, but it is different from the rescue team D is familiar with.

Here, a large map divides into several smaller dungeons, and players control a group of Pokemon to explore the dungeons.

The dungeons are filled with many dangers, including hostile Pokemon, dangerous traps, and terrain.

But danger and opportunity coexist, correspondingly, Pokemon can acquire various treasures and items while exploring.

Such as various Evolution Stones, held items that enhance Pokemon's abilities, Berries with various effects, and Rare Candies that increase Pokemon's level, etc.

Players can control Pokemon to use moves to fight enemies, and upon defeating enemies, they gain experience which can be used to level up.

When their level is sufficient, Pokemon can evolve into more powerful forms.

D glanced over and found that there are ten Pokemon available for initial character selection.

Including Charmander, Eevee, Totodile, Squirtle, Chikorita, Meowth, and others.

The older Indeedee sibling said that after defeating the boss, new Pokemon character options can be unlocked.

The younger Indeedee sibling chose Eevee, the older one chose Charmander, and after understanding the gameplay, D chose an Oddish.

Once the characters were confirmed, each person with their two Pokemon officially entered the dungeon to start their adventure.

Initially, the upper levels of the dungeon weren't too dangerous, with only some low-level Pokemon and basic items scattered on the grass.

The younger Indeedee sibling, who loves collecting things, picked up some Berries for stamina recovery.

Meanwhile, D and the older Indeedee sibling cleared the hostile Pokemon in the dungeon smoothly, leveling up to lv8.

Then, the three climbed up the stairs to the second level, where the Pokemon had stronger capabilities than those on the previous level.

However, with the cooperation of Oddish's move [Acid] and Charmander's [Ember], they thrillingly cleared away the wild Pokemon in this level.

Subsequently, Charmander leveled up to lv16 and evolved into Charmeleon.

The younger Indeedee sibling's Eevee also evolved into Flareon using the Fire Stone.

In the third layer of the dungeon, some strong wild Pokemon started to appear.

The most troublesome of them was a Gyarados.

Charmeleon and Flareon of the Indeedee siblings were fully countered by Gyarados, taking heavy damage from two Hydro Pumps.

Frightened, the younger Indeedee sibling let out a panicked Growl, fumbling to grab an Oran Berry from the backpack to restore stamina.

Meanwhile, the older Indeedee sibling calmly managed Charmeleon to retreat, frowning as if wishing he could magically enter the game to face the Gyarados himself.

Luckily, at the critical moment, D's Oddish defeated some of the Magikarp followers around Gyarados and accumulated enough experience to evolve into Gloom.

Combining the use of [Poison Powder], [Sleep Powder], [Stun Spore], and [Giga Drain] moves, they successfully defeated Gyarados.

The fallen Gyarados dropped a pile of Rare Candies and held items.

"Indeed!"

Seeing this, the younger Indeedee sibling immediately looked at D with an expression full of admiration.

The older Indeedee sibling also thought D was amazing.

"Piece of cake, piece of cake," D grinned and said, "After all, I'm a Trainer, I know how to counter Gyarados, c'mon, brother, eat all these Rare Candies."

However, the older Indeedee sibling felt that D, who was even more impressive, needed those Rare Candies more.

"No, Gloom doesn't need level-up to evolve now, we just need to find a Leaf Stone and a Sun Stone to evolve into a stronger Pokemon," D shook his head and explained, "Compared to Gloom, Charmeleon will see a great boost in strength after evolving into Charizard, we'll rely on you in the next layer."

The older Indeedee sibling understood.

He took those Rare Candies and fed them to Charmeleon.

In a flash of light, Charmeleon evolved into the majestic and handsome Charizard.

Continuing on, the younger Indeedee sibling's Flareon found a Leaf Stone in a tree hollow.

D's Gloom used it to evolve into Vileplume, and learned the moves Toxic, Moonlight, Moonblast, and Substitute.

Using D's known Substitution Poison Pervert Strategy in addition to the Indeedee siblings' assistance, they thrillingly reached the dungeon's deepest level.

Next, they just need to defeat the boss Tyranitar to clear the level.

D originally thought this would be easy, but didn't expect that Tyranitar could use Alliances!

The battle started, and it immediately used [Screech], greatly reducing the defense of Vileplume, Charizard, and Flareon.

D sensed trouble and retreated with Vileplume, while alerting the Indeedee siblings.

But before he could speak, the on-screen Gyarados used Earthquake.

The ground began to shake violently, causing the younger Indeedee sibling's Flareon to immediately lose stamina to zero, losing its combat ability.

Fortunately, at a crucial moment, D managed Vileplume to use Substitute, successfully resisting the attack.

After the move ended, he immediately started using Moonlight to restore health.

---

And the older brother Indeedee controlled Charizard to charge forward, using Flamethrower against Tyranitar.

However, that Tyranitar seemed to read the motion like a book.

At the moment Charizard acted, it stomped the ground fiercely and used Stone Edge.

Charizard was defeated in one move just the same.

D tried to defend, but after Tyranitar defeated Charizard, it directly charged at his Vileplume, using Giga Impact, instantly knocking it out.

The words "game over" appeared on the 3ds screen.

Outside the screen, one person and two Pokemon looked at each other.

His sister Indeedee frowned: "Ai ai! Ai ai?" (Brother, you liar! Didn't you say you'd already cleared this stage before?)

The older brother Indeedee had a bead of sweat form on his forehead.

It tried hard to recall the past events and replied: "Ai ai..." (I don't quite remember, the guardian Pokemon back then wasn't Tyranitar, it was Meganium, and my Charizard defeated it in two moves...)

D: "..."

"Don't worry." D reassured: "It seems the boss in the maze keeps changing, we just got unlucky this time with Tyranitar countering us. Next time, let's regroup and try again."

But such random mazes and high-difficulty final bosses indeed added playability for players.

Moreover, its battles weren't turn-based, but rather had a bit of an action game feel.

The moves weren't limited to four; players could pre-arrange move combinations beforehand.

Then in battle, players could freely switch between move wheels to execute their moves.

The Game Freak in this world is something!

D immediately decided to give it another go.

This time he arranged a wide-ranging, more versatile lineup based on the variety of Pokemon they had.

His sister Indeedee played Psyduck, his older brother Indeedee continued with Charmander, while D switched his Pokemon from Oddish to Eevee.

Psyduck, when evolved into Golduck, could use moves of both Water Type and Psychic Type, and could also use Screech to lower the opponent's defense.

Charizard had the coverage of Fire, Flying, and Dragon Types.

D planned to evolve Eevee into Leafeon.

Before evolving, it could use Tail Whip, Charm, Growl, and Helping Hand to assist Charizard. After evolving, it could use the combo of Sword Dance and Leaf Blade, matching with Golduck's Screech use.

Not only that, Leafeon could use Sunny Day to change the weather.

Sunny Day not only boosted Charizard but also activated Leafeon's ability [Chlorophyll], increasing its speed.

After finalizing the lineup, the game started again.

With D directing and everyone cooperating, they safely reached the lowest level.

This time, the final Boss changed to Primeape.

Golduck started off with Screech, Charizard kept Primeape occupied around, D took the opportunity to use Sunny Day, fully boosting with Sword Dance, and finally, cooperating with Charizard, they successfully defeated Primeape.

Victory!

"Ai ai!"

The Indeedee siblings exclaimed in surprise.

The three of them were engrossed in the game here, even Koraidon and Cyclizar were drawn over.

Seeing the Pokemon interested in the gaming console, D couldn't help but ask: "Do you want to give it a try too?"

"Gagao!"

Cyclizar seeing D having so much fun wanted to give it a try too.

Alcremie also looked eager.

Koraidon had played before, and wasn't as intensely interested in the game console as Cyclizar.

Seeing this, D handed them the game console, then took out his Rotom Phone, opened the online shopping app, and said to them:

"Then I'll buy a few more gaming consoles so you can all team up and play together."

Fearing other Pokemon would want to try too, D ordered ten 3ds consoles in one go, informing the store owner that he would pick them up in person.

After saying that, D went out to contact a working Dragonite and gave it a commission task.

Fifteen minutes later, Dragonite returned to the ranch with a bag full of 3ds consoles.

D distributed them to the Pokemon, taught them how to operate, then sat on the sofa watching them play.

In his mind, he imagined the game's settings, feeling some aspects quite interesting.

Pokemon exploring dungeons, treasure hunting, finding various rare items.

Even after defeating the final boss, they could invite them back to the rescue team guild headquarters to become teammates for exploration.

Not only that, what intrigued D further was the maze containing various Berries and treasures.

"If only the World Tree had such a maze." D thought.

In this way, Pokemon training hard and wanting to become stronger would have a place to expend their energy.

Just like the Skiddo taught by three Gogoat.

It could enter the maze to train itself, becoming stronger.

And Farfetch'd could also go into the maze to practice sword techniques.

However, the pity was that the interior of the World Tree did not have such a place.

Building one would require finding God of Space Palkia to open up a connection to Mystery Dungeon World, allowing Pokemon to go in for exploration.

Mystery Dungeon could further spin off a series of commissions.

Such as seeking treasure, aiding Pokemon in need, exploring the maze, and such content.

In this way, the Explorer Team Guild Headquarters would truly become a headquarters.

```

---

The Pokemon on the World Tree can use it as a center to explore the labyrinth.

However, so far, he has not seen Palkia, and even if he did, Palkia might not be willing to help.

Moreover, Giratina seems to have some incompatibility with those two Legendary Pokemon.

Forget it, there's no use thinking about this.

D turned his head and put the matter out of his mind.

While the Pokemon were playing games over there, he opened the Pokemon Forum, entered the Hoenn section, and started surfing.

The netizens in the Hoenn Region were still in heated discussions about the recent appearances of Team Aqua and Team Magma.

D noticed that some insiders revealed that the Champion of the Hoenn League and the Gym Leaders were already prepared to respond.

Steven had already set out to stop the plans of those two organizations.

The Gym Leader of the Sootopolis Gym, Wallace, had also left the gym and was missing. Some Trainers went to challenge him, only to find the Sootopolis Gym was closed.

"Wallace..."

D's mind conjured the image of Steven's good friend.

Considering their relationship, Steven must have told him everything.

Plus, since Wallace seemed to have some connection with the Draconid people, if D's guess was correct, he must have gone with Steven to deal with Groudon and Kyogre.

The situation in the Hoenn Region was becoming increasingly tense, yet amidst all this, D saw an unexpected trending topic on the forum.

[The Millennial Comet is coming! Head to the Pokemon Amusement Park to observe this week-long spectacle together!]

Millennial Comet?

Upon seeing this term, a Pokemon's name immediately appeared in D's mind.

Jirachi!

Is the time for the Jirachi movie fast approaching?

*

Hoenn Region, southwest of Mt. Chimney, directly north of Lavaridge Town.

On this land known as Forina, a purple-haired man in a white tailcoat was searching for something.

"I will, I will prove myself!" Butler said with determination, memories of past events surfacing in his mind.

Back then, he was still a scientist with Team Magma.

During his tenure with Team Magma, to complete Maxie's mission, Butler developed a device aiming to use the Groudon shell fossil remnants he had obtained by chance to revive Groudon.

However, when he tried to activate the device in front of the Team Magma officials to begin the revival, the device exploded due to insufficient energy, leading to the experiment's failure.

The higher-ups in Team Magma ridiculed him and kicked him out of the team.

Butler harbored resentment against those people, unwilling to accept the reality, swearing vengeance to revitalize Groudon through the fossil remnants!

But to achieve revival, he first needed to find a stable energy source for the device.

Thus, Butler came to Forina.

Because a legend was told in the area.

Forina was said to house a Pokemon called the Wishing Star.

This Pokemon awakens once every thousand years, capable of absorbing energy from the Millennial Comet, nourishing Forina with this energy, making it more prosperous and beautiful.

As long as he finds that Pokemon and utilizes its power, he can surely maintain the device's operation and resurrect the Super Ancient Pokemon Groudon!

When that time comes, he will certainly take the revived Groudon to confront those who once mocked him, proving that he was right!

At that moment, a secret cave appeared ahead of him.

Butler's eyes lit up, and he quickly ran inside the cave, whose ceiling connected to an outer opening.

A beam of sunlight shone through that opening.

Within that beam, a peculiar crystalline structure lay quietly.
```
