# 462: Chapter 459: Searching for Jirachi, Teaming Up with Absol

The sun shone brightly, and the scorching sunlight poured over this rich and magnificent Indigo Sandstone Peak Forest.

As far as the eye could see, this rugged and unique terrain stretched all the way to the distant horizon, with forests scattered throughout, and winding rivers traversing the Peak Forest.

Standing on the mountain peak and looking down, there were no signs of human presence within hundreds of miles.

In addition, the abundance of water resources and the full-grown wild fruits in the green valley made it easy to imagine the large number of wild Pokemon groups that must inhabit here.

Pursuing the rumors of Jirachi, the three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team slowly landed on a hill.

Mew and Teddiursa flew from Zapdos and together they gazed at this beautiful and spectacular forest land.

Teddiursa was captivated by the scenery, and it couldn't help but mutter:

"Mee... Mee?" (So beautiful... Is this the Forina Region?)

"Miu?"

Mew tilted its little head, using its pretty blue eyes to overlook the scene below.

If that Swellow was correct, this should indeed be the Forina Region, the place where Jirachi is dormant.

Zapdos was also observing this unfamiliar land.

"Chirp." (Anyway, we'll know once we go down and ask.)

Upon hearing this, the three Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team exchanged glances, and then flew down together.

Here, like other places, also inhabited many Pokemon.

Breloom, Electrike, Linoone, Tropius, Nuzleaf, Altaria...

These local Pokemon quickly noticed the three unfamiliar visitors.

The three of the Heroic Expedition Team also saw them.

To inquire about Jirachi's information, Mew flew to a group of Breloom and asked:

"Miu?" (Excuse me, is Jirachi over here?)

Upon hearing the unfamiliar name Jirachi, this group of Breloom appeared somewhat bewildered.

"Nota?" (Jirachi? Who's that?)

"Miu!" (It's a Pokemon that looks like a star!) answered Mew, as D had said.

However, the group of Breloom still shook their heads.

Beside them, Teddiursa muttered: "Mee." (Looks like they don't know about Jirachi.)

So, what should they do now?

Teddiursa quickly came up with a good idea!

They could simply use the Rotom Phone to ask D instead!

Thinking of this, Teddiursa summoned the Rotom Phone and said to it: "Mee!" (Rotom Phone, please help us contact D!)

"Roger that, Roto!"

The screen of Phone Rotom flickered twice, and then the call interface appeared.

Accompanied by two rings, the video communication was finally connected.

D's face then appeared on the opposite side of the screen.

Through the screen, D noticed the special landscape behind Teddiursa.

He quickly recognized their location.

"Looks like you've successfully arrived in the Forina Region," D said.

The special landscape of the Forina Region is very unique in the entire Pokemon World,

after all, its prototype is China's Wulingyuan.

"Mee!" Teddiursa nodded repeatedly.

Then, it reported the current difficulty it was encountering to D.

"Mee..." (Just that the Pokemon here seem to not know Jirachi...)

Beside, Mew also looked very puzzled.

So, several Pokemon together looked at the screen, waiting for D's answer.

"I think I know what the issue is."

After brief contemplation, D thought of a reason.

"Because the characteristic of Jirachi as a Pokemon is that it can only awaken once every thousand years, and since it's been a thousand years since its last awakening, these Pokemon were born later, so it's understandable they don't know it."

"Mee?!" Teddiursa suddenly knitted its brows, so how could they find Jirachi?

"That's easy," D continued: "You can first go find a Pokemon named Absol, it should know Jirachi's location."

In that movie, there was an Absol, which seems to be a Guardian of Jirachi, able to track Jirachi's location, and it was the one who guided Ash and others to the Forina Region, enabling Jirachi to complete its mission.

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa quickly turned and asked the group of Breloom: "Mee mee?" (Then do you know Absol?)

This, the Breloom did know.

Absol, it's the Guardian of this Forina Land, they had once been helped by it.

Although unsure why these three foreign Pokemon were looking for Absol, one of the Breloom still offered assistance.

It pointed a direction with its finger, indicating: "Nota!"

Meaning that Absol lives over there.

Mew and Teddiursa looked towards that direction, first thanking these Breloom, then rode Zapdos and flew in that direction.

During this time, the video communication on the Rotom Phone was not hung up.

D also took the opportunity to observe the Forina Region and the wild Pokemon residing there through the screen.

The Nuzleaf standing on the big tree, the Tropius gathering to drink water by the river, the cautious Poochyena in the grass...

Needless to say, the types of Pokemon inhabiting there were really abundant.

This situation was also inevitable.

Jirachi absorbs the energy from millennial comets, then uses this energy to nurture this land, making the soil fertile and the vegetation more Overgrow.

---

Fertile soil leads to the growth of abundant resources, thus nurturing more wild Pokemon.

But there is still one issue.

What should he do to awaken Jirachi from its slumber?

Reverse-series cuisine?

That might just turn Jirachi's Serene Grace into Doom Desire...

Let's think from a different perspective.

It's well known that Jirachi can absorb energy from meteors to nurture the Forina Land.

And as D knows, the Natural Energy from the World Tree can achieve the same effect.

So the question is, if Jirachi absorbs enough Natural Energy, can it avoid falling asleep?

As D pondered, an unfamiliar Pokemon suddenly appeared in front of the Heroic Expedition Team.

It was a four-legged Pokemon with white fur, red eyes, and a head with a horn resembling a bow.

The Absol gazed calmly at the few Pokemon that intruded here.

Teddiursa and Mew noticed it too.

D introduced them, "That's Absol."

A human voice?

Absol's eyes instantly turned to the Rotom Phone floating in mid-air.

Mew looked at Absol and asked, "miu?" (Hello, Absol, do you know Jirachi?)

Upon hearing the name Jirachi, Absol's gaze immediately became wary.

It looked at the human and then at the three unfamiliar Pokemon.

"Woooo?" (What do you want?)

Teddiursa keenly noticed the change in Absol's tone.

It quickly realized that this Pokemon seemed to have misunderstood something, so it hurriedly explained:

"Eemo eemo!" (We are the Heroic Expedition Team, not bad Pokemon! We want to find Jirachi because we want to help it wake up more often than just once every thousand years!)

Hearing this, Absol fell silent.

As Jirachi's Guardian, it naturally understood Jirachi's situation.

But...

Thinking of this, Absol looked at the three Pokemon in front of it and said, "Woooo." (Jirachi has already disappeared.)

Disappeared?

Hearing this, the three Pokemon from the Heroic Expedition Team were slightly taken aback.

Absol then turned around and led them to a cave.

"Woooo." (Jirachi was supposed to be asleep here, but three days ago, it disappeared, and nearby Pokemon said humans were spotted around here, but I couldn't track him down.)

Only when Jirachi awakens can it follow the aura to determine Jirachi's location.

"Eemo..." Teddiursa suddenly had an unpleasant thought.

Could it be that Jirachi was captured by a Human Trainer?

Upon hearing this, Absol immediately spoke, "Woooo!" (Impossible! Jirachi hasn't awakened yet and can't make such a decision on its own!)

"Absol is right." At this time, D's voice sounded again.

Absol immediately fixed its gaze on the human in that machine.

D noticed Absol's gaze and smiled, explaining:

"Absol, don't worry, we have no malice, I am D, and our search for Jirachi is solely to help it."

Beside him, Teddiursa nodded repeatedly, "Eemo!" (That's right! It's D who entrusted us to come here to help Jirachi!)

Mew also said, "miu~" (D is a really nice human~)

Absol wasn't naive; it didn't immediately believe them but remained skeptical of the situation.

Because it was humans who took Jirachi away, it wasn't sure whether this human was honest or just another trying to capture Jirachi.

Absol fell into silence.

Meanwhile, D had already found the location of the Pokemon Amusement Park online and shared it with Teddiursa and Mew.

If he guessed right, the crystallized Jirachi had been taken by former Team Magma scientist Butler.

Butler and his girlfriend Diane were planning a grand Pokemon Amusement Park near Forina for tourists to enjoy during the seven days of the Millennial Comet's visit.

They even held online ticket sales to attract visitors.

However, this was all just a facade.

Butler's true intention was to use Jirachi to absorb Cosmic Energy, to create a Groudon from its fragments, proving himself to Team Magma.

The money he made from the amusement park was all invested back into his research.

"Alright, I've sent the locations where Jirachi might currently be to Mew, Teddiursa, and Zapdos. You can follow the navigation to reach there."

"But with a few days left before the Millennial Comet arrives, I'm afraid Jirachi hasn't yet awakened,"

Teddiursa and Mew were overjoyed to hear this.

"Eemo!"

"Miu!"

As expected of D, truly reliable!

Meanwhile, Absol was utterly stunned.

How did this human know the location of Jirachi?

Mew started verifying the location of the Pokemon Amusement Park on the Rotom Phone and, upon finding it wasn't far from here, immediately prepared to head there.

Teddiursa then turned to Absol, inviting it, "Eemo eemo!" (Absol, come with us to help Jirachi!)

Absol: "..."

---

Thinking about his earlier doubts regarding this human and the three Pokemon, Absol turned his head away somewhat embarrassed.

But now, compared to itself, Jirachi's matter seemed much more important.

"Ooao." (Then I'll leave it to you.)

Seeing the successful reunion of the Heroic Expedition Team and Absol, D finally eased his mind slightly.

"Then you guys go ahead; I need to study methods to keep Jirachi awake in the meantime. If anything happens, contact me," he said.

"Ehm!"

Teddiursa responded happily and then hung up the phone.

To avoid attracting attention, Mew used the Poke Ball to capture Zapdos inside.

It transformed itself into the appearance of a Swablu, riding on Absol's back along with Teddiursa, under the navigation of Phone Rotom, heading towards the venue of the Pokemon Amusement Park.

Meanwhile, D walked out of the house, observing the playful Pokemon in the pasture thoughtfully.

As far as he knows, the world doesn't hold just one Jirachi.

But whether it's the Jirachi in Forina or elsewhere, they all keep the eco-habit of sleeping for a thousand years and waking for seven days.

Of course, not every Jirachi needs to absorb energy from a millennial comet.

This Jirachi in the Forina Region is very special; it absorbs Natural Energy not for itself, but to nurture the entire Forina Land.

Moreover, as seen from the movie, Jirachi doesn't directly grant wishes as the rumors outside suggest.

Its ability is more about relocating items; when a wisher wants endless food, Jirachi uses Psychic powers to transfer food from elsewhere.

Therefore, wishing directly for Jirachi to not sleep won't work.

D walked forward, through the small path in the pasture, arriving at the lake connected to the Reversed World.

Then he crossed the lake and went to the Reversed World, reaching the front of the World Tree unhindered.

D didn't climb up but stood on the Sky Island at the bottom, lost in thought.

Would the cuisine made from the leaves of the World Tree be effective for Jirachi?

Back when the World Tree hadn't fully grown, he once used the leaves to make a dish named Leaf Cake.

That dish had three effects.

Namely: permanently increasing Grass Type Move's power by 25%, shortening the crop growth period when Grass Type Pokemon use Grass Energy to cultivate crops, and the final one which only affects him as the Master of the World Tree.

And just as D was deep in thought, an extraordinary scene happened.

The World Tree in front seemed to have sensed his thoughts, and suddenly a green light shone brightly atop its huge canopy.

In the glow, two leaves emitting a gentle radiance floated down, landing right in front of D.

D was slightly startled.

He looked down, and the next second, the information of those two leaves emerged in his mind.

[Leaf of Awakening: Leaves with miraculous effects, when used to make cuisine, they can keep certain specific Pokemon awake without needing sleep.]

[Leaf of Life: Leaves of the World Tree, rich in Natural Energy, when placed somewhere, can nurture the local ecological environment, making it lush and beautiful.]

?

Seeing these two leaves fall before him, D was slightly stunned.

He then looked up in disbelief at the World Tree before him.

Could it be... the World Tree is helping him?

From what he knew, there seems to be a Natural Will within this world...

And the Power of Viridian is the manifestation of this Natural Will.

Nature bestows this power upon the chosen human.

After pondering for a while, D roughly guessed the current situation in his heart.

The Natural Will of the World Tree sensed his current troubles, thus actively helping him!

Is this the effect of the "Master of the World Tree"?

Awe-inspiring indeed!

*

Beyond all cosmos, Arceus gazed at that human named D.

"Interesting, even though not familiar with that Pokemon, just rumors, he willingly wants to help it?"

"Since so, I'll grant you a gift then."

With a tiny move of intent, Arceus let two leaves gently fall from that tree.

As the Creator God, He doesn't mind stepping in personally to alter the rules on that Pokemon.

As for why helping that human, it's because He saw a new possibility in him.

Not only one world exists among these cosmos; some worlds are peaceful and stable, while others are in peril.

Arceus needs some people to help those worlds on the brink of destruction.

This isn't His first time doing such a thing.

Long ago, Arceus once chose a human named "Ko" from a certain world, transferring her to that Hisuin Region, troubled by temporal rifts.

Arceus's eyes reflected the scene of D picking up those two leaves.

Though young, he still doesn't know that every gift of fate already carries its price in secret.

---

---

---

---

# 463: Chapter 460: Mayor Thomas's Request, the Agitated Vigoroth!

Alliance Year 200, May 7th, Summer.

Staring at the two leaves that fell from the World Tree on the table, D was lost in thought.

The Leaf of Awakening had already been made into a Leaf Green Ball by him, which just needs to be fed to Jirachi to save it from the fate of a thousand-year slumber.

And that Leaf of Life, glowing with a soft green light, could take Jirachi's place in nurturing the Forina Region.

With it, Jirachi could set off on a journey or travel to other places for adventure.

Everything is ready except for one thing.

All that was left was for Jirachi to arrive.

It had been three days since the last phone call from the Heroic Expedition Team.

During the period, Teddiursa and Mew had reported the progress of the assignment to him.

They had successfully found Jirachi at the Pokemon Amusement Park.

Although a man named Butler tried to stop them, fortunately, they dispatched Zapdos in time, shattering Butler's dreams with a Thunderbolt.

At this moment, the Heroic Expedition Team should be hurrying towards the ranch with Jirachi, right?

Tonight is the arrival of the millennial comet.

If D wasn't mistaken, Jirachi should also wake up tonight.

"Knock knock knock!"

Suddenly, there was a knock on the door.

Hearing this sound, D was a bit surprised. Who would be visiting the ranch at this time?

He put down the leaf in his hand and turned to open the door.

As the door opened, the aged face of Mayor Thomas appeared at the entrance.

"Mayor Thomas?" D was slightly stunned, then invited the elderly man inside to sit for a while.

However, Mayor Thomas shook his head, "D, there's something I need your help with today."

"Please, go ahead," D replied.

Mayor Thomas then began to explain: "It's about Cyclizar's mother. That Cyclizar is already fifty-three years old and hasn't looked well recently. I took it to the Pokemon Center for a check-up yesterday."

"Miss Joy at the Pokemon Center said its time is almost up, and it doesn't have much left, so I would like you to take Cyclizar to see it."

Hearing this, D was a bit shocked, "Cyclizar's lifespan is only fifty-something years?"

Mayor Thomas shook his head, "Not exactly; normally Cyclizar can live a long, long time. But that Cyclizar was injured heavily while protecting me in its youth."

"Maybe because of that incident, its body became weak, and its lifespan shortened."

D immediately understood; wasn't this just like Alder's Volcarona?

"I see." D turned around, took a bottle of Life Sheep Milk from the fridge, and called the unaware Cyclizar, preparing to follow Mayor Thomas to town.

"Huh?"

Cyclizar looked puzzled.

It looked at D, then at Mayor Thomas. Perhaps catching a hint from his expression, Cyclizar seemed to realize something ominous.

Koraidon, who was resting in the shade not far away, noticed the situation and quickly joined them.

"Roar!"

D understood Koraidon's intention, it was saying it wanted to go too.

"Alright," D agreed and turned to tell the Indeedee Sibling, "I'll leave the house to you for now; I'm taking Cyclizar and Koraidon into town."

"Indeedee,"

The siblings, a bit worried, complied obediently.

Koraidon carried D, and Cyclizar carried Mayor Thomas, and they soon arrived at Mayor Thomas's home.

At the door, Mayor Thomas looked at Cyclizar and couldn't help but sigh:

"Little Cyclizar, go see your mother!"

The ominous feeling in Cyclizar's heart was confirmed.

It charged into the room, with Koraidon following closely behind.

Inside, the elder Cyclizar was lying on the floor with drooping eyelids, looking listless.

Anyone could tell that it was nearing the end of its life.

Cyclizar seemed to realize something, immediately running up to the elder Cyclizar and let out an anxious growl.

"Huh!"

Hearing its offspring's voice, the mother Cyclizar opened its eyes to look at its child.

It struggled to extend its paw, gently caressing its offspring, as if comforting it not to be sad.

Cyclizar's eyes began to well up with tears.

"Roar..."

Seeing such a scene, Koraidon lowered its head sadly.

But soon it turned its gaze to D.

D took a bottle of Life Sheep Milk from his pocket and stepped toward the elder Cyclizar, feeding it with Mayor Thomas's confused gaze.

"Don't worry; everything will be fine."

The once feeble mother Cyclizar, after drinking the Life Sheep Milk, suddenly felt a warm energy coursing through its body.

In an instant, it felt as if it had returned to its youthful days.

Strength welled up, and the mother Cyclizar, emerging from its previous state, let out a surprised cry, then looked curiously at its body.

"Huh?!"

"What is that..." Mayor Thomas's eyes widened.

Cyclizar's tears abruptly stopped, and realizing what had happened, it let out a joyous call.

---

"Gaaow!"

"That's the milk produced by the Gogoats on my ranch," D explained to Mayor Thomas.

Mayor Thomas immediately thought of the expensive goat milk sold in the town.

His aged face was full of disbelief.

"What exactly is going on here?"

"It's probably Calyrex's power!" D took a deep breath, "Calyrex blessed my ranch, so that the milk produced by the Gogoats is blessed by nature. It can help Pokemon recover stamina and even heal the most serious of injuries."

In other words, Life Sheep Milk can violently extend the lifespan of Pokemon whose life spans have been reduced due to injuries.

"That kind of milk actually has such effects..." Mayor Thomas murmured, full of disbelief.

Meanwhile, the Cyclizar mom, who had recovered, was already in a tight embrace with Cyclizar.

Nearby, Koraidon watched with great envy. It too wanted to snuggle up to its mom, but thinking of its current state, it couldn't help but hesitate.

Watching this scene, D was full of emotion.

What can one say?

Pokemon are such strong yet fragile creatures.

They possess powerful strength and are naturally endowed with unparalleled combat abilities.

But no matter how strong they are, like humans, they are ordinary lives living in this world.

Moderate battles can enhance their strength, honing their physique.

But once battles become excessive, or if they sustain severe injuries, it will consume their lifespan, leading to early demise.

Not only Alder's Volcarona, but many trainers in the world are facing such situations.

Such a thing happened to the former Champion Mustard of the Galar Region.

In his early years, he was an undefeated Champion.

But due to years of fierce battles, his partner Pokemon left this world early.

After that, Mustard's condition began to decline, and his undefeated record was broken.

At this moment, the Cyclizar, having calmed down, turned its head but suddenly noticed Koraidon dazing off beside it.

Koraidon's eyes were a bit moist, seemingly having cried earlier as well.

Seeing this, Cyclizar's face was full of confusion.

"Gaaow?" (Why are you crying too?)

Koraidon: "..."

Koraidon didn't want to answer.

Cyclizar was even more puzzled, staring at Koraidon, yet unable to make heads or tails of it.

At this moment, Mayor Thomas had already started expressing gratitude to D.

He was already very concerned about the health of his old buddy. When Miss Joy mentioned it was close to its end, Mayor Thomas was so distressed that he had several sleepless nights.

And now, seeing Cyclizar gradually recovering, the old man's heart finally felt at ease.

He wiped the tears from the corners of his eyes, his voice filled with emotion, "I don't know what to say, but thank you so much this time, D."

"It's nothing," D smiled and shook his head, "When I first came here, you also helped me a lot."

Talking about this matter, Mayor Thomas couldn't help but break into a smile:

"There was no real help; it was all your efforts, managing that abandoned ranch and putting it in order, even surpassing your grandfather. If that old man could see how the ranch has transformed, he would be overjoyed."

D smiled gently.

Mayor Thomas wanted them to stay for lunch, but D, thinking about Jirachi, politely declined the invitation.

After bidding farewell, he returned to the ranch with Koraidon and Cyclizar.

Yet as soon as D got home, he saw chaos over at the Berry Garden.

Even a few berry trees had fallen to the ground.

Seeing this, D was utterly confounded.

What happened? Could it be that a wild Pokemon broke into the ranch?

He hurriedly took Koraidon and Cyclizar over to check.

Upon arriving, he found Indeedee and Butterfree had already arrived there ahead of him, even the three Gogoats had come to the scene.

These Pokemon were angrily looking at the Pokemon bound by Vine Whip lying on the grass.

It was an ape-like Pokemon, predominantly yellow and white, with brown stripes on its back, and a tuft of orange hair on its head.

D was slightly taken aback.

The name of that Pokemon instantly popped into his mind.

Vigoroth?

At this moment, Indeedee and Butterfree also noticed D's arrival.

The group of Pokemon turned around and angrily complained to D about the behavior of this Pokemon.

"Ai!" (This rascal knocked down several berry trees!)

"Fwee!" (It even tried to attack us!)

"Bwee!" (So annoying!)

D suddenly felt a headache.

The Slakoth sleeping in his orchard evolved into a Vigoroth.

He looked at the Vigoroth, and even though its body was bound by Gogoat's Vine Whip, it was still struggling hard, making "huff huff" sounds from its throat.

It looked like it was about to jump up and hit someone in a fit of rage.

D stepped forward, trying to communicate with it, "Vigoroth? Do you still recognize me?"

Vigoroth seemed eager to move.

It recognized this human; he was the one who rescued it from the forest fire years ago.

It wanted to nod, but the boiling blood in its body didn't allow it to sit still for even a minute, just wanting to jump up and cause a commotion.

---

"Let it go first!"

D said to Gogoat.

He had some understanding of Pokemon like Vigoroth, knowing that their rampant destruction was entirely due to their physical conditions.

After evolving from Slakoth to Vigoroth, maybe the energy accumulated during their Slakoth stage bursts out completely.

This makes their blood boil and their hearts start to beat ten times faster.

If Vigoroth doesn't exercise, its stress will build up excessively, causing its physical condition to worsen.

This makes Vigoroth very hard to tame; their nature is wild and they can't calm down without causing trouble, swinging their arms around and destroying things.

For Trainers, this is easily solved by letting Vigoroth battle constantly, allowing them to burn off excess energy until they evolve into Slaking.

But now...

Once Gogoat released the Vine Whip binding on Vigoroth, it started causing havoc everywhere.

It didn't attack D but started to wreak havoc on the surrounding fruit trees, and in the blink of an eye, it knocked over another fruit tree.

D: "..."

Seeing Vigoroth about to charge at the next fruit tree, D hurriedly called for Gogoat.

"Gogoat, use Vine Whip!"

"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh!"

With the sound of the whip slicing the air, the agitated Vigoroth was once again tightly bound.

D tried using Pokemon battles to help Vigoroth expend its energy.

He sent out Flareon, Cyclizar, Miltank, and Butterfree to take turns battling with Vigoroth.

But soon, these Pokemon came to D with complaints.

Flareon and Butterfree said Vigoroth doesn't know how to hold back and hit them really hard! They don't want to battle with Vigoroth anymore.

Even the energetic Miltank was not spared from Vigoroth's antics.

Though Cyclizar could knock Vigoroth down, before long, it would start causing trouble again.

Vigoroth's energy was just too abundant, even with the ranch Pokemon helping to burn its energy.

But if they weren't careful, a recovered Vigoroth would start causing chaos in the ranch again.

In one afternoon, it knocked down five fruit trees, messed up Farfetch'd's leeks, and wreaked havoc on the farmland.

A furious Farfetch'd chased after it with its leek.

Although Vigoroth was temporarily knocked down, after a little over an hour of rest, it regained its energy and started wreaking havoc again.

In the end, both D and the ranch Pokemon were exhausted by this restless Pokemon.

Looking at the devastated ranch, D felt heartbroken.

Vigoroth isn't really suited to ranch life.

They should be taken care of by Trainers, as Trainers can take them to battle other Trainers and challenge Gyms.

In contrast, most of the ranch Pokemon are used to a laid-back life and don't enjoy such intense battle life.

Just as D was at a loss for what to do, the Heroic Expedition Team returned with Jirachi.

Along with them came four familiar faces.

Standing on the far left were what seemed like a pair of siblings, the sister wearing red, and the brother in a green T-shirt with black-framed glasses.

In the middle was a cap-wearing boy, with a Pikachu on his shoulder.

On the far right was a somewhat mature-looking, dark-skinned boy with squinty eyes.

Seeing them, D was slightly stunned.

May, Max, Ash, Brock...

Weren't they traveling in the Hoenn Region? How did they end up coming to Paldea with Mew and the others?

---

---

---

---

# 464: Chapter 461: Ash's Arrival

"Yima!"

"Miu!"

Mew and Teddiursa happily flew over to D from afar.

D smiled as he patted their heads, then looked at the four people in front of him.

Mew and Teddiursa turned around and looked towards Ash and the others along with D.

At this moment, Ash and his friends were completely captivated by the scenery in the ranch.

Because there were so many Pokemon here they had never seen before!

A few days ago, they were still traveling around the Hoenn Region to collect Gym Badges.

Upon hearing the news of the approaching millennium comet, they went to the Pokemon Amusement Park to witness the spectacle that only occurs once every thousand years.

However, instead of encountering the millennium comet, they met three Pokemon calling themselves the Heroic Expedition Team.

At that time, Butler had just handed over the Sleeping Cocoon to them, and this group of Pokemon arrived.

After a battle, they learned all the truth from that Teddiursa about Butler's evil plan and decided to help them.

When everything was over, Mew told them it had a way to change Jirachi's unfortunate fate of sleeping for a thousand years, so they came along, crossed the sea, and arrived in this distant place.

Among the four, only Brock tried hard to stay calm.

He looked at the man in front of him with his squinted eyes and asked:

"Would you be the Trainer of the Heroic Expedition Team?"

May looked at the Heroic Expedition Team beside her.

The legendary Zapdos and Mew, and that mysterious Teddiursa...

She couldn't help but mutter, "This is truly incredible!"

D smiled slightly, "Hello, I'm D, the owner of this ranch."

Ash shifted his attention from the Pokemon around them and began to introduce himself:

"Hello, I'm Ash from Pallet Town!"

"I'm Brock."

"My name is May, and this is my brother Max!"

D nodded but didn't speak because there were more important things to do at the moment.

Besides Ash and his friends, Absol and a Jirachi were also invited to the ranch by the Heroic Expedition Team.

D's gaze fell on the innocent and naive Jirachi hiding behind Absol.

"Follow me!"

He said to the crowd in front of him.

The group walked through the ranch and entered the living room.

Seeing guests arrive, Indeedee started preparing tea and snacks for them.

"Sorry for the trouble!"

In the living room.

Ash and Brock curiously watched Indeedee bring cups of tea to them.

Ash couldn't help himself and took out his Rotom Phone to scan Indeedee.

[Indeedee, female, Emotion Pokemon, provides caring services to humans and Pokemon to collect appreciation. Female Indeedee are very good at taking care of children!]

The electronic voice from the Rotom Phone came out.

Both Ash and Brock were very surprised.

"Indeedee... what a magical Pokemon!" Ash couldn't help but say.

D smiled slightly, "Indeed, Indeedee is a very reliable Pokemon."

Ash then pointed his Rotom Phone at Koraidon nearby.

But the response he got was [Unable to find related information, it's an undiscovered Pokemon].

Ash widened his eyes in disbelief at Koraidon.

"Ash, now's not the time to be concerned with this kind of stuff!"

May commented with an exasperated tone.

Ash snapped back to reality, chuckling apologetically, "Got too excited seeing so many Pokemon I've never seen before!"

Seeing Ash like this, D chuckled.

Brock brought the conversation back to the main topic, looking at the mysterious man with calmness:

"D, do you have a way to help Jirachi?"

"Yes, that's why I commissioned the Heroic Expedition Team to find Jirachi." D replied.

He looked at Jirachi, who was curiously observing everyone around it next to Mew.

Their eyes met at the perfect moment.

Jirachi's gaze and demeanor were as pure as a child's.

Mew nudged it and called out, "Miu～" (He's D, a very nice person～)

"D?"

The next second, Jirachi's innocent voice echoed in everyone's ears.

"Yes, I am D."

D looked at the adorable Pokemon, then introduced it to Ash and the others:

"That's the nature of Jirachi, it sleeps for a thousand years and can only wake for seven days. Because it only awakens every thousand years, its personality and mental growth are childlike."

"How pitiful..." May said sympathetically.

Max looked up at the floating Jirachi and earnestly pleaded, "D, please help Jirachi!"

"Don't worry, leave it to me!"

D turned around, fetched the special Leaf Green Ball from the kitchen, and walked to Jirachi.

"This is a dish made with secret techniques and special ingredients. Once Jirachi eats it, it won't have to sleep anymore."

"Dish?"

Jirachi tilted its head, looking confusedly at the green ball in front of it.

"Yes." D nodded, gently asking, "Would Jirachi like to wake up every morning and play with friends, like Absol does, and then go to sleep together when the sun sets?"

---

"You'll never have to sleep for such a long time again, nor worry about waking up to find all your familiar friends gone. You can wake up like the regular Pokemon in this world when the sun rises."

These words touched Jirachi's heart.

Its gaze flickered slightly, recalling the Pokemon friends it made the last time it awoke.

Its tone full of hope for that kind of life: "Is that really possible?"

"Of course." D said.

Jirachi looked back at Mew and Teddiursa, and then at Ash and Max.

Even though they had only been together for just one day, Jirachi had already become friends with them.

Looking at the encouraging gazes from everyone, Jirachi nodded vigorously, and then ate the Leaf Green Ball.

The next second, a vibrant light erupted from Jirachi's body.

When the light dispersed, it seemed something had changed unknowingly.

Jirachi looked down at its body in confusion.

"Is this enough?"

"Yes, indeed." D smiled and said, "Now you won't have to sleep like before."

Hearing this, Jirachi's eyes immediately lit up.

Beside it, Mew happily flew over to celebrate.

"Miu!"

The two Pokemon happily flew around in the room.

Watching this scene, Ash and others all sighed with relief.

"That's great."

"But by the way, was the dish Jirachi just ate made by Mr. D?" Ash couldn't help but ask.

"Yes, it was." D smiled and explained, "That's my special ability, you can think of it as the same type as psychic abilities."

"Psychic abilities?" Brock immediately perked up, asking with a face full of surprise, "Like those humans with psychic abilities?"

As he knew, the Gym Leader Sabrina from the Kanto Region possessed psychic abilities.

And Ash also recalled his experience challenging the Saffron Gym.

"It seems you've met humans with psychic abilities before." D said with a smile.

Brock nodded, about to say something when suddenly a cry from a Pokemon was heard outside the door.

Everyone's attention was instantly drawn to that sound.

"What's happening?" Ash puzzled, "Is it the Pokemon in Mr. D's ranch fighting?"

D knew full well about that sound.

The group went out to check, and as expected, just as D had anticipated, the irritable Vigoroth was jumping around the ranch.

And Farfetch'd was chasing it madly, carrying a leek.

D glanced in the direction of the Scallion Garden, and sure enough, saw the scallions there had been ruined by Vigoroth again.

"It's a Vigoroth!" May immediately recognized the Pokemon.

Her father Norman's gym raised several Vigoroth.

"That Farfetch'd is chasing it!" Ash's eyes followed Vigoroth and Farfetch'd, asking, "Mr. D, what's going on?"

D: "..."

He sighed helplessly and explained, "That Vigoroth was once a Slakoth I took in. It used to just sleep in the Berry Garden, but in the past few days, it suddenly evolved into a Vigoroth and started causing chaos everywhere."

"That's right, Vigoroth is a Pokemon with a lot of energy, and they won't calm down until they exhaust all their excess energy." May nodded.

Exhaust? How to exhaust? Through Pokemon battles?

Thinking of Pokemon battles, Ash immediately got excited, rolling up his sleeves and saying:

"Mr. D, let me help!"

D: "But Vigoroth's energy is quite abundant!"

"There must be a way to calm it down!" Ash told Pikachu on his shoulder: "Pikachu, let's go!"

"Pika-pi!"

Pikachu rushed out immediately, blocking the Vigoroth.

Seeing a Pokemon coming to intercept, Vigoroth immediately went into a furious state, pounding its chest with its fists, then charging at Pikachu while growling.

Pikachu instantly engaged in a bout with Vigoroth.

Seeing this, May couldn't help but facepalm, saying, "Ash is so reckless!"

D thought to himself that this really fit Ash's style.

Watching this scene, an idea suddenly popped into his mind.

Could he entrust Vigoroth to Ash's care?

Over the past few days, the Pokemon in the ranch had been tormented by Vigoroth, and Alcremie and the others weren't fond of battling Pokemon, unable to meet Vigoroth's challenges anytime and everywhere.

At a time like this, if a trainer could capture Vigoroth, they could exhaust Vigoroth's energy through constant Pokemon battles.

More importantly, he trusted Ash's character.

"I should ask Vigoroth about its thoughts!" D made a decision in his heart.

This way, it would be a win-win situation, helping Vigoroth find a suitable trainer, and the crops in the ranch would be spared from ruin.

Meanwhile, in the sky, atop a Meowth-shaped hot air balloon,

Meowth, Jessie, and James were observing the ranch below through binoculars.

"The twerps are battling a Vigoroth, Meowth!"

"Let me see!" Jessie grabbed the binoculars from Meowth's hands, first looking at Ash and Pikachu, then slowly shifting the lens.

---

"Wow! There are so many Pokemon on this ranch! And a bunch I've never even seen before!"

James beside her also got excited: "If we could catch them all and present them to the Boss..."

As soon as he said that, the trio all pictured Boss Giovanni praising them in their heads, promoting them and giving them a big bonus.

"What a wonderful feeling, meow!" Meowth made up his mind on the spot: "It's decided, we'll be the advance team exploring the Paldea Region, meow!"

All of a sudden, a curious voice came from the side.

"Rooaar?"

The trio turned their heads and saw several Dragonite with red satchels hanging at their sides. No one knew when they had appeared around the balloon, but they were staring at them with big, shiny black eyes.

One Dragonite with a scar on its face was glaring at them fiercely, looking anything but friendly.

"So many Dragonite, meow!" Before Meowth could finish talking, he felt a chill down his back, because for some reason those Dragonite were full of hostility toward them!

Dragonite are extremely strong Pokemon, and especially with this many of them, there was absolutely no way the trio could handle it.

Realizing this, the trio immediately dropped their binoculars and huddled together, shivering.

"Rooaar?"

"Rooaar."

"ROOAAAR!!"

The Dragonite began to talk among themselves.

Jessie stared wide-eyed and asked, "Meowth, what are they saying?"

Cold sweat was already beading on Meowth's forehead: "They heard what we just said about catching all those Pokemon for the Boss, meow!"

The next second, one of the Dragonite slashed at the hot-air balloon with Dragon Claw.

With a hiss-hiss, air spewed out from the balloon, which immediately lost its balance and went flying off into the distance.

Only the trio's screams were left behind, fading in the wind.

"What an annoying feeling!"

After driving off the bad guys, the Dragonite happily flew back to the ranch.

By this time, Ash's battle with Vigoroth had already ended, and the Vigoroth, having burned through a huge amount of stamina, finally calmed down.

However, only D knew this was just temporary; it wouldn't be long before Vigoroth started another round of rampaging.

Just then, Max suddenly saw over a dozen Dragonite descending from the sky into the ranch.

He immediately let out a cry of surprise: "So many Dragonite!"

Hearing that, Brock, Ash, and the others quickly looked up at the sky as well.

Seeing all those Dragonite with satchels on their backs, Brock's face was full of shock.

"Those are..."

"They're the delivery staff on my ranch."

D's voice came from the side.

"Delivery staff?!" Brock was stunned.

Using Dragonite as delivery Pokemon—this Mr. D was way too extravagant, wasn't he?

So, so incredible!

May's face was also full of amazement.

Only Ash looked totally fired up. He was only now belatedly realizing that this rancher named D might be a very powerful Trainer.

Not only had he caught Mew and Zapdos and sent them off as an exploration team to travel, he'd also captured so many Dragonite...

It was decided! He was going to have a Pokemon battle with Mr. D!

Ash immediately challenged D.

D: "..." Sure, that was very Ash.

But it was already eleven o'clock, almost noon, and he needed to go prepare lunch.

Thinking of this, D said, "This afternoon, okay? It's almost mealtime. I'll go make lunch. You can all stay here on the ranch today—feel free to look around while I'm busy."

D called over Big Brother Indeedee and said to him, "Please show our guests around the ranch."

"Ee-dee." Big Brother Indeedee nodded lightly, then stepped forward and started leading the way.

"Sorry to trouble you!"

The four of them politely thanked Big Brother Indeedee.

Then Big Brother Indeedee began leading everyone on a tour of D Ranch.

Ash held up his Rotom Phone the whole time, using the Pokedex to learn about all the Pokemon he'd never seen before.

D, meanwhile, walked over to the resting Vigoroth and asked:

"Vigoroth, do you want to go see the outside world?"

Vigoroth: "Roo-roo?"

"I mean going on a journey with a Human Trainer, constantly challenging Gyms and battling professionals. That way you'll become stronger and stronger. If you're willing, I'll help you find a suitable Trainer," D said.

Hearing this, Vigoroth's face showed longing.

Ever since it evolved, the blood in its body had been boiling nonstop.

That feeling made it impossible for it to stay still. It just wanted to run through the mountains and have all-out fights with other Pokemon.

Vigoroth was clearly tempted.

No good—just thinking about it was making it excited already!

The Vigoroth that had been sitting on the ground resting suddenly leapt to its feet, pounding its fists hard against its chest, letting out a passionate growl.

"Roo-roo!"

D: "..."

Alright, he'd gotten his answer.

In that case, he could ask Ash to catch it later.

D turned around and went back to the house to start cooking.

He'd wanted to check on Jirachi, but discovered that all those Pokemon had run off.

He didn't even need to think to know that Mew and Teddiursa had taken Jirachi and Absol to the World Tree to visit the Pokemon Guild.

D wasn't in a rush to call them back. Only after he finished making lunch did he use his Rotom Phone to summon them home.

By then, Ash and the others had also finished touring the ranch.

Thinking back on all the rare and unusual Pokemon on the ranch, Ash's face was full of excitement.

When he saw the videophone in the living room, he suddenly remembered something.

"Mr. D, could I borrow your videophone for a bit?"

"Of course." D nodded. He glanced at Ash's Rotom Phone, a little puzzled why Ash wasn't just using that.

Maybe the videophone had more of a "feel" to it?

While D was musing, Ash had already dialed the number.

Very soon, the call connected.

Professor Oak's face appeared on the screen.

Seeing the faintly familiar background behind Ash, Professor Oak was a bit surprised: "Hm? Ash, are you at a Pokemon Center right now?"

"No! We ended up in the Paldea Region because of something that happened on the way. Once things are settled here, we'll go back to the Hoenn Region and keep traveling." Ash explained.

"The Paldea Region?!" Professor Oak was quite shocked.

Ash nodded excitedly: "Yeah, that's right! We came to a ranch that raises tons of Pokemon!"

Just then, Professor Oak saw D walking past behind Ash with plates in his hands.

"D?" he blurted out in disbelief.

D had already heard Professor Oak's voice. He smiled at the screen and greeted him: "Long time no see, Professor Oak!"

"Huh? Professor Oak, you know Mr. D?" Ash was very surprised.

Professor Oak nodded with a smile: "Yes, I've been to D Ranch before. By the way, D is a very strong Trainer. Have you had a Pokemon battle with him yet?"

Hearing that, Ash's eyes burned with fighting spirit: "Mr. D has already accepted my challenge! We're going to battle after lunch!"

"Ah! Then you'd better give it your all!" Professor Oak said, laughing.

---

---

---

---

# 465: Chapter 462: Pikachu: Am I Fighting Calyrex?

"Everyone, come out!"

Looking at the sumptuous dishes on the dining table, Ash was very happy as he took out several Poke Balls from his pocket and threw them with force.

With flashes of white light, all of Ash's Pokemon appeared in the living room.

Pikachu, Treecko, Taillow, and Corphish.

May and Max had been on the road these days and hadn't had such a formal meal in a long time.

They both swallowed a bit, and May couldn't help but say, "Mr. D's cooking is amazing!"

Brock picked up a Poffin and a Pokeblock, drawing them close to study them carefully.

"This rich aroma, this crystal-clear luster, it's something only very skilled Pokemon Breeders can make. Mr. D, are you also a Pokemon Breeder?" Brock asked.

"It's not that exaggerated, I'm just getting better with practice." D smiled and shook his head: "With so many Pokemon on the ranch, I have to make Poffins and Pokeblocks for them every day. Over time, I've honed my skills."

"Pika Pika..."

Upon hearing this, Ash's Pikachu picked up a Poffin and put it into its mouth.

The next second, a look of happiness appeared on its face.

"Pika~chu~"

"Looks like Pikachu likes it too!" Ash smiled.

D chuckled: "If you like it, have some more."

He turned his head towards the ranch to call his Pokemon back home for a meal.

A clear whistle sound spread across the ranch.

Pawmot, Dragonite, Alcremie, Gholdengo, Ogerpon, and other Pokemon playing outside immediately came running from all directions.

Koraidon and Cyclizar had been waiting in the living room for a while, while Eevee and its friends went to play on the World Tree.

Even though they had visited the ranch in the morning, Ash and the others were still inevitably drawn to the sight of so many Pokemon they had never seen before.

"The variety of Pokemon here is really rich!" May exclaimed.

Max looked around and suddenly noticed something, asking: "Huh? Where are Jirachi and Mew?"

"They were probably taken to the secret base by Mew and Teddiursa!" D explained: "That's a hidden place created using the move Secret Power by Mew, even I don't know its exact location!"

Ash was just about to say something when, in the next second, a Pokemon with a big head suddenly appeared in the living room.

The Pokemon like Pikachu and Treecko, who were eating, were startled by the newcomer and let out screams.

Brock, upon seeing this Pokemon, was immediately shocked and widened his eyes.

"That Pokemon is?!"

Ash immediately took out his Rotom Phone and pointed it at Calyrex.

The next second, Rotom's electronic voice came from the phone.

"Calyrex, the King Pokemon, possesses the powers of healing and blessing, and is a Pokemon full of compassion!"

"Calyrex?!" May exclaimed: "Could it be..."

She immediately thought of the legendary story that was causing quite a stir online.

Brock, having opened the Pokemon Forum, quickly found a user named Calyrex in his followers list.

"That's right, it's the legendary Pokemon!" Brock nodded in awe.

Looking at D beside him and recalling what Professor Oak had said about visiting D Ranch, an unbelievable thought immediately crossed Brock's mind.

No wonder Professor Oak published a paper on Calyrex afterwards...

So, the Trainer who captured Calyrex was Mr. D?

"No need to be so surprised." D chuckled softly: "Just treat Calyrex like an ordinary Pokemon."

Calyrex also came to eat upon hearing the whistle sound.

Upon hearing this, it gently nodded and looked at the young humans in front, speaking:

"Don't mind, I am no longer a king, now I am just an ordinary farmer."

Ash and the others widened their eyes, looking so stunned that they couldn't speak.

"Alright, let's eat!" D said.

Everyone gathered around the dining table, and Ash, Brock, May, and Max occasionally glanced at Calyrex.

This was their first time dining with a Legendary Pokemon, it felt so incredible!

And Ash was full of eager anticipation.

"Mr. D, may I challenge Calyrex later?"

D was slightly taken aback.

Calyrex, though, looked at the boy with interest, asking: "Do you wish to battle with me?"

Ash nodded vigorously, his eyes glowing with longing:

"Yes because meeting all the Pokemon in the world and becoming a Pokemon Master has always been my goal!"

"And after that?" Calyrex asked again.

"After that..." Ash thought carefully for a moment, then answered: "I will travel to every corner of this world with Pikachu and the others, to encounter Pokemon I haven't met before!"

"Ash..." May looked at Ash in awe.

D didn't know what to say for a moment.

Calyrex seemed touched by Ash's words.

It gently nodded, then accepted Ash's challenge: "Alright, I accept your challenge."

"Great!" Ash was very excited.

D, meanwhile, looked at Ash's Pokemon with hesitation.

Pikachu, Treecko, Taillow, Corphish...

Except for Pikachu, the other three Pokemon were ones that Ash had not captured for long, right?

At present, Ash's Pikachu hadn't reached its peak strength yet.

---

And the opponent they faced was none other than a Calyrex beyond its prime...

Pikachu: I'm gonna fight Calyrex?

How could I possibly win?

But thinking it's Ash, D found it quite normal.

After all, Ash had just set out on his journey and, after completing the Rainbow Hero's trial, challenged Ho-oh.

Eager to battle, Ash ate his lunch very quickly.

Seeing this, May was at a loss for words: "This guy Ash is really something..."

Brock, who had traveled with Ash for years, crossed his arms and nodded with a smile: "That's Ash's style!"

After lunch, the group went to the battle field in the pasture.

Calyrex floated to the battle site, while D stood with May and the others.

"Huh? Aren't you going to command, Mr. D?" May asked.

D shook his head: "Calyrex knows what to do."

On the field, as expected, Ash sent out Pikachu to fight.

"It's decided, go! Pikachu!" Ash pointed forward.

Pikachu, standing on his shoulder, jumped down immediately and charged towards Calyrex.

"Pika Pika!"

The battle officially began.

Even though Calyrex didn't move, Pikachu still felt a tangible psychic power from it.

This made Pikachu's expression turn serious.

But it didn't retreat; instead, it courageously faced forward.

"Pikachu, use Thunderbolt!"

"Pika—chee!"

With the command given, powerful electricity burst from Pikachu's body.

The intense electric light transformed into countless streaks of lightning rushing toward Calyrex.

Seeing this, Calyrex didn't dodge. Its thoughts flickered, and a transparent Light Screen appeared before it, completely blocking Pikachu's Thunderbolt.

"Pika Pika?!" Pikachu was shocked.

And then Calyrex launched its attack.

Its psychic power materialized and fiercely struck Pikachu.

The move was so swift that Pikachu couldn't react and was blasted away.

"Pikachu!" Ash shouted hastily: "Hang in there!"

Upon hearing this, Pikachu persisted.

Injured but determined, it looked at Calyrex on the opposite side.

Seeing this, Ash's mind raced, and he swiftly changed strategy: "Pikachu, use Quick Attack!"

"Pika Pika!"

With the command issued, Pikachu began to run swiftly, eventually turning into a blur of white light rushing at Calyrex.

However, facing this, Calyrex simply raised its hand lightly, instantly conjuring a ball of psychic energy and sending it flying at Pikachu.

The next moment, Pikachu collided with this Psychic attack.

With a violent explosion, Ash's Pikachu was once again knocked away, collapsing on the ground, unable to continue the battle.

Thus, the battle ended.

"As expected of a Legendary Pokemon!" Brock couldn't help but marvel at the sight.

May and Max were also astonished by Calyrex's immense power.

From the sheer strength Calyrex displayed earlier, Ash's Pikachu stood no chance.

However, Ash wasn't discouraged by the loss; instead, he sincerely thanked Calyrex.

Calyrex nodded slightly and then used Heal Pulse on Pikachu before disappearing, heading over to the Glass Greenhouse to play on the phone.

"How do you feel?" D stepped up and asked with a smile.

Ash nodded vigorously: "Yeah, Calyrex is truly amazing!"

Saying this, he turned his head, looking at D's other Pokemon, speaking with spirited determination:

"Mr. D, let's continue battling! Treecko and Corphish can still battle!"

D: "..."

Alright then! He had promised Ash earlier that morning, and Ash couldn't go back on his word now.

Thus, D casually called upon a few Pokemon and battled with Ash in one match after another.

By the time they finished, it was already five in the afternoon.

D went to tend to the pasture work and prepared today's goods to sell to Zack.

Meanwhile, Ash took Pikachu and the others to help Vigoroth expend its energy.

The young ones are always full of energy; Ash seemed tireless, and D felt like his stamina could rival Vigoroth's.

Brock and Max began observing the Pokemon ecosystem in the pasture.

Witnessing so many Pokemon in the pasture, Max was extremely envious.

He wished he could grow up quickly, have his own Pokemon, and set out on a journey with them!

After seeing Zack off, D returned to the pasture and saw Ash and Vigoroth lying on the grass, panting heavily.

D noticed that Vigoroth seemed to have a fondness for the boy willing to go wild with it.

Seeing this, D nodded silently, getting a general idea.

After dinner, Mew, Teddiursa, and Jirachi returned to the pasture.

Because today was the second day of the Millennial Comet.

Though the spectacle of the Millennial Comet streaking across the sky couldn't be seen from Porto Marinada Town, the night sky at the pasture was equally filled with stars.

When it got dark, D took Ash and the others to stargaze in the pasture.

Jirachi and Mew also sat quietly on the grass, joining everyone in gazing at the summer night sky.

---

"So beautiful!" May couldn't help but murmur.

At this time, Ash and Max suddenly remembered the legend they heard about Jirachi.

They looked at the Star Pokemon with curiosity and asked, "I heard that if you make a wish to Jirachi when it wakes up, Jirachi can fulfill any wish, is that true?"

Mew and Teddiursa also looked at Jirachi curiously.

Hearing the talk about itself, Jirachi had a confused look in its eyes: "Wish?"

D leaned on Koraidon, stroking its big paw, smiling as he watched the scene but did not interject.

Max leaned forward, nodding vigorously: "Yes, wish! I want snacks, endless snacks!"

"Snacks, snacks..." Jirachi repeated, then happily flew up.

The Wish Paper on its head lit up.

Suddenly, bags of snacks appeared out of thin air.

Seeing this, Max and Ash's eyes lit up instantly.

"It really can be fulfilled!" Max was very excited.

Meanwhile, in a store in Porto Marinada Town.

Team Rocket's trio, who were working there, were complaining to each other.

"It's all your fault for saying those things where the Dragonite could hear, which led to the destruction of the Meowth Hot Air Balloon!"

"That was our last activity budget! Now we have no choice but to work here!"

"Who could have thought of such a thing?" James couldn't help but complain: "Besides, you were thinking the same thing!"

"Stop talking meow!" Meowth was tidying up the shelves: "Quickly earn enough money, then I can repair the Meowth Hot Air Balloon, and we can leave this lousy place meow!"

But at that moment, the snacks on the shelves they were responsible for started disappearing one by one.

Seeing this, Meowth was startled: "Haunted meow!"

The store was empty except for the three of them, as night fell, an eerie wind blew, making Jessie and James start feeling scared.

Just then, the warehouse door opened.

It was the store owner coming to check the work progress of these three new employees.

But when the store owner saw the empty shelves, he froze on the spot.

He glared at the three people, shouting: "Before you started working, I clearly said no stealing snacks from the shelves!"

"It wasn't us meow!" Meowth shook its head quickly.

But there were only them here, no one else.

The owner slammed the door shut: "Hmph, since you have no money, you'll stay here working until you pay off what you owe! Scovillain, keep an eye on them and make them work hard!"

After speaking, the owner left.

Leaving a strange-looking Pokemon with two heads glaring at them fiercely.

+

"It really is true! Jirachi can really grant wishes!"

In the ranch, May looked at the snacks scattered around, feeling very excited.

Just as the group prepared to make another wish to Jirachi, D's voice suddenly came from the side.

"This is the store's snacks from Porto Marinada Town; they have the shop's logo on them."

Ash and others turned their heads.

"These snacks were probably transferred by Jirachi from nearby shops." D pretended to speculate.

"Huh?"

May ran over to have a look, and indeed saw the related pattern on them.

"Doesn't that mean Jirachi doesn't have the power to create wishes?" Brock noted this point.

"Natural energy is conserved," D explained: "These snacks couldn't have appeared out of nowhere."

"I see." Ash looked at the snacks, suddenly realizing they had been moved, making the store owner probably very troubled?

Thinking of this, Ash said to Jirachi: "Jirachi, could you help restore these snacks to their original place?"

"Don't you want the snacks anymore?" Jirachi asked confused.

Before Ash could respond, Teddiursa explained the reason to it.

"Imma imma!"

After hearing, Jirachi showed a realization.

It flew into the sky again, the Wish Paper on its head lit up once more.

In the next second, the snacks disappeared one by one.

"By the way, does Jirachi have plans for the future?" May suddenly recalled something.

Since Jirachi doesn't need to sleep for a thousand years anymore, it can go anywhere it wants, right?

Hearing the question, everyone turned their eyes to Jirachi.

Jirachi looked at its two good friends it made since waking up, Mew and Teddiursa.

This morning, they took it to the beautiful big tree and visited the Heroic Expedition Team's secret base. Mew even shared many toys with it.

Moreover, the tree has lots of fun things.

Jirachi really liked it there, thinking it could play for a thousand years and never have enough.

But... Forina still needs it.

That land and the Pokemon living there need it to absorb energy from the stars and then nourish the land.

Thinking of this, a conflicting expression showed on Jirachi's little face.

What should it do?

---

---

---

---

# 466: Chapter 463: Jirachi's Choice

Jirachi still hadn't been able to make a decision.

In the past it had always slept within the crystal, only having seven days of awakening every thousand years.

Because of this, from the moment it was born until now, Jirachi had never gone anywhere.

During these two days, Mew and Teddiursa had been introducing it to all kinds of new and fascinating things.

The chivalrous Heroic Expedition Team, delicious Poffins, the bustling World Tree, beautiful natural scenery…

Jirachi listened with deep envy. It was full of longing for that kind of life, and really wanted to personally experience it all like Mew and the others.

But…

The Forina Land could not do without it; the Pokemon there and every single flower and blade of grass all needed its power.

If it weren't there, all the flowers and grass would surely wither, and the Pokemon living there would all go hungry and lose their homes.

Jirachi didn't want to see such a thing happen.

Thinking of this, its heart had already made a decision.

Jirachi looked at May and answered firmly, "Jirachi wants to go back to the Forina Land, to absorb the energy of the stars and help the Pokemon and nature there!"

Hearing this, May opened her mouth.

"But if you do that, you won't be able to go visit other places, right?"

However, Ash suddenly thought of a good idea.

"Then Jirachi just has to go back to the Forina Region when the thousand-year comet comes to use the energy in the stars to nourish the flowers and plants there! The rest of the time it can freely go out and have fun!" he said.

But Jirachi shook its head and replied in its childish voice:

"No, that won't work. 'Nurturing' isn't something that can be done all at once. The land can't withstand it. That power has to be slowly transmitted from Jirachi's body into the earth."

Hearing this, Ash was out of ideas as well.

For a moment, everyone's spirits were a bit low.

D watched this scene. He had just been about to speak when he suddenly saw the Absol that had been guarding Jirachi silently stand up and walk to Jirachi's side.

"Absol?" Jirachi stared at it in a daze.

Absol's voice was low: "Awoo." (It's okay, don't worry. Everyone can already take care of themselves. Jirachi, just go and do what you want to do.)

Jirachi was slightly stunned.

"Absol…"

Ash didn't understand what Absol said.

"What is Absol saying?" May asked, puzzled.

D didn't understand either. He took out his Rotom Phone, and the Rotom inside automatically translated Absol's words.

"Absol is telling Jirachi to go do what it likes…" Ash murmured.

But there were still many worries in Jirachi's heart.

It simply couldn't stop worrying about everyone in Forina.

Every flower and each little blade of grass there had been nurtured by it personally.

All these years, everyone had gotten used to it.

If it suddenly left…

"I-ma."

"Miu…"

Teddiursa and Mew also felt very sad.

D looked at that Absol and thought that it really did live up to the name of Guardian.

Since that was the case, he might as well lend them a hand.

Thinking this, D said, "I have a way to let Jirachi take care of the Forina Land without needing to absorb the energy of the stars."

As soon as he said this, everyone and every Pokemon around turned to look at him.

D told them to wait here for a bit, and he himself went back home and took out that leaf the World Tree had given him.

[Leaf of Life: A leaf from the World Tree, containing dense Natural Energy. Placing it somewhere can nurture the ecological environment of that place and make it rich and beautiful.]

He brought the leaf back to everyone's side.

Ash and the others immediately fixed their eyes on that large leaf emitting a soft green glow.

"That is…"

"It's glowing!"

"I-ma?" Teddiursa and Mew felt a familiar aura coming from this leaf.

"This is a treasure I happened to obtain before. It can release Natural Energy and nurture the land nearby, making the plants around grow more lush."

"If we place it in Forina, it can take Jirachi's place. That way, Jirachi will be able to travel freely." D said slowly.

"That's amazing!" Max said.

"Something this precious… if it's not here, will it bring any bad effects to your ranch, Mr. D?" Brock asked calmly after thinking for a moment.

Hearing this, Ash and Jirachi also looked at D.

D shook his head with a smile. "Don't worry, it won't. The soil of this ranch was already very fertile, and I'm a professional farmer. Even without this leaf, I can rely on my own strength to make this land fertile."

For example, plowing, fertilizing, and planting reasonably, and so on.

As he spoke, he handed the Leaf of Life to Absol.

"Take it!"

Absol was taken aback.

Jirachi looked at the leaf, a confused expression appearing on its face. Like a simple child, it voiced the question in its heart:

"Why is D giving such a precious treasure to Jirachi?"

D answered with a smile, "Because that way Jirachi won't have to keep sleeping all the time."

Jirachi was slightly taken aback; from this human in front of it, it could feel a strong kindness.

"D…" Jirachi called softly.

"Hm?" D looked at it.

Jirachi happily flew into D's arms, its tone full of gratitude. "Thank you, D, you're the best!"

D smiled faintly.

He reached out and stroked Jirachi's soft body.

Off to the side, May watched this scene and couldn't help sighing with emotion. "This really is wonderful!"

Ash also nodded, genuinely happy for Jirachi. "Yeah, this way Jirachi will never have to worry about the Forina Region again."

The summer night breeze blew gently as the stars in the sky twinkled.

Everyone spent a pleasant night with Jirachi on the ranch.

Early the next morning, Ash and the others were ready to leave.

Because their journey in the Hoenn Region wasn't over yet; they still needed to challenge Gyms, collect badges, and then take part in the Ever Grande Conference.

And besides that, they also wanted to go with Jirachi to return that leaf to the Forina Region.

D wasn't surprised when he heard this.

Before they set off, D brought Vigoroth over in front of Ash.

He looked at Vigoroth, then at Ash, and said to it:

"These past two days, thanks to you calming Vigoroth down. This kid really likes you. It's longing for life outside too, but I can't leave the ranch. Could you take it to see the outside world for me?"

Ash froze for a moment when he heard that.

Brock, however, heard what D meant. "Does this Vigoroth want to become one of Ash's Pokemon?"

Vigoroth's blood boiled as it pounded its chest with its fists.

"I get it." A smile appeared on Ash's face. "In that case, Pikachu, I choose you!"

A capture battle was about to begin!

Brock and May stayed over here to watch.

Jirachi floated in front of D and asked, "Will Jirachi still be able to see D in the future?"

"Of course. As long as you want to, you can see me anytime," D nodded gently.

Jirachi's eyes lit up all at once. "That's great!"

It was decided—after it returned that leaf to Forina, it would come live at the World Tree together with Mew, Teddiursa, and the others!

D then looked over at the nearby Heroic Expedition Team, nodded to them, and praised:

"Nice work. You did a great job completing this mission."

"Ee–ma～"

"Miu～"

Mew and Teddiursa immediately grew a bit embarrassed.

"And Zapdos, I've already heard about your heroic performance in that battle. You're amazing!" D praised without holding back.

Hearing that, a suspicious blush spread across Zapdos's cheeks.

It turned its head away proudly. "Chuu!"

At this moment, Ash's battle had already reached its conclusion.

He put Vigoroth into a Poke Ball and said excitedly, "I caught Vigoroth!"

"Well then, goodbye, Ash," D said to him.

Ash nodded vigorously. "Yeah, goodbye, Mr. D. Someday in the future, Pikachu and I will definitely come travel in the Paldea Region. When that time comes, we'll challenge you and Koraidon again!"

D said with a smile, "I'll be looking forward to that day. Until then, I wish you a smooth journey."

"Goodbye!"

"Goodbye!"

With the goal of becoming a Pokemon Master, Ash and his friends left D Ranch and continued their Gym Badge journey. What might be waiting for them in the future?

The figures of Ash and the others grew more and more distant.

In a patch of grass, Jessie, James, and Meowth looked in that direction.

"The twerps have left, ya know!" James muttered.

"So what are we gonna do now, meow?" Meowth asked.

When the boss of the shop they'd worked at yesterday saw the snacks return, he finally realized he'd misunderstood them.

Not only did that boss cancel their jobs, after learning that their "ship" had been destroyed and they'd been forced to drift here and work part‑time, he even gave them some funds to help them get home.

"What else? Of course we're gonna keep trying to nab that twerp's Pikachu!" Jessie said without the slightest hesitation.

Those Dragonite were way out of their league, and anyone who could get so many Dragonite to make deliveries had to be a big shot—definitely not someone they could afford to mess with.

By comparison, their plan to capture Pikachu sounded much easier.

More importantly, if they ended up dragging Team Rocket and Boss Giovanni into trouble with people they shouldn't provoke, that would be a disaster.

On top of that, Team Rocket currently had no plans to extend into the Paldea Region, so they might as well obediently head to the Hoenn Region and help Boss Giovanni investigate Team Magma and Team Aqua over there.

"If that's the case, what are we waiting for? Let's set off right now!"

James could almost see a bright white tomorrow waiting for them.

All of a sudden, a white light flashed, and a Wobbuffet popped out of its Poke Ball.

"Wobbuffet!"

---

---
