# 467: Chapter 464: Taking Pictures of Pokemon, Zygarde Observing in Secret

League Year 200, May 12.

The Heroic Expedition Team, along with Ash and the others, smoothly returned to the Hoenn Region.

Far away in Paldea, D received a message from Mew and Teddiursa.

It said they had successfully delivered that leaf to the Forina Region, and parted ways with Ash and the others.

Jirachi no longer needs to stay in the Forina Region to nurture the land; it wishes to see the world outside, like ordinary Pokémon.

So, after bidding farewell to Absol and other Pokémon in the Forina Region, Jirachi chose to embark on a journey with the Heroic Expedition Team.

Looking at the photo sent by Teddiursa on the phone screen, D couldn't help but smile.

The background of the photo is the Forina Region, and the time of the photo seems coincidentally when the millennial comet just passed across the sky.

Under the sky full of stars, Mew, Teddiursa, and Zapdos clustered around Jirachi.

They looked at the camera, all of them displaying joyful expressions.

"Ah, this is wonderful!"

D was very satisfied with this ending.

With this, Jirachi no longer has to endure such a pitiful fate.

From now on, it can fly freely in the sky like most Pokémon in this world.

D saved the photo to his phone, planning to print it out later for collection.

He acted immediately, ordering a machine specifically for printing photos online, and dispatched a delivery Dragonite to pick it up.

Thirty minutes later, Dragonite returned to the ranch with the machine.

D imported the photo into it, and soon, the photo that captured the story of the Heroic Expedition Team and Jirachi was printed out.

"Ah gah see?"

Seeing D busy over there, Koraidon's big head leaned in, seemingly curious to see what he was doing.

D took the opportunity to stroke Koraidon's head, explaining to it, "I'm printing photos."

"Ah gah see!"

Koraidon saw Teddiursa and the others in the photo and understood.

Meanwhile, a new idea suddenly popped into D's mind.

——He wants to buy a camera and a photo album to capture the Pokémon on the ranch, preserving their [present] well!

Although a device like the Rotom Phone can store them more conveniently, D still feels paper photos have more commemorative significance and nostalgia.

That afternoon, he went to the department store in Porto Marinada Town to buy a camera and a family album.

Once back at the ranch, D called over the Pokémon in the ranch to start photographing them.

Pawmot, Alcremie, Koraidon, Cyclizar, Dragonite, Butterfree, Combee, Miltank, Dugtrio, Garganacl, Snorlax, the Eevees, Wooloo, Psyduck, Ogerpon, Gholdengo, Indeedee Sibling, Flutter Mane...

For most Pokémon on the ranch, it was their first time experiencing a photo shoot, so they were all very curious about D's actions.

Butterfree flew to D's side, curiously looking at the camera being adjusted, "Pui-yi?"

"Bui-bui!"

The Eevees also gathered around, pawing at D's leg with their pads, indicating they wanted to see too.

D squatted down and showed them the camera's screen.

The camera lens at this moment was pointed at Dragonite in front, and one Eevee accidentally touched a button, the screen's image began enlarging, and Dragonite's big face immediately appeared on the screen.

"Bui!"

The Eevees let out an exclamation, it's Dragonite!

D aimed the lens at his partner Eevee and pressed the shutter.

With the sound of a click, his partner Eevee's face was fixed on the screen.

"Bamo!"

Pawmot saw Eevee's photo and was very envious; it wants to be photographed too!

Other Pokémon also want to join in.

Seeing their intention, D smiled and said to the group of Pokémon in front:

"Alright, now continue to play as you normally do, don't mind me, I'll be beside you, capturing the most natural photos."

So next, the Pokémon cooperated very well and began playing on the ranch just like usual.

D roamed among them, continuously pressing the shutter.

The Eevees cooling in the shadows.

The line of obediently sitting Psyducks.

The Dragonite soaring in the sky.

Koraidon's handsome look back.

The Wooloo sunbathing on the grass.

Flareon by the fireplace.

By night, the Pokémon returned to their nests.

Taking the opportunity, D continued to capture their sleeping faces.

Without realizing it, there were over a hundred photos stored in the camera.

Once the Pokémon in the house entered dreamland, D did not go to sleep immediately; instead, he sat at the desk, turned on the lamp, using moonlight from the window, and slowly put these printed photos into the album one by one.

Behind him, in the darkness, Koraidon lazily yawned; it glanced up at D's back and then settled peacefully on the floor, gradually falling asleep.

After finishing the album, D gently flipped through the photos of those Pokémon above.

Playful Pokémon, daydreaming Pokémon, flying Pokémon...

Looking at these photos, D's heart was filled with warmth.

Ah, Pokémon are just so adorable!

He put away the album, turned to his partner Eevee who was sound asleep in the nest, and lowered his head to give it a kiss.

---

Eevee wasn't startled awake; instead, it comfortably shifted its body, paws up, revealing its fluffy and soft belly.

This meant that the Eevee was completely relaxed at the moment, and it trusted its surroundings immensely.

How cute! D thought to himself.

He smiled, returned to his bed, lay down, and looked at Koraidon by the bedside and Pawmot at the foot of the bed. Feeling completely relaxed, he closed his eyes.

It was such a happy day.

Time to sleep!

*

The following morning, D got up early to start a new day's work.

It was summer, and the weather was extremely hot, which significantly increased people's demand for watermelons.

D received a large number of watermelon orders every day.

Thanks to Dragonite's speedy delivery, it usually only took half an hour for deliveries to reach customers' doorsteps.

Customers ordering from D Ranch never had to worry about watermelons going bad during transit.

Early in the morning, the working Dragonites lined up to receive watermelons from D, flying them to various locations across Paldea.

"Phew!"

After sending off the Dragonites and checking for any missed orders, D finally let out a deep breath.

By nine in the morning, the sunlight grew scorching, with the wind carrying waves of heat.

D stood under the eaves, squinting as he glanced at the distant hillside.

The weather was so hot that not even the cicadas in the hills were chirping. Looking afar, he could see the Abundant Shrine surrounded by lush foliage.

In contrast to the real world, the temperature in the Reversed World was just as always, quite cool.

So, Eevees, Ogerpon, Gholdengo, Alcremie, and other Pokemon all went there to escape the heat.

Gogoat also went to the World Tree to teach that Skiddo.

Now, only D, Koraidon, and the others remained on the ranch.

Just as D was about to return to his room for a rest, Koraidon, who was relaxing under the eaves, seemed to suddenly notice something, suddenly opening its eyes and looking in a certain direction.

"Aga-sii?! "

Next to it, Cyclizar also lifted its head.

"Huh?" D turned to look at Koraidon and asked, "What's wrong?"

Koraidon changed from a squatting position to its four-legged big golden retriever form.

It let out a "Aga-sii" before dashing forward, diving into a thicket, and fetched a green, caterpillar-like unknown creature in its mouth.

Koraidon carried the creature back to D, released it from its mouth, and placed it on the wooden floor.

When D took a clear look at the thing, he was instantly frozen in place.

This is... a Zygarde cell?!

What's going on?! How could a Zygarde cell appear in the Paldea Region?

D reached out to pick up the "cell" that was trembling slightly.

The lump of cells seemed terrified, instinctively burrowing into D's palm when picked up.

"Ga-oo?"

Cyclizar tilted its head, looking at the unknown creature, full of confusion, wondering what that thing was?

D, seeing Cyclizar's curious expression, explained to it:

"It could be considered a Pokemon, I suppose?"

Zygarde, the third deity of the Kalos Region, the famous Z God, and the guardian of ecosystems, has always silently monitored the ecosystem's actions.

In the anime, because Kalos was filled with tree-shaped negative energy cores, the Z God even left Ash's Greninja behind to help "chop trees."

Among all the Legendary Pokemon, Zygarde is a very special existence.

Because it can divide itself into "core" and "cell" forms, distributing them across nature.

If danger arises, these cells start to gather, transforming into Zygarde to display their abilities.

In the anime, the little blob that Bonnie found was Zygarde's core.

The one appearing in his ranch now, however, was a cell.

Staring at the cell in his hand, a bold guess emerged in D's mind.

Could it be that this cell was sent by Zygarde to act as a monitor?

It's well-known that cells lack personal will, only acting on core instructions.

With no Zygarde in the Paldea Region, cells appearing here could very likely mean Zygarde sent them.

But D was a bit unsure.

Because as a guardian of ecosystems, it's not unusual for Zygarde to appear anywhere. It oversees the whole world's ecosystems, not just Kalos.

"Koraidon, have you seen this little guy near the ranch before?" D asked.

"Aga-sii..."

Koraidon shook its big head.

"I see." D seemed pensive.

So, was this cell attracted by the World Tree's aura?

After all, if the World Tree is born, it would greatly benefit the entire ecosystem.

No matter the possibility, Zygarde holds no malice towards them.

So, after a brief moment of thought, D brought the cell back home.

This little thing is capable of forming into a Gundam-like being!

I'll collect it for now; perhaps in the future, the World Tree will attract more Zygarde cells and cores.

When that time comes, I'll bring them all home and see if I can build a Gundam.

As Calyrex was playing on his phone in the air-conditioning, he suddenly sensed an unfamiliar aura.

---

He raised his eyes and saw that green, unknown creature in D's palm.

Calyrex was slightly stunned: "That is?"

"This little guy?" D answered with a smile. "This is Softie."

"Softie?" Calyrex's face showed surprise.

"The new name I gave it!" As D spoke, he took out some snacks that Pokemon liked from the cupboard, trying to feed them to Softie.

But that green clump of cells didn't eat; it instinctively burrowed into D's palm, seeming very nervous.

Seeing this, D wasn't surprised.

Because Zygarde's cells had no self‑will. They might not need to eat like other Pokemon; they only needed to absorb some Natural Energy, or sunlight and water, to survive.

He thought it over carefully, then set Softie on the windowsill, placing a piece of snack and a small saucer of water beside it.

The cell lay there motionless, only blinking once in a while, as if observing this place.

No one knew how much time had passed before Softie seemed to run out of energy.

Only then did it slowly crawl a short distance to the snack, open its tiny mouth, and instinctively begin to eat.

At the same time, in the Kalos Region, inside Terminus Cave.

Zygarde, through its cells, had witnessed everything it encountered.

That human carried a dense aura of Natural Energy. Was that presence from before emanating from him?

Zygarde was deeply puzzled. Could that person be a human chosen by the ecosystem?

And when it saw that human actually give its cell the name "Softie," the entire Pokemon fell silent.

Softie…

It was Zygarde, the Guardian of order. How could it be called that name?!

"No. I know far too little. I need to continue monitoring for a while longer before I can reach a conclusion."

The green glow in Terminus Cave gradually sank into stillness as Zygarde continued overseeing the outside world's ecosystem.

*

Hoenn Region.

Inside a huge volcano, Steven looked with a grave expression at the slim man opposite him, who had a head of red hair, wore black‑rimmed glasses, and exuded an air of calm composure from head to toe.

Fiery red magma flowed slowly, and on the surrounding Ground lay many humans dressed in Team Magma uniforms and Pokemon such as Poochyena.

The wounds on their bodies all testified to how fierce a battle had just taken place here.

"Why do you insist on stopping me?" Maxie asked in a calm tone. "Everything I do is solely to expand the landmass and increase the living space for humans and Pokemon. Whether for humans or Pokemon, that would bring tremendous benefit."

Behind him, a massive Pokemon covered in crimson armor plates lay with its eyes tightly shut, slumbering in the magma.

Steven's expression was extremely serious as he tried to stop this extremist man:

"That will only destroy the balance of this world! It will plunge the land into endless Inferno! When that happens, both humans and Pokemon will be severely affected!"

"Hmph, even if you are the Champion, you cannot stop me. Look!" Maxie spread his arms wide, as if presenting the Pokemon behind him to Steven. "Look at this slumbering Super Ancient Pokemon, Groudon—how beautiful it is! I've waited so long for this day to come! No one can stop me!"

Steven furrowed his brow and tightened his grip on the Poke Ball in his hand. Beside him, Metagross stood firmly, guarding him.

At Maxie's side, a Camerupt was lightly tapping the Ground with its hooves, looking ready to charge at any moment.

A great battle was on the verge of erupting!

An identical scene was playing out in the seafloor cavern.

Archie let out a feverish laugh:

"Hoo‑hahaha… I really pity you! You went to all this trouble to follow me here, but now… no one can stop us! No one! Even without the Treasure Pearl, we can awaken Kyogre!"

Wallace cast an elegant glance at the slumbering Kyogre.

He curled his lips slightly and said softly, "Looks like I won't be able to make it back to Sootopolis City before dark today. I wonder how things are going on Steven's side… But Milotic, we absolutely cannot let that guy down, can we?"

"Mii!"

With a melodious cry, the Milotic at Wallace's side shot forward fiercely in Archie's direction.

---

---

---

---

# 468: Chapter 465: Hoenn Incident Erupts, Groudon and Kyogre Awaken!

"Boom!"

Metagross's powerful arms slammed fiercely onto Camerupt.

The heavily struck Camerupt let out a painful growl, collapsing to the ground, panting heavily.

Steven frowned, looking at the defeated Maxie, and said, "This is the end!"

Maxie's face was full of reluctance and anger: "How come?! I've come all this way, how can I fall here?!"

"No, even if I didn't get the Treasure Pearl, with the Meteorite. I can definitely awaken Groudon!"

With that, Maxie looked down at the Meteorite in his arms.

It was what Team Magma had dug out from Meteor Falls after their plan to seize the Treasure Pearl failed.

These meteorites, which fell from space to the Earth, contained incredibly powerful energy. As long as it's thrown into the magma, it can surely awaken Groudon!

Thinking of this, Maxie unhesitatingly threw it into the magma where Groudon was located.

Seeing this, Steven immediately tried to stop it, but it was already too late.

As the Meteorite fell into the magma, the energy within it directly reacted with the magma, causing a huge explosion, and the lava in this volcano was about to erupt!

The scorching lava began to bubble, like a boiling oil pot, splattering in all directions.

Steven's expression was tense, and at that moment, he suddenly noticed that the Pokemon that was originally slumbering in the magma had opened its eyes at some point!

Those ancient golden pupils looked down at Maxie in front of them.

The red magma slowly flowed around its body, and then, a heavy roar came from Groudon's mouth, sweeping a terrifying aura in all directions.

Steven's expression became more solemn than ever: "Metagross, be prepared!"

Nearby, Metagross swung its arms, preparing to battle the gigantic red Pokemon at any moment.

Not far away, Maxie looked extremely excited seeing Groudon awake:

"Groudon, you have finally awakened, it was I who summoned you from your slumber..."

However, before he could finish his sentence, Groudon let out an even more terrifying roar.

Immediately, the ground began to tremble, and huge rocks fell continuously from the top of the volcano.

Amidst the rumbling sound, a huge rock fell beside Steven, missing him by only a few centimeters.

Maxie was shaken to the ground, while Steven and Metagross hurriedly dodged.

When they finally found a safe spot, they suddenly discovered that Groudon's figure was no longer visible in the magma.

A bad premonition suddenly surged in Steven's heart.

He recalled an ancient legend circulating in the Hoenn Region, and at this moment, he completely ignored Team Magma and Maxie, flying out of the volcano on Skarmory.

Steven took out his Rotom Phone and quickly contacted the Hoenn League.

Meanwhile, deep in the underwater cavern.

The once-calm seawater suddenly surged.

At the moment Groudon awoke, the slumbering Kyogre in the underwater cavern seemed to sense something and suddenly opened its eyes.

An overwhelming aura instantly swept out.

Upon seeing this, several Team Aqua members were startled and quickly reported:

"Bo-Boss, Kyogre is awake!"

Archie, who was battling with Wallace, was equally shocked by this:

"What's going on?! Wasn't it sleeping?!"

Wallace twitched his lips, directing Milotic to use Waterfall on Archie while saying:

"It's just asleep, not dead!"

The massive wave accurately struck Archie's Sharpedo.

At this moment, Kyogre also noticed the small humans who had intruded into its cavern.

It decisively ignored them, then turned around and left the underwater cavern, swimming rapidly toward the sea surface.

Because Kyogre had sensed that Groudon was heading in that direction, seemingly intending to expand its territory!

*

Hoenn Region, Rustboro City.

It began to rain.

The drizzle pattered onto the streets, catching people by surprise with this sudden shower.

Trainers traveling by the roadside, a little girl out to buy ice cream, and office workers holding briefcases hurriedly sought shelter under the street shops.

The office worker shook the rain off his briefcase, muttering:

"Damn it, the weather forecast clearly said today would be a sunny day."

Now, with the rain pouring harder, he had no way to meet his client.

The office worker anxiously glanced at his watch, hoping that this sudden rain would stop soon.

Just then, a shout suddenly came from beside him.

"Look!"

The office worker looked up in the direction pointed by a young Trainer, and then he saw a baffling scene.

The once-clear sky was now shrouded in heavy clouds, with a terrible storm brewing within, as if some great terror was about to be born.

The rain poured harder, quickly submerging Rustboro City and the surrounding towns in it.

However, compared to Rustboro City, Lavaridge Town was under a blazing sun.

The air temperature rose higher, and the sun in the sky became more glaring.

The intense heat scorched the Earth, causing some Trainers traveling outdoors to begin showing symptoms of heatstroke.

---

Events like this are happening in the Hoenn Region.

The two extreme weather patterns are constantly intertwining in the Hoenn Region, and this phenomenon immediately caused panic among people everywhere.

At this time, Gym Leaders from various cities sprang into action, organizing people and Pokemon to evacuate in an orderly manner.

Inside the Weather Institute.

More than twenty researchers are currently seated in front of computers, monitoring the phenomenon in the Hoenn Region via satellite and radar.

The air is filled with a heavy atmosphere, and no one is speaking, making the sound of keyboards clacking seem especially abrupt in this space.

Some executives stand at the back, looking at the constantly fluctuating screens with serious expressions.

Suddenly, one of the researchers shouted:

"Report, traces of the Super Ancient Pokemon Groudon have been discovered!"

The satellite image quickly enlarged, and then people saw on the screen a huge Pokemon covered in red armor.

It has left the land and stepped into the sea.

What shocked the researchers even more was that with each step the Pokemon took, it left behind a solid piece of 'land' beneath its feet.

This seemed to result from the magma in its body reacting with seawater.

Before everyone could react, a second alert sounded again.

"Report, Kyogre has also appeared within the monitoring range!"

"According to the radar image, these two Super Ancient Pokemon are moving towards each other!"

Upon hearing this, the group immediately thought of the legend circulating in the Hoenn Region.

In the legend, when the Earth God Groudon and the Sea God Kyogre awaken, they will engage in an endless battle.

An executive frowned and asked in a deep voice: "How long until they meet?"

"At the current speed of these two Pokemon, it is estimated they will meet in five hours and thirty-two minutes! The meeting location..."

As the researcher's voice dropped, the computers in the institute simulated the next movements of the two Pokemon based on their speed.

On the screen, a red and a blue dot kept moving on the map, quickly stopping over a sea area.

"Fifty kilometers west of Sootopolis City!"

*

Paldea Region, D Ranch.

After lunch, D was leisurely using the Psyduck Spray Bottle to tend to the flowers in the garden.

A few Eevee were playing around him, chasing each other around his feet.

In the fragrant field of flowers, Butterfree and Combee leisurely fluttered around, diligently collecting nectar.

With the spray of water from the Psyduck Spray Bottle, the air immediately refracted a beautiful rainbow.

The nearby Eevee quickly noticed this rainbow and curiously surrounded it, trying to touch it with their paws.

Just then, one of the playful partner Eevee suddenly felt a wet spot on its nose, as if a drop of water had fallen on it.

"Bui?!"

Partner Eevee raised its head, only to see that a dark cloud had drifted over the previously sunny sky.

Drizzling rain fell from the cloud, covering the entire ranch.

Seeing this, D paused the watering of the flowers, slightly stunned, and looked up at the sky in surprise.

It's raining?

Strange... The weather forecast clearly said there wouldn't be any rain today, right?

Once it rains, the land on the ranch turns to mud.

In this situation, if Pokemon continue to play outside, they will become dirty.

Before the rain becomes heavy, D hurriedly leads the group of Pokemon back home.

The Eevee are peeking out the windows, looking at the ranch covered by the continuous rain.

"Bui..."

"If you feel bored, you can go play with Treecko and the others at the World Tree." D said: "It doesn't rain there, the ground is dry."

"Bui!"

The Eevee's eyes lit up; this was a good idea, decision made! They would go play at the World Tree!

Thus, the Eevee ran off.

Not long after, D received a message from Mewtwo.

The message said that tonight there will be a Pokemon tea party at the World Tree, and the Eevee intend to participate and won't be coming back to the ranch tonight.

D: "..."

It's true; "sons" are difficult to keep around!

He helplessly replied to Mewtwo with a "good," indicating that he knew.

Once the Eevee left, the house became empty again.

Feeling bored, D started surfing the internet, but soon he was attracted by several trending topics.

[Extreme weather in the Hoenn Region!]

[A volcano in the Hoenn Region begins to erupt!]

[Various parts of the Hoenn Region are battered by torrential rain and tsunamis!]

[Mysterious giant Pokemon silhouette appears on the sea in the Hoenn Region!]

Such topics.

Seeing these trending topics, D was slightly stunned.

Have Groudon and Kyogre already awakened?

So could this sudden rain in the Paldea Region also be influenced by those two Pokemon?

D quickly clicked on these trending news topics to check the specific situations.

The information stated that extreme weather suddenly appeared in the Hoenn Region, with some cities covered by high temperatures and intense drought, while others were hit by continuous torrential rain.

Coastal cities, such as Slateport City and Dewford Town, even experienced tsunamis.

This horrifying disaster occurring in the Hoenn Region has quickly garnered worldwide attention.

---

People from other regions are asking about the situation over there.

In response, some netizens living in the Hoenn Region have taken many photos with their phones and uploaded them online.

The background of the photos is a sky shrouded in dark, dense clouds, and the photographer's location is on an island. Looking around, one can see giant waves sweeping over the distant sea.

The scene seems like the arrival of the apocalypse.

Other photos have a background of a Sunny Day, but the sun in the sky is unprecedentedly intense.

To show netizens in other regions, the photographer also recorded a video.

The video shows his arm starting to sunburn shortly after being exposed to the sunlight.

The Hoenn League did not keep this disaster from the public, and they straightforwardly informed the people of Hoenn via their official account:

[The disaster recorded in legends has reemerged in the Hoenn Region. The Earth God Groudon and the Sea God Kyogre have awakened, and their influence is causing a disaster throughout Hoenn.

We ask all citizens not to panic and to cooperate with Gym Leaders and Miss Jenny to evacuate. We are working our hardest to stop this disaster!]

"Sure enough, Groudon and Kyogre have awakened..." D murmured upon seeing this scene.

I wonder how things are going on Steven's side.

D continued scrolling through the posts from netizens.

Very soon, he saw some incredibly daring individuals.

These Trainers did not obediently evacuate but instead rode on their respective Pokemon to chase the giant Pokemon moving across the sea.

They even went live on Pichu TV, showing people the true form of the Earth God Groudon.

In the background, several helicopters appeared, mostly bearing the emblem of the Hoenn League, but D noticed one helicopter with a TV station's logo.

The bold outdoor streamer exclaimed: "Oh, that's a helicopter from the Hoenn League, and there's a reporter from Purple Orchid TV Station here too!"

"But speaking of which, is this giant Pokemon really the legendary Earth God Groudon? Its size is enormous!"

The streamer wanted to say more, but soon, a Hoenn League staffer rode an Altaria over to dissuade him:

"It's dangerous here. Please leave immediately and head to a nearby city for shelter!"

The daring streamer initially wanted to refuse, but just then, Groudon was distracted by the helicopters above.

It opened its mouth, Earth Vein Energy surged wildly throughout its body, eventually pouring into the earth, and a multitude of giant "Stone Blades" burst forth from the surface of the sea.

That is... Groudon's signature move, Precipice Blades! D recognized it immediately.

One helicopter failed to dodge in time and was struck, descending toward the sea.

Fortunately, at the crucial moment, the pilot of the helicopter was ejected, avoiding a fatal crash.

Witnessing this scene, the League staffer furrowed his brows and immediately ordered:

"Everyone, keep your distance from Groudon, do not approach lightly!"

Immediately, the helicopters hovered in place, no longer daring to approach Groudon.

Only then did Groudon return its gaze and continue onward.

The streamer seemed to be frightened by the recent scene and quickly ended the live stream to take shelter.

*

Hoenn Region, over the sea, fifty kilometers away from Sootopolis City.

Steven had arrived here early by helicopter.

The sky was covered in dark clouds, and gusts of sea winds swept continuously.

Steven furrowed his brows, his silver hair fluttering in the wind, his handsome face filled with solemnity.

Suddenly, a burst of exclamations came through his headset.

"Groudon and Kyogre have arrived!"

Steven jerked his head up.

A huge shadow had appeared over the sea below, and Groudon was now visible on the nearby sea surface.

The next moment, the black shadow in the sea leapt out of the water, levitating above the sea surface, gazing at Groudon opposite.

Groudon also halted, its sturdy claws slightly clenched, gazing down at Kyogre on the sea.

Rain poured down furiously, drowning out the helicopter's engine sounds.

Steven gripped the Poke Ball in his hand tightly; at this moment, the fate of all Hoenn rested on his shoulders.

The three parties converged; an epic battle was about to erupt!

---

---

---

---

# 469: Chapter 466: Divine Battle! Motorcycle Mask's Debut!

The sea breeze tousled Steven's silver hair.

Holding a Poke Ball, he looked calmly at the two Super Ancient Pokemon below:

"Groudon and Kyogre have already converged. Next, I'll try to stop them. Leave that side to you!"

His voice was transmitted far away through the radio headset.

Outside the distant Pacifidlog Town, Wallace stood elegantly on a large Wailord.

He gazed at the distant sea, where an ancient tower silently stood in the fog-laden waters.

The tower top ascended straight to the clouds, seemingly connecting the ocean and sky.

That was the Sky Pillar; it's said that reaching the summit would allow one to summon the legendary Sky God, Rayquaza, at the Dragon Altar.

Being from the people of Sootopolis, Wallace had always shouldered the duty of guarding the Sky Pillar.

Though they, the people of Sootopolis, weren't qualified to enter, given the current circumstances, he had to try regardless.

This was the backup plan Wallace discussed with Steven beforehand.

In case the plan to stop Team Magma and Team Aqua failed, and Groudon and Kyogre awakened unfortunately.

Steven would immediately rush to stop them, while Wallace would proceed to the Sky Pillar to seek Rayquaza, urging it to appear and let the legendary Sky God reappear to quell the terrestrial and oceanic conflict.

A myriad of thoughts surged through Wallace's mind, but the cold sea wind mixed with drizzle brought him back to reality.

"I understand, Champion." Wallace detected the gravity in Steven's tone and, as usual, attempted to enliven the mood with a light voice.

But it didn't work that well.

Because Groudon and Kyogre had already begun their first round of conflict.

Great power caused the world to overturn, with massive tsunamis soaring skyward, prompting Steven's helicopter to rise rapidly to avoid being capsized by towering waves.

Steven nearly fell due to an untimely dodge, but fortunately, he clutched the side handle to stabilize himself.

Hearing the commotion, Wallace ceased joking and spoke rapidly:

"I'm setting off. Hang in there, Steven!"

"Hmm."

After ending communication, Wallace took a deep breath before addressing the Wailord beneath him:

"Wailord, we should go!"

"Growl!"

The world resounded with a peculiar yet heavy hum.

Then the Wailord beneath Wallace surged its massive tail fin, heading toward the distant Sky Pillar.

*

"Mr. Steven, are you alright?!"

The stabilized helicopter pilot quickly glanced at Steven behind him.

Steven shook his head, looking at Groudon and Kyogre who were continuously colliding below, while reports of the current situation across the Hoenn Region came through the headset.

Downpours, tsunamis, scorching Drought seemed to intensify alongside the battle between the two Legendary Pokemon!

Some regions even experienced extreme weather where torrential rain would fall one moment, and in the next, the air temperature skyrocketed to over forty degrees.

If Groudon and Kyogre continued fighting, the resultant shockwaves would soon inflict devastating blows on the entire Hoenn Region!

They must be stopped!

With this thought, Steven ceased observing and chose to join the divine battle as a third party.

He tossed two Poke Balls.

Amid three flashes of white light, a Skarmory and a Metagross appeared beneath the helicopter.

Steven leapt onto Skarmory, turning back to the helicopter pilot:

"You leave here first and seek refuge!"

The pilot hesitated slightly: "But, but..."

"No buts!" Steven frowned, out of time!

The pilot anxiously looked at their Champion, then nodded forcefully: "Yes! I'll leave everything to you then!"

Upon saying this, the helicopter took off.

Steven rode Skarmory into the battlefield between Groudon and Kyogre.

"Metagross, Flash Cannon!"

"Growl!"

A powerful beam erupted swiftly from Metagross, condensing countless rays into a robust beam aimed at Kyogre, which had leapt from the sea.

"Boom!"

Tremendous power caught Kyogre off guard, its colossal body crashing back into the ocean.

Groudon then noticed the human intervening in their domain struggle.

With an ancient roar, countless magma energy surged from its body into the earth vein, manifesting as giant stone pillars erupting forth.

"Boom boom boom boom boom!"

Metagross keenly noticed this scene, alarms blared in its mind, and it immediately reacted, speeding forward.

Massive stone pillars continuously emerged from behind, at a remarkable rate!

"Metal Claw!"

Steven's voice came from high above, and Metagross trusted its Trainer unconditionally from beginning to end.

Upon hearing the command, it swiftly swung its metal claw.

In the next second, a massive stone pillar that protruded before it shattered into several pieces.

"Growl..."

Precipice Blades finally halted as Groudon's golden eyes stared at Metagross.

Just when Metagross stopped to pause briefly, a giant shadow unexpectedly appeared beneath the ocean surface.

---

The next second, the shadow leapt up, looking down from above at Metagross.

Then, countless blue-white dazzling beams crisscrossed from Kyogre's body.

Metagross couldn't dodge in time and was directly blasted into the ocean.

A splendid and dazzling fiery red mushroom cloud erupted on the sea surface.

In an instant, all sounds vanished.

When everything returned to normal, huge waves followed and surged.

Witnessing that scene firsthand, Steven's pupils contracted slightly.

That power...

But fortunately, Metagross did not lose its combat ability.

It emerged from the water, and without any retreat, continued to face off against the two Legendary Pokemon.

Groudon and Kyogre simultaneously noticed this tiny Pokemon.

Its demeanor, even as a small being, was haughty, thoroughly enraging these two Legendary Pokemon.

The crimson magma flowed again, and huge stone pillars thrust out from the ocean surface.

Kyogre dove into the water, raising its head, gathering an extremely cold ray in its mouth, and shot it fiercely at Metagross.

The two Legendary Pokemon planned to first work together to repel the two intruders, Metagross and Steven!

"Metagross, Agility!"

Steven ordered in a deep voice.

Metagross instantly entered an agile state, continuously dodging Groudon's Precipice Blades.

Kyogre's Ice Beam grazed past it, and in an instant, large swathes of seawater were frozen.

Metagross charged at Groudon.

Steven's gaze followed, and when the distance was just right, he suddenly commanded: "Meteor Mash!"

Metagross's four metal arms instantly merged into one, and a deep blue light burst from it, descending like a meteor, blasting into Groudon.

"Crack!"

The enormous impact caused the land beneath Groudon to crumble.

"Roar!"

The enraged Groudon let out a roar, its mouth wide open, golden pupils locked on Metagross, and a fiercely burning flame surged from its mouth, instantly engulfing Metagross that hadn't yet pulled away.

The powerful force directly sent Metagross flying back, heavily crashing onto the frozen sea by Kyogre, rolling several times.

"Metagross!" Steven frowned.

However, before his words could fall, Kyogre's massive body once again leapt from the sea.

The blue-white beams converged at a point, and in an instant, blasted Metagross into the frozen sea.

Having dealt with Metagross, Groudon and Kyogre's gaze once again turned to Steven and Skarmory in mid-air.

The next second, a Hydro Pump roared toward Skarmory.

"Squawk!"

Skarmory immediately flapped its wings, taking the Trainer and dodging at high speed.

At this moment, the tragic scene appeared in the eyes of countless people.

High above, the female reporter from Purple Orchid TV Station was tearful, loudly calling: "Sir Steven!"

In the distant city's underground shelter.

Countless citizens nervously watched the images on the screen.

"We're doomed! Steven alone can't deal with those two Legendary Pokemon simultaneously!"

"Is that the legendary Earth God and Sea God? Such terrifying power!"

Hoenn League Headquarters.

The director of the Hoenn League watched the screen with a grave expression.

Suddenly, a liaison officer stormed in and reported:

"Report! Other regions have offered their support; the Champions from Kanto Region and Sinnoh Region are on their way here!"

The director, over fifty years old, said solemnly: "Until then, hold on!"

After speaking, he took off his suit and turned to leave the room.

The liaison officer was astonished: "Director, what are you planning to do?"

Director: "I can't let Steven fight alone; in my youth, I was an Elite-level Trainer, and I can help somewhat!"

*

Sootopolis Battlefield.

After dealing with that insignificant human, Groudon and Kyogre turned their attention back to each other.

They once again engaged in a new round of conflict.

On the ice, Steven and Skarmory, who had been knocked down, found Metagross, stuck due to lack of strength beneath the ice layers.

"Cough, cough, cough!"

Through the cold ice, Steven used a Poke Ball to rescue Metagross, quickly taking a Super Potion from his pocket to heal Metagross.

Moments later, Metagross weakly opened its eyes.

Seeing Metagross was okay, Steven breathed a sigh of relief, stood up, and looked into the distance at the unending battle between Groudon and Kyogre, then checked the communication device by his ear.

"Wallace!" Steven called in a deep voice.

"I'm here!"

Sky Pillar.

Wallace was rushing up the stairs to the tower top with all his might; he could discern the hint of urgency in Steven's voice and knew things might not be great over there, so he quickened his pace.

But this ancient tower seemed weakened by age, with its structure and floor becoming extremely fragile.

Wallace, with a careless step, missed the ground and fell to the next level.

"Hiss!"

He took a sharp breath from the pain, but there was no time to rest in this situation.

Wallace gritted his teeth and continued rushing upward.

He didn't know how long it took, but he finally reached the last flight of stairs.

Wallace gasped heavily, raised a foot, and stepped onto the tower top.

"Rayquaza!" He shouted: "Please come out and help us, stop the fight between Groudon and Kyogre!"

---

The surroundings fell into silence, enormous waves slamming against the tower as this ancient Sky Pillar stood unmoving amid the storm.

Rayquaza gave no response.

Wallace's heart instantly sank.

*

Paldea Region, Porto Marinada Town, D Ranch.

Holding an umbrella, Mayor Thomas hurried to D Ranch.

The weather had been unstable recently, and he worried that D lacked experience dealing with this kind of weather, so he planned to come over and give him a few reminders.

But when Mayor Thomas arrived at the ranch, he learned from Little Sister Indeedee that D had gone out.

Looking at Little Sister Indeedee who was receiving him, the old man's face was full of surprise: "Gone out? Did D say where he was going?"

Little Sister Indeedee: "Ai-ii..."

Seeing the worry in Mayor Thomas's heart, it comforted him: "Ai-ii!"

"All right then. When D comes back, remember to tell him to call all the Pokemon on the ranch back into the house, lock doors and windows, and prepare enough food." Mayor Thomas instructed.

"Ai-ii!" Little Sister Indeedee nodded happily, indicating that it understood.

Only then did Mayor Thomas turn and leave.

*

"Rayquaza didn't respond..."

Wallace's slightly heavy voice came through the communicator.

"I've tried many methods, but there's no way to summon it..."

Hearing this, Steven's brows furrowed tightly.

Was there really no way?

Together with Metagross and Skarmory, he looked toward Groudon and Kyogre in the distance.

News had come from the Hoenn League that the four Elites and Champions from other regions were on their way here to provide support.

Before they arrived, he had to hold out.

As if sensing their Trainer's feelings at this moment, the badly scarred Metagross floated to hover in front of him.

Steven was slightly taken aback: "You want to keep fighting?"

Metagross nodded its heavy head.

Beside it, Skarmory let out a cry and flapped its wings, seemingly preparing to join Metagross in stopping those two Super Ancient Pokemon.

"I understand." Steven took a deep breath. "In that case, I'll be counting on you!"

At his side, only these two could fly; his other Pokemon had no way to battle Groudon and Kyogre on this vast ocean.

Metagross and Skarmory gave Steven a look, then once again charged toward Groudon and Kyogre.

Standing on the ice, Steven watched their figures.

The hearts of a Trainer and their Pokemon are connected; even without words, they clearly understand each other's thoughts.

This time, Metagross went to face Kyogre, while Skarmory went for Groudon.

Explosions and fiery light kept blooming over the distant sea.

Torrential rain, giant waves, beams of light, flowing magma, solidified earth...

This scene, worthy of being called grand, was reflected in Steven's pale blue eyes.

In that instant, the flow of time seemed to slow down.

Until Metagross was blasted away by Kyogre's Origin Pulse and crashed down onto the ice.

The tremendous impact sent Steven sprawling as well.

His Rotom Phone was forced out of his pocket and tumbled away.

The female reporter from Purple Orchid TV Station watched this scene and couldn't help bursting into tears.

"Mr. Steven..."

At this moment, everyone watching the live broadcast felt their hearts grow unbearably heavy.

Someone noticed Steven's physical condition.

"He was already injured when he and Skarmory were knocked down by Kyogre just now."

"Then doesn't that mean Mr. Steven has been forcing himself to keep battling..."

"Steven..."

Some prayed for a miracle, while others cheered for Steven with all their might.

"Hang in there, Mr. Steven!"

Steven seemed to hear the cries of the people.

Lying unconscious on the ground, he slowly opened his blurry eyes.

After a moment, his vision gradually cleared.

What entered his sight was a silver Metagross-style Rotom Phone.

At that moment, a certain person's voice suddenly surfaced in Steven's mind.

That person had told him: "If those Super Ancient Pokemon, Groudon and Kyogre, really do awaken, and the Hoenn League can't resolve it, you can contact me. Maybe I'll be able to help."

That person had also said: "Me? I'm a Rancher."

Thinking of this, Steven stretched his hand forward and gripped the Rotom Phone.

He opened the phone, unlocked the screen, launched Poke Communications, and tapped open his chat with Wing King.

[Steven: If you're there, please contact me!]

The other side replied very quickly. It was only two short words, yet they unexpectedly brought a sense of reassurance.

[Wing King: I'm here.]

[Steven: Please help the people of the Hoenn Region!]

After that message was sent, there was no immediate reply. Staring at the explosions over the distant sea, Steven suddenly felt like he was clutching at straws.

Well, yeah—after all, the other party was far away in the Paldea Region; even if he agreed to help, what good would it do?

After all, coming all the way from there to Hoenn would take a very long time.

Yet just then, Steven suddenly noticed a massive shadow once again rising beneath the distant sea surface.

Was that Kyogre?

No! Kyogre had already leapt into midair, lunging at Groudon!

Then what was that?!

The shadow gradually shrouded the entire sea, and the vast ocean completely turned into a domain of shadows.

The next second, a silhouette even larger than Kyogre and Groudon burst forth from within.

A gigantic body, black wings, black-and-red stripes, golden structures, blood-red eyes...

---

The Pokemon is...

The infamous Giratina!

Steven recognized the identity of the Pokemon at a glance.

His face immediately showed an astonished expression.

What's going on?!

At this moment, this question also lingered in the hearts of everyone watching the live broadcast.

Inside the Weather Institute, a researcher identified the suddenly intruding Pokemon through satellite.

Soon, he discovered something shocking.

The researcher shouted loudly: "Look! There's a human standing on the Pokemon's head!"

All the surrounding researchers and high-level staff collectively turned their gaze there...

The screen kept zooming in, and soon, everyone indeed saw a human atop the Pokemon's head.

It was a man, tall in stature, wearing a mask colored black and dark green, with a cloak billowing behind him.

The man stood amidst the storm, his cloak flapping loudly in the fierce wind.

"What... exactly is going on with this guy?" Someone muttered in disbelief.

"Is he Giratina's Trainer?"

"How could it be?!"

The researchers had just witnessed the terror of the Legendary Pokemon.

As far as they knew, Giratina was also a Legendary Pokemon.

And now, a human had actually managed to capture a Legendary Pokemon?!

Who exactly is the man wearing the mask?!

Before people could figure out the answer, the scene on screen changed again.

With the arrival of the mysterious man and Giratina, Groudon and Kyogre, previously locked in an intense battle, immediately turned their attention.

These two Legendary Pokemon turned to look at Giratina, gazing at the unknown Pokemon whose pressure matched theirs.

"..."

Groudon and Kyogre showed solemn expressions.

While Giratina glanced at these two Pokemon disdainfully.

"Is that all?"

It spoke to D on its back through telepathy.

D: "...You decided to follow me here on your own."

He originally just wanted Giratina to help open a door and take a shortcut to the Hoenn Region, but little did he know that upon hearing about the battle here, Giratina excitedly rushed over.

"Humph!" Giratina snorted excitedly: "Leave it to me!"

With that, a strange glow lit up in its blood-red eyes.

The energy in the surrounding space wildly surged, forming into massive meteors behind Giratina, bringing destruction as they fell from the sky.

Draco Meteor!

With such dense meteors falling from the sky, there's no need for precise aiming; they bombarded the massive Groudon and Kyogre bodies.

Groudon got hit on the head, its huge body smashed a large crater into the ground.

Perhaps dazed by the impact, once it came to its senses, Groudon roared furiously, spewing Hyper Beam skyward, shattering the incoming meteors.

Meanwhile, Kyogre was struck into the sea by the meteors.

Just when D thought it was heavily struck, Kyogre suddenly leaped from beneath the sea, directly flying towards Giratina!

Countless blue-white beams flashed around it, crossing and shooting towards Giratina.

"Shadow Force!" D decisively commanded.

Giratina gave a cold snort of arrogance, and the next moment, its massive body vanished from sight.

Kyogre's Origin Pulse missed!

Witnessing this, everyone showed astonishment.

"Disappeared?!"

On the icy surface, Steven gazed up at the sky; even one as knowledgeable as him couldn't help but be shocked at the sudden appearance of the ally.

Was the person riding Giratina [Wing King]?

With the sudden disappearance of that Pokemon, Kyogre and Groudon instantly became wary.

But suddenly, the surrounding space began to distort.

A purple dimensional door suddenly appeared, from which Giratina emerged, its giant claws glowing with mystic purple light tore apart the space, slicing at Kyogre in front!

"Oof!"

Suffering a heavy blow, Kyogre was instantly knocked back, emitting a painful cry, and fell into the sea.

Giratina looked proud.

It truly was the strongest Legendary Pokemon!

However, just when it was basking in triumph, D's voice suddenly came: "Watch out behind you!"

Not far away, Groudon had already condensed Precipice Blades.

Stone pillars of earth burst forth from the sea, rapidly striking towards Giratina.

---

---

---

---

# 470: Chapter 467: The Great Hero Rayquaza, The Incident Concludes!

Giratina swiftly dodged to the side, fortunately, with D's prior warning, it wasn't hit by that Pokemon's attack.

But Groudon's sneak attack provoked Giratina's anger.

It let out an enraged roar, turning its body, a thick beam of black and red light shot out instantly from Giratina's mouth.

Groudon, undeterred, used Hyper Beam to engage in a clash with Giratina.

The black and red energy intertwined in midair, mutually annihilating, red electric energy continuously flickering and pulsing, for a moment, the surrounding space seemed to be distorted.

Giratina was eager to compete against Groudon.

It slightly increased its energy output, and Groudon immediately felt a pressure.

The golden pupils revealed a grave expression, earthy energy continuously surged from Groudon's entire body.

In the next second, the Hyper Beam that had been suppressed by Giratina was pushed back up.

However, at this moment, a sudden change occurred.

Kyogre emerged from beneath the sea once more, leaping out with numerous bluish-white rays exploding from its body, aiming to strike Giratina from the side.

Seeing this, D frowned slightly.

He took out a Poke Ball with his right hand, accompanied by a flash of white light, Dragonite appeared beside Giratina.

"Thunder!" D said in a deep voice.

Dragonite was already extremely skilled at using Thunder.

Almost instantly, a massive bolt of lightning descended from the sky, empowered by the rain, suddenly striking Kyogre.

The enormous electric energy sent Kyogre crashing back into the ocean again.

And the Origin Pulse, because of Kyogre dropping, passed by Giratina, missing its mark.

Dragonite also noticed the large Fish-type Pokemon on the opposite side at this moment.

It had once tried to capture the opponent for the ranch to give to D, but it was defeated by that opponent.

Seeing that Pokemon attacking again, Giratina became even angrier.

It gave up its playful attitude towards Groudon, its enormous energy instantly swallowed Groudon's Hyper Beam, engulfing Groudon in that terrifying energy as well.

"Wow, that's amazing!"

In the underground shelter, people watching the live broadcast were completely captivated by the divine battle.

The Giratina and that Masked Man were so strong, able to handle both Groudon and Kyogre simultaneously!

Inside the Weather Institute.

Seeing the mysterious trainer and Giratina who suddenly appeared possessing the ability to stop this disaster, the executives and researchers also breathed a sigh of relief.

"That's great!"

At the Sootopolis battlefield, a weakened Steven leaned against an equally weakened Metagross, a relieved smile also appearing on his face.

He used a radio communication to contact Wallace: "It's alright now, a mysterious person stepped in to help us."

Wallace, sitting frustrated at the Sky Pillar, was slightly taken aback hearing this, then asked incredulously: "What?"

"I've told you about him before." Steven said: "He's the one who provided us with intelligence through the Pokemon Forum, that Internet-using Giratina is his Pokemon."

Wallace certainly had heard of that Internet-savvy Legendary Pokemon Giratina.

Not only that, but he also heard about how someone quarreled with Giratina online and got personally visited by Giratina for a lesson...

Thinking of this, Wallace was full of disbelief.

After a long time, he came back to himself and said to Steven: "After this is over, we must properly thank him."

Steven: "Mm."

[Leader of the Wings] really helped them a lot this time!

*

Groudon staggered to stand up from the ground, and Kyogre also resurfaced from the water.

They looked gravely at the unfamiliar Pokemon in midair.

D also looked at Groudon and Kyogre below.

He thought to himself that these two had too much health...

But continuing this fight wasn't a solution either, he pondered and tried to negotiate with them.

"Groudon, Kyogre, let's end this!

Stop the fighting and return to where you belong!"

However, Groudon and Kyogre just silently gazed at him and Giratina, showing no intention of ending it there.

The rain poured down more heavily.

The sea became pitch-black, and the natural energy between heaven and earth was being rapidly absorbed by those two Legendary Pokemon.

D: "..." This just goes on and on!

Looks like these matters can only be resolved by professionals!

Just as Giratina wanted to make a move to harshly punish these two Pokemon, D suddenly reached out to stop it.

Giratina: "?"

"Let's leave it to the Pokemon that specialize in handling this!"

With that said, he nodded to Dragonite.

Meanwhile, people watching the live broadcast also noticed that the mysterious Masked Man suddenly halted.

"What's happening?"

"What's going on?"

"Wait! Are that Groudon and Kyogre absorbing energy to recover their strength?"

Just as people were puzzled, a distant dragon's roar approached from afar.

Then, the cloud-covered sky suddenly opened up a hole, sacred light poured onto the earth.

And with the light, a mysterious pokemon with a completely black body, dragon-like form descended.

---

Seeing this scene, everyone in the Weather Institute sat up in shock.

That is...

The legendary Sky God, Rayquaza!

Wait! Did the mysterious trainer just stop his actions to summon Rayquaza?!

Quickly, the scene on the satellite screen confirmed their suspicions.

The pitch-black Rayquaza flew in front of the mysterious person, glanced at him, and then at the Dragonite beside him.

Indeed, Rayquaza was summoned by him!

At this moment, not only were the researchers and executives aware of this.

Even the people in the shelters realized this fact.

"According to legend, when the Earth God and Sea God are locked in conflict, the Sky God Rayquaza appears to stop them..."

"That is the legendary Sky God, Rayquaza!"

"Who is that guy? Not only did he capture Giratina, but he can also summon Rayquaza..."

Steven was also astonished.

Rayquaza! Wing Sovereign summoned Rayquaza!

In this moment, countless guesses flashed through Steven's mind.

Has Wing Sovereign captured Rayquaza, or is he a member of the legendary Draconid people?!

Either possibility is remarkable enough.

Above the sea.

Groudon and Kyogre also noticed the sudden arrival of Rayquaza.

Their expressions suddenly became unprecedentedly serious.

Rayquaza then directed its gaze towards Groudon and Kyogre.

Images of Dragonite and a group of Pokemon calling it a hero flashed in its mind, stirring up overwhelming excitement.

Dragonite surely trusted it, hence it summoned it to stop these two!

"Roar!"

Rayquaza let out a dragon roar to the sky, deciding to demonstrate what a hero should do in front of Dragonite today!

Under everyone's gaze, an intense energy erupted from Rayquaza's body.

The energy turned its entire form green.

In the next second, Rayquaza sped out like an arrow shot from a bowstring.

A green streak crossed the sky!

Groudon and Kyogre on the sea surface were instantly pierced like skewered fruits.

A massive explosion ensued on the bodies of the two Pokemon.

The instinct-driven Kyogre quickly turned and plunged back into the ocean, abandoning the fight altogether!

And Groudon, standing up from the ground, also turned and fled.

As these two Pokemon departed, the clouds enveloped the Hoenn Region began to disperse.

The rain stopped, and the temperature gradually dropped.

Finally, the crisis was resolved.

D breathed a sigh of relief; indeed, such matters should be left to the professionals!

Dragonite looked intently at Rayquaza.

"Roar!" (As expected of Senior Rayquaza, how impressive!)

Rayquaza, feeling the reverent gaze of Dragonite, opened its mouth to respond: "Roar!"

Dragonite joyfully invited: "Roar!" (Senior Rayquaza, come visit the ranch next time, I will treat you with delicious food!)

"Roar!"

Rayquaza gladly accepted the invitation.

It nodded in agreement, took a look at D, then at Dragonite, before turning and soaring into the sky.

The dark figure soon vanished into the horizon.

Giratina watched Rayquaza's figure and said in a deep voice: "That guy is strong!"

D thought to himself; no kidding, it can take on seven rivals in the movies.

Thinking this, D turned his head; Kyogre's figure had already disappeared from the sea, while Groudon headed toward the land.

Finally, it's over!

He initially thought of chasing Groudon to see how it returned home but suddenly noticed the helicopter not far away.

After a brief silence, D said to Giratina: "Let's head back too!"

He was merely here to help and had no intention of gaining fame; hence, he concealed his true identity.

The excitement was over, and Giratina, satisfied with the activity, took D back to the Reversed World.

As they disappeared from sight, the members of the Hoenn League were slightly stunned.

"Gone?"

"Such a mysterious trainer!"

"What a pity, I didn't even have the chance to thank him!"

"Regardless, he is the great hero who saved our Hoenn Region!"

After the disaster, the Hoenn Region was renewed, as Gym Leaders and League employees worked with the people to rebuild their homes.

The catastrophe in the Hoenn Region garnered widespread attention across major regions.

Especially the mysterious trainer who suddenly appeared with Giratina.

His majestic appearance riding Giratina was uploaded online; quickly, netizens recognized the mask he wore.

It was modeled after Paldea Region's Pokemon Cyclizar.

Hence, people affectionately termed the mysterious figure as "[Motorcycle Mask]".

D was unaware of these matters.

He had just returned to the ranch, changing out of the rain-soaked clothes.

The cape was prepared by his sister Indeedee, who had specially made it for D after learning of his plans.

Because his sister Indeedee believed it was the hero's standard attire.

Despite D repeatedly expressing that he was not a hero, his sister Indeedee still prepared the cape for him.

Helplessly, D could only wear it.

However, now everything was over!

After being caught in the rain, D caught a cold shortly after returning home.

He took a hot shower, wrapped himself in a blanket, and drank the ginger tea brewed by his sister Indeedee.

Beside him, Koraidon protested in a sulky manner about not being taken out this time.

"Achoo!"

D sneezed, sniffed, and said to Koraidon: "I had to, because you're too recognizable."

"Grrrr..." Koraidon drooped its head.

"Alright!" D hugged Koraidon's head and rubbed vigorously: "Next time, if possible, I'll definitely take you out, okay?"

Koraidon finally agreed.

Then D saw a message on his phone from Lono, stating he wanted to visit the ranch.

D gladly replied affirmatively.

So, Lono arrived at the ranch that afternoon with gifts from Levincia City.

Excitedly, she chatted with D about the recent events in the Hoenn Region and the sudden appearance of Motorcycle Mask.

"That Motorcycle Mask was amazing! He must be a very skilled trainer, right? It was really terrifying; those two Legendary Pokemon were so powerful and destructive!"

"It's unimaginable; if they appeared in the Paldea Region, what terrible things they might cause!"

Lono looked frightened.

She noticed D, who seemed a bit unwell, and asked:

"But by the way, D, did you watch the livestream?"

"Huh?" D snapped out of it: "Oh, of course, I saw it."

Lono eagerly asked: "Do you think that Motorcycle Mask is from our Paldea Region?"

D, earnestly fabricating, said: "I'm not sure, because Cyclizar exists not only in the Paldea Region."

When trainers from other regions travel here, they often take Paldea Region Pokemon with them.

Over time, other regions also have Pokemon like Cyclizar and Maushold.

# 471: Chapter 468: After That

With the capture of Maxie and Archie, the shadow looming over the Hoenn Region finally dispersed.

Ordinary members who witnessed the destructive power of Groudon and Kyogre withdrew from Team Magma and Team Aqua, choosing to return to normal life.

However, the senior executives of these two organizations have all disappeared without a trace.

The International Police dispatched professional officers to track down Courtney, Matt, Shelly, and others.

For the time being, the crisis in the Hoenn Region has temporarily ended.

Kanaz City, the old residence of the Stone Family.

Steven's arm was wrapped in a white bandage, and bright sunlight streamed in through the windows, accompanied by a gentle breeze, causing the silvery grey curtains to flutter softly.

A pot of blooming flowers sat on the windowsill, and outside the window was a clear blue sky, free of clouds, bringing a sense of peace and happiness to those who looked at it.

Steven gazed absentmindedly at the scene, lost in thought.

At that moment, the door opened.

The president of Devon Company, Steven's father Joseph Stone, walked in with a private doctor.

"Father." Steven snapped back to reality and called out to the newcomer.

Joseph Stone looked worried, still feeling immense fear whenever he recalled what happened that day.

If that mysterious person hadn't appeared at that time, Joseph Stone dared not imagine what might have happened to his son.

"It's all over now!"

Joseph Stone comforted his son's feelings.

Steven, looking helpless, said: "Father, I am not a child anymore."

"You'll always be a child to me!" Joseph Stone said.

Steven: "..."

The private doctor pretended not to hear anything, silently stepping forward to examine Steven's body.

A moment later, he put down the instrument in his hand and said to Joseph Stone:

"Everything's fine now, Mr. Steven has a very good physique and has basically recovered. The bandage and splint can be removed in three days, but you should still take care not to overexert yourself afterward."

Hearing this, Joseph Stone let out a long sigh of relief: "Great, now I can be at ease!"

The work done, the private doctor turned and left the room, leaving space for the father and son.

Then Joseph Stone got to the main topic.

"Steven, we need to thank that person properly!"

"I know." After a pause, Steven continued: "I've already arranged to visit him in a few days."

"That's good, that's good." Joseph Stone nodded, lifting two suitcases from his side: "This is a little gift I prepared for him; remember to take it with you when the time comes."

Steven was startled, looking at the suitcases, he asked: "What's inside?"

Joseph Stone opened the suitcases, and the next second, two suitcases full of cash were revealed under Steven's gaze.

"..."

Steven was silent for a long time, then asked: "Isn't this a bit too tacky?"

Moreover, from his understanding, that person known as Wing King didn't seem to be lacking money or concerned about fame and fortune.

Recently, speculation about his identity had been fermenting online for several days, but from start to finish, there was no sign of him appearing.

Many netizens even went to Giratina, trying to find out the identity of the Motorcycle Mask, but Giratina didn't reveal a single word.

This only enhanced the mystery of that person's identity.

The Trainer of Giratina, who summoned the Sky God Rayquaza, single-handedly stopped the Earth God and Sea God...

Such a mysterious and powerful Trainer has been admired throughout history.

In the Hoenn Region, many have even spontaneously formed a fan club for [Motorcycle Mask], wearing matching Cyclizar masks to gatherings.

After Steven said this, Joseph Stone also realized that the gift he chose might be too tacky.

But he thought again, maybe that mysterious person needed money?

After all, raising and training Pokemon is very expensive.

Not to mention that Giratina is so big, doesn't it eat several berries and Pokeblocks at a time?

"Send it over first, then when you visit him, carefully observe what he might need, and we'll prepare something more personalized." Joseph Stone said.

After all, that person saved his son, and Steven is his only son. For Steven, he would be willing to give up Devon Company.

"...Alright then." Steven reluctantly agreed.

"Then you should rest well, I won't disturb you anymore!" Joseph Stone smiled as he bid farewell to his son.

After he left, Steven took out his Rotom Phone and opened the chat interface with [Wing King].

After a brief moment of thought, he sent a message asking—May I know your name?

A while later, a reply came from the other side.

Steven immediately took out his phone.

There on the screen appeared a line of words.

[Wing King: D, my name is D.]

*

In the Paldea Region, at D Ranch.

After the rain had cleared, the temperature in the air dropped considerably.

After replying to Steven's message, D became a country rancher again.

He took a basket to the Apple Garden, picking apples covered in dew.

Over in Snorlax Park, Dragonite was vividly recounting the exploits of senior Rayquaza to a group of Pokemon including Dustox, Cyclizar, and Pawmot.

"Roar! Roar!" (Senior Rayquaza is amazing! With just one move, it defeated those two Pokemon that wanted to destroy the world!)

---

"Gao!" (Wow!)

The surrounding Pokemon listeners all showed faces of admiration, how unfortunate! They couldn't witness that moment firsthand!

After picking the apples, D returned to the kitchen to check the household food supplies.

Upon discovering that the food was almost all consumed, he prepared to head to town while the time was still early.

Before leaving, D found the Pokemon playing at Snorlax Park and said to them:

"You play at home, I'm going to town to buy some meat, don't run around, got it?"

"Ow!"

"Bamor!"

"..."

A group of Pokemon happily agreed, then continued listening to Dragonite narrate the events that had occurred.

And D slowly drove his old pickup truck to Porto Marinada Town.

He parked his car by the roadside and started making his way toward Porto Marinada Market.

While passing a street, D suddenly saw several children wearing masks chasing and playing along the roadside.

As they ran, they shouted: "Groudon, stop! I'm Motorcycle Mask here to stop you today! Hoo, don't run!"

One of the children playing Groudon hurriedly scrambled away in front.

But suddenly, they accidentally bumped into something and fell to the ground with a thud.

The children behind also stopped, dumbfounded, and looked up at the tall man before them.

D looked at the Cyclizar masks on their faces, and couldn't help but fall into silent contemplation.

After a moment, he reached out and pulled up the child playing Groudon.

"Don't play on the road; there are cars, it's dangerous!"

"Got it!"

The group of children replied in unison, then turned around and ran to play in an open space.

Watching this scene, D shook his head helplessly.

It's too exaggerated...

How many days has it been? That incident has spread so fiercely.

Is this the power of the internet? It's terrifying!

Fortunately, he wore a mask at the time, or else now his underpants might have been stripped bare by these internet users.

Quickly buying the needed groceries from the street, D drove back to the ranch.

Placing the ingredients in the refrigerator, D then took out the Rotom Phone, checking recent news online.

The incident in the Hoenn Region remained a top headline, and news about [Giratina] and [Mysterious Trainer wearing a Cyclizar mask] persisted fiercely.

D even saw some internet users had formed the [Motorcycle Mask Club], only fans of Motorcycle Mask could join.

D: "..."

But what concerned him even more was that Giratina's fans had increased again, surpassing eight figures, reaching tens of millions.

In the Pokemon World, this is quite an exaggerated number!

This group of internet users began treating Giratina as a benevolent Legendary Pokemon, one that specifically fights to protect humanity.

However, aside from him, no one knew Giratina was just there to join the fun...

No wonder it's a big internet celebrity, Giratina.

Speak of Cao Cao, and Cao Cao appears; as D was browsing Giratina's personal homepage, a message from Giratina popped up in the background.

In the Legendary Beast chat group.

Giratina posted a screenshot of its fans, showcasing it to Mewtwo and Calyrex.

[Giratina: Hahaha, impressive, right? Rotom said my fan count broke the record!]

[Mewtwo: ...]

[Calyrex: This time you stopped the conflict between those two Pokemon, saving the humans living on that land, you are undoubtedly a hero.]

Listen, listen to Calyrex's words, how pleasing to the ear!

Giratina was very pleased.

Though it originally just went to join the fun, now that people say it went to save the Hoenn Region, then it went to save the Hoenn Region!

But speaking of this, Giratina suddenly remembered something.

It started tagging D in the group.

[Giratina: @D, where's that very powerful Pokemon? Bring it into the group, it has the qualification to join!]

D: "..."

Looks like Rayquaza's strength really concerns Giratina!

Except, he currently doesn't have the chance to give Rayquaza a Rotom Phone...

[D: When there's a chance in the future, I'll invite it to join the group.]

Anyway, they'll have the opportunity to meet again in the future.

When Demon God Hoopa's movie starts, all the Legendary Pokemon will appear.

Then, when other Pokemon are being controlled by Demon God Hoopa, they'll have to rely on Rayquaza to 1v7 them.

Except, Giratina seems to be one of the "7"?

"Oh well, I'll make decisions based on the actual situation at that time!"

Giratina being controlled is no issue; they have Black Rayquaza!

Dialga and Palkia being controlled is also not a problem; they still have Black Rayquaza!

This is the confidence Black Rayquaza gives them!

With Groudon and Kyogre crises resolved, D then had nothing special to do.

Today is May 18th, Year 200 of the League calendar, and in thirteen days, the World Tree will mature, then he can help the World Tree and the Pokemon there relocate.

*

On May 22nd of the League calendar, at 9 a.m., Steven, whose health had recovered, boarded his private plane heading for the Paldea Region with Metagross.

After a five-hour long-distance flight, the plane landed on a beautiful expanse with fresh air, possessing lovely rural scenery on the outskirts of Porto Marinada Town.

Even before landing, Steven spotted a bustling and prosperous ranch below.

---

Looking across, the entire ranch is surrounded by wooden fences, and a small river cuts through the middle.

Large fields lie by the river, the crops are growing well and seem ready for harvest soon.

On the other side, an orchard is situated next to a barn and a few other buildings, where a few Miltank and chickens are grazing on the pasture.

A stone bridge connects to the other side of the river, where another orchard stands, facing what looks like a park, and under a large tree, a fluffy Snorlax is lying down.

Nearby on a small hill, a group of fluffy, round sheep Pokémon are leisurely sunbathing.

This must be D Ranch.

"D is really a rancher!"

Steven felt incredibly amazed by everything he saw.

The rancher who caught Giratina, such facts are hard to believe.

Just at this moment, the pilot in front was about to land but suddenly saw many Dragonite flying across in front of the plane.

The pilot couldn't help but exclaim.

Hearing this, Steven looked out and saw several Dragonite with backpacks curiously staring at the private plane they were on.

"Mr. Steven, what should we do?"

Surrounded by so many Dragonite, the pilot was quite nervous, especially since one of the Dragons with a scarred face looked at him with an eye that made him shiver.

"Don't worry, they mean no harm." Steven calmly said, "Just land normally."

"Yes!"

The pilot, under the pressure from the Dragonite, slowly landed the private plane on a piece of open land.

Just as Steven disembarked with two suitcases, he saw ahead a tall man in a blue work outfit standing on the grass.

Several Pokémon stood beside the man.

There's a Butterfree, Dragonite, Cyclizar, Pawmot, and one unfamiliar Pokémon.

Steven had never seen such a Pokémon.

It has a crimson body, a pair of golden eyes, colorful feathered adornments on its head, exuding a wild aura all over.

"Finally meeting you, Mr. Steven." D laughed gently, greeting the silver-haired man before him.

Steven snapped out of his daze, "Hello, Mr. D."

Then, Steven turned to the pilot and said, "You can go to the town for a rest, I'll contact you when we head back."

"Yes!"

The pilot responded and took off in the plane.

Watching this scene, D thought indeed, he's not just any young master of Devon Company.

Steven Stone, the famous heir in the Pokémon World!

However, Steven himself was quite humble, so D invited, "Come in and have a seat!"

"Thanks for the trouble."

Steven replied courteously, and then followed D into the living room carrying the two suitcases.

He placed the cases by the sofa and said to D, "These are gifts my father prepared for you as thanks, please accept them."

D was slightly taken aback, then smiled and shook his head, "No need to prepare gifts, we just went over to help."

But Steven shook his head, "But the calamity in Hoenn was stopped thanks to your intervention, in any case, the Hoenn League should express gratitude to you."

And as the Champion of Hoenn League, he came especially to convey his thanks.

However, Steven didn't inform the other members of Hoenn League about D's real identity.

Because judging from D's mask-wearing behavior, he seemed to prefer keeping his identity hidden.

If he didn't know the whole story, Steven thought he'd never believe the hero who saved Hoenn was actually a rancher living in the countryside.

Hearing this, D grinned without continuing the topic, instead asking:

"Is everything in Hoenn okay now?"

Steven nodded, "Thanks to your intervention, Groudon and Kyogre's impact on Hoenn was minimized, except for some towns near oceans and volcanos, other cities have smoothly resumed operations."

"That's good." D said.

He turned around and took out two Treasure Pearls from the cabinet, placing them in front of Steven.

These were the Red Orb and Blue Orb, previously returned by Teddiursa.

"Now that the matter is settled, these Treasure Pearls should be returned to their rightful owners." D said.

However, Steven shook his head, "We are not their owners."

D was slightly stunned.

Steven continued, "To be precise, these Treasure Pearls belong to Groudon and Kyogre."

During previous investigations, he found related information in some ancient texts.

Whether it's the Red Orb or Blue Orb, both are part of Groudon and Kyogre's original power.

A long time ago, they lost these orbs for unknown reasons until humans stumbled upon them.

Thinking of this, Steven made a request, "Mr. D, please continue to keep these Treasure Pearls with you!"

D looked surprised, "Hmm?"

Steven revealed his concern, "I fear one day someone might use these Treasure Pearls again to awaken Groudon and Kyogre."

"And now, only my friend and I in the whole Hoenn know the Pearls are with you, this way we don't have to worry they will fall into the wrong hands in the future."

---

Hearing Steven's words, D quickly understood his meaning.

He looked at the two Treasure Pearls and, after a moment, smiled and said, "Alright."

"However, there is something I would like to ask of you." D said.

"Go ahead."
