D turned to look at the Pokémon beside him and continued:

"We don't want our peaceful life to be disturbed by others, so please do not reveal my identity information."

Upon hearing this, Steven was slightly taken aback, then solemnly promised, "I understand."

"Then there's no problem." D smiled.

"Additionally..." Steven hesitated.

Noticing his expression, D asked, "What's wrong?"

Steven asked the question on his mind, "D, are you from the Draconid people?"

"Draconid people?" D was somewhat surprised.

The Draconid people are an ancient tribe in the Hoenn Region, and the Sky Pillar where Rayquaza resides was built by them.

Steven must have thought that since he could summon Rayquaza, he might be one of the Draconid people.

Looking at Steven, who seemed puzzled, D shook his head and said:

"No, I have no relation to the Draconid people. The reason I can call upon Rayquaza is due to some past events."

Dragonite had heard of Rayquaza's heroic legend and specifically went out to find Rayquaza to hear about its past heroics.

Perhaps Rayquaza found Dragonite likable, so it gave Dragonite one of its scales.

Afterwards, Rayquaza would accept Dragonite's invitation to visit the ranch...

"I see." Steven now knew the answer he was seeking.

It seems Rayquaza doesn't operate solely in the Hoenn Region.

Beyond Hoenn, there exists a broader world.

With the crisis over the Hoenn Region now averted, Steven suddenly felt the urge to see the outside world.

Surely, there are many fascinating stones waiting for him in those places.

Speaking of stones, Steven then took a specially made box out of his pocket.

The money in those two briefcases were gifts prepared by his father, Joseph Stone.

But the item inside this box was a gift he personally prepared for D.

Inside is one of his most precious collections, which Steven intended to give to D.

He pushed the box in front of D, "D, this is a personal gift I've prepared for you, to thank you for the help you've provided to the Hoenn Region during this time."

D was taken aback, another gift?

He looked at the two briefcases by the sofa.

Steven appeared helpless and couldn't help but explain, "That is the thanks my father asked me to bring you."

Upon hearing this, D immediately understood.

Mr. Stone was thanking him for saving his son; the items in those two briefcases were a father's gifts.

And the items in this box were Steven's own gifts for him.

D reached out to take it. He had intended to wait until Steven left to look, but accidentally touched the clasp on the box.

The next moment, the lid of the wooden box loosened and then popped open, revealing a stone inside that looked like a glass marble with peculiar patterns.

When D's gaze landed on the stone, the information about the stone fully appeared in his view.

[Ampharosite: A stone with extraordinary power that allows Ampharos to mega evolve into Mega Ampharos when used with a Key Stone.]

D was slightly stunned.

Across from him, Steven observed D's expression.

Seeing D's shock, Steven felt a little regretful.

He suddenly realized not everyone shares his interest in stones.

He had instinctively gifted what he considered his most precious possession to D, without considering whether D would appreciate it.

Seeing D's stunned look, he must have been expecting the box to contain an ordinary stone, right?

Steven carefully chose his words and said, "Sorry, I might have been in too much of a hurry when I left, accidentally bringing the wrong box."

D was puzzled, "Huh?"

Steven was also taken aback, observing D's expression, which seemed to show a hint of reluctance.

Steven's heart skipped a beat, then he tentatively asked, "D, do you also like stones?"

"This stone is beautiful," D sincerely praised.

Sparkling glass marbles are the most treasured possessions for young boys; no boy could resist them.

Hearing this, Steven felt a sense of relief.

"In that case, I'll give it to you. To be honest, I really like stones; I am interested in all kinds of stones and have many such stones at home."

D: "..." That's so Steven.

Speaking of which, Steven seemed unaware of the stone's value.

The Ampharosite, also known as a Mega Stone, when paired with a Trainer carrying a Key Stone, allows Ampharos to surpass its limits and mega evolve.

However, at this moment in time, the research on [Mega Evolution] has not been discovered yet, and Professor Augustine Sycamore is still studying Mega Evolution and has not yet achieved further breakthroughs.

This is also the reason Steven did not have Metagross mega evolve in the recent divine battle.

---

---

---

---

# 472: Chapter 469: Alder: I Endorse Life Sheep Milk!

D accepted the precious gift from Steven.

It's just a pity that there are no Pokemon in the ranch that can use this stone currently, although there is a Mareep by the World Tree, it still has a long way to go before evolving into an Ampharos.

Moreover, to enable a Pokemon to undergo Mega Evolution, relying on the power of a Mega Stone is not enough.

A trainer also needs to wear a Key Stone, but D currently does not have a Key Stone and does not have a trainer-Pokemon relationship with the Mareep by the World Tree.

Therefore, this stone can only be used for collection for now, or kept for future exchanges with other trainers once Mega Evolution becomes available.

"..."

The only problem is that there are no Pokemon on the ranch that can undergo Mega Evolution.

Every time he thinks of this, D can't help but feel a pang of regret.

If only Dragonite, Koraidon, and Pawmot had their respective Mega Stones.

However, the imagination is beautiful, but reality is harsh.

Such things simply don't exist in this world.

Fine!

Since Steven gave him a stone, he should also give Steven some specialties from the ranch!

Thinking of this, D asked, "By the way, how is Metagross doing now?"

He previously noted that Steven's Metagross is a Pokemon that challenges both Groudon and Kyogre, the two Legendary Beasts, at the same time.

Regarding D's inquiry about Metagross's situation, Steven was a bit surprised but still replied:

"That battle left it with severe injuries, but fortunately, it was treated promptly, and Metagross has now recovered."

"I see!" D got up, moved a box of Life Sheep Milk from the warehouse, and placed it in front of Steven.

Steven looked bewildered: "This is..."

D introduced, "This is a specialty from my ranch. It can instantly restore a severely injured Pokemon to health, no matter how serious the injury. Given that you're a trainer, you must be engaged in Pokemon battles every day, right?"

"Yes, yes." Hearing this, Steven opened his mouth incredulously.

D did not elaborate further, he merely smiled and said, "Take it! Maybe one day you will find it useful."

With D's sincere offer hard to refuse, Steven had no choice but to accept the box of sheep milk.

Because there were still some finishing touches to attend to in the Hoenn Region, Steven did not stay there long.

After delivering the gift, he embarked on his journey back, carrying the box of sheep milk.

Meanwhile, back at D Ranch.

Looking at the two suitcases full of banknotes, for a moment D didn't know what to say.

Mr. Stone's gift was truly straightforward...

*

On Steven's private plane.

Steven opened the packaging and saw the chibi version of a Skiddo printed on the glass jar, with a line of small text below.

"Life Sheep Milk..." Steven muttered its name.

He suddenly found the name somewhat familiar, so he took out his Rotom Phone to search it.

Soon, many news articles related to this sheep milk popped up in the search engine.

[Shocking! 70-year-old drinks Life Sheep Milk and...]

[Unbelievable! The effects of Life Sheep Milk are so exaggerated, it's comparable to a Resurrection Potion in games!]

[Highly recommended Life Sheep Milk! My Pokemon was badly injured in a battle, there wasn't enough time to get to a Pokemon Center while in the wild, but thanks to Life Sheep Milk, my Pokemon wasn't in danger!]

At the end, Steven even saw an interview video.

And the person being interviewed was someone he was very familiar with.

Fiery red hair, an unruly outfit, a tall man.

Is that... Alder from the Unova Region?

Steven was a bit surprised that Alder had a connection with Life Sheep Milk?

He clicked on the video.

A female reporter was interviewing Alder.

[Female Reporter: Wow! Mr. Alder, this is truly astonishing, your Volcarona has actually recovered to its peak form. As far as I know, it previously had an incurable disease! How did you do it?!]

[Alder: Haha! It's all thanks to D Ranch's Life Sheep Milk. Volcarona's life was on the brink of ending. Fortunately, D from D Ranch offered us a Helping Hand, and after drinking the Life Sheep Milk, Volcarona's body recovered!]

[Female Reporter: D Ranch's Life Sheep Milk? Wow, it sounds like it has extraordinary effects!]

[Alder: That's right! D Ranch's specialty Life Sheep Milk, if you want some, just order from their little shop!]

Steven: "..."

What's with this strong advertising vibe?!

Steven had previously heard some rumors about Alder's Volcarona.

It was said that the Volcarona was badly injured, and there was no way to cure it using modern medical technology.

And now, not only has it recovered its strength to peak form, but there's even been some breakthroughs.

Thinking of this, Steven looked at the box of Life Sheep Milk in front of him, suddenly realizing how precious this sheep milk was.

He opened an online shopping app, searched for D Ranch's shop, and went in, only to find that the Life Sheep Milk there was marked as sold out.

"Because it's too popular, sales have been limited?" Steven murmured.

He went to place an order, only to find out the shop currently does not support cross-regional delivery.

Steven: "..."

However, the next second, the page refreshed automatically, surprising Steven with the appearance of the Hoenn Region on the list of delivery areas!

Steven paused slightly.

Is this....

At the same time, an announcement was also posted in D Ranch's little shop.

[D Ranch's Little Shop: Good afternoon, dear customers! Starting today, our shop will officially lift the ban on delivery routes to the Hoenn Region! If you have any items you wish to purchase, you can start ordering now!]

This message caused quite a stir.

Some other regional internet users suddenly found out that Dragonite Delivery surprisingly began supporting delivery services to the Hoenn Region!

What does this mean?

Discrimination, outright discrimination!

Some people went into the shop's message board, demanding the owner open delivery routes to other regions, wanting to experience the convenience of Dragonite Delivery as well!

However, they received a response from the shop owner:

[Sorry for any inconvenience this may have caused. However, for the safety of the delivery Dragonite, the shop temporarily does not support delivery services to other regions!]

This reply was quickly picked up by some people.

Did D Ranch's shop observe the eradication of evil in the Hoenn Region, hence opening delivery routes there?

But the regions where they live clearly have no bad guys, so why hasn't D lifted their region's ban?

This is what some Kanto, Johto, Sinnoh, Unova, Kalos regions' users thought.

Users from the Kanto Region and Johto Region are well aware of the situation.

Because many living in those two regions have heard of the rumors about Team Rocket.

The members of that organization are particularly fond of stealing or snatching the Pokemon of traveling trainers.

Hence, users from Kanto and Johto resolutely shut their mouths.

---

---

---

---

# 473: Chapter 470: The Day the World Tree Ripened

After Steven left, life at the ranch returned to its usual peaceful state.

With the number of Dragonite couriers increasing and the eradication of Team Magma and Team Aqua that plagued the Hoenn Region, D finally launched Dragonite Delivery's cross-regional service.

The distance between the Hoenn Region and the Paldea Region is not too far; a full-speed flight by Dragonite takes only three hours for a round trip.

Such a journey is not a big deal for Dragonite at all.

Initially, D was worried that Dragonites would get tired flying such long distances.

But soon, he discovered that his Dragonite employees relished the task.

One Dragonite told D that back on Dragonite Island, they wouldn't stay on the island all day because it was too dull.

After evolving into Dragonite, most of them would fly over the ocean for a long time every day.

Fishing, exploring unknown sea areas, training amidst storms, sometimes even rescuing ships in distress and humans lost at sea.

Some Dragonites would even fly around the planet to explore just how vast the world truly is.

During their travels, they would stop on other lands to rest or do other things.

These Dragonites would occasionally encounter humans and be captured by Human Trainers.

After meeting a trainer they liked, some never returned to Dragonite Island, instead choosing to travel and live with their Human Trainer.

Hearing the Dragonites' explanation, D felt relieved.

The Dragonites happily went out to deliver every day, while he stayed at the ranch to care for Pokémon, manage the ranch, plant crops, package orders for customers, and finally entrust them to Dragonite for delivery.

During this time, some companies contacted D specifically.

They wanted to partner with D to establish a specialized Dragonite Delivery Company, taking over the entire Paldea's delivery business and turning each Dragonite into a more professional courier.

But D rejected this suggestion.

Because if it were done that way, Dragonites would become real "working Dragonites," always having endless work every day.

Instead of the current routine of starting work at six in the morning, finishing by five in the afternoon, handling the orders they wish to, and passing on the others to different Dragonites.

Even though it sounds tiring, in reality, each Dragonite only runs five or six errands a day.

This is because D Ranch's small shop limits its daily product quantity, unlike other shops, it closes right at five in the afternoon.

If there was too much cargo, the Dragonites wouldn't keep up, possibly needing to work overtime for evening deliveries.

Moreover, D doesn't lack money now, so there's no need to exploit the Dragonites like that.

Just seeing them fly happily every day, or lazing around on the ranch's grass during their downtime, dozing with their chubby, round bellies exposed, makes D content.

After D's refusal, those companies gave up their original plans and instead started training Pelippers and Pidgeottos as professional couriers.

In the calm and warm life, time marched on alongside the growth of Pokémon, reaching the 7th of June in the League Year 200.

This day was destined to be extraordinary for D and the Pokémon at the ranch.

Because on this ordinary day, Calyrex would perform the final maturation of the World Tree.

Recalling the last commotion caused by the World Tree, D felt that this time it would be quite a grand event.

Therefore, he deliberately scheduled it for midnight.

Paldea Region, Porto Marinada Town, 11:54 pm.

In the distant town, everything was dark; at this hour, the local residents were already in deep sleep, resting peacefully in their beds.

Yet, at D Ranch, it was a scene of bright lights.

Koraidon, Cyclizar, Gogoat, Butterfree, Alcremie, Garganacl, Miltank, Dragonite, Calyrex, Ogerpon, Gholdengo...

A gathering of Pokémon had assembled, ready to witness the birth of the World Tree.

In the Reversed World, Mewtwo and Giratina had made their preparations.

The time spent together had given Mewtwo a new understanding of this Pokémon who enjoyed surfing the internet among humans.

At this moment, it understood well that it needed to cooperate with Giratina to elevate the tree to the sky.

Once D and the Pokémon had gathered, Mewtwo turned to Giratina and said, "Let's begin."

As long as Giratina can bring the World Tree into a special [Anti-gravity Floating] state, Mewtwo can use its Psychic power to elevate the entire tree into the sky.

Giratina hovered around the World Tree, a hint of excitement evident in its blood-red eyes.

"Understood!"

Accompanied by a deep roar, Giratina suddenly emitted a pitch-black light.

The light gradually enveloped both the World Tree and the massive Floating Island where it was located.

Moments later, the light completely merged into the World Tree.

Giratina returned to Mewtwo's side, saying, "It's your turn!"

Mewtwo remained silent.

It calmly gazed at the giant World Tree before it, then raised its right hand, blue light emanating from its eyes.

Overwhelming Psychic power erupted from Mewtwo, quickly enveloping the entire World Tree and the Floating Island below in its grasp.

---

In the next second, the enormous World Tree, along with Mewtwo and Giratina, simultaneously vanished from the Reversed World.

Waiting on the tree, D and Calyrex only felt a momentary blur before their vision gradually cleared, realizing the World Tree had been moved high up in the sky.

Above them was a sky full of stars and a bright, clear moon, with a blue dome inverted overhead.

Looking down from the World Tree, one could see a layer of clouds shrouded in night.

Every scene represented that they had left the Reversed World and reached the heavens!

The giant World Tree stood firmly on the cloud layer, without wavering, nor swaying!

The surrounding Pokemon were astonished by this beautiful and magnificent scene, uttering incredulous growls.

"Gao... "

"Meh... "

"Mama... "

"Bamoh!"

After doing all this, Mewtwo did not stop.

It scanned the surroundings with eyes that emitted a blue halo.

The powerful Psychic instantly distorted the surrounding space, creating a wondrous Trick Room...

Then, it erected Light Screens around using its Psychic power.

Finally, it used Psybeam on the Light Screen to create an illusion, erasing the internal World Tree from view.

This way, humans could no longer actively probe the presence of the World Tree from the outside.

After completing this series of tasks, Mewtwo's Psychic power was completely exhausted.

It landed wearily on the World Tree, then turned its gaze to Calyrex.

Next, it was Calyrex's turn to act!

Calyrex still wore a gentle smile on its face.

It nodded lightly to Mewtwo and then lifted off the ground, slowly levitating into the air.

In the next second, an incomparably vast energy surged from its body.

Green light spread like an ocean, engulfing D and the entire World Tree.

Once this light completely enveloped the World Tree, a strange humming sound echoed through the heavens and earth.

Next, the light surged into the World Tree.

"Buzz!"

There was a moment of brief silence in the air, as if all sound had vanished.

In the next second, a ripple of green Natural Energy radiated outward from the World Tree, spreading along the curvature of the atmosphere, enveloping the entire planet.

*

In the Cosmos, Rayquaza, gnawing on a meteorite, suddenly sensed something amiss.

It lowered its head and gazed at the entire planet, seeing the originally blue planet now emitting a green glow!

"Howl!?" That's?!

Rayquaza, fully astonished, immediately abandoned the meteorite. Its dark body sharply turned direction, diving toward the place where the Natural Energy was most concentrated.

*

Johto Region.

Standing on Mount Tensei, the Legendary Pokemon Ho-oh, who was meeting its guard unit, suddenly raised its head to look at the starry sky.

The intense natural aura appeared again! And compared to the last time, it was even more potent!

That time, it was definitely not its imagination!

What could it be? Ho-oh couldn't resist being drawn by the pure Natural Energy.

Nearby, Lei Gong looked puzzled.

"What's happening, Lord Ho-oh?"

But before Lei Gong could finish speaking, it saw the entire sky being dyed green.

Lei Gong looked surprised.

Ho-oh, however, gazed into the distance.

It said to its four followers, "A great phenomenon has been born, I must investigate, you continue with your mission!"

After speaking, Ho-oh spread its beautiful wings and flew to the sky, leaving behind a splendid rainbow.

The Legendary beasts and Marshadow stood on the mountaintop, watching that scene.

Their manes gently fluttered in the night breeze; while Entei didn't understand what happened, judging by the sign, it must be something very important!

With Ho-oh heading for investigation, they decided to first complete their mission.

Once Ho-oh's presence completely disappeared, the Legendary beasts exchanged glances, then vanished into the night.

NNow, as they departed, Marshadow returned to the shadow realm.

*

Kalos Region, Terminus Cave.

The Z God Zygarde, resting with closed eyes, suddenly opened its pale eyes.

It's here again!

The energy wave from last time appeared again!

This time, it distinctly sensed the opponent's presence.

That is definitely not some natural and ecosystem warning; the aura... is nature itself!

Zygarde's inner thoughts stirred, wishing to see firsthand.

But after calming down, Zygarde decided to remain at Terminus Cave, monitoring nature.

Only this time, it dispatched a self-aware "Zygarde Core" to investigate.

In the Kalos Region, within a forest shrouded in night.

A Zygarde Core with a blue diamond emblem on its chest suddenly lifted its head to the sky, then moved towards the direction of the energy.

*

Hoenn Region.

Inside a large volcano.

The scarlet lava churned continuously. Just back here not long ago, yet to fall into slumber, the Earth God Groudon suddenly opened its eyes.

Simultaneously, in the underwater cave, Kyogre reacted.

They clearly sensed a massive, pure Natural Energy.

Such concentrated Natural Energy...

If absorbed, they could surely revert to their original forms!

At this moment, Groudon took its large steps, emerging from the lava.

It stepped out of the volcano, first gazing into the distant sky, then lifting its legs, walking step by step in that direction.

In the deep, dark underwater cave, Kyogre simultaneously left the cave, entering the ocean.

It swished its tail fin, briskly swimming toward the source of energy.

Kyogre sensed that Groudon was also starting to move at this moment.

It couldn't let the opponent get ahead!

If that guy restored to its original form first, it would be disastrous!

The Hoenn's twin gods started their action again.

Meanwhile, in the Weather Institute, a dozing researcher before his desk suddenly received an alert.

This young researcher just opened his eyes and witnessed a terrifying scene.

Seeing that the Legendary Pokemon, who almost destroyed the Hoenn Region half a month ago, had revived once again!

Since that event, the Hoenn League strengthened monitoring of Groudon and Kyogre for emergencies.

Gazing at the radar display showing moving red dots, this researcher initially thought he was having a nightmare.

He pinched his thigh hard, feeling the pain, suddenly realizing it wasn't a dream; Groudon and Kyogre really awakened again!

"Oh my God!"

The researcher appeared panicked, hurriedly sounding the alarm within the institute.

Soon, everyone gathered before the monitoring equipment.

They nervously stared at the images on screen.

Through satellite imagery, Groudon had already left its volcano habitat, moving towards distant eastern lands!

Initially, the group of researchers thought these two Pokemon were going to fight over their territories again.

Soon someone discovered something strange.

Because those two Pokemon weren't heading toward each other's location!

"Immediately predict Groudon and Kyogre's movement trajectory!" a senior officer ordered immediately.

"Understood!"

The researcher at the computer swiftly typed on the keyboard.

Soon, the trajectories of the two Pokemon fully appeared on the screen.

Unexpectedly, their destination wasn't somewhere in the Hoenn Region but far overseas, beyond Hoenn!

"How can this be?!" a researcher exclaimed in shock.

Didn't Groudon and Kyogre awaken this time for territorial battles?

What's attracting them in that place?

---

---

---

---

# 474: Chapter 471: Rayquaza Joins the Chat Group!

No one knows the answer to this question.

At this moment, the vast Weather Institute is utterly silent.

Everyone's gaze is incredulously fixed on that giant screen.

The screen is divided in half, and the images of those two Super Ancient Pokemon have been completely captured by the satellite.

On the left, Groudon moves its sturdy legs, walking slowly on the ground, its massive tail dragging on the ground, occasionally leaving a mark.

On the right, Kyogre moves in the sea, the satellite unable to capture its full view.

But judging by the radar display, Kyogre is moving at an extremely fast speed.

It has already surpassed Groudon, officially leaving the Hoenn Region!

That direction... is the Unova Region!

"Contact the Unova League immediately and ask them to prepare for a response!" a senior member instantly issued the order.

"Yes!" The assistant beside him immediately attempted to contact the Unova League.

"What about Groudon?!" A researcher shifted his attention from Kyogre, looking at Groudon walking on the ground.

If Groudon's destination is also the Unova Region.

Then at its pace, who knows how many years it would take to cross such a vast ocean and reach the other side of the sea.

From Groudon's performance in the previous godly battle, it cannot fly like Kyogre by utilizing its massive pectoral fins.

Upon hearing this, everyone present fell silent.

A senior hesitated for a moment before speaking, "Temporarily track Groudon to prevent it from intruding into towns and affecting the citizens there."

He recalled the image of Groudon using the magma it released from its body to create land in the sea and paused, then added:

"Just wait until it leaves the Hoenn Region."

*

D is still unaware of these external changes.

He stands excitedly on Sky Island, looking up at the giant World Tree.

[World Tree (Mythical Level Plant): The embodiment of nature, the origin of the ecosystem, the mother of all, nurturing the entire planet and all beings with its own natural energy. It is said it can sustain a world.]

"From the mature stage to the mythical level, hmm, such a description isn't unexpected," D murmured.

It seems the Pokemon living in the guild's layer and the Dragonite on the lower level noticed the change in the World Tree and all came running out.

The flying Pokemon flapped their wings, curiously flying around the World Tree.

Those that can't fly climbed onto the branches of the World Tree, looking down at the endless sea of clouds below.

"Woo! How magnificent!"

Milotic's eyes sparkled with a brilliant light.

The other Pokemon looked around as well.

The vibrant World Tree, the ethereal sea of clouds, the beautiful, deep starry sky...

Scenes like these were things they couldn't see in the Reversed World before.

D also found this scene extremely beautiful.

He sat with Koraidon and Pawmo on the branches of the World Tree, admiring the beautiful starry sky together.

Below at the Exploration Team Guild Headquarters.

Calyrex, Mewtwo, and Giratina consumed a lot of strength to cultivate the World Tree. They stood there resting.

The Pokemon on the World Tree ran around them.

Pikachu and Meowth brought them Berry Juice for replenishing energy.

Tatsugiri commanded its Venusaur head chef to make delicious sushi for these three great contributors.

The three Treeckos watching the orchard brought a bunch of Berries to lay before Calyrex and the other Pokemon.

The night was intoxicating. Ninetales lit a bonfire using Ember, and the Pokemon held a grand bonfire party under the starry sky and moonlight to celebrate the growth of the World Tree.

The firelight illuminated the face of each Pokemon, all of which wore happy smiles.

Mewtwo sat on a bench by the Slowpoke Fountain, gazing gently and warmly at the lively scene.

Isn't this the life it had always longed for?

Wonderful, Mewtwo closed its eyes, savoring the taste of happiness quietly.

Perhaps it had interacted too much with humans, the internet celebrity Giratina was no longer as ferocious and arrogant as it initially was.

Facing this group of weaker Pokemon, it laughed heartily as it joined in, bathing in their adoring gazes.

Calyrex, on the other hand, sat elegantly on a chair with a smile, savoring the tea brewed by these Pokemon.

D, sitting on the World Tree's branches, looked down at this scene with a sense of nostalgia.

He slowly exhaled and then looked up at the starry sky above.

Starting from today, the World Tree side is considered completely finished!

The survival problem of the Pokemon staying here was also completely resolved by him. From now on, no matter what Pokemon joins, they can live well in this place.

They won't have to worry about going hungry, being bored, or facing danger.

After all, with Mewtwo stationed here, if the Pokemon here are ever in danger, it will most likely act immediately.

Paradise.

The World Tree side is truly a paradise for the Pokemon, D thought.

*

Meanwhile, beyond all the cosmos.

The Creator God Arceus continues to observe that special world.

Today, as the tree has grown, the entire world is enveloped in a natural aura.

---

Among all the worlds He has created, it appears to be so special.

The image of the Pokemon on the World Tree collectively hosting a bonfire party was reflected in Arceus' eyes.

Looking at the Pokemon Guild behind them, Arceus' eyes twinkled.

But soon, He calmed down.

"No, it's not the time yet."

Because in that world, His avatar had not yet awakened from slumber.

As thoughts surged, Arceus had a new plan.

He murmured softly:

"D, I know your pact with Giratina; thus, that matter shall be my final test for you."

If he manages to stop that rampaging avatar, then He will descend in person.

"Show me!"

Arceus murmured.

His voice gradually faded, and the void space fell silent once again.

*

On the World Tree.

Mewtwo, who was resting, suddenly opened its eyes and looked up at the sky.

It sensed an overwhelmingly powerful presence rushing towards here at great speed!

Mewtwo's mind moved slightly, and in the next moment, its figure flashed before D, who was grooming Pawmot's fur.

"An unknown Pokemon has intruded!" Mewtwo became alert.

Hearing this, D was slightly startled and raised his head along with Koraidon and the others.

The next second, he saw Black Rayquaza descend with an awe-inspiring and flashy presence before the World Tree.

Gazing at this giant tree, Rayquaza couldn't believe its eyes.

"Howl!"

It widened its eyes, looking at the World Tree in shock.

"It's alright, I know it." Seeing Rayquaza, D sat down again: "It should mean no harm."

Meanwhile, Dragonite and the others also noticed the arrival of Rayquaza.

A few excited Pokemon flew out, loudly calling: "Awoo!" (Rayquaza senior!)

Hearing the familiar sound from the tree, Rayquaza focused, only to see Dragonite and the others here too!

Rayquaza was greatly surprised, then quickly flew forward to the top of the World Tree.

Here, it saw more Pokemon!

Rayquaza was dumbfounded, unable to immediately figure out what was going on.

"Awoo awoo!"

Dragonite quickly explained the current situation to it.

After a while, Rayquaza finally understood.

"Howl..."

Even so, it still felt a wave of surprise.

Rayquaza turned to look around, spotting Cyclizar, Beautifly, and the Pokemon from D Ranch.

It saw D sitting on a branch, waving to greet it.

In addition, Rayquaza also noticed the Pokemon that helped it stop Groudon and Kyogre that day.

That Pokemon, upon seeing it, suddenly became excited.

The next second, Rayquaza saw it lift its head and shout to D on the tree:

"Howl!" (D, quickly invite it into the group!)

Rayquaza: "?"

D: "..."

He rode Koraidon down, helplessly said to Mewtwo: "Help me out, Mewtwo."

Mewtwo glanced at Rayquaza, then at D, and nodded calmly.

Two minutes later, D returned to the World Tree with a brand new Rotom Phone.

He handed this Rotom Phone to Rayquaza, who looked confounded, and explained:

"This is a Rotom Phone, um... a machine made by humans. Living alone in the ozone layer must be quite boring, right?"

"If you have a Rotom Phone, the Rotom inside will accompany you and bring you updates about world events as they happen."

"Howl..."

Rayquaza blankly received the Rotom Phone with Dragon Claw.

D just wanted to introduce it further when Giratina excitedly took over the conversation, eagerly explaining the functions and operation methods of the Rotom Phone to Rayquaza.

D, accompanied by Koraidon, helplessly stood aside, thinking that Giratina truly deserved its fame as a social influencer; its social skills were indeed extraordinary.

About thirty minutes later, Giratina zealously helped Rayquaza register all accounts, then once again asked D to pull it into the chat group.

D was already numb.

Expressionlessly, he added Rayquaza as a friend and then pulled it into the Legendary Beast Chat Group.

The next second, a notification popped up in the chat group.

[Rayquaza joined the chat group!]

Giratina was finally satisfied.

It stopped pestering Rayquaza, giving it time to explore the functions of the Rotom Phone on its own.

Rayquaza levitated fifteen centimeters above ground, holding the Rotom Phone with its left claw while its right claw awkwardly tapped the screen, following Giratina's instructions.

Dragonite huddled nearby, occasionally advising Rayquaza.

The atmosphere appeared incredibly harmonious at this moment.

After a while, Rayquaza put aside the Rotom Phone, beginning to tell Dragonite, Cyclizar, Beautifly, and the others about its heroic deeds of defeating Groudon and Kyogre and saving the world days ago.

During this time, the Pokemon living on the World Tree all joined in.

A group of Pokemon listened intently, their eyes full of admiration as they looked at Rayquaza.

"Mi!" So impressive!

Three Tatsugiri bounced around excitedly on the grass.

---

Milotic and Treecko show great admiration for Rayquaza.

Rayquaza seems happy, grinning broadly with a joyful expression.

D:"..."

Oh well, he should already be used to this kind of thing.

But speaking of which, looking at Rayquaza in its normal form, D suddenly becomes curious if it can undergo Mega Evolution now?

Thinking of this, D asks.

"By the way, Rayquaza."

Rayquaza turns its head to look at him.

D asks:"Can you undergo Mega Evolution now? It's changing form, transforming into another appearance to unleash a stronger power than before."

If he remembers correctly, Rayquaza uses the meteor energy stored within its body for Mega Evolution, and as long as it uses Dragon Ascent, it can change into the Mega form.

Upon hearing this question, Rayquaza is slightly taken aback.

It opens its mouth wide and responds:"Roar?"(You humans call that transformation Mega Evolution?)

Rayquaza's thoughts are transmitted through telepathy.

D nods:"Yes, Mega Evolution."

Upon hearing this, the surrounding Pokemon, including Mewtwo, Dragonite, and Calyrex, all turn their gaze over.

Rayquaza shakes its head:"Roar."(It used to be possible, but now the energy is insufficient, and I can't transform into that form.)

Insufficient energy? Is it because the energy in the meteors Rayquaza usually consumes is too weak?

Speaking of which, D suddenly thinks of a certain item from the anime.

It is an ancient, large stone containing massive primordial energy, considered by some to be the origin of Mega Evolution.

The energy within that giant stone can allow Rayquaza to undergo Mega Evolution.

However, that giant stone should now be stored in a hidden ruin in the Hoenn Region.

In the anime, Steven and Alain cooperate to unravel the mystery of the ruins and successfully enter them.

The moment that giant stone appeared, it attracted Rayquaza and even sparked a contest between Groudon and Kyogre.

Unfortunately, it was finally taken back to the Kalos Region by Lysandre to execute his evil plan.

"Rayquaza can now be considered one of us; if there's a chance, I could help Rayquaza fight for that giant stone and give it to Rayquaza, helping it Mega Evolve and enhance its power," D thinks.

Because when the plot involving Demon God Hoopa unfolds, they will need the strength of Mega Rayquaza.

Moreover, if they seize the giant stone, it will also prevent Lysandre's evil plan; it would be perfect.

But speaking of which, besides Rayquaza, Mewtwo can also undergo Mega Evolution.

Unlike Rayquaza, Mewtwo possesses two Mega Stones.

One can help it evolve into the X form, specializing in close combat with formidable strength.

The other helps it evolve into the Y form with terrifying spiritual power, capable of reducing skyscrapers to ashes with just a slight use of telekinesis.

What's even more interesting is that Mewtwo was born only in modern times, yet its Mega Stones have existed in this world since ancient times.

"I wonder if Steven has any Mega Stones for Mewtwo."

At this moment, D suddenly becomes curious about Steven's stone collection.

It must contain many Mega Stones for Pokemon.

The campfire gradually dies down, and the Pokemon on the World Tree start to show signs of fatigue.

It's getting late, D bids farewell to the Pokemon on the ranch and then, along with Calyrex, returns to the ranch to prepare for rest.

Giratina also dives into the shadows of the World Tree.

Rayquaza accepts the invitation from the Pokemon on the World Tree and stays.

Because these Pokemon still want to hear it recount its heroic tales.

Rayquaza looks around. It has a fondness for this place, filled with natural energy, and now accepting the invitation, it happily resides on the World Tree.

Meanwhile, Groudon and Kyogre are on their way here.

---

---

---

---

# 475: Chapter 472: Groudon Crosses the Sea, Shocking the World!

The next morning, bright sunlight streamed into the room through the window.

D, who was in the middle of a dream, was suddenly awakened by a phone call.

He groggily opened his eyes, and when he saw the name on the screen, he immediately snapped awake.

Mewtwo?

Why is Mewtwo calling him now?

D sat up from the bed and pressed the answer button.

The next second, Mewtwo's face appeared on the screen.

It looked at D from the other side and said in a deep voice, "A Pokemon has arrived."

"What?" D was slightly taken aback.

But immediately, Mewtwo switched the camera.

Before D could react, he saw a Pokemon with a noble and elegant appearance, possessing a pair of dazzling, colorful wings, perched on the branch of the World Tree, gazing down at the Pokemon Guild below.

Is that... Ho-oh?!

D was astonished, how did Ho-oh come over as well?

Could it be attracted by the World Tree, just like Rayquaza?

Mewtwo's deep voice continued from the other side: "It seems to want to stay as well, requesting permission, you should come and handle it."

D: "..."

The sudden appearance of a Legendary Beast left D momentarily at a loss, only feeling that his mind was still foggy from just waking up.

After a while, he rubbed his eyes and said, "Alright, I'll go over and have a look now."

"Ah gassi?"

Koraidon opened its sleepy eyes, hearing the noise.

"Come with me out for a bit!" D said to Koraidon while quickly getting dressed.

"Ah gassi~"

Koraidon stretched lazily, shook its head, then happily followed behind D out of the room.

Last night stayed up too late, even the Pokemon in the ranch had a rare chance to sleep in today.

Only Calyrex was up early, sitting on the sofa playing on the phone.

Seeing D rushing out, Calyrex was a bit surprised: "D? What are you going to do?"

D answered, "Another Legendary Pokemon has been attracted by the World Tree, I'm going to check it out."

As he spoke, D suddenly remembered the scenes Calyrex had foreseen in the past.

Back then, Calyrex had seen the World Tree rise into the sky and witnessed Ho-oh perch on it.

It seems Calyrex's Future Sight is finally coming true today.

Hearing this, Calyrex wasn't too surprised.

The tree inherently had a great allure for Pokemon, and the natural energy contained within provides tremendous benefits to every Pokemon.

Even Legendary Pokemon like itself are no exception.

If it weren't for D, it might have already gone to live on the World Tree.

However, compared to there, Calyrex would rather live with D.

Because with D around, it would no longer feel lonely.

Leaving the house in haste, D rode Koraidon and flew into the sky, ascending above the clouds.

The World Tree is located just below Rayquaza's ozone layer, a place rarely visited by humans or airplanes, so D wasn't worried about being discovered.

Even if adventurers came here, it wouldn't be a problem.

Mewtwo's magical space not only warps the internal dimensions but also confuses intruders' minds, causing them to encounter 'ghost walls' or unconsciously veer in another direction until they are far from the World Tree.

But these are no concern for D.

As soon as he entered the area, Mewtwo's Psychic powers automatically transported him to the Sky Island where the World Tree resides.

Riding the elevator to the top floor, as soon as D touched down, he saw Mewtwo levitating with both feet off the ground, eyes focused on Ho-oh not far away.

Ho-oh was brightly observing the actions of those Pokemon around.

Watching them weave through shops, making sushi to greet Pokemon customers, or picking Berries from the orchard and moving them into the warehouse.

At this time, the two Pokemon seemed to sense something and turned their heads to look back.

D, with Koraidon beside him, approached, seeing Mewtwo made him feel Mewtwo increasingly resembled a security guard.

"Hello, Ho-oh."

D greeted Ho-oh politely.

"You know me?" Ho-oh appeared quite curious.

"Mew and Teddiursa have told me about you before." D explained.

Upon hearing this, Ho-oh immediately understood.

"You are the Trainer of those two Pokemon."

D smiled, "No, I'm just the logistics for the Heroic Expedition Team."

Ho-oh didn't understand what logistics meant, but from the recent conversation with Mewtwo, it became clear that D was the owner of this World Tree.

Looking up at this big tree, Ho-oh felt emotional, and then gracefully inquired:

"This place is much more beautiful than where I used to live, if possible, could I stay here? As thanks, I can offer assistance within my abilities."

Ho-oh initiated a residency application!

Agree or refuse?

If agreed, there will be another Legendary Pokemon on the World Tree from now on, increasing Ho-oh's favorability.

If refused, Ho-oh may leave directly, lowering its favorability.

Facing such a kind-hearted Pokemon that has always believed in humans, D felt he had no reason to refuse.

Moreover, if Ho-oh moved in, it might bring Marshadow, Entei, Suicune, and Lei Gong along in the future.

---

Thinking of the adorable Pokémon in Ho-oh's guard.

Entei with its majestic mane, Lei Gong with sleek fur, and Suicune who can purify water sources.

D gladly agreed.

"No need for help, if possible, I only hope you can protect this tree and the Pokémon living on it."

Like providing unlimited Holy Ash to the item shop in the Explorer Team Guild.

That way, even if a Pokémon accidentally loses life in the future, Holy Ash can be used to revive it directly.

Hearing this, Ho-oh felt quite surprised in its heart.

No wishes, just wants it to protect the Pokémon living here?

It turned its head, gazing at this human with bright eyes.

It sensed that this human had a kind heart, loved peace, loved Pokémon, and cherished everything in this world.

"This person meets the conditions to become the Rainbow Hero," Ho-oh thought secretly.

"Then please take care from now on!" D laughed.

Ho-oh let out a clear cry, happily agreeing.

So far, Ho-oh can be considered officially as a member of the World Tree.

Along with yesterday's Rayquaza, the World Tree now possesses a formidable force.

The Black Rayquaza perched at the top of the tree along with Ho-oh.

Mewtwo living within the tree.

And Giratina residing in the shadow of the World Tree.

Wait!

D suddenly realized something.

Rayquaza and Ho-oh were both attracted by the aura released when the World Tree matured yesterday.

Then the question arises, besides them, could other Legendary Pokémon also have been attracted?

*

At the same time, in the Hoenn Region, inside the Weather Institute.

Even though it was already nighttime, the researchers continued tracking Groudon and Kyogre.

The satellite map showed that the icons representing those two Super Ancient Pokémon were moving at different speeds.

Groudon was still over the sea between the Hoenn Region and Unova Region.

While Kyogre was moving rapidly past the Unova Region, continuing forward!

This scene immediately overturned the researchers' daytime speculations.

"What is going on?! Weren't their targets the Unova Region?"

"No... Based on Kyogre's direction, it's swiftly heading towards the Paldea Region!"

The Paldea Region... which was on the other side of the planet!

What on earth could there be that drives this Super Ancient Pokémon to move tirelessly towards that destination?

After a brief silence.

A senior officer ordered: "Notify the Paldea League immediately!"

However, before the assistant could dial the Paldea League's number, a researcher in front shouted in alarm.

"Kyogre has disappeared!"

The attention of everyone around was immediately caught.

"What's going on?!"

"I don't know! Satellite monitoring shows that Kyogre seemed to leave the sea, flew into the air, and then vanished into thin air!"

"How could this happen..."

Someone murmured in disbelief.

Could it be that Kyogre had Psychic abilities and could use Teleport to go elsewhere?

*

At the World Tree, as D was talking with Ho-oh about how it sensed the World Tree's aura, D suddenly saw Mewtwo's eyes reveal a serious expression.

It sharply raised its head, looking forward.

The next second, D and Ho-oh saw a large Pokémon, resembling a whale, burst through distorted space and rushed in from outside.

Kyogre opened its gaping mouth, letting out an excited hum.

It looked intently at the giant tree before it.

This was the place, it hadn't sensed wrong!

With such dense Natural Energy, if it slept here long enough, it wouldn't take long for it to undergo Primal Reversion and return to its original form.

By then, Groudon would no longer be its match!

Just as Kyogre was preparing to find a place to settle down, a few figures suddenly flew out in front of it.

The Sky Dominator Rayquaza with its black dragon-shaped body,

A noble and elegant bird-type Pokémon with feathers as magnificent as a rainbow,

An anthropomorphic Pokémon with a large tail, surrounded by a terrifying spiritual power,

And a Pokémon with a crimson body, robust build, and wild gaze.

These four suddenly appeared Pokémon blocked Kyogre's path.

Kyogre was dumbfounded, a large question mark appeared above its enormous head.

Huh?

But soon, Kyogre saw a familiar figure on one of the Pokémon.

It was the human who suddenly intervened during its fight with Groudon!

How, how could this be?!

Kyogre was astonished.

It had no way to take on four at once!

Looking at so many Pokémon before it, confronting them would probably end badly.

What should it do?

Kyogre's mind raced rapidly.

At that moment, it heard the human opposite speak.

"You want to live on this tree too?"

Kyogre didn't know what to say, looking at Rayquaza and the other Pokémon opposite, it could only honestly admit: "Yes."

"You can stay," D said.

If Kyogre also moved to the World Tree, it could be placed on the same layer as the Dragonites, and then transform the sea around Kyogre into a Primordial Sea, creating a Water Type Pokémon paradise.

---

Kyogre was completely astonished upon hearing this. Could there really be such a good thing? Moving in without having to fight other Pokémon?

"But I have a few conditions. If you're willing to agree, Rayquaza and I will let you join the World Tree," said D.

Kyogre immediately looked over: "What conditions?"

D: "First, you cannot harm the other Pokémon on the World Tree."

Kyogre was not a bad guy; it was the creator of the ocean, and it always had a very cordial attitude towards the Pokémon residing in the sea it created.

So far, it had never harmed other Pokémon.

"Alright," Kyogre agreed.

D paused for a moment, then suddenly asked a question: "Can you sense Groudon's location?"

Kyogre: "...It's on the way here."

Upon hearing this, D secretly thought, of course.

If Groudon could also move into the World Tree, it could then serve as the center to build an ecosystem suitable for Ground, Rock, and Fire Type Pokémon to inhabit.

But the problem lies here.

Groudon and Kyogre are eternal rivals, and they fight upon meeting. At that time, the Pokémon on the World Tree would probably be disturbed beyond endurance.

Thus, the second condition is also the most important one. Only if both Groudon and Kyogre agree, would D allow them to move in.

"Second, if Groudon later wants to settle on the World Tree, you may not fight."

D looked towards Kyogre opposite him, speaking word by word:

"I will ask Rayquaza and Mewtwo to supervise you. If you fight, you will be expelled and never allowed to come to the World Tree again."

Hearing this, Kyogre immediately fell silent.

But looking at the World Tree filled with Natural Energy, it suppressed its instinct, firmly saying:

"Alright! As long as that guy doesn't come looking for trouble!"

"Fine, then there's no problem," D nodded.

He asked Mewtwo to first take Kyogre to the part of the sea where Dragonite lived to familiarize itself with the environment, while he took out his phone to check where Groudon had gone.

Meanwhile, in Unova Region, Castelia City port.

Trainers from this city gathered at the dock, excitedly and curiously looking out towards the distant sea.

"Hey, have you heard? The Legendary Pokémon Groudon from Hoenn Region is moving towards our Unova Region!"

"Wow! This is truly shocking! That's a Legendary Pokémon, I've never seen a Legendary Pokémon with my own eyes in my lifetime!"

"Is it almost here?"

"The news said soon!"

Just then, a gigantic figure slowly walked over from the distant sea surface.

"Here it comes, here it comes!"

"Wow! It really is Groudon!"

Some began to worry: "It's not here to attack the Unova Region, is it?"

At that moment, the sound of a police siren came from Castelia City port.

Local police officer Miss Jenny rushed in, anxiously dispersing the crowd: "Everyone, leave here quickly! It's dangerous here!"

A group of police officers began to put up the police line.

Unova Region Champion Alder and several Elites also arrived here.

They folded their arms, looking rather surprised at the distant figure.

"Is that guy Groudon?"

"Prepare for battle!" Alder's expression was serious.

Looking at the information revealed, the power of this Legendary Pokémon is not something ordinary Pokémon can compare to!

The group immediately started making preparations.

Time ticked by, Groudon's figure moving from afar to near, gradually passing by in front of them.

However, surprisingly, Groudon did not attack them but bypassed Castelia Port, heading straight ahead!

Alder and the others were completely stunned.

"Its target isn't the Unova Region?"

"It doesn't appear to be."

"Where exactly is it going?"

Alder didn't know the answer, but seeing that Groudon wasn't coming to attack Unova Region but bypassing it, he felt very relieved.

"We should be glad Groudon didn't come ashore! If the same thing that happened in Hoenn were to happen again, it would be terrible!"

Hearing this, the rest pondered and found it to be true.

The crowd watched as Groudon's figure slowly left.

Groudon continued walking forward.

It walked from sunrise to sunset, and then from sunset to sunrise.

Along the way, some bold reporters and trainers tracked Groudon's journey in real-time.

They uploaded all the photos and videos online.

In an instant, the whole network was astounded.

Where exactly is Groudon headed?

This question lingered in people's minds, and more and more people started paying attention to this event.

Even Professor Oak was attracted, flying overhead in a helicopter to closely observe this Legendary Pokémon.

Thus, people saw Groudon walking through one region after another.

As time slowly passed, just when people started feeling a bit tired, Groudon stopped its journey of walking through the whole world.

At that moment, netizens worldwide were in an uproar.

They watched the broadcast together.

On the screen, Groudon suddenly halted.

It stood in the boundless sea, raised its head, gazed at the sky, letting the surrounding waves crash against the earth beneath its feet, Groudon remained motionless, only posed as if gazing at the sky.

People followed Groudon's gaze, but aside from the boundless blue sky and white clouds, they saw nothing else.

"What is Groudon looking at?"

"Is it contemplating life?"

"Wow! Never thought Groudon is actually a philosopher!"

However, some sharp-eyed individuals noticed Groudon occasionally showed a look of anxiety and helplessness.

It seemed to be facing an unsolvable issue.

At last, Groudon suddenly began to rage.

It unleashed fury relentlessly, continuously attacking the surrounding filming planes, until it drove away or shot down all the planes before it stopped.

---

---

---

---

# 476: Chapter 473: Groudon Joins the Chat Group!

The Groudon event is still fermenting.

More and more people are being drawn to it.

They arrived in flying Pokémon or private planes, hovering mid-air, observing the Groudon over the distant sea with telescopes.

In the meantime, some daring Trainers attempted to fly up and form teams to challenge Groudon, aiming to defeat and capture it.

Not only did they fail, but they also provoked Groudon into a state of rage, knocking them all upside down.

"It's truly magnificent!"

In a helicopter, Professor Oak from the Kanto Region murmured, holding a telescope and gazing at the rare Legendary Pokémon in the distance.

"What exactly did Groudon come to the Paldea Region for, even spending so much time traveling from the Hoenn Region?"

This question lingered in Professor Oak's mind.

Is it for migration, or is there something here attracting Groudon?

No one knows the answer to this question.

The situation is momentarily at a stalemate.

Elsewhere on the ocean, led by Geeta, Paldea's Champion team was looking speechlessly at the Earth God, Groudon.

At this moment, Groudon was just a few kilometers away from the Paldea Region.

Geeta recalled the epic battle between Groudon and Kyogre a few days ago, feeling a bit worried.

If Groudon lands in the Paldea Region, what impact will it have on the once peaceful Paldea Region? Geeta didn't dare to imagine!

Capturing it is impossible, Geeta just hopes this Pokémon can swiftly leave here and return to where it should be.

In the pasture.

D watched Groudon, now a viral sensation, with mixed feelings.

For some reason, seeing Groudon standing alone on the sea looking up at the sky, he always felt it carried a sense of pitiful grace.

Not only that, but the Legendary Beast chat group was also talking about Groudon.

[Giratina: Could this guy also be here for the World Tree?]

[Mewtwo: There's a 90% possibility.]

[Giratina: Then why hasn't it come up yet? Just standing there looking?]

[Rayquaza: Probably because it can't fly?]

D: "..."

Stop this awful bullying!

In the end, D couldn't stand it anymore.

He traversed through Giratina's Reversed World, going directly from the pasture to the Explorer Team Guild area.

This was an entrance Giratina opened a few days ago to facilitate D's travels between the World Tree and pasture.

By passing through a small segment of Reversed Space, he could directly reach the World Tree, no longer needing to ride a flying Pokémon to get there.

D found Mewtwo and asked it to use Teleport to bring Groudon over.

Mewtwo had seen Groudon's feats in the chat group.

Upon hearing D's request, it fell silent for a moment, then nodded in agreement.

A powerful psychic energy radiated from Mewtwo, and the next second, Groudon, who had been on the sea, was no longer in sight and instead appeared at the World Tree.

Meanwhile, on the sea, everyone watching exclaimed in surprise.

"It's gone?!"

"Where did Groudon go?"

Confused people flew around the sea and dived underwater to search.

Despite searching for a long time, there was no sight of Groudon.

In the end, no one could find an answer.

The onlookers could only regretfully disperse, and the event became an unsolved mystery, enlisted by some Pokemon scholars as one of the world's top ten phenomena, known historically as the "Groudon Disappearance Mystery."

In the Hoenn Region, at the Weather Institute.

Watching the tiny red dot representing Groudon vanish from the sea surface, the researchers were dumbfounded and astonished.

"V-Vanished?"

"Kyogre also disappeared in that same location!"

Even more bizarrely, they could no longer track the locations of those two Super Ancient Pokémon!

"What exactly happened?"

"Did they travel through time and space?"

After the shock, a researcher showed a joyful expression.

"In that case, will those two Pokémon no longer fight in the future?"

"We in the Hoenn Region won't have to worry about encountering that previous problem again!"

"That's great!"

*

Atop the World Tree.

The sudden change startled Groudon.

Its body tensed immediately, earthy energy surging all over, ready to unleash its signature move, Precipice Blades, anytime.

However, right after, when Groudon sensed the dense natural aura surrounding it, it displayed an expression of disbelief.

This is... that tree!

It finally made it up here!

No, what exactly happened?

Groudon immediately turned its gaze towards the human and several Pokémon in front of it.

It even spotted Rayquaza among them.

This group, whose imposing presence was no less than its own, surrounded it entirely.

Not far away, some ordinary Pokémon were curiously glancing over.

Groudon: "..."

"Hello, Groudon." D introduced with a smile, "This isn't our first meeting, right? I'm D, and welcome to the World Tree."

Groudon opened its mouth slightly, looking in disbelief at the human.

Its intuition told it that the human before it was likely the master of this place!

Moreover, seeing the behavior of these surrounding Pokémon, it was clear they were all centered around him!

---

Seeing Groudon, that big lummox, dazing off there, D shook his head helplessly and continued:

"Then I'll keep it short. I know why you're here; you've been attracted by the natural energy of this tree. I can let you stay here, but you have to agree to a few conditions."

"What conditions?" Groudon's rough, deep voice finally echoed in D's mind.

D: "First, you must not harm the other Pokemon on the World Tree. Second, you must not fight with Kyogre here. Kyogre has already agreed that as long as you don't provoke it, it won't provoke you either."

"If you violate either of these two points, then Rayquaza and Mewtwo will drive you out of the World Tree."

Compared to Kyogre, expelling Groudon is much simpler.

As long as Mewtwo uses Teleport to throw it off, with its own strength, it won't be able to climb back up.

Indeed, there are benefits to not being able to fly...

Hearing this, Groudon fell silent.

Compared to territory, strength is more important. As long as it works hard to absorb natural energy and becomes stronger, Kyogre will no longer dare to compete with it for territory.

With this thought in mind, Groudon decisively agreed.

"Alright!"

D nodded and said, "Then there's no problem."

This time, he personally led Groudon to the third layer, below the Sea Domain where Kyogre resides.

This is an unused space, currently without any Pokemon living there.

Because of this, D intends to have Groudon transform it into an area suitable for Rock, Ground, and Fire Type Pokemon to live in.

He told this plan to Groudon.

Groudon had no objections; it was planning to create a lava pool for sleep anyway.

Therefore, Groudon directly left D behind and started working enthusiastically.

First, using Earth energy to create a small volcano, then filling it with magma from its body.

In the blink of an eye, a lava pool was born.

After doing this, Groudon didn't stop to rest.

Standing on the volcano, its entire body surging with Earth Vein energy.

In an instant, a rumbling sound echoed through the space.

The ground began to tremble, rocks started to move.

Soon, a hot valley region was formed.

Centering around the volcano where Groudon stands, the air temperature gradually decreases with distance, and at the outer ring, it has already become normal.

There, Groudon created valleys, caves, and jagged rocks, perfectly suitable for Pokemon like Onix and Tyranitar with Ground and Rock Types to live in.

And around the volcano, even steaming fountains appeared.

That hot area is suitable for Fire Type Pokemon like Charizard and Vulpix to live in.

With this, the construction of this layer is complete!

D also gathered a group of Water Type Pokemon to use Rain Dance in the dry valley on the outskirts.

Drizzling rain gradually gathered into a winding stream.

Then, D bought Berry Seedlings and planted several Berry Gardens by the river, asking Calyrex to help ripen them.

In the blink of an eye, the branches were laden with heavy Berries, the air filled with the joy of harvest.

Of course, these aren't for Groudon but for the Pokemon that will settle here in the future.

Groudon stood on the far volcano, watching D busying himself.

Groudon was quite surprised by the human's actions.

Was planting those fruit trees for it to eat?

After all, besides it, there are no other Pokemon here.

But as the Earth God, it doesn't need to eat, surviving by absorbing natural energy.

At this point, Groudon saw the human leaving with the group of Water Type Pokemon.

Groudon was prepared to return to the lava for a good rest.

The previous great battle had exhausted a lot of its energy, plus traveling such a long distance, it was now completely drained, needing a period of sleep to recover.

With this thought in mind, Groudon turned and headed into the volcano.

However, it wasn't long before it saw the human returning.

This time, the human held a strange machine in hand.

"This is a Rotom Phone, Rayquaza, and Kyogre both have one, and this one will be yours from now on!"

D carefully explained the functions of the Rotom Phone to Groudon.

Upon hearing that Rayquaza and Kyogre both have one, Groudon immediately perked up, its competitive spirit not allowing it to fall behind.

It had to have one too!

"...Alright, as it stands now. If there's anything you don't understand, you can ask the Rotom inside the phone, but be careful not to drop it into magma," D said.

Seeing that Groudon seemed to have accepted the existence of the Rotom Phone, D took out his own phone and added it to the group chat.

Now, the chat group already had six Legendary Pokemon, and with Groudon, there were seven.

The next second, the upper-layer Kyogre, Rayquaza on the treetop, Ho-oh perched on a branch, Giratina in the shadow of the tree, Mewtwo by the farmland, and Calyrex all received a notification.

---

[Groudon has joined the chat group!]

The friendly Pokemon led by Calyrex welcomed Groudon.

Kyogre didn't respond, and D guessed it must be asleep right now.

After all, the previous battle had drained a lot of its energy.

If not for the World Tree, both it and Groudon would probably have to sleep for a thousand years before they could wake up again.

Groudon isn't yet familiar with the functions of the Rotom Phone, so it didn't reply.

After explaining everything, D finally turned and left this place.

And after he left, Groudon also fell into a fatigued sleep.

However, this time was different from before; in this space filled with natural energy, it wouldn't take long for Groudon to recover.

*

Five minutes later, D stood on Sky Island with Koraidon, looking up at the towering giant tree in front of him.

Thus, the work at the World Tree was considered completely finished!

Next, they just needed to slowly have the Heroic Expedition Team invite the Pokemon friends met during their journey to join the World Tree.

It's quite amazing to think about; not long ago, he was still watering the World Tree with Skiddo, lying under the tree enjoying the breeze.

Unbeknownst to him, the sapling had already grown so large.

Not only that, but it had also attracted so many Legendary Pokemon.

Including Koraidon, the number of Legendary Pokemon had reached eight.

D suddenly felt that the World Tree could be transformed into a Legendary Beast Battle Tower.

First floor Koraidon, second floor Mewtwo, third floor Giratina, fourth floor Groudon, fifth floor Kyogre, sixth floor Calyrex.

And perched at the top of the tree were Ho-oh and Rayquaza.

With this lineup, even if Team Rainbow Rocket came, there would be nothing to fear.

The sun gradually disappeared beyond the horizon, leaving a trace of dusk in the sky, the evening breeze gently blowing, and the leaves above lightly swaying.

The breeze caressed his hair; D exhaled slowly, feeling utterly refreshed.

It was truly a happy day!

Now, it was time for them to go home!

When D and Koraidon returned to the farm, the sky had already begun to darken.

The Pokemon who had been playing outside all day also returned home, lazily sprawling on the carpet, waiting for D to make dinner for them.

Leaving the World Tree and bidding farewell to those Legendary Pokemon, D reverted to being a farmer working the land in the countryside.

Upon returning home, he playfully teased a few Pokemon before starting to prepare dinner for them.

The atmosphere was filled with warmth, the laughter of the Pokemon, the sound of D cooking—it seemed like just another ordinary day.

Calyrex greatly enjoyed these moments, so every time at this hour, it would come from the Glass Greenhouse to the living room, sit on the sofa, play on its phone, and listen to D cooking.

D also noticed the sudden teleport of Calyrex; he suddenly remembered something and asked while chopping vegetables:

"By the way, Calyrex, the World Tree has stabilized, don't you want to live there?"

Calyrex gently shook its head: "No, compared to there, I want to stay here and live with you all."

D understood; Calyrex thought the farm was more lively!

He smiled and nodded: "Alright, then please take care going forward!"

Night enveloped the earth, the bright moonlight casting its glow over the farm.

Zygarde's core finally reached its destination.

It stood on a nondescript stone, observing this unfamiliar area and the household nestled in the night.

---

---

---

---

# 477: Chapter 474: The First SSS-Rank Cuisine—Legendary Breakthrough for Gogoat!

Accompanied by the ascension of the mature World Tree, the pasture returned to its usual tranquility.

Because Giratina opened the way between the pasture and the World Tree, Eevees still run from the Reverse World to play on the World Tree.

The Gogoats occasionally go there too, to guide the Skiddo that had been rescued by Mewtwo.

Meanwhile, D began a leisurely life of sitting in the sunset, sipping tea and listening to music as the evening breeze blew from the wilds.

That afternoon, Mayor Thomas came to the pasture to chat with him about the fireworks festival held at the end of the month.

"D, the fireworks festival is going to be held at the end of this month. Don't forget to bring your Pokemon to join in!"

The white-haired old man said with a big smile:

"The town has bought many rare fireworks and has invited numerous shops to set up stalls. Compared to before, this year's fireworks festival is especially grand and spectacular!"

"Of course I'll be there." D nodded with a smile.

"Then it's settled!" Mayor Thomas happily put on his hat, stood up, and prepared to leave the pasture: "The town will also prepare free food and drinks for everyone to enjoy while watching the fireworks. Ah, just thinking about it is exciting!"

D chuckled; the fireworks festival is a traditional festival in the Paldea region, passed down from a long time ago.

This event, held on the last day of summer where people and Pokemon gather on the seaside beach to watch the beautiful fireworks bloom in the night sky, is indeed alluring.

But then again, is Lono also coming to Porto Marinada Town for the fireworks festival?

"Then I'll take my leave first, see you at the fireworks festival later!" Mayor Thomas said goodbye to D with a smile.

D escorted the old man to the door, watching his figure gradually disappear before returning to the pasture.

On the coffee table in the living room were some cookies and small snacks, personally made by Mayor Thomas's wife. He always brought some as gifts when he came by.

It's said those were Cyclizar's favorite snacks in the past.

Cyclizar indeed loved them, and even Koraidon seemed to find the taste pleasant.

The two Pokemon sat on the floor munching away, and before long, they had finished all the cookies.

D: "..."

He returned to the bedroom and sat at his desk to write a letter.

The letter said that these vegetables were freshly picked from the pasture, and there were too many at home to finish, so he was sending some as a gift.

Then, D gathered some fresh fruits and vegetables into a bag, hung it on Cyclizar, and let it deliver them to Mayor Thomas in town as a gesture of goodwill.

"Gao~"

Cyclizar gladly accepted the task and dashed out of the pasture with the bag.

Nearing noon, D prepared to go to the fields to pick some vegetables for lunch.

But as soon as he stepped out, he saw three Gogoats jumping out from the Reverse World.

And in their mouths, they each held three leaves emitting a faint green glow.

Seeing this, D was slightly taken aback.

The Gogoats ran up to him and placed the leaves down.

D, amazed, asked: "Did the World Tree give these to you?"

"Baa!"

The Gogoats happily nodded.

D quickly understood.

The Gogoats intended for him to use these three leaves to make a dish for them!

D recalled a dessert he once made using the leaves of the World Tree.

With this thought, he turned around, took the leaves home, and pulled out his cooking manual to look up information on the World Tree's leaves.

This was a habit D had developed over the years. Each time he developed a new dish, he would record the necessary ingredients, preparation methods, and effects.

Soon, he found the relevant information.

[Leaf Cake (Incomplete Version) (S-): A cake made by mixing the leaves of the World Tree with eggs, MooMoo Fresh Milk, and other ingredients. Take a moment to listen, and you'll feel the breath of nature.

Cooking Effect: Breath of Nature.

1. Permanently increases the power of Grass Type moves by 20%, effect only for Grass Type Pokemon, cannot stack.

2. After Grass Type Pokemon consume it, they can respond to the rhythm of nature, slightly shortening crop growth time when using Grass Energy in cultivation, only effective for Grass Type Pokemon, effect cannot stack.

Additional Effect: Master of the World Tree, you have received the World Tree's recognition. As a being nurturing the World Tree's Gogoats, you can now access all areas within the World Tree, this effect only applies to D.

Evaluation: Listen to the breath of nature! Maybe once the World Tree matures, dishes made from its leaves will possess even better effects.]

"Sure enough!" D couldn't help but mumble.

This cake, made from the leaves of an immature World Tree, already has such an effect.

Now, if made from mature leaves, who knows what the original effects might turn into, he didn't dare to imagine!

"Now, I'll only know if I try."

With that thought, D began to make the Leaf Cake.

First, he ground the World Tree's leaves into a paste with a mixer, then added eggs, milk, and other ingredients.

While pouring in the milk, D had a sudden thought—what if he tried using Skiddo's goat milk instead?

After all, in the current pasture, the quality of goat milk produced by Skiddo far surpasses that of the MooMoo Milk from Miltank.

---

Thinking of this, D immediately changed the ingredients, replacing the milk with Life Sheep Milk.

After mixing everything evenly, he poured it into a mold to set, and finally put it in the oven to bake.

Once it was almost done baking, he took the cake base out of the oven and spread fresh cream produced by Alcremie on top.

With that, a fresh Leaf Cake was completed.

However, unlike last time, this Leaf Cake was actually glowing!

D looked at the cake emitting a bright green light with surprise, and the next second, detailed information about the cake appeared in his mind.

[Leaf Cake (SSS): A cake made by mixing Tree of Beginning Leaf with eggs, Life Sheep Milk, and other ingredients, visibly filled with the breath of nature.

Cooking Effect: Master of Nature,

1. Permanently increases the power of Grass Type Moves by 100% (Gogoat only).

Other Grass Type Pokemon only get 50% of the effect, not stackable.

2. After eating, Gogoat can respond to the rhythm of nature, allowing crops within a certain range to mature within five seconds using Grass Energy cultivation.

Other Grass Type Pokemon after consumption can significantly shorten crop growth time, only effective for Grass Type Pokemon.

3. Master of the World Tree, this effect only applies to D.

4. Chosen by Nature, after consumption, gains special ability "Power of Viridian", inheritable, this effect only applies to D.

Power of Viridian: Communicate with Pokemon hearts, significantly enhance affinity with Pokemon, harness nature's energy to heal Pokemon injuries.

Evaluation: Perfect effect, unique culinary creation in the world!]

D: "!!!"

Whoa! An SSS-level dish appeared, and its effects are so exaggerated?!

D was completely shocked.

Permanently enhancing Gogoat's Grass Type Moves by 100%, and Gogoat gains the same power as Calyrex to instantly mature crops.

Based on Gogoat's current control over Grass Type Energy, D could only think of two words: invincible!

As for the Power of Viridian...

He understands, but what does "inheritable" mean?

Is it inheriting moves?

However, he is still single, unable to trigger this effect.

After recovering from the shock, D immediately took the sliced Leaf Cake to find Gogoat.

Smelling the enticing aroma, Gogoat immediately cheerfully lowered its head, bleating as it ate the Leaf Cake.

During this time, D also ate a piece.

And when all the Leaf Cake was consumed, in an instant, Gogoat's body suddenly erupted with an extremely dense Grass Energy.

Through the newly acquired Power of Viridian perception, D could clearly see Gogoat undergoing transformation!

It wasn't just because of the Leaf Cake, but also because of the long-term nourishment from the World Tree, transforming its essential life nature.

In other words, Gogoat is no longer an ordinary Pokemon.

Its life level and energy tier have reached the level of a "Legendary Pokemon"!

Realizing this, D couldn't help being utterly amazed.

"The god creation has succeeded!"

A moment later, the glow on Gogoat's body gathered and retracted.

They looked at D in front of them with excitement.

D finally came back to his senses, slowly speaking: "Congratulations, Gogoat."

"Baa!" Gogoat was very excited.

"Come, let me see your transformation!" After recovering from the shock, D began to check the growth of the three Gogoats.
