He really wanted to know what Gogoat had become now.

Thus, the man and three goats turned towards the battle area of the ranch.

The three Gogoats lined up, eagerly awaiting D's instructions.

"Come, let me see the changes in Vine Whip!" D said.

"Baa!"

One of the Gogoats nodded, then extended two dark green Vine Whips from its body, sweeping around like a serpent.

Watching the scene, D silently nodded, murmuring to himself:

"As expected, Vine Whip has changed too, with a deeper color, probably due to the dense Grass Type Energy?"

"Farfetch'd, come here!" D turned around and called over Farfetch'd, who was practicing swordplay in the wooden dummy area.

"Quack?" Farfetch'd approached and asked D what was going on.

D pointed to the Vine Whip on Gogoat and said to it, "Try attacking that Vine Whip with your leek."

"Quack..."

Upon hearing this request, Farfetch'd was a bit confused but complied obediently.

A flash of white light swept by, and Farfetch'd shot out like an arrow, swinging its leek that had transformed into a great sword, with powerful momentum toward the Vine Whip.

But the Vine Whip was not cut through.

Seeing this, Farfetch'd was shocked: "?! "

"As expected, the power and toughness have also increased." D muttered, "Farfetch'd, come back!"

Then, he said to Gogoat, "Next, try Leaf Blade."

Leaf Blade launched an attack on the opponent, which could be considered Gogoat's second-best move.

It nodded, then slightly lowered its body, as Grass Energy surged in its mouth, forming a large grass sword.

"Attack that wooden dummy over there and see." D said.

Gogoat turned around upon hearing and rushed forward with the Leaf Blade in its mouth.

With a booming sound, the wooden dummy was instantly smashed to pieces.

"Quack?!" Farfetch'd's eyes widened; that move had an immense power!

"Yes, the power of Leaf Blade has also increased." D silently nodded.

Once Gogoat returned, he spoke again, "Now then, use your signature Grassy Terrain!"

"Baaa!"

With the command given, the next moment, a dense green light erupted from Gogoat's body.

The energy transformed into a vibrant field, enveloping the entire pasture.

Endless grass gently swayed within, while the green light surrounded the Pokemon, appearing almost as an aura.

No, it wasn't just the Pokemon; even the air carried traces of that green aura!

D looked up and was astonished to see the Grassy Terrain covering not only the pasture but even the skies above!

A faint "aura" surged from Gogoat's body.

Witnessing this, two words immediately surfaced in D's mind.

Aura!

Indeed, it was no longer just a terrain or domain but had transformed into an aura-like presence.

The Leaf Cake had elevated Gogoat's Grassy Terrain into an aura!

And according to D's knowledge, only two Pokemon in the world currently possessed an aura.

One is the Life God Xerneas, which inherently has a Fairy Aura that boosts all Fairy Type Pokemon's move power automatically on appearance.

The other is the God of Destruction Yveltal, which inherently has a Dark Aura that boosts all Dark Type Pokemon's move power automatically on appearance.

And now...

Could it be that an aura was also born from Gogoat's body?!

Upon realizing this, D couldn't help but show an excited expression.

From the current situation, Gogoat's Grass Aura far surpasses Xerneas's Fairy Aura and Yveltal's Dark Aura.

Because Gogoat's Grass Aura not only enhances the power of Grass Type Pokemon's moves but also facilitates energy healing!

And Gogoat's ability [Grass Pelt] can further enhance their defense.

A truly versatile Pokemon that can fight, heal, and tank.

If paired with Calyrex or Ogerpon as teammates, D couldn't even imagine what heights this combination could reach!

Looking at the three Gogoat before him, D suddenly felt he might become the Grass Type Elite now.

Maybe he could learn from Lance and fill his team with a bunch of Dragonite, like stuffing his team with three Gogoat.

Adding Ogerpon, Calyrex, and Arboliva from the World Tree would surely form a Grass Type Elite team.

However, more than that, D wondered how the current Gogoat would compare in strength to Leon's Charizard.

The experience of being swept by Leon's Charizard three times in a row was still vivid in his memory, but now Gogoat had grown.

The energy feedback from the World Tree allowed their life levels to transform, with Leaf Cake significantly elevating their move power, and the original Grassy Terrain evolving into a Grass Aura...

Thinking of this, D couldn't help but take out his Rotom Phone and sent a message to Leon far away in the Galar Region.

[Wing King: When are you coming to Paldea? My Pokemon has grown again, and I'd like to have a match with you.]

# 478: Chapter 475: Farfetch'd Hits Gold, the Gambler's Springtime!

Unfortunately, Leon didn't come over.

Ever since he learned the truth about the Galar Region from Calyrex last time, Leon began to secretly investigate Eternatus in the Galar Region.

In addition to regularly accepting Trainer challenges and participating in various exhibition matches, Leon is almost overwhelmed with work now.

Seeing this, D could only reply, "Alright, maybe we can battle next time when there's a chance!"

Leon quickly replied.

[Leon: Don't worry! Once everything here in the Galar Region is settled, I'll visit your ranch, and we'll have a three-day, three-night battle!]

[King Wing: ...]

After the chat ended, D began to ask Leon about the main business at hand.

[King Wing: Have you been to the Slumbering Weald?]

Last time, Calyrex told Leon he could go to the Slumbering Weald to find Zacian and Zamazenta, seeking their Helping Hand. D wonders if Leon has found them yet.

[Leon: I've been there. I successfully met Zacian and Zamazenta, and told them what Calyrex had shared with me. But those two Pokemon told me it's not the time yet and asked me to come back later.]

Hmm? Not the time yet?

Looking at the reply above, D was slightly stunned.

He immediately turned and told Calyrex about this.

After a brief contemplation, Calyrex explained:

"Perhaps it's because the person destined to restore the glory of the corroded Sword and Shield hasn't emerged yet."

"With just the current power of Zacian and Zamazenta, they cannot defeat the Darkest Day."

D instantly understood; isn't that about the protagonist of Sword and Shield and her rival?

That would be Gloria and Leon's younger brother, Hop.

However, Hop is just five or six years old now, still a little brat...

Zacian and Zamazenta's weapons had been corroded by time; it takes their courage and faith to restore their glory.

What surprised D was, did Leon truly not possess the power to reinvigorate the corroded Sword and Shield?

Or is such an opportunity exclusive to the protagonist?

Just as D was feeling surprised, Leon initiated a video call.

D pressed the button to accept.

The next second, Leon's sunny, handsome face popped up on the screen.

After a brief greeting, Leon began discussing another matter.

"By the way, when I was in the Slumbering Weald last time, Zacian and Zamazenta specifically asked me about Calyrex's current whereabouts and situation."

"Hmm?" D was somewhat surprised, "And then?"

Leon scratched his head, "I told them the location of your ranch and showed them a photo Calyrex posted online."

The photo showed Calyrex sitting elegantly in a garden enjoying afternoon tea.

"After that, Zacian and Zamazenta seemed relieved, and after expressing their thanks, they disappeared into the depths of the Slumbering Weald," Leon said.

D nodded, "They might just be worried about Calyrex! After all, Calyrex wasn't in a great situation before. Now that they see Calyrex has recovered, Zacian and Zamazenta can rest easy."

"Indeed!" Leon also felt deeply moved.

Later, he specifically went to the Crown Tundra, and in Freezington Village, he investigated the legends of Calyrex, fully understanding Calyrex's plight back then.

If it wasn't for D, this great King Pokemon might still be wandering alone across the vast tundra.

And the deeds it accomplished might have been buried beneath the tundra forever, never to be known.

The Galar Royal Family would continue to proclaim their stolen achievements across the Galar Region.

At this moment, a knock sounded behind Leon.

D suddenly realized that Leon was seemingly backstage at some conference.

Upon hearing the sound, Leon smiled broadly and then bid farewell to D:

"Enough said, I'm going to battle a Pokemon challenger now. Let's chat again when there's time!"

D responded with a good-bye.

After the call ended, D shared the remaining Leaf Cake with the other Grass Type Pokemon in the ranch.

Furthermore, he asked Eevee to bring some to the Arboliva on the World Tree.

Although the Leaf Cake wouldn't make Arboliva as strong as Gogoat, it would certainly bring them a decent boost.

D turned back to his room to cook.

Meanwhile, Farfetch'd stared determinately at the fractured wooden dummy that Gogoat had smashed with the Grass Sacred Sword.

Moments later, the fire of determination reignited within its heart.

*

"What? You plan to continue eating the Berry Platter to comprehend powerful Moves?"

In the kitchen, while cooking, D sensed Farfetch'd's intention and showed a surprised look.

"Quack!" Farfetch'd nodded its head.

Seeing its demeanor, D immediately understood.

Farfetch'd had just witnessed the powerful sword technique displayed by Gogoat and wanted it for itself!

"Alright then!" D agreed readily, since Farfetch'd no longer worries about forgetting Moves.

After countless bouts of forgetting and relearning, the method of using those Moves has long been deeply etched into its heart.

Even if the brain forgets, the body remembers the actions, quickly allowing it to comprehend them once more.

With that in mind, D promptly prepared five Berry Platters for Farfetch'd to try its luck with five consecutive draws.

He also prepared Pokemon digestive pills to help Farfetch'd digest all the food it consumed.

Farfetch'd, brimming with fighting spirit, carried the five Berry Platters out the door.

Meanwhile, D stayed in the kitchen to continue preparing lunch.

In the open space in the ranch.

With a determined gaze, Farfetch'd looked at the five Berry Platters before him.

The first one brought a familiar feeling; Farfetch'd felt he had forgotten Iaido Slash.

About such matters, Farfetch'd remained calm, neither panicked nor dissatisfied. He simply picked up the next Berry Platter.

From the second, Farfetch'd forgot Fury Cutter.

From the third, Farfetch'd comprehended meditation.

From the fourth, Farfetch'd learned Feather Dance.

The fifth.

As the last Berry Platter went down, Farfetch'd suddenly opened his eyes, feeling an unprecedented surge of power.

In the next instant, a layer of blue light appeared in his determined eyes.

A powerful psychic power flowed from Farfetch'd's body into the giant leek in his hand.

The leek began to glow.

"Quack!"

His right wing swung forward sharply, the huge glowing leek sweeping forward, as an invisible Psycho Cut followed, slicing the grass ahead perfectly flat.

"Quack?!"

At the end of the move, Farfetch'd looked at his wing in surprise.

His brain told him the move was called Psyblade, a Psychic Type move!

Realizing the significance of the move, Farfetch'd quickly found D and dragged him outside to show off the newly learned move.

Looking at the impeccably trimmed grass, D immediately recognized Farfetch'd's move.

It was Psyblade, the signature move of the Paradox Species Iron Leaves of the Legendary Pokemon Virizion!

[Psyblade: A Psychic Type move that slashes opponents with invisible blades. When in Electric Terrain, the move's power is enhanced.]

While not as potent as Palkia's Spacial Rend, it's still considered a powerful move.

Especially when the psychic power causes the leek to glow, transforming it into a lightsaber, which makes it look quite cool!

"That's Psyblade, the signature move of Iron Leaves, Virizion's unique Paradox Species. Very impressive." D introduced to Farfetch'd.

Farfetch'd had always relied on melee attacks as his main offensive tactic; now, with a move capable of launching sword qi from afar, it was quite advantageous.

It just so happens to cover Farfetch'd's weaknesses, broadening his attack range.

As expected, upon hearing the word "legendary," Farfetch'd's face immediately radiated excitement.

At this moment, he suddenly felt a sense of vindication.

Thinking about it, he's indeed had a tough journey!

Previously, to master powerful moves, he ate Berry Platters endlessly, turning into a plump duck and nearly ate himself to death.

But now, he finally reaped the rewards of his hardships.

The dawn has come for the gambling duck!

Overwhelmed with excitement, Farfetch'd danced on the spot, laughing "quack quack quack."

D: "..."

Farfetch'd isn't a pure gambling duck!

Normally, at times like this, wouldn't one be discontent with Psyblade being too weak, and instead pursue the stronger Spacial Rend?

After all, Farfetch'd originally came to the ranch in search of the way to cut through waterfalls.

D wanted to remind him, but seeing Farfetch'd so ecstatic, he decided to let it be.

Otherwise, it might end up not mastering Spacial Rend, and even forget the Psyblade. That would be troublesome.

*

Outside the window, the sun burned fiercely, the glaring sunlight baking the earth. The crops in the fields wilted under the heat, and even the cicadas on the mountain behind stopped singing.

After lunch, D lounged lazily at home, enjoying the air conditioning.

Meanwhile, the Pokemon, who weren't bothered by the heat, sat under the eaves leisurely nibbling on watermelon.

Just then, a Trainer with a backpack, sweating profusely, arrived at the ranch.

"Mr. D, I've finally collected enough Badges. Can I challenge the Grass Trial now?"

Under the eaves, a young boy looked excitedly at D, who opened the door for him.

D: "..."

He still remembered this boy, who had come with a Fuecoco in the spring aiming to challenge the Grass Trial.

But he was just a novice, only possessing one Gym Badge, far from meeting the criteria for the trial challenge.

Thus, D decisively rejected him back then.

Hearing he had now collected enough Badges, D felt a bit surprised.

"Come in and rest a bit," he noted the sweat on the boy's forehead.

"Yes!" The boy, very excited, quickly followed D into the living room, "Can I challenge the Grass Trial now?"

D: "..."

Looking at the boy's expectant eyes, D really wanted to say the Gogoat now was not what it used to be.

Achieving legendary status, this Gogoat was not one ordinary Trainer could challenge anymore.

But seeing the boy's enthusiasm and effort for the Grass Trial, D paused for a moment, then had his sister Indeedee pour him a cooling mung bean soup.

The boy thanked him and gulped it down in one go.

Once he finished, D asked, "Where's Fuecoco?"

Upon hearing D inquire about Fuecoco, the boy immediately took out a Poke Ball.

With a flash of white light, a crocodile Pokemon with vibrant red scales appeared in the living room.

---

The Fuecoco from back then has now evolved into a Skeledirge!

Seeing this, D was a bit surprised.

"Grown so fast?"

"That's right! During this time, Skeledirge and I have been training hard, constantly challenging the Gyms!" The boy replied with determination.

D sensed a youthful spirit in him, and he nodded with a smile:

"Alright then, I'll accept your challenge!"

Any Trainer, when faced with such a challenger, would probably agree to a battle invitation.

Hearing this, the boy's eyes lit up: "Great!"

Moments later, the two of them arrived at the battlefield.

The boy sent out his Skeledirge, while D sent out a Gogoat.

He looked at the opposing Trainer and said, "In this period, not only have you grown, but Gogoat has also evolved. Winning won't be that easy!"

"If not once, then twice. If not twice, then three times!" The boy's determination was strong: "Neither Skeledirge nor I will give up!"

"Very well, lots of energy." D smiled slightly: "Then, show me your growth!"

"Whistle!"

The crisp sound of Nemona, the Indeedee, whistling came from the side.

The battle officially started.

"Skeledirge, use Torch Song!" The boy immediately shouted.

The Skeledirge in front opened its large mouth, as if singing, spewing a fierce flame.

Starting with Torch Song?

Watching the approaching flames, D softly said: "Gogoat, Double Team!"

Gogoat's eyes grew resolute, it gazed at the oncoming flames, and its body flickered rapidly, with several identical shadows appearing all around in an instant.

One of the shadows was hit by the flames, turning into a phantasm and disappearing.

"Disappeared?!" The boy immediately reminded: "If we can't find Gogoat's position, then let's put it to sleep, Skeledirge, use Sing!"

The next second, a drowsy song emanated from the spirit flame on Skeledirge's nose.

However, D didn't plan to give it this chance.

"Gogoat, Earthquake!"

"Baa."

Gogoat on the field responded deeply, and the next second, all the Double Team shadows raised their forelimbs and then stomped heavily on the ground.

A powerful shockwave immediately rushed towards Skeledirge from all directions.

[Sing] was interrupted, and the intense seismic wave directly delivered a heavy blow to Skeledirge, the immense force causing it to lose its ability to battle.

An instant KO!

Witnessing this scene, the boy was astonished.

"Lost, lost? So strong!"

D sighed inwardly, it seemed he would have to revise the challenge rules for the Grass Trial again.

"You did very well." D praised: "To grow to this level in just a few months, as a top Trainer would say, you've become a very good Trainer."

The boy was smart, he heard the praise in D's words, but he remained calm.

Because compared to Mr. D, they still had a long way to go.

"I've decided!" The boy's tone became very firm: "I will continue to train with Skeledirge, we'll definitely come back to challenge the Trial again!"

After speaking, the boy withdrew Skeledirge into the Poke Ball and hurriedly ran off after bidding farewell to D.

D: "..." A second Nemona?

Helplessly watching the figure in the distance, D slowly exhaled.

Such small episodes in life were not the first time for D, he was long used to it.

Life goes on, the footsteps of time hurriedly pass by.

In the blink of an eye, it was June 30th in League year 200.

This day was the day of the Fireworks Festival.

---

---

# 479: Chapter 476: Girl, Fireworks, and Pokemon

Early in the morning, D went to buy groceries in Porto Marinada Town and saw some people and Pokémon moving stalls and fireworks toward the beach.

Walking along the street with Cyclizar, D found Porto Marinada Town exceptionally lively today.

Even the shops along the street had banners promoting the fireworks festival.

A diverse group of people gathered by the street, leisurely chatting with their Pokémon.

"Looks like tonight will be really bustling!" D started to feel a bit excited.

Beside him, Cyclizar was also infected by the mood and let out a happy cry: "Gaa-ow!"

After buying groceries, D and his Pokémon returned to the ranch.

D chatted with the Pokémon at the ranch about the fireworks festival.

Upon hearing that another fireworks festival was happening, the Pawmot were all very excited.

Ogerpon recalled the scene from last year's fireworks festival: the sky filled with beautiful fireworks at night was breathtaking!

"Boni!" It was determined to participate again this year!

Cyclizar dashed into the room and quickly returned with a ball in its mouth, placing it in front of D and looking at him expectantly.

"Alright, I get it, I'll play ball with you then!" D said with a smile, patting Cyclizar's head.

Not far away, Koraidon looked disdainful; playing ball seemed so childish!

In conversation, D glanced around but didn't see any sign of the Eevee here at the ranch.

There's no need to guess; those playful little guys have run off to play at the World Tree again.

He took out his phone and made a video call to Mewtwo.

It didn't take long for the little faces of the Eevee to appear on the screen.

D: "If you don't come back, I won't take you to the fireworks festival tonight!"

"Bui…"

Upon hearing this, the Eevee showed conflicted expressions.

After carefully asking, D learned that a bonfire event was happening tonight at the World Tree too, and they just agreed to participate with the Pokémon there.

"I see…" D nodded and quickly came up with a good idea: "If that's the case, you should stay at the World Tree! You can see the fireworks from there too, be sure to give me a video call so we can watch the fireworks together."

"Bui!" That's a great idea!

Faces full of joy appeared on the Eevee.

After hanging up, D looked over at the Indeedee Sibling: "This year, you should join in too, sister!"

"Ai-yi…" Although sister Indeedee felt tempted, she showed a hesitant expression.

D immediately understood her thoughts: "Don't worry, the Pokemon Tavern having a day off won't be a problem, those Pokémon guests will likely be attracted to the fireworks festival instead."

Upon hearing this, sister Indeedee felt relieved.

She and her brother happily agreed, planning to head to the beach in the evening with D to watch the fireworks.

Then, D stepped outside and into the ranch.

Dragonite was standing by the field watering the crops, while Gogoat waited nearby, ready to care for the crops with Grass Energy as usual.

On the nearby grassy field, the Miltank were leisurely eating dew-covered grass.

Observing this gratifying scene, D couldn't help but smile.

He looked toward the direction outside the ranch but saw nothing.

"Is Lono not here yet?"

After some thought, D took out his phone to call Lono.

Soon, Lono's lively voice came through: "Hello?"

D raised an eyebrow and looked toward the direction of the ranch entrance: "Are you almost here?"

"About to set off!" Lono's side was filled with rustling sounds: "I'm preparing gifts for Cyclizar and the others; sorry for the disturbance today!"

D: "You don't need to bring gifts…"

"Hehe!" A silly laugh came through: "I always feel bad for bothering you, D, but rest assured, I'm all set and will be setting off soon!"

Checking the time, D said: "I'll have Dragonite go and pick you up."

Without waiting for Lono's agreement, he found his Dragonite.

Shortly afterward, Dragonite joyfully flew off with a shoulder bag.

"Let's leave it at this, I've got some stuff to take care of, and Dragonite has already set off; it'll soon be in Levincia City." D said.

"Alright, noted!"

After hanging up, D went to the field to pick crops that had been ripened by Gogoat.

Meanwhile, in Levincia City.

Lono stood by the street with shopping bags, unable to stop smiling.

"Hehe."

Bellibolt felt its Trainer's happiness and joyfully started swaying its round body.

Suddenly, a message from Geeta came through, interrupting Lono's thoughts.

Huh? From the champion? Could it be a special task? Lono suddenly tensed up.

She had struggled to secure two days off from the champion!

Back then, all she mentioned was having some personal matters; if the champion found out she took time off to play, she'd be doomed!

Quickly opening her Rotom Phone, she found out that the champion was just here for some leisurely chat.

[Geeta: Has Levincia Gym stopped accepting challenges now?]

[Lono: Yes, I've put up a notice at the door stating the suspension of challenges!]

[Geeta: I see! Are you two not in Levincia City?]

---

[Lono: I've got some personal stuff to take care of, so I'll be away for two days.]

Lono replied, trembling with nerves, but thankfully the Chairwoman didn't ask much. Only then did she relax, standing in place and waiting for Dragonite to come pick her up.

Paldea League Headquarters.

Rika looked at the smile on Geeta's face and couldn't help feeling a little speechless.

"That silly girl has finally gotten smarter." Geeta said with satisfaction. "Let's hope there's some progress today!"

Rika: "...Chairwoman, the Levincia Gym has already received complaints from Trainers, and if those two really go well and end up dating, getting married, and having kids, I'm afraid there'll be no one left to serve as Gym Leader at the Levincia Gym."

As far as Rika knew, a sloppy attitude toward work was a very serious matter to the Chairwoman.

Yet this time, the Chairwoman's reaction wasn't at all what Rika had imagined.

Geeta just shook her head carelessly. "It's fine. These losses for now are all necessary. As for marriage and kids, Rika, you're thinking way further ahead than I am!"

Rika: "..."

Geeta said with a squinty-eyed smile: "Relax! Even if Lono gets pregnant, there's still D, isn't there? How could he possibly just watch Lono waddle around with a big belly greeting challengers."

Rika was slightly stunned, and then a scene popped into her mind of Lono resting at home while D brought his own Pokemon over to moonlight as Gym Leader.

But then she thought it through: with D's strength, there probably wouldn't be any Trainer who could beat him and get a Gym Badge from him, right?

"But it's still way too early to be talking about that now."

Right now they still didn't know where D stood. Although Lono ran off to D Ranch for a few days every month under the excuse of "challenging a Trial," D's thoughts were unknown to everyone.

Geeta knew full well you shouldn't pop the champagne too early. She sat in her chair, fell into brief thought, and then made a decision.

"Oh right, Rika, pass it down: everyone in the Paldea League gets the day off today. Tell them to go have a proper day of fun!"

Rika was stunned for a moment, then asked: "What about you?"

Geeta: "We're going to Porto Marinada Town together. I heard they've made a ton of preparations for this year's fireworks festival! It's definitely going to be super lively!"

*

"Thanks for the hard work, Dragonite!"

Levincia City. Looking at the Dragonite that had just descended from the sky and landed in front of her, Lono smiled and patted its head.

Dragonite wasn't unfamiliar with this human who visited the ranch often. It let out a happy roar and then crouched down to make it easier for her to climb on.

But before that, Lono opened the satchel on Dragonite's body and stuffed the gifts she'd bought into it.

Next, Lono took out a Poke Ball, recalled Bellibolt into the Poke Ball, and only then climbed onto Dragonite's back.

Thirty minutes later, Dragonite smoothly returned to the ranch.

When they landed, D was in the middle of hauling cardboard boxes full of crops together with Koraidon.

Lono waved her arms hard at him from afar. "Long time no see, D!"

"It hasn't been that long, has it?" D's face was full of helplessness.

"Hehe!"

D put the goods down helplessly and continued, "Go sit and rest in the living room for a bit first! The fireworks festival only starts at night. We can head out around six or seven."

"Then I'll be imposing!"

D had originally planned to finish this bit of work and then go entertain his guest.

But maybe because the fireworks festival was being held, a lot of tourists from other places had been drawn here. All morning, he'd been receiving one Trainer after another coming to challenge the Trial.

There were even Trainers from other regions who said they wanted to see the true face of Paldea's hidden Boss and asked D to use his full strength against them.

That guy was said to be the Champion of the last Ever Grande Conference, extremely powerful, with an ace Blaziken that cut down gods and Buddhas alike.

D certainly didn't hold back for that.

Since these people treated him as the hidden Boss of the Paldea Region, then he might as well properly play the hidden Boss role!

So D promptly switched to Koraidon and gave that Conference Champion a clean six-to-zero sweep, leaving the other's expression frozen in terror, mumbling "So strong, what terrifying strength!" as he staggered off the ranch in a daze.

D: "..."

By the time everything was done, it was already six in the evening.

After a quick dinner, D and Lono finally brought a bunch of Pokemon along to attend the fireworks festival.

By the time they arrived, quite a lot of tourists and Pokemon had already gathered on the beach.

There were young Trainers on their journeys, couples who had come together on dates, parents who'd brought their kids to watch the fireworks, and elderly grandpas and grandmas with equally aged Pokemon sitting on benches, gazing at the beautiful starry sky and the sea below.

Not only that, D also noticed that the market in the distance had grown bigger.

From takoyaki stalls, to the Magikarp-uncle who sold Magikarp, to the shops selling Berries and handmade Poke Balls—D even spotted a mysterious little fortune-telling stand among them.

A Hypno with a pendulum was using Hypnosis on the tourist sitting across from it.

And there were tons of little kids playing with Poke Ball toys.

Just like Mayor Thomas had said, this year's fireworks festival really was extra lively!

The two of them found a part of the beach that wasn't so crowded and sat down.

No sooner had D plopped down than Cyclizar came running over with a rubber ball in its mouth. It dropped the ball in front of D, signaling that it wanted to play.

"All righty!"

D stood up, picked up the ball, and hurled it with force. Cyclizar immediately dashed off in that direction.

---

Yet, halfway along the way, it suddenly saw Koraidon speeding past, snatching the ball right in front of it!

"Gao!"

Cyclizar was shocked, turned its head, and complained to D about Koraidon's behavior.

D felt a headache: "Koraidon!"

Koraidon immediately dropped the ball, turned around in a panic, and ran away.

D: "..."

Lono chuckled beside him: "Is this how you usually get along with Koraidon and the others?"

"Yes!" D nodded with a smile: "With them around, it feels like I'll never be bored!"

Saying this, D threw the ball that Cyclizar had snatched back.

Cyclizar excitedly chased after it.

Lono's Luxray and Electrode watched, full of envy.

They ran to Lono, expressing their desire to play too.

Lono was quite troubled, as she hadn't brought any Pokemon toys this time!

D noticed this and smiled as he retrieved some pre-prepared toy flying discs from his bag, saying: "Use mine!"

Luxray and Electrode turned their heads to look at this human.

D grinned at them.

His Pokemon affinity was maxed, and he quickly gained the trust of Luxray and Electrode.

Then, the two of them started playing the flying disc game with a group of Pokemon.

Ogerpon and Gholdengo weren't interested in catching flying discs; they held hands curiously exploring the stalls, with the masked Ogerpon playing the role of the human, and Gholdengo as its Pokemon.

In this way, the two Pokemon perfectly mixed into the crowd, experiencing the feeling of strolling through the market.

In the dim corner, Flutter Mane floated silently in mid-air, observing these humans.

Suddenly, Lono's Mismagius floated over curiously, asking Flutter Mane what it was doing.

Flutter Mane murmured: "Dream!" (It's so beautiful!)

Mismagius turned its head and saw the brightly lit bustling market, the dark blue night sky overhead, a cool sea breeze sweeping in, waves gently hitting the beach, leaving layers of white foam.

It's truly beautiful!

Thus, Mismagius and Flutter Mane appreciated this splendid scenery together.

The Indeedee Sibling happily sat next to D, spread out a picnic blanket on the beach, then placed a picnic basket filled with snacks on it, ready to wait for the Pokemon to come eat when they were tired.

Meanwhile, Pawmot ran back and forth with its good friend Slugma on the beach, gazing at the round moon overhead.

After playing with the Pokemon for a while, D felt a bit tired and sat on the beach to rest.

Lono, too, breathlessly pushed away Luxray's big face that came over and sat on the beach to rest.

Neither of them spoke.

Looking at the round moon overhead and the lively crowd, D suddenly felt this moment was wonderful.

He couldn't help but recall the words of his sister Indeedee not long ago.

It said it sensed affection for him from Lono.

Thinking of this, D turned his head to look at the girl beside him.

Unexpectedly, Lono was also looking at him, and their gazes met in the night.

An inexplicable atmosphere began to flow slowly in the air.

"Today's moon is really beautiful!" Lono awkwardly turned her head to the sky, feeling her face getting a bit warm.

D followed her gaze, indeed seeing a round bright moon, he exhaled slowly, sensing the atmosphere was a bit off, and changed the subject:

"By the way, I've always had a question to ask you."

"Hmm? What question?" Lono tilted her head.

D sincerely asked: "Levincia City and Porto Marinada Town are so far apart, don't you find it troublesome to frequently come from there?"

"..."

"..."

At this moment, Lono suddenly felt a bit complicated inside.

She looked at D in front of her, an expression of resignation on her face, with a tone somewhat helpless, as if she had made some kind of decision: "D, you're such an idiot!"

D was slightly stunned.

Lono blushed and continued: "Of course, I'm here because of you!"

Upon hearing this, D was instantly dumbfounded.

At this moment, accompanied by the cheers of the surrounding crowd, Mayor Thomas's prepared fireworks were all ignited.

The next second, dazzling fireworks rose into the night sky, turning into brilliant flowers blooming behind Lono.

In that radiant glow, D saw his own reflected image in Lono's clear eyes.

Suddenly, it seemed like all the sounds around disappeared.

"Thump! Thump! Thump!"

Amidst the fireworks, D heard the sound of his own heartbeat.

Lono turned her head, looking at the fireworks in the night sky, then said softly:

"I like you!"

Her voice was extremely light, as if casually stating a matter of routine.

"Boom!"

A huge firework shot up to the sky.

The next second, a golden Pikachu head appeared in the night sky.

---

---

---

---

# 480: Chapter 477: Family Values

League Calendar Year 200, November 3rd, Winter.

The cold wind blowing down from the top of Glaseado Mountain swept across the whole Paldea Land, with pure white snowflakes fluttering down from the sky.

In the cold weather, wild Pokemon also retreated to their nests, making it hard to spot them.

The farm too was covered in a thick blanket of white snow, the snowflakes dancing in the air like little sprites, lively and floating.

In winter, the farm is not suited for growing crops, and early on when the heavy snow arrived, D gave the Dragonites a holiday, letting them stay cozy at the World Tree for a nice winter.

The farm shop has temporarily ceased operations, and suddenly, the whole farm seemed to have come to a standstill.

Eevee and Ogerpon went off to play at the World Tree again, leaving only a few Pokemon at home.

Slugma nestled in the fireplace in the living room, the heat from its body flowing through the pipes to every corner of the room, even the storage room was warm.

D leaned back in a rocking chair by the fireplace, his mind vacant, while Cyclizar and Koraidon lay lazily on the rug nearby.

Glancing at the two Pokemon beside him, D's thoughts involuntarily drifted back to that summer three months ago.

Back then, amidst fireworks filling the sky, Lono confessed his feelings to him.

D was completely flustered at the time, not knowing how to respond.

Lono appeared a bit disappointed seeing his reaction.

D still remembers what he said to Lono.

He said: This is a bit sudden.

Seeing Lono's disappointed look, D felt a bit sorry.

He admitted that he did have some feelings for her, and truthfully told Lono that he had no experience in relationships and didn't know what liking someone felt like.

D has always taken feelings very seriously, and after coming to this world, he had thought about building a family of his own.

Just like Mayor Thomas and Mrs. Dantes, looking after the Pokemon on the farm with his loved one, living together, sharing life's ups and downs, and growing old together.

Then having a child, taking him/her to meet Pokemon, to the ice plains to see Seals, diving to see Luvdisc, to the desert to see Krookodile, to the Bamboo Forest to see Pancham... living a plain and peaceful life.

Though Lono didn't receive an immediate response, she was very happy knowing that D had feelings for her too.

Then later...

The two met Geeta and Rika on the beach.

Geeta said she was there to inspect Porto Marinada Town, but D couldn't shake the feeling that Geeta's smiling face seemed a bit odd.

After that night, they each returned to their own homes.

Lono would still visit the farm whenever she had time, bringing small gifts and homemade treats for D and the Pokemon.

With Lono's arrival, the farm always seemed particularly lively, just like her, even D felt more energetic.

Recalling these things, a smile couldn't help but appear on D's face.

Today is Sunday, and the Levincia Gym should be closed, based on past experience, Lono should already be on her way to the farm.

D glanced at the time on his Rotom Phone and realized it was almost noon.

Realizing what he was doing, D suddenly noticed that he started looking forward to Lono's weekly visits.

His Indeedee sister had been secretly observing D's expressions all along.

After a while, she couldn't help but ask, "Indeedee?"(Is D waiting for Lono to come over?)

D, a bit embarrassed, replied earnestly, "No, I'm checking tomorrow's weather."

However, his emotions were keenly captured by his Indeedee sister, who seemed very pleased:

"Indeedee!"(In that case, we'll soon welcome a little master!)

In her view, as long as two people like each other, they can soon have an egg.

Their Pokemon were like this, humans must be the same!

D: "..."

"Ahem." Realizing the misunderstanding his Indeedee sister had, D cleared his throat and couldn't help but correct her, "No, it's not like that!"

His Indeedee sister turned her head in confusion, "Indeedee?"

D: "With matters like this, there's a significant difference between humans and Pokemon. For Pokemon, as long as they like each other, and stay together every day, they can soon have an egg."

"But for humans, it's not the same. Liking each other is important, but the prerequisite is to see if two people are suitable to live together. After mutual understanding and adaptation, then they can hold a wedding, and live together."

"Indeedee..." His Indeedee sister suddenly felt that humans are so troublesome.

It was Pawmot who perked up hearing his Indeedee sister talk about the little master.

It flew over excitedly, "Pawmo Pawmo?" (Will the little master live with us?)

His Indeedee sister answered for D, "Indeedee!" (Of course! At that time, my brother and I will take good care of it!)

D: "..."

Pawmot's eyes twinkled, and who knows what it thought of, and then it asked:

"Pawmo Pawmo?" (So in that case, will D already have a granddaughter? Will D's granddaughter come to the farm to visit and be friends with me and Slugma?)

This question confused the Indeedee Sister.

"Ai-yi?" (Isn't this kind of thing a bit too early?)

D: "..."

He rubbed Pawmo's fur and said to it, "Stop watching The Slugma-Powered Home so much!"

"Paw-mo~"

Pawmo snuggled in D's arms, acting cute.

With Pawmo's antics, D began to imagine scenes of his children's children visiting the ranch in the future.

By that time, he would probably be in his seventies or eighties.

Oh no, he's been led astray by Pawmo!

D quickly shook his head to dismiss the images from his mind.

He took out his phone and sent a message to Lono.

[Wing King: Are you coming over today?]

Lono didn't reply immediately, but after a while, D suddenly heard a knock on the door.

Pawmo, close to the door, immediately ran over to open it.

As the door opened, D turned around and saw Lono standing at the entrance, smiling.

"It's really cold today!" She kept rubbing her hands, frozen.

Actually, Lono was quite afraid of the cold. During past winters, she mostly stayed indoors with the Pokemon.

D stood up with a smile: "Come over here to warm up, after all, it snowed just yesterday. I heard an old saying, 'it's not cold when it snows, but it's cold when the snow melts.'"

"Got it!"

Lono giggled as she walked in and sat by the fireplace, warming herself with the Slugma. The orange glow from it turned the area around the fireplace a warm shade of orange-red.

D went to pour Lono a cup of hot tea to warm up.

Meanwhile, Indeedee and Pawmo curiously gathered around Lono, watching her.

"Hi! Good morning!" Lono greeted the two Pokemon with a smile: "And Little Cyclizar and Koraidon too!"

"Aga-see~"

"Gao-ao!"

At this moment, D walked over with a cup of hot tea. As he handed the cup to Lono, he suddenly noticed something different about her today.

The painted Magnemite on her head was gone!

"Where's Magnemite?" D asked.

Only then did Lono remember, she touched her head, feeling a bit embarrassed:

"I always felt that kind of look is something only kids would like, a bit too childish."

Since that day after parting, Lono felt it was time to make some changes.

She couldn't be as childish as before because she was an adult now and should become more mature and steady like an adult.

D was slightly stunned, wondering if Lono made the change for him.

At that moment, Lono looked over with hope, blushing as she asked, "How about now?"

"What?"

"Do you like me a little more now?"

D froze, feeling a surge of emotion in his heart.

A moment later, he moved his lips, and what he intended to say next felt difficult for the usually reserved D.

But the girl was being so forward, he couldn't keep staying passive, as that wouldn't be manly.

Thinking of this, D took a deep breath. Though still a little embarrassed, he said seriously,

"Although I don't know exactly what liking is, if it's about looking forward to seeing you every day, wanting to talk to you, being together at the ranch, then I guess... I might be liking you too!"

"Aga-see~"

Koraidon looked on happily at this scene.

The Indeedee Sibling was also very pleased, as this meant they wouldn't need to worry about D being alone forever.

Though there were plenty of Pokemon to keep D company, humans are better off living with humans.

Because there are some things Pokemon simply can't replace humans for.

And both Pawmo and Slugma were quite delighted.

This way, D would have a granddaughter!

Then his granddaughter would visit the ranch to see Slugma and become friends with them, just like in The Slugma-Powered Home!

---

---

---

---

# 481: Chapter 478: The Haunted Forest

Outside the window, it began to snow again.

The fire in the fireplace was burning brightly, and a group of Pokemon were comfortably snoozing in front of it.

D was still trying to shake off the recent conversation, as he watched Lono playing with her phone from across the sofa.

Driven by curiosity, he asked Lono why she liked him.

Lono pondered over the question for a while and realized she didn't know the prepared answer.

Lono said, when she first arrived at the ranch, she felt curious about this mysterious Rancher.

Later, when she saw him enthusiastically helping Grusha heal and taking care of so many Pokemon at the ranch, her curiosity grew.

As she visited the ranch time and again, Lono said she gradually developed a fondness.

Upon hearing this, D froze on the spot.

Is this what you call love at first sight?

That such a thing could happen to him was something D found hard to believe.

At this moment, Lono's voice brought his thoughts back to reality.

"Geeta is taking her vacation in the Alola Region this winter!"

This was indeed a rare event, as Geeta had never left the Paldea Region for work or relaxation before.

Even when other regions invited her to the World Championship, Geeta would decline and send her Champion-level Trainers instead.

"Hmm?" D was equally surprised.

Lono placed her phone before D, and sure enough, he saw Geeta dressed lightly in casual clothes, enjoying sunbathing by the sea.

Considering D's knowledge of Geeta, the workaholic taking a vacation was indeed an unusual event.

Although the Paldea League goes on break during winter, and Gym Leaders are not obligated to remain in their gyms to accept challengers,

Geeta would usually stay at the Paldea League to handle affairs.

But this year, Geeta was acting quite out of character!

What concerned D more was that he had never been to the Alola Region.

Reflecting on his current financial situation, D began considering whether to purchase a villa ranch in the Alola Region, and perhaps take the Pokemon there to spend the winter?

The Alola Region's tropical climate provides a warm and pleasant living environment all year round.

As for how to facilitate the travel of so many Pokemon between locations, it was simple enough: just buy two Pokeball Transmission Machines and place one at each location.

Suddenly, a knock on the door was heard.

This caught D by surprise.

Who would visit the ranch in such snowy weather?

He got up to open the door, but found no one outside.

With the Pokemon retreating to their own little homes, the ranch was a snowy white expanse, devoid of any human presence.

D felt puzzled, about to close the door, when he heard a squeaking sound from below.

Looking down, he saw a plump Greedent raising its head, looking at him pitifully.

Greedent's fur was covered in snow and wind, shivering from the cold.

"Greedent?" D instantly recognized this Greedent.

It was one of the Pokemon he had rescued.

After the forest recovered, Greedent returned there to live.

"Squeak!" Greedent nodded repeatedly.

"Come on in!"

D invited it into the living room and closed the door securely.

The cozy room made Greedent sigh in contentment.

Lono and the surrounding Pokemon cast curious gazes in their direction.

"Why did you come over suddenly? What about the others?" D asked.

Greedent rubbed its paws and then replied: "Squeak squeak!"

With the Power of Viridian, D quickly understood its intent.

Everyone is in the forest now; it came to seek help because there's a haunting in the forest!

"Haunting?" D was quite shocked, "Is it a Ghost-type Pokemon? Take your time, tell me slowly."

Greedent nodded: "Squeak squeak!" (I don't know if it's a Ghost-type Pokemon, but all the forest inhabitants have been frightened by it recently! It secretly follows behind Pokemon, sometimes sneaking into nests when everyone is asleep. Upon opening your eyes, you'll see a ghost floating right in front, causing some Pokemon to faint from fear!)

A "ghost" playing tricks on other Pokemon?

D guessed that it was likely the work of a Ghost-type Pokemon.

However, according to his knowledge, there were no Ghost-type Pokemon inhabiting the area behind the mountain… could it be an outsider Ghost-type Pokemon?

After some thought, D decided to follow Greedent and check it out.

He turned to Lono and said, "Greedent is asking for help; I need to step out for a bit. Can you wait here?"

But Lono refused this suggestion; she stood up and said, "I'll go with you!"

"Alright, let's go then!" D instructed the other Pokemon to stay home, taking only Flutter Mane, another Ghost-type Pokemon, with him.

While walking, Lono curiously asked, "Can you understand Pokemon talk?"

D grinned mysteriously: "Would you like to try?"

Lono was slightly stunned: "Eh?"

Just then, she saw D take out a pack of snacks from his pocket.

"After eating this, you can understand what Pokemon mean," D said, handing her the Translation Nuts Cake.

Hearing this, Lono was quite surprised.

When she ate the snack and heard the Greedent in front muttering "How cold it is," she was so shocked she couldn't speak.

She could actually understand Pokemon talking!

"How is this possible?!" Lono widened her eyes in disbelief.

"My psychic ability." D laughed.

Psychic ability... Lono immediately thought of those human beings who possess special abilities.

"I can't believe there's such a thing..." Lono murmured softly.

"It's not that exaggerated." D laughed and said, "Even without psychic abilities, some Trainers can communicate with their Pokemon; the key is the bond between their hearts."

At this moment, the group formally entered the back mountain.

The back mountain in winter was also covered with white snow. Looking around, there were almost no signs of those Pokemon that were usually active.

Only some Ice Type Pokemon like Snom, who enjoy living in snowy environments, could be seen.

Greedent jumped down from D's body and quickly led the way in front.

Soon, D saw the Berry Forest he and Koraidon had planted by hand.

Greedent: "Zizi!" (It's around here!)

D looked around but saw nothing.

He nodded towards Flutter Mane beside him.

However, primitive Flutter Mane didn't understand: "Dream?"

D: "..."

"Flutter Mane, can you sense the presence of Ghost-type Pokemon around?" D directly asked.

Flutter Mane finally understood D's meaning.

It slipped into the shadows and began to search the surrounding area for those suspicious ghosts.

At this moment, other Pokemon living in this Berry Forest saw D's figure.

The group of Pokemon once saved by D began to gather around, joyfully jumping in the snow to greet him.

D smiled and looked at these lovely Pokemon: "Tinkaton, long time no see! Grafaiai, how's life recently? Maushold, huh? How did you change from a family of four to Tandemaus?"

The two small white mice replied: "Zizi!" (The kids grew up and left us to explore on their own!)

"Is that so?" D nodded, quite surprised that this kind of Pokemon could actually devolve!

Lono watched D's interaction with this group of wild Pokemon from the side.

D was indeed very popular with the Pokemon!

Meanwhile, a dog-like Pokemon with a candle on its head was enviously hiding in the corner, looking in that direction.

It wagged its tail continuously, spinning in place, and made excited growling sounds, wanting to rush over and play with those two humans.

But at this moment, the Pokemon suddenly sensed a terrifying presence behind it.

Turning its head, it saw a large-sized, wildly spirited Pokemon watching it quietly from behind, not knowing when it had come.

"Grrroooww!"

The Pokemon was startled and instinctively burrowed underground, leaving only a lit candle on the ground, and quickly fled towards the distance.

Flutter Mane did not pursue but returned to D to report the incident.

"Dream."(There's a strange guy.)

There really is? D promptly said, "Take me to see it."

"Dream."

Flutter Mane led the way in front.

D and Lono walked side by side, chatting about these wild Pokemon along the way.

Tinkaton and Greedent, who were afraid of ghosts, hid behind D, advancing carefully.

The group passed through the Berry Forest and soon arrived at a broad clearing.

D immediately recognized this place.

This was the cemetery where he previously buried the Pokemon who perished in the forest fire!

Flutter Mane floated up, pointing to a candle burning with purple flames on the ground.

It reached out with its wing claws and poked it, and the next second, the Pokemon hiding underground jumped out in a panic, looking terrified at the two humans and the group of Pokemon.

"Is that Pokemon a Greavard?" Lono immediately recognized the Ghost-type Pokemon.

D nodded: "It seems to be it now."

It is said that Greavard is the reincarnation of stray dog Pokemon that died without interacting with humans.

They usually sleep quietly underground, almost motionless, sticking their heads out and lighting the eerie flame atop them.

Once someone approaches, Greavard emits a strange growl and leaps to the ground, surprising many, but it does not seem to intend harm.

Because this kind of Pokemon has a gentle nature, loves to be close to people, and fears loneliness.

As long as someone plays with it for a while, it will be happy and always follow them.

Among the Pokemon living in the Paldea Region, Greavard is known for its friendly disposition toward humans, making it easy to be captured and become a Pokemon of Human Trainers.

What surprised D was whether this Greavard was a Ghost-type Pokemon reincarnated from the Pokemon who perished here?

"Woowang..."

Feeling the hostility from these people and Pokemon, Greavard seemed a bit scared, its body almost pressing to the ground, even its tail drooping.

Initially afraid, Greedent and the others didn't know what to do when they saw the ghost that scared them was such a Pokemon.

D pondered for a moment and said to them, "It's called Greavard. It's probably playing tricks because it feels too lonely and wants to play with you, not intending to scare you."

---

---

---

---

# 482: Chapter 479: Ash and Slaking, the Awakening of Groudon and Kyogre

"Squeak?"

Greedent looked surprised at the unfamiliar Pokemon in front of it.

As D finished speaking, Greavard couldn't help but look up and wag its tail at him, making a happy panting sound.

"Greavard is also very cute!" Lono said softly, "But speaking of which, there's a rumor that Greavard is a Pokemon reborn from the soul of a deceased Pokemon. Have there been Pokemon passing away here before?"

D nodded, "The forest fire from the year before last took away the lives of many Pokemon here. This place is the cemetery I built for them, where the bodies of those unfortunate Pokemon are buried."

Saying that, D squatted down and waved at the Greavard.

Upon seeing this, Greavard eagerly wagged its tail and trotted over.

Lono also squatted down beside him.

Greavard looked at D, then at her, before happily wagging its tail and nudging Lono, as if inviting them both to play together.

The two played with Greavard for a while, and seeing that the ghostly issue was solved, D prepared to head back, noticing that Lono started shivering from the cold.

Was Lono really afraid of the cold?

Realizing this, D stood up and spoke to the surrounding Pokemon:

"Alright, Greavard doesn't mean any harm, it probably just wants to play games with you."

"Squeak..."

Only then did Greedent and the other Pokemon realize.

Although Greavard had scared them over this time, it seemed it had never actually hurt them.

Since that's the case, why not let Greavard stay here and live?

Greedent and Tinkaton happily made the decision.

"Everyone should go back now! It's so cold, staying outside might make you sick," D said with a smile.

The surrounding Pokemon responded in unison, bidding farewell to the two, each heading home.

D headed in the direction of the ranch with Flutter Mane.

However, half way there, Flutter Mane suddenly stopped, as if it had discovered something, and turned around to look behind.

"Hmm? What's wrong, Flutter Mane?"

D turned and followed Flutter Mane's gaze, seeing the Greavard quietly following them.

Greavard, realizing it had been discovered, buried its upper body in the snow, with its tail wagging.

"It seems like it really wants to play with us!" Lono said.

D could see it too, so he waved at Greavard, calling out, "Greavard, come over!"

"Woof!"

Upon hearing the call, Greavard immediately sprinted over, circling around the two.

Looking at this pitiful Greavard, recalling D's mention of the forest fire, Lono made a decision.

"D, let's adopt it!" she said.

The thought had crossed D's mind too, the ranch was missing a watchdog Pokemon.

Especially Greavard, among many canine Pokemon, it was one of the most loyal to Trainers.

Although its evolved name is rather funny... from Greavard to Houndstone.

But it's just perfect for helping herding and protecting the Wooloo on the ranch.

With this in mind, D nodded and then squatted down to ask Greavard:

"Greavard, would you like to live with us?"

"Woof?" Greavard stopped running and looked up.

"It means living in our place, there are many other Pokemon there who will become your friends, so you'll never feel lonely again." D said with a smile.

Understanding the words, Greavard eagerly nodded, letting out an excited woof.

"Then let's look after each other from now on!"

"Woof!"

The two adopted the Greavard and brought it back to the ranch.

As for this new member of the ranch, Pawmot and the others were all very curious, gathering around to look at the Pokemon.

But Greavard seemed rather silly and not too bright, just following D and Lono around like a little tagalong.

D knew that Greavard considered him and Lono as its owners now.

This kind of Pokemon is inherently close to humans, not to mention that they had food prepared for Greavard.

"So simple-minded!"

D couldn't help but feel.

And Greavard's behavior was especially strong when Lono was preparing to leave the ranch and return to Levincia City.

It barked anxiously, appearing as if it didn't want her to leave.

Seeing this, Lono softly comforted, "Don't worry, I'm just going to Levincia City to handle some human work, it won't be long before I come back to see you."

D also nodded, "That's right, don't worry!"

Although the Paldea League had granted a break for Gym Leaders, Lono also had her family in Levincia City. Since it's close to the New Year, she had to return to visit her parents.

Though the two are currently in a relationship, they have yet to reach the stage of meeting the parents.

Before that, they need to go through a period of adjustment.

Because he and Lono are two completely different people.

One is a rancher living in the countryside, the other a Gym Leader living in the big city.

If they wish to live together, they must first understand and get to know each other.

Upon hearing their reassurance, Greavard finally stopped obediently.

---

"Go on!" D handed a cup of hot fruit juice with cold-resistant properties to Lono: "Drink it on the way, so you won't feel cold."

"Mhm!" Lono responded happily, then ran forward with a flushed face and gently hugged D.

D's body stiffened, his face turned red, and then he willingly hugged back.

"Hehe!" Feeling D's body stiffen, Lono chuckled, then let go and quickly ran to Dragonite, waving his hand: "I'm leaving!"

"Mhm." D nodded.

With that, Dragonite flapped its wings and carried Lono away from the ranch.

Watching their figures go into the distance, D then took Greavard and returned home.

As soon as he entered the house, he saw his sister Indeedee covering her mouth, secretly laughing, looking very happy:

"Ai~ei~"

D: "..."

He didn't even need to ask to know what his sister Indeedee was thinking.

D deliberately put on a serious expression to mask his inner embarrassment.

But right at that moment, a phone call suddenly came in.

D clicked to find out it was Professor Oak calling.

He pressed agree, and the next second, Ash's face appeared on the screen.

"Long time no see, Mr. D!" Ash's excited voice came from the other end: "I didn't know your number, so I asked Professor Oak to help contact you!"

As he spoke, the camera shifted, and Professor Oak standing nearby also appeared on the screen.

Professor Oak, wearing a white coat, waved at D and greeted him with a smile: "Good afternoon, D!"

"Good afternoon, Professor Oak." D also responded with a smile: "What's the matter with Ash finding me?"

"Here's the thing!" The camera returned to Ash: "I came to report to you about Slaking's current situation."

"Hmm? Has Vigoroth already evolved into Slaking?" D was quite surprised.

"That's right!" Ash nodded: "And thanks to Slaking, I got second place at the Conference! I was just a little bit away from winning the championship, it's such a pity..."

Second place?

Could it be that Ash's experience has been altered?

D's mind flickered.

At this moment, Ash had already turned the camera towards Slaking.

D suddenly saw a differently colored Slaking lazily lying on the floor of the Professor Oak's Research Institute.

Slaking was yawning, seemingly sensing something, it opened its eyelid and looked towards the Rotom Phone.

"Long time no see, Slaking!" D greeted Slaking through the screen.

Slaking continued to lazily gaze over, yawning as if using this manner to respond.

D: "..."

Good, that's very Slaking.

Seeing this, Ash was a bit embarrassed:

"The Slaking that really gets into it isn't like this!"

D: "...I know."

After all, Slaking's impressive 160 attack species strength and 100 speed species strength are no joke.

It's truly a double A in power and speed Pokemon.

Unfortunately, burdened by the [Truant] ability, if it had [Huge Power] instead, Slaking's status and win rate in trainer battles would likely soar straight to the top.

D nodded, very satisfied with Ash's training of Slaking:

"Seeing Slaking live well with you, I'm relieved. If I remember correctly, Ash, you are thirteen this year, right?"

Ash nodded repeatedly: "That's right! I've been traveling for three years now!"

D praised: "Getting second place at the League Competition at thirteen, that's quite an achievement!"

In contrast, Ash seemed a bit embarrassed.

He scratched his head: "Nah! It's all thanks to Pikachu and the others!"

D smiled slightly: "So now that your journey in the Hoenn Region is over, where are you planning to travel next year? Are you coming to Paldea?"

"No, Mr. D is a powerful champion, My strength isn't enough to challenge you yet, but I will keep traveling, and when I become a Pokemon Master, I'll head to Paldea and challenge you for real!"

Ash's eyes were full of determination.

"So next, Pikachu and I are planning to travel to Sinnoh Region, heard they're hosting the Lily of the Valley Conference there!"

"Lily of the Valley Conference, huh?" D's mind was full of thoughts.

So, Ash's rivals in Sinnoh Region, Paul, and the Legend Beast guy Tobias, will be appearing too.

"Then you better go for it!"

"Yes, I will!"

After the call ended, out of curiosity about this year's Conference, D immediately used the Rotom Phone to see if he could find the Conference's video streaming online.

Guess what, he actually found it.

D clicked Pichu Tv, then projected it on the TV, and started watching match after match.

If he remembered correctly, Ash's original ranking was quarterfinals.

At first, Ash's matches were all smooth sailing, although there were some minor crises in the middle, overall it went quite smoothly.

And soon, the matches reached the round where the top 8 compete for the top 4.

Ash's opponent in this round was the popular Trainer Tetsuya.

D remembered Tetsuya, who originally was the champion of this year's Conference and was the one Ash lost to.

But this time, Ash won?!

D curiously continued watching the match.

Ash's team lineup included: Glalie, Corphish, Swellow, Slaking, Grovyle, and Pikachu.

---

And Tetsuya's lineup is: Sceptile, Shiftry, Hariyama, Donphan, Meowth, Metagross.

The battle was extremely intense, and at the last moment, Ash was only left with a weakened Slaking and Pikachu, while Tetsuya still had a Meowth wearing boots.

That Meowth was incredibly strong and defeated Pikachu with Fury Swipes, forcing Ash to send out the weakened Slaking.

Perhaps it was Ash's fighting spirit and unwavering determination that inspired Slaking, or perhaps it just didn't want to lose the match.

During the battle, Slaking unexpectedly stood up and unleashed its full strength. After a fierce fight, it finally used Body Slam to defeat Meowth and successfully advanced to the top four.

In the semifinals, Ash also narrowly won, but in the final, he lost to a Trainer with a Gengar.

Looking at the screen showing the fallen Pikachu, D felt regret:

"Tsk, such a pity, otherwise Ash could have won the championship this year."

But being the runner-up is already quite an achievement. If Ash can use the same lineup in next year's Lily of the Valley Conference in the Sinnoh Region, he might achieve even better results.

However, Ash still maintains the habit of capturing new Pokemon and training them whenever he arrives in a new region...

Moreover, with black horses frequently emerging in the Lily of the Valley Conference, Ash's Pokemon, trained for less than a year, would have no chance against Tobias with Legendary Beasts.

If it were him, he would probably bring out his strongest Pokemon to battle.

Pikachu, Charizard, Sceptile...

It's just a shame that Greninja and Infernape hadn't joined at this time.

But maybe that's just Ash?

*

Year 200 of the League, November 20, winter.

In the coldest months of winter, all is silent.

Wild Pokemon hibernate, staying indoors, except for some Ice Type Pokemon, others are hardly active outside.

Due to the difficulty of finding food in winter, they try to minimize energy consumption, relying on stored energy to get through the cold winter.

And on such a day.

Groudon and Kyogre, slumbering on the World Tree, awoke.

Due to the abundance of Natural Energy, these two Super Ancient Pokemon reverted to their original forms, transforming into Primal Reversion Form.

Sensing the peak power within them, Groudon and Kyogre brimmed with confidence; one marched out of the volcano, and the other surfaced from the sea, howling to the sky, each wanting to teach the other a lesson.

Thus, Groudon left its volcanic habitat, and Kyogre quickly moved towards Groudon, sensing its presence.

However, when they arrived at the "meeting" point, they saw D waiting there with Rayquaza, Mewtwo, Koraidon, Ho-oh, Giratina, and Calyrex.

Groudon:!!!

Kyogre:!!!

Looking at the two arch-enemies below, D felt his determination harden, his fists clenched!

"Want to fight, huh?" D grinned, a hint of a cruel smile on his face.

Groudon and Kyogre glanced at the terrifying presence behind him, instinctively taking a step back.

The Dragonites on Dragonite Island were drawn by the scene, curiously surrounding and becoming spectators.

"Remember what I said before? If you fight, you'll be expelled. It seems you didn't take my words to heart!" D said with a smile that didn't reach his eyes.

---

---

---

---

# 483: Chapter 480: Calyrex's Ultimate Form 1v2! Recognition from Groudon and Kyogre!

A drop of cold sweat ran down the foreheads of Groudon and Kyogre.

Even though they had returned to their peak state through Primal Reversion, facing so many Legendary Pokemon, even teaming up couldn't guarantee victory.

Moreover, their nemesis Rayquaza was also among them.

Of course, the most serious matter was being expelled from this World Tree.

Kyogre didn't want to leave, nor did Groudon...

At this moment, a hint of retreat began to form in the hearts of these two Super Ancient Pokemon.

They exchanged a glance through the air, surprisingly sensing a rare harmony in each other's eyes.

Unfortunately, D had no intention of letting these two go.

Luckily, he discovered them early enough. What if one day they started fighting without him noticing and wrecked the World Tree he painstakingly grew?

D's gaze fell on Groudon and Kyogre below.

Under normal circumstances, the simplest way to make a Pokemon obey was clear.

That was to conquer them through brute force, proving to that Pokemon that your strength could overpower them.

Thus, they would acknowledge the power of that Pokemon.

With this in mind, D already had a plan.

He did not ask for Rayquaza, Ho-oh, and Mewtwo's Helping Hand but instead turned his gaze to Calyrex.

Calyrex immediately understood D's intention.

It nodded slightly, then said softly, "It's been a long time since I last battled anyone. In that case, I'll just get some exercise!"

Having said that, Calyrex, riding Spectrier, stepped on the void and arrived in front of Groudon and Kyogre.

Groudon and Kyogre instantly became tense.

Because they felt an unfathomable terrifying aura emanating from this seemingly inconspicuous Pokemon!

D, riding Koraidon, floated up to position himself behind Calyrex, watching these two Super Ancient Pokemon.

"Let's have a battle! Calyrex will duel you both, and you can strike simultaneously. If you win, I'll overlook today's matter, but if you lose, you must follow my command from now on and absolutely no more fighting."

Sometimes D even thought of tying Kyogre and Groudon together with a rope, using physical contact to foster closeness.

But now, the most direct method was to conquer them with strength.

Upon hearing D's words, Groudon and Kyogre instantly displayed expressions of disbelief.

One versus two? Is he underestimating them?

Both Super Ancient Pokemon felt their pride was trampled, showcasing expressions of defiance.

"Roar..."

Primal Groudon let out a low growl.

Meanwhile, Kyogre cast a deep gaze at the opposing Calyrex.

Ho-oh, Rayquaza, Giratina, and Mewtwo floated in the rear, watching the spectacle with interest.

Mewtwo maintained a calm demeanor.

In its understanding, these were considered "gods" dwelling in this world, ranked among the strongest in Pokemon.

It too was created by humans as the strongest Pokemon.

Between it and these Pokemon, who had the greater strength?

Ho-oh flapped its wings, hovering in mid-air. It could see that those two Pokemon had absorbed a tremendous amount of Natural Energy, transforming into their current state.

Even it couldn't guarantee beating either one, yet D and Calyrex could take them both on?

In terms of battling two at once, Rayquaza was the most skilled.

Yet, it focused more on the transformations of Groudon and Kyogre than the battle.

Envy arose in Rayquaza's eyes.

Those two had reverted to their original forms, whereas Rayquaza could not transform into a stronger form due to insufficient energy.

Natural Energy differed from Mega Evolution Energy; to perform Mega Evolution, Rayquaza needed to venture into the depths of Cosmos and consume more Meteorites.

Meanwhile, Giratina took out a Rotom Phone, eager to record the battle out of sheer nosiness.

"Then, let's begin!" D commanded, "Calyrex, use Nasty Plot!"

Groudon, positioned within the sea, was instantly flooded with scorching Earth Vein Energy.

The energy surged into the land, transforming into Precipice Blades that stabbed at Calyrex from the ocean.

Simultaneously, Kyogre launched its attack.

Opening its massive maw, it swiftly condensed an Ice Beam at Calyrex.

Despite facing these two potent Moves, Calyrex remained unflustered.

Once Nasty Plot concluded, the Spectrier beneath it neighed and vanished into the void.

Precipice Blades and Ice Beam struck only air, causing an explosion that scared away a few spectating Dragonite.

Seeing the vanished Calyrex, Groudon and Kyogre became instantly cautious.

Yet the next moment, as D's voice rang out, Calyrex's figure quietly reappeared from the void.

"Solar Beam, aim at Kyogre!"

At Calyrex's level, using Solar Beam no longer required energy gathering beforehand.

In the instant of its appearance, a beam comprising Grass Energy swiftly engulfed Kyogre.

The immense impact elicited a wail from Kyogre as its gigantic form plunged into the ocean.

---

A flash of white light streaked across, and Calyrex's Solar Beam actually forced Primal Kyogre back to its regular form!

Seeing this scene, Groudon was greatly startled.

But at this moment, it couldn't afford to worry about the situation of its archrival and good teammate.

"Roar!"

Groudon let out a loud roar at Calyrex, spewing fierce flames from its mouth.

The defeated opponent made Calyrex's Spectrier neigh excitedly, with dark energy swirling around them.

Calyrex, riding its beloved steed, directly charged at Groudon.

Suddenly, the light in the world dimmed.

It was as if night had fallen.

Soon after, countless starlight bodies appeared in the air, twinkling like stars in the night sky.

The watching Dragonites were captivated by this extraordinary sight, all turning their curious gazes toward it.

However, in the next second, the stars suddenly went into a frenzy, madly rushing towards Groudon, engulfing it in the starlight.

"Boom!"

A massive explosion suddenly erupted, with a look of pain on Groudon's face. The severe injury caused by Astral Barrage also forced it back to its normal form, lying dejectedly on the ground.

Meanwhile, Calyrex stood unscathed in mid-air.

Looking at Groudon and Kyogre below, D shook his head and said, "Looks like you've lost."

Though forced out of their Primal Reversion Form, these two Pokemon had not lost their ability to fight.

They silently looked at the powerful Pokemon, and after a moment, Groudon and Kyogre turned their gaze to D.

Proud as they were, they had to admit at this moment that the Pokemon trained by this human had far surpassed them.

At this moment, Groudon and Kyogre looked at D without the contempt they held for ordinary humans.

His strength was enough to earn their recognition.

Groudon and Kyogre spoke in unison: "We will abide by our promise with you."

Seeing these two Pokemon subdued, D finally showed a slight smile.

"Very well then, from now on, I hope you'll remember my words: no fighting inside the World Tree. If you break the rules again, I will expel you."

Groudon and Kyogre had come to understand.

This human was the master of the World Tree after all! Whether it was them or Rayquaza, they were merely guests here.

On someone else's territory, they needed to be more disciplined.

Groudon and Kyogre agreed and then turned around, ready to head back to their own homes.

Watching the departing Pokemon, D finally breathed a sigh of relief.

With everything resolved, he too should head back to the ranch!

The observing Pokemon also gradually dispersed.

Rayquaza and Ho-oh were the first to leave, with Rayquaza's black silhouette shooting straight into the sky, piercing through the ozone layer to the cosmos to look for meteors imbued with cosmic energy to munch on.

Ho-oh also flapped its wings, flying toward the distance to continue fulfilling its mission. However, unlike before, Ho-oh decided to return to the World Tree to rest for a while after each period of exertion outside.

And Giratina also slipped into the shadow of the World Tree, returning to the Reversed World.

Only one Mewtwo remained floating in the air, unable to recover its senses for a long time.

Could it be a match for this Pokemon named Calyrex?

"No, probably not. Its strength has already surpassed all the Pokemon I've seen," Mewtwo murmured.

There is always someone stronger. The sky is vast above the sky.

Mewtwo thought of Team Rocket, which attempted to create the world's strongest Pokemon, and the human named Giovanni.

How laughable.

Forget it, none of the past matters to it now; the most important thing is to live well with everyone here.

Mewtwo shook its head, then its silhouette flickered and disappeared from sight.

*

"Indeed... Calyrex's current strength has reached a new level."

Back at the ranch, D couldn't help but open Calyrex's personal homepage, looking at the tens of millions of followers, and murmured with emotion.

The Power of Faith from tens of millions of people allowed Calyrex to knock Groudon and Kyogre out of their Primal Reversion Form with one move.

This level even allows it to compare with the Mega Evolved Black Rayquaza.

So the question arises, can Black Rayquaza fight seven at once, and can Calyrex do the same now?

Moreover, unlike Rayquaza, Calyrex can freely change its types, giving it a very wide range of attack coverage.

Not only that, Calyrex can also fly.

Whether it's Spectrier treading on the void or Calyrex using Psychic to lift them into the air, they can both fly.

Flying greatly enhances Calyrex's mobility, and with its ghostly Psychic abilities, D feels that Calyrex is almost invincible now.

"I wonder if it's enough to fight Arceus..." D fell into thought.

To his knowledge, Arceus is not at full power right now, having lost five Origin Creation Plates.

Electric, Grass, Water, Ground, and Dragon.

These five plates together form the Life Orb, which is currently missing.

Plus the Type Matchups and mutual neutralizations between different plates...
