"That means currently only Electric Type, Grass Type, and Water Type moves can hurt Arceus," D thought to himself.

Ground Type moves will be negated by the Flying Plate, and Dragon Type moves will be neutralized by the Fairy Plate.

---

That's precisely why, in the movie, even when missing a few Plates, Arceus can still easily defeat its three little subordinates, almost killing them.

If Dialga hadn't used the Power of Time to send Ash back in time at the crucial moment, they probably would have been wiped out.

Speaking of the Pokemon on the ranch, there's Pawmo and Zapdos for Electric Type.

For Grass Type, there's Gogoat and Calyrex.

For Water Type Pokemon... we can temporarily borrow Kyogre.

As a Trainer, he can also use a Tera Orb to perform Terastal on Calyrex, enhancing its Grass Type Moves.

But D always feels that this still isn't enough.

Besides the Pokemon's inherent strength, perhaps he can also boost their power through external means.

Like Mega Evolution, Z-moves, Dynamax, and Terastalization.

Let's not talk about Dynamax for now, because that thing can only be used in the Galar Region.

None of the Pokemon on the ranch can Mega Evolve either.

So he'll have to focus on the Z-moves in the Alola Region.

Coincidentally, D plans to buy a villa ranch in the Alola Region so he can take his family's Pokemon there for a holiday during the cold winter months.

No sooner said than done, D immediately takes out his computer and logs onto the Hau'oli Real Estate Company's website.

Soon enough, someone from the real estate company calls to inquire about his needs.

"Hello sir, this is Hau'oli Real Estate Company from the Alola Region. Are you looking to move and settle in the Alola Region?"

"I want to buy a villa there, preferably with a large open space where Pokemon can play."

"Alright, I'll help you find a suitable home, and will contact you immediately once there's one that fits."

The call ends, and all that's left is for him to wait patiently.

It's already November 20th today, and New Year is next month, so even if D buys the place, he won't be able to move immediately.

"Looks like I'll have to wait until next year. "

Just then, a message pops up in the Legendary Beast chat group.

D opens it to find that Giratina has posted a video.

The video content is of Calyrex just now trouncing Groudon and Kyogre.

D: "..."

Giratina seems to never get enough of watching the drama.

[Giratina: Why do you two have to fight every day? Last time was the same, and this time as well. D says you've fought many times, haven't you determined who's stronger? I really want to know which one of you is more powerful @Groudon@Kyogre.]

Perhaps out of boredom and unable to fight, after returning to their nests, Groudon and Kyogre start fiddling with the Rotom Phone.

Once the Rotom reads out Giratina's question aloud, the competitive spirit in the two Super Ancient Pokemon flares up.

They immediately instruct Rotom to reply to the Pokemon called Giratina.

[Kyogre: Of course I'm stronger!]

[Groudon: I see, you don't take me seriously. If you weren't always interrupted by those people, you'd have lost already.]

[Kyogre: Hmph, it's not certain who would lose. I think it should be you, overestimating yourself.]

Groudon is infuriated.

[Groudon: What? Want to try?]

Kyogre is not one to back down either.

[Kyogre: Bring it on! Who's afraid?]

But as soon as they replied, they remembered their promise to D.

Both Pokemon fall silent.

After a long pause, Kyogre, getting anxious, starts trash-talking Groudon in the group.

[Kyogre: Some Pokemon can't even fly, huh. The other day, who was the fool standing in the sea looking up at the sky anxiously?]

Groudon: !!!

The magma below churns up from Groudon's anger.

It roars, asking Rotom to retort on its behalf.

[Groudon: You're the fool!]

Yet in the face of facts, its response seems weak and powerless.

[Kyogre: Suddenly speechless, aren't you?]

At that moment, Groudon just wants to punch out Kyogre, but because of its promise, it can't, so it can only jump up and down angrily, causing the ground to shake.

Then, Giratina, ever the instigator, comes up with a nifty idea.

With the notion of turning red first to drive the others, it suggests a clever plan to the bickering Pokemon.

[Giratina: Enough with the arguing. I have an idea! You two can be like me and Calyrex and register for a Pokemon Forum account to see who gets more followers!]

---

---

---

---

# 484: Chapter 481: On a Cold Winter's Day

In the end, Groudon and Kyogre still didn't register an account on the Pokemon Forum.

Because their legendary pride makes them disdain using such methods to attract humans.

Giratina's persuasion plan failed; it originally wanted to see if these two Pokemon could surpass its fan base, but it seems they have no such desire.

Groudon and Kyogre, who wouldn't give in to each other, are still quarreling in the group, indulging in verbal spats.

D initially wanted to stop them, but after thinking it over, he decided to let them argue, as long as they don't fight physically, it's all fine.

Thus, the footsteps of time quietly passed by, and in a blink, it reached the year-end of League Year 200.

The New Year is approaching. On this evening, D specially drove his pickup truck to the town to buy New Year's supplies.

It's deep winter already, and there might be a heavy snow in a couple of days, which could bury the road leading to the town.

Therefore, D plans to prepare more supplies while the weather is nice today.

Parking by the roadside in Porto Marinada Town, D jumped off the truck with several Pokemon.

"Steaks, flour, rice, snacks... and firewood."

D read aloud the items listed on his shopping list, walking toward his destination.

People in the Pokemon World also celebrate the New Year; although there's still some time before the new year, the streets are already filled with festive atmosphere.

Pokemon wearing scarves, children in red cotton coats — just looking at them feels joyful.

On both sides of the road, there are workers and Machamp carrying fireworks.

These fireworks will be set off on the evening of New Year's Day.

Some town shopkeepers recognize D, and when he passes by, they greet him with smiles.

"Good morning, D! Taking your Pokemon out to shop?"

D smiled and replied: "Yes! New Year is coming, buying some supplies back home so we can 'hibernate' comfortably when the time comes!"

"Hibernate?" The florist lady heard this unfamiliar term and showed an intrigued expression.

On the flower racks beside her, several Hoppip and Skiploom stood lazily.

"It means staying home warmly through winter, like Skitty cuddling in a warm den to sleep on snowy days." D explained with a laugh.

The shop owner suddenly understood: "That's quite nice. Oh right, we're having an event at the store. To thank customers who have shopped here before, we're gifting a pot of flowers. You've come at just the right time, this pot is for you!"

Saying that, the shop owner handed over a pot of blooming flowers.

D had previously bought a lot of flower seeds from her shop and was a big customer.

Seeing this, D didn't refuse and accepted the pot with a smile, handing it to Koraidon next to him: "Hold this!"

"Ah-ga-sii..." Koraidon obediently held the flower pot with its paws.

"We've got things to do, so let's catch up later, see you!" D bid farewell to the shopkeeper.

"Alright, see you later. Next year we'll have a batch of flowers unique to the Kalos Region, remember to come and check them out!"

"No problem!"

Leaving the flower shop, D headed towards the department store.

Ogerpon and Alcremie are very familiar with this place. As soon as they entered, several Pokemon eagerly looked at D.

D pushed a cart and laughed, saying to them: "Go ahead, pick what you want and bring it over! I'll cover it."

"Bo-ni~"

"Bam-oh~"

"..."

The Pokemon made happy sounds and then ran towards the shelves.

D followed them with his cart, took out his Rotom Phone, and snapped a photo to send to Lono.

[Mighty Wing: Shopping with Pokemon at the supermarket, what are you up to?]

Lono quickly replied.

[Lono: Listening to my mom's chatter! Those two are really something, didn't go anywhere at all this year.]

D felt a tug at his heart; he had heard Lono talk about her parents.

Supposedly, they were both Trainers in their youth, fell for each other at first sight during their travels, and then got together.

After the journey ended, the duo married with Pokemon and settled down in the bustling city of Levincia.

Are they nagging Lono thinking she fell in love too soon?

[Mighty Wing: Do they think you're in love too early?]

[Lono: Huh? No way, they're hoping I get married sooner, and I'm already an adult, okay! Puffing with anger.jpg]

D: "..."

The image of Lono puffed up like a little puffer fish popped into D's mind, and he couldn't help but laugh.

At that moment, Ogerpon and the others bounced back, clearly they had picked what they liked.

Seeing this, D put his Rotom Phone away, took a tour in the supermarket again with the Pokemon, and after purchasing the items he wanted, he left the place.

Loading the goods onto the pickup truck, ensuring nothing was missed, D started the vehicle and returned to the ranch.

Before they even got out of the truck, Greavard was already running towards them from afar.

That look was precisely like a dog greeting its owner home.

To guard against the upcoming snow, D parked the truck in the garage, then opened the door and got out, picking up the approaching Greavard.

Greavard excitedly wagged its tongue, then licked D's hand.

"Alright, alright!" D patted its head, saying: "Let's go home!"

---

"Woof!"

Excitedly, Greavard circled around D.

D looked helpless, trying not to step on Greavard while moving the goods from the car into the house with Koraidon and the others.

After just finishing that task, dense snowflakes began to fall from the white sky.

The cold north wind howled by, and from the distant hills came a faint "woo woo" sound, as if the wind was sobbing.

Standing by the window and looking into the distance, all he could see was a vast expanse of white.

Seeing this scene, D let out a long sigh of relief.

Luckily, he had prepared ahead and moved Snorlax, Wooloo, Butterfree, and Combee to their designated places.

Outside, the snow was falling heavily, yet inside the house was warm and cozy.

In the fireplace, Slugma leisurely ate Berries, and the warmth from its body spread through the pipes to every corner of the room.

Basking in this pleasant warm air, Cyclizar wore a contented expression on its face, lying comfortably on the carpet.

Cyclizar: (˵¯͒¯͒˵)

Pawmot pawed at the window, gazing at the heavy snowfall outside. It called to its friends behind it, seemingly wanting Slugma to come and take a look too.

Sure enough, Slugma, hearing the noise, moved slowly over. It settled in the pipe behind Pawmot, peering through the opening to look at the snow outside.

"Woo?"

"Buuh...(⋟﹏⋞)"

It looks so cold out there!

"If you find the outside too scary, you can close the curtain!" D said with a smile, passing behind Pawmot.

Pawmot immediately turned around, patting its chest: "Buuh!"

It wasn't scared at all! It's a brave Pawmot!

As the sky outside gradually darkened, D petted Pawmot's head and then headed to the kitchen to prepare dinner for everyone.

Given the chilly weather today, soup would be perfect.

Spicy and fragrant, the steaming hot soup felt so comforting once it filled the stomach!

Just thinking about it made one's fingers itch in anticipation.

Since that's the case, let's make a pot of rich soup for dinner!

With that thought, D turned around and took out a few potatoes, a piece of beef, and some other ingredients from the cupboard.

He diced the potatoes and beef, then added the ingredients into the pot to stew until the potatoes and beef were tender and flavorful.

The pot bubbled with steam, and the enticing aroma gradually wafted throughout the room. The Pokemon, playing in the living room, caught a whiff of the smell and swallowed, then were drawn over.

"Take a seat at the dining table first, dinner will be ready soon!"

"Gah-s!"

Led by Koraidon, many Pokemon immediately dashed to the dining room.

Meanwhile, D turned off the stove and took a spoonful of the soup to taste it.

Hmm, it had a mildly spicy taste, but it wasn't too strong. The soup was infused with the rich fragrance of stewed potatoes and the aroma of beef. Drinking it dispelled all the coldness, enveloping one in warmth.

Perfect!

D glanced at the soup's effect and found that, unsurprisingly, it provided Cold Resistance, as he expected.

Concerned that the Pokemon might not be full, D also prepared some Berries and snacks, placing them on the dining table.

"Let's eat!"

All the Pokemon began to feast eagerly.

Being able to enjoy a warm bowl of soup during a cold winter day truly brought happiness!

After dinner, D boiled another pot of hot water, lying in the bathtub to enjoy a relaxing soak.

Pawmot and Eevee, being smaller Pokemon, also came running in, lazily floating in the bathtub.

Beside them, the big Pokemon led by Koraidon couldn't fit in, so they sat pitifully next to it, watching.

"Gah-s..."

D: "..."

At this moment, the Rotom Phone suddenly rang.

Someone was making a video call!

D picked it up and saw that it was Cynthia calling.

He silently hung up the video call and dialed a voice call instead.

"Hello? Miss Cynthia, why are you calling at this time?" D asked.

Cynthia's cool voice came from the other end: "I'm very sorry, I have a perplexing matter that I would like you to resolve."

Surprised, D asked, "Hmm? What is it?"

Cynthia: "As it is, ever since you told me about those places related to the Creator God last time, I went to those places to investigate."

Cynthia slowly recounted her experiences after she left the ranch last time.

That time, D pointed her in three directions.

They were the Ruins of Alph in the Johto Region, Midina in the Sinnoh Region, and Arche Valley in the Kalos Region.

The first place she investigated was the Ruins of Alph, where she discovered a large number of Unown along with some ancient Sinnoh People murals migrating to the Johto Region.

After that, Cynthia returned to the Sinnoh Region, went to Midina, and there met Sheena and Kevin, who possessed Triumphant Light and were chosen by Arceus, the guardians of Midina's ruins.

There, Cynthia learned about Arceus and the Life Orb.

After gathering this information, Cynthia went to the Kalos Region to visit the people hiding in Arche Valley.

The people there worship Arceus and have even built special statues to honor Him.

Furthermore, Cynthia also met a mischievous Pokemon named Hoopa, who lived there with the people of Arche Valley.

"Is Arceus the god who created the world we live in?" Cynthia asked.

D replied, "If the records are correct, then yes, that's the case."

He paused, then asked, "I wonder if Miss Cynthia has learned about the history, several hundred years ago, when the Sinnoh Region was still called the Hisuin Region."

Cynthia was slightly startled: "Hisuin Region?"

D nodded: "I once learned some related information in an ancient ruin; that ruin is a remnant from the Hisuin Era."

"It's said that about 1000-2000 years ago, the earliest humans living in Hisuin were called the [Ancient Sinnoh People]."

"They worshiped the Pokemon Arceus, who created the world, and built ancient sites in Hisuin, such as the Solaceon Ruins and Snowpoint Temple."

"That ancient history clearly stated that the world was created by Arceus."

Upon hearing this, Cynthia nodded thoughtfully.

Arceus created the world, after which He also created the Pokemon Dialga and Palkia, who manage time and space, to uphold the material foundation of this world.

Simultaneously, Giratina was created, watching over another space, supporting each other with the real world.

Then came the Lake Gods, Mesprit, Uxie, and Azelf...

Recalling the mythological tales she had investigated, for a moment, Cynthia felt her mind clear.

Her heart was filled with joy because this meant that the ancient myths and legends circulating in the Sinnoh Region had been thoroughly investigated.

For a myth scholar, this was akin to hitting the jackpot.

"Thank you very much, Mr. D; since that's the case, I won't disturb your rest anymore. When I have time later, I will personally visit you in the Paldea Region," Cynthia said softly.

"No need to be polite."

After a brief exchange, they ended the call.

The water in the bathtub cooled down a bit, so D stood up, dried himself, changed into pajamas, and then helped dry the wet fur of Pawmot with a hairdryer.

Pawmot sat indulgently on the chair. Once its fur was dry, it enthusiastically hopped onto the bed, ready to sleep.

D chuckled, extended his hand to pat Koraidon's head beside him, and then left the room to check whether the doors and windows were securely closed.

He glanced outside the window as he went, and saw that the outdoors were already completely covered in white snow.

The wind blew, trees swayed, snow flew, and crows cawed outside.

D pulled the curtains, instantly isolating everything outside.

"Phew... time for bed!"

D turned and went back to the room, lay down on the bed, and, with the light and warmth from Slugma, comfortably closed his eyes.

Pawmot lay comfortably beside the pillow, and a group of Eevee also ran up, curling up beside D, and comfortably drifted off to sleep.

---

---

---

---

# 485: Chapter 482: Raifort and the Treasures of Ruin

The next day, the heavy snow that fell all night still showed no signs of stopping.

Looking out the window, the snowy landscape stretched far into the distance, as if the whole world had turned into a world of ice and snow.

Snowflakes fluttered down, and without much thought, D could guess that a thick layer of snow had already covered the roof of his house.

Trapped indoors by the blizzard, after finishing breakfast, D had nothing to do and sat by the fireplace reading a book.

The Pokémon seemed unfazed.

They continued to frolic happily around the room as before or simply lay quietly beside D's rocking chair, keeping him company as he read.

For them, just being by D's side seemed to bring endless joy.

Despite being unable to go outside, D found rare tranquility listening to the sound of snow falling outside.

He actually quite enjoyed the feeling of hiding in a warm home with his Pokémon during these hushed moments.

Just then, the Rotom Phone resting nearby suddenly rang.

D picked it up and found it was a message from Raifort.

[Raifort: I finally solved the mystery of the Treasures of Calamity!]

[Emperor: What do you mean?]

[Raifort: These past days, I've been researching a large amount of data and records. Now, I've finally pinpointed the exact locations of the shrines sealing the four Treasures of Calamity! Next, we just need to go to those places to break the seals, and those four artifacts can reappear in the world!]

Mesagoza City, faculty dormitory.

Raifort, quite excited, informed D across from her of her discovery and sent over the related photos she had found.

[Raifort: It's right here! In the past, Pokémon envoys used sacred stakes to seal the four Treasures of Calamity in the shrines.]

[Raifort: Each Pokémon corresponds to one shrine. It's said that eight stakes were placed near each shrine to suppress the Power of Calamity. So, if you pull out all the corresponding stakes, you can release the Pokémon known as the Treasures of Calamity!!]

According to Raifort's research, the patterns on the shrines were variants of the "Fortune" character and shou circular patterns.

Of course, those were not the Paldea League's exclusive characters but ones from the distant Eastern Lands, the homeland of the Treasures of Calamity.

In that ancient nation, these patterns were generally considered symbols of "warding off evil."

[Emperor: The Power of Calamity? If we hastily remove the stakes and break the seal, won't it cause the disaster that once struck the Paldea Kingdom to reoccur in the world?]

D recalled the scenes he saw when exploring the Great Crater of Paldea with Raifort before.

That ancient Paldea Royal City was buried underground forever due to the earthquake caused by Ting-Lu.

[Raifort: Of course not! According to the descendants of the Pokémon envoy who sealed the Treasures of Calamity, to remove the stakes, one must have a bond with Pokémon. This bond will calm the four ruinous Pokémon.]

[Raifort: If it's someone like you who excels in using Pokémon, you might be able to do it, so I must ask for your help this time too! Please help me one last time!]

D: "..."

He remembered that in the game, Raifort entrusted the player with everything.

But now it seemed Raifort wanted to witness it herself.

[Emperor: Are you coming with me?]

[Raifort: Hmm, I'm afraid not. I originally planned to go, but since I skipped classes to go on adventures, the principal has been watching me... so, I'm relying on you, D!]

D was speechless; Raifort truly lived up to her tomb-raiding roots!

In his mind, he thought about those Treasures of Calamity capable of bringing disaster.

If he reversed their traits with a series of inversion dishes, the world would gain four "auspicious beasts."

[Emperor: Okay, but it's snowing heavily right now, and we're preparing for New Year at the ranch. I'll come over after the New Year.]

[Raifort: No problem! I've been waiting for so long, a few more days won't matter, but after you catch the Pokémon, make sure to show them to me!]

D agreed decisively.

[Raifort: I'll send you the exact locations of the Sealing Shrine now. You can register them on your map app; the Rotom Phone will automatically navigate for you. Even though some shrines are halfway up the mountain, with Koraidon, you'll definitely reach them smoothly.]

With that, Raifort shared a link.

D opened it, and the phone interface automatically switched to the Pokémon map app.

Then, four markers appeared on the screen.

The Dust-shrouded Shrine sealing Ting-Lu was located in the foothills north of Casseroya Lake.

The Icerend Shrine sealing Chien-Pao was south of the Asado Desert, at the end of a mountain trail.

The Firescourge Shrine sealing Chi-Yu was located beneath a waterfall at one of Paldea's top ten scenic spots, Fury Falls.

D remembered that place well; it had a beautiful bamboo forest, surrounded by streams, with a very picturesque view.

And the Grasswither Shrine sealing Wo-Chien was located beneath a cliff in the southeastern part of Paldea.

After a quick glance, D closed the map app and sent Raifort an OK gesture.

[Raifort: I'll be waiting for your good news over here in Mesagoza City!]

With the conversation over, D silently pondered his next plans.

There were many things to do next year. To repay Giratina for helping raise the World Tree into the sky, he needed to help Giratina "teach" Arceus a lesson.

At that time, the Grass Type Calyrex would undoubtedly be a main force.

Before then, he needed to do his best to help Calyrex enhance its strength.

Acquiring Calyrex's Grassium Z was one such task.

With the Z Ring, it would allow Calyrex to use the more powerful Z Move [Bloom Doom], launching a full-force assault on Arceus.

Alright... he simply wanted to see to what extent the fully-realized Calyrex, empowered by millions of believers, could match up against Arceus's incarnation.

For the Grassium Z and the Z Ring, he could try to curry favor with the four island guardians of the Alola Region, possibly earning their approval and obtaining these two items.

Therefore, D planned to buy a villa in the Alola Region.

That way, they could stay in the Paldea Region during the normal spring, summer, and fall.

When the cold winter arrived, they would head to that tropical area for a holiday with everyone from the ranch.

Speak of the devil.

A call came through from the Hau'oli Real Estate Company in Alola Region's Haoao City.

"Hello, is this Mr. D? This is Hau'oli Real Estate Company. We've found a house that suits your requirements."

"Hmm? Where is it?" D asked.

"On Akala Island in the northeastern part of the Alola Region, someone is selling a villa with a ranch there."

Akala Island...

D checked it on his phone and quickly found a satellite image of Akala Island.

The image showed that the terrain of this island was very flat, with a large volcano to the north, a hill with terraced waterfalls to the northwest, and a jungle to the south.

On the southwestern side of the island was a peninsula consisting of Diglett's Tunnel and Konikoni City.

At the center of the island, surrounded by pastures and meadows, was Paniola Town.

---

---

---

---

# 486: Chapter 483: Purchasing Land

Compared to other regions, the Alola Region is a very special one.

There hasn't been a Pokémon League established there yet, but it is protected by four Pokémon known as the Island Guardians.

These four Pokémon are the Legendary Pokémon of the Alola Region, the Tapu family.

And on Akala Island, there is also an Island Guardian, which is Tapu Lele.

If D remembers correctly, Kiawe's home is also in that place.

Compared to the other main islands, Akala Island really is a good place to open a ranch.

Volcanic ash that once floated out from the volcano fell to the ground, becoming nutrients for the soil, making that land particularly fertile.

Thinking of this, D made a decision in his heart.

He said to the property company staff on the other end of the phone: "Can I take a look at the house first?"

"Of course you can, are you in the Alola Region? I can come to pick you up and go together to Akala Island for an inspection."

D initially wanted to say no, but he thought again, if he could take a shortcut through Giratina's Reversed World, it would only take two to three hours to reach Alola.

Coincidentally, it's snowing heavily in the Paldea Region, with nothing to do, so he decided to take this opportunity to visit the Alola Region and check out the house.

"I have some matters to handle at the moment, probably in two hours I can reach Hau'oli City." D said.

"No problem, I'll be waiting for you here."

After the communication ended, they added each other as friends, and the other quickly sent a location share.

D clicked to take a glance, then told his sister Indeedee, and prepared to ride Koraidon to visit the Alola Region.

"You all play at home, I'm going out for a bit and will be back soon, and Greavard, remember to eat well at home."

Saying that, D reached out and patted Greavard's head.

"Woof!"

Greavard barked, wagging its tail happily, seeing D off, standing in the snow, watching them go far away.

Even when D's figure was completely out of sight, Greavard was still wagging its tail and looking in the direction.

Pawmot and Cyclizar were also looking that way.

Suddenly, a cold gust carrying snowflakes swept in.

Cyclizar shivered instantly.

Sister Indeedee stepped forward, gently saying to them: "Ai yi." (Come inside first.)

Only then did the Pokémon return to the house.

Without D at home, Cyclizar and others felt extremely bored.

The Eevees had gone off to play on the World Tree, and Alcremie went to the Salt House to play with Garganacl.

Now only the Indeedee siblings, Greavard, Pawmo, Cyclizar, Flutter Mane, Dragonite, and Dratini were left at home.

At this time, Pawmo suddenly thought of a way that wasn't so boring.

It excitedly looked at everyone, saying: "Bamor!" (Let's play hide and seek!)

*

Meanwhile, D and Koraidon were speeding along in the Reversed World.

After nearly two hours of flying, they finally arrived in the Alola Region.

Standing on the bustling street, looking around, people and Pokémon from various regions crowded everywhere.

According to the map app, this was Hau'oli City on Melemele Island.

As the most popular tourist city in the Pokémon World, further buoyed by the season, the liveliness of Hau'oli City truly topped the charts.

Seeing so many people, D felt overwhelmed.

He definitely didn't want to be swept away by the crowd, quickly heading aside with Koraidon, taking out his phone for navigation.

Fortunately, the property company in Hau'oli City wasn't too far from here; crossing a few streets, one human and one Pokémon stood before the company's door.

D went upstairs, expressing his purpose, and soon a man dressed in a business suit came over.

"Are you Mr. D?"

"It's me." D nodded slightly.

"Welcome to the Alola Region, please wait a moment, and I'll take you to visit that ranch on Akala Island."

The man turned, assigning his work to a colleague, then returned to D, saying:

"Alright, let's go! By the way, what mode of transport do you prefer to reach Akala Island? Take a yacht to enjoy the beautiful tropical ocean scenery, or directly take a flying Pokémon taxi?"

"Let's fly directly!" D thought for a moment, saying, "My Pokémon can fly directly."

Hearing this, the man looked curiously at Koraidon by his side, "That's a very strong Pokémon! Since that's the case, let's head over directly!"

With that, the man also took out a Poke Ball.

Along with a flash of white light, a Charizard appeared before D.

The man mounted Charizard, while D mounted Koraidon, heading together toward Akala Island.

Gradually, the earth beneath became farther away, Hau'oli City was left behind, below was a shimmering blue ocean, above was a clear blue sky, several chirping Wingulls flapping their wings as they flew.

The cool sea breeze gently blew, it must be said, the climate in the Alola Region is incredibly pleasant.

"You're not from Alola, right?" the man chatted.

"No, I'm from the Paldea Region." D nodded.

"No wonder, I've heard that agricultural products from the Paldea Region are very popular! Every month, special cargo ships bring loads of agricultural products to Alola." the man said with a smile, seemingly very fond of Paldea's agricultural products.

---

"Perhaps it's because many farmers in Paldea are devoted to Calyrex The King of Bountiful Harvests?" The High Priest of the Cult of Calyrex began his sermon again.

Sure enough, the man in front was drawn to the topic.

"Calyrex? Is that the Legendary Pokemon that can go online?"

"That's right, it's truly amazing that there are Pokemon in the world that can use human devices." D pretended to be a mere passerby, speaking with a straight face.

"Pokemon are quite intelligent; my Charizard watches movies with me." The man patted Charizard on the head.

The Charizard snorted out a flame, excitedly responding to its Trainer.

Both Pokemon flew swiftly, and before long, a lush island appeared on the distant sea.

A volcano stood on the island, surrounded by abundant vegetation.

Seeing this, the man said, "We've arrived! This is Akala Island! I remember the location of the ranch... near Paniola Town!"

As they reached their destination, Charizard began to descend.

D and Koraidon followed closely, and after passing through a thick meadow, they saw a quaint town with a strong western style.

The town was small, with primitive dirt roads between them, filled with the flavor of Western cowboys.

D even felt as if a cowboy in a jacket would charge down the road at any moment, firing shots at another gang.

However, fortunately, his imagination did not come true. The two continued forward, soon leaving the town and flying further into the field.

Gradually, everything around them became spacious. As far as the eye could see, a vast plain came into view.

Fertile pasture grew on the plain, and a small river meandered through it.

Not far away, a fenced ranch lay on this plain.

"We're here; this is it!"

The man landed with Charizard in the ranch, followed closely by D and Koraidon.

"The owner of this ranch is a young person who moved with their family to the Kalos Region not long ago. They intend to sell the place for 2 million League Coins."

2 million...

This large piece of land along with the villa is actually not very expensive.

"I'll take a look around first." D said, showing expertise in this area.

"No problem." The man nodded.

D took a tour of the ranch, discovering complete facilities.

The barns for Miltank, enclosures for Mareep, stables for Mudbray, lodging for Pokemon like Lillipup.

D knew, such places typically use Pokemon like Lillipup to guard the ranch.

Additionally, there's an open field for Tauros to run around.

At first sight of the field, D was impressed.

"It's said that the previous owner raised fifty Tauros here, and they run around the field daily; it's quite a sight!" The man introduced, looking at the field.

D nodded, surveyed the surroundings, and asked, "What about farmland? Is there suitable farming land here?"

"Farmland? Of course there is." The man led D to a large area of neglected land: "However, due to the climate of the Alola region, some crops from other regions aren't suitable for planting here."

"Locals in Alola typically grow crops and fruit trees like coconut trees, guavas, mangoes, bananas, pineapples, coffee beans, and sugarcane."

Coffee beans can be made into coffee, sugarcane can be processed into sugar; these Alola specialties often get sold to other regions.

D nodded thoughtfully.

The Alola Region has a typical tropical marine monsoon climate, with small annual temperature differences, warm and humid throughout the year, abundant rainfall.

All of this allows for lush plant growth here, with wild trees mainly consisting of palm species and tree-like ferns.

Having finished the outdoor tour, next up would be checking out the villa.

Though called a villa, it wasn't very large.

The house had three stories, much bigger than D's current residence.

Its internal features were complete: living room, kitchen, dining room, bedroom, guest room, study, wine cellar, and an oversized bathroom.

D nodded in satisfaction.

"This is it."

After deciding, the two returned to Melemele Island.

The distances between islands in the Alola region aren't far, and even riding a regular flying Pokemon can cover them within twenty minutes, let alone fast Pokemon like Charizard and Dragonite.

D paid the money, then got the land deed and property certificate.

From now on, that piece of land and the ranch belonged to him!

With the certificate, D looked around, ready to bring back some gifts for the Pokemon at the ranch.

The Alola region has many specialties, the most famous being an oil-fried bread called Malasada.

D and Koraidon bought several large bags from a roadside shop to take back to the Pokemon at the ranch.

Additionally, he bought some tropical fruits like coconuts and pineapples, which are rare in the Paldea region.

---

Next, D planned to head to a secluded lakeside to contact Giratina and have it open the door to the Reversed World.

But just then, D suddenly heard a familiar voice.

"D?"

He turned around and saw Geeta standing not far away, wearing a bikini, looking at him with surprise.

"What a coincidence, Chief!" D smiled and greeted Geeta, remembering that Lono had mentioned Geeta was vacationing in the Alola Region this year.

Geeta: "Are you vacationing in the Alola Region too?"

D shook his head: "Just here for some business, I'll be heading back soon."

He didn't tell Geeta that he was buying a house in Alola, worried that she'd think he was running away.

As expected, Geeta quietly breathed a sigh of relief upon hearing this.

She smiled: "In that case, hurry on! If you ever need help, feel free to contact me."

Geeta had indeed helped him a lot, whether it was with the Eevee Egg or the ancient gold coin for Gholdengo's evolution. Thinking of this, D smiled and nodded:

"Then I won't disturb your vacation, Chief!"

Geeta clasped her hands and laughed softly: "I'm really looking forward to your and Lono's wedding! Let me be your witness then!"

D: "...Isn't it too early for that?"

He noticed something amiss on Geeta's face, recalling the fireworks festival, when he suddenly realized that Geeta seemed quite concerned about his marriage plans.

However, Geeta gently shook her head: "Time flies. Look, in just three years the world has changed so much, maybe we'll be old before we know it!"

D sincerely asked: "Are you planning to get married, Chief?"

If he remembered correctly, Geeta would be thirty in a few years.

Geeta: "Maybe after I find the next Chief Champion for Paldea? But all that depends on destiny."

D: "..."

After parting ways with Geeta, D returned to the Paldea Region through the Reversed World.

When he arrived at the ranch, the Blizzard finally stopped.

D and Koraidon carried bags into the house, only to see Pawmot's silhouette.

D was quite surprised: "Where is everyone?"

Pawmot: "Pawmo!" (We're playing hide and seek!)

D immediately understood, and he said to Pawmot: "Pause the game; I brought you some tasty treats from outside, come and try them!"

Then he shouted into the house: "Everyone, come out!"

In the next moment, all sorts of Pokemon dashed out from all directions.

In the blink of an eye, a group of Pokemon appeared before D.

D took Malasadas and other specialities from his bag and distributed them to the Pokemon.

Cyclizar received a piece of Malasada, seemingly enjoying its taste.

Greavard picked up a tropical fruit, wagging its tail while eating.

The Indeedee Sibling accepted the pineapple D handed over, having seen this fruit before, they knew how to handle it.

Flutter Mane hugged a coconut with its wing claws.

It looked left and right, then imitated Pawmot eating chocolate beans and took a big bite.

With a crunch, the coconut remained untouched, while Flutter Mane widened its eyes in shock, clearly that hurt its teeth.

"Mane!"

Flutter Mane was incredulous, quickly covering its aching mouth with wing claws.

D: "..."

Is that how you eat coconuts?

He hurriedly went forward to check Flutter Mane's teeth, luckily the ancient Pokemon's teeth were sturdy enough, preventing any injury.

---

---

---

---

# 487: Chapter 484: Zapdos and Lightning Steamed Bun

League Year 200, December 15th, Winter.

On this morning, the Heroic Expedition Team that had been traveling for a year returned to the ranch.

Along with their return came Jirachi, the Seven-Night Wishing Star.

Accompanied by Zapdos landing in the ranch, Jirachi happily dove into D's arms.

This Pokemon's tone was filled with joy: "D! We're back!"

Mew and Teddiursa also ran over, and D cuddled the three Pokemon in his arms playfully, until their fur was all ruffled before letting go.

"Was the journey enjoyable?" D asked with a smile.

Mew and Teddiursa shook their bodies, trying to smooth down their raised fur.

Without fur, Jirachi replied: "It was fun! I saw so many things and made lots of friends!"

"That's good." D looked at them covered in snow, then called the few Pokemon into the living room to prepare some snacks for them.

The four Pokemon immediately feasted, their cheeks stuffed with food.

D watched this scene with a smile.

"Oh, by the way, while you were away, the World Tree has grown, and with the help of Giratina's power, it has already flown into the sky!"

"What?!"

"miu?"

Upon hearing this, Mew and Teddiursa showed expressions of surprise on their faces.

D nodded: "You'll be able to go over and take a look later."

The few Pokemon happily agreed.

And off to the side, Zapdos also looked eager.

It originally came to this place for the World Tree, and now, has that tree finally grown?

Zapdos trembled with excitement.

A yellow feather slowly floated down naturally from its body.

Indeedee, standing beside, noticed the feather.

Cleaning was its job, so Indeedee immediately picked up a broom and swept the feather into the trash can.

D, sitting on the sofa, keenly saw this scene.

With just a glance, the feather's information appeared in his mind.

[Feather of Zapdos: A feather of Zapdos, containing formidable Thunder Power, perhaps it can be used to make dishes, effect unknown.]

Zapdos dropped a feather?

"Wait!" D quickly called out to stop.

Indeedee's actions paused, standing beside the trash can with the feather, looking over in confusion.

And off to the side, Zapdos, Mew, and Teddiursa all turned their heads to look at the feather.

Zapdos immediately recognized it, wasn't that its feather?

Shedding feathers was not a surprising thing for it, as every year, many feathers naturally shed from its body.

"That feather is not trash, don't throw it away, hand it to me!" D said to Indeedee.

Though puzzled, Indeedee still handed over the feather.

As D looked up, he saw Zapdos staring at him with sharp eyes.

D: "..."

Zapdos: ?

What does D want with its dropped feather? Does he want to keep it as a collection?

But it's just a common feather with no value, isn't it?

Zapdos looked puzzled.

D thought about it and realized that instead of awkwardly making the Lightning Steamed Bun and being caught by the owner later, it would be better to frankly tell Zapdos his plan.

So, he began explaining: "This isn't just a normal feather; with my ability, I can turn it into a dish with magical effect. Once a Pokemon eats it, it can become stronger."

Upon hearing this, Zapdos felt slightly speechless, full of disbelief.

...To eat a feather that fell off its body?

Ugh! Just thinking about it feels a bit nauseating!

Is that really something that can be eaten?

D quickly sensed Zapdos's thoughts. If it were to be described in human terms, this action might be like making cuisine using human sweat or hair?

Ew... That's indeed quite disgusting.

D awkwardly explained to Zapdos: "Although it sounds a bit awful, the final product is quite good; Pawmot has eaten Lightning Steamed Bun before, which is why he has grown into what he is today."

Pawmot... Zapdos knows that Pokemon.

The other possesses powerful electric energy, not less than Zapdos.

But D says this Pawmot also ate Lightning Steamed Bun... when was that?

"Chirp?"

D cheerfully replied: "It was when the Heroic Expedition Team first encountered you, Teddiursa found one of your feathers and gave it to me."

After D's mention, Mew and Teddiursa also remembered.

"What..." It seems that did happen!

Zapdos: "..."

Whatever, as long as I'm not the one eating it, let D do whatever he wants!

After traveling outside for a year, not feeling tired out there, now back in the familiar ranch, within this safe environment, the few Pokemon felt exhausted, tired and powerless.

D noticed their condition and said: "If you're tired, just go and have a good sleep!"

The few Pokemon obediently went to sleep.

They did not go to the World Tree and stayed directly at home.

Zapdos started sleeping standing on the floor, while Mew, Teddiursa, and Jirachi ran to D's bed.

The three Pokemon nestled together and started dreaming.

---

D went to the glass greenhouse to find Calyrex, asking it to use telekinesis to crush this feather into powder form.

Soon, he returned home with a jar of Zapdos's feather powder.

D took some of it to make Lightning Steamed Buns, reserving the rest for future use.

Mix the dough, ferment, knead the dough, add the feather powder, then shape into buns, and finally, place them in the pot to steam.

With that, five Lightning Steamed Buns were completed.

This whole process took quite some time, and by the time the buns were done steaming, the Pokémon sleeping in the room had awakened.

When D placed these five yellow, round buns in front of them, both Zapdos and Mew were incredibly shocked.

Because they could feel a powerful energy emanating from these buns!

Zapdos was full of astonishment and shock.

This was food made from its feathers?

[Lightning Steamed Bun (S+): Legendary Cuisine, made from the feather powder of legendary Pokémon, said to bestow control over lightning upon consumption.

Cooking Effect: Thunder God, upon consumption allows the Pokémon to gain the Power of Thunder and Lightning, enabling control over thunder and lightning at will like the feather's owner, capable of summoning massive lightning with a flick of a finger, exclusive to Electric Type Pokémon, and only once per Pokémon.

Evaluation: A unique delicacy in the world, feed it to your Electric Type Pokémon! Warning, do not consume indoors!]

D looked at the information on the buns with a smile, then turned to look at Zapdos, and asked:

"How is it? Want to try one? Eating it will make you more powerful!"

His tone was full of temptation.

Instantly, Zapdos showed a hesitant expression.

It wanted to get stronger, but the thought of the dish being made from its own feathers made it feel a bit queasy.

"Can we get stronger too if we eat it?" Teddiursa beside him couldn't help but ask.

D shook his head: "No, this dish only works for Electric Type Pokémon like Zapdos, but if you want, you can try the taste."

Upon hearing this, Teddiursa shook his head: "Nah! There are only a few buns, better to leave them for the Electric Pokémon who need them!"

Mew and Jirachi shared this sentiment.

D was very gratified towards these understanding Pokémon.

He patted their heads, then looked at Zapdos.

"Want to give it a try?"

Zapdos: "..."

This proud Bird-type Pokémon eventually could not resist the temptation.

No matter what, it wanted to try!

"Great, then let's head outside!"

Right now, it's still snowing outside, everyone is staying indoors, and with the farm being remote, D wasn't worried about the commotion being noticed by others.

The group went outside, where Zapdos stood in the snow and ate the Lightning Steamed Bun.

At first, there was no reaction on its body.

But once Zapdos finished the entire bun, a clap of thunder suddenly boomed in the sky.

The previously gray sky was suddenly filled with a cloud, and then, a massive bolt of lightning fell straight from the cloud, landing on Zapdos.

A strong airflow surged from Zapdos's body in all directions, and Zapdos, in the midst of it, was filled with excitement.

Because it could clearly feel the power of thunder and lightning inside its body undergoing a transformation!

Its power became stronger!

"Chirp!"

Suddenly, Zapdos raised its head and let out a sharp cry.

A dense electric energy was released from its body towards the sky.

As the electric energy entered the thundercloud, in the next second, the gathered thundercloud was shattered by Zapdos.

The change stopped, and everything around returned to silence.

The world was white once more.

Zapdos's aura became even stronger.

"Congratulations, Zapdos." D said.

Looking at Zapdos, it seemed to have gained a significant boost.

In this world, there are many Zapdos, and the most powerful among them is the one on Shamouti Island, known as the Thunder God.

All the other Zapdos are just regular Zapdos.

"I wonder how this Zapdos now compares to that Thunder God..." D murmured to himself.

Zapdos, on the other hand, was full of excitement.

When the group returned to the living room, they saw Zapdos staring sharply at the four remaining buns on the plate.

D: "..."

Who was it that found eating its own feathers gross just now?

Zapdos seemed to have forgotten what it had just said.

D could only explain: "Each Pokémon can only eat once, the second time won't be effective."

Upon hearing this, Zapdos immediately showed a look of regret.

At that moment, it even considered giving D more feathers to make more buns.

"Chirp..." What a pity...

But soon, a new thought emerged in Zapdos's mind.

If its feathers could be made into a dish, then what about the other two birds?

Moltres and Articuno.

Thinking of this, Zapdos asked out loud.

"Chirp?" (Do Moltres and Articuno's feathers have effects?)

D was slightly stunned, quickly understanding Zapdos's meaning.

"If I'm not mistaken, they probably do. Moltres's feathers can be made into Flame Steamed Buns, and Articuno's feathers can be made into Frozen Steamed Buns." he explained.

---

Zapdos nodded its head, its eyes flashing with light.

It's decided! It would go back and steal the feathers from those two Pokemon!

D had no idea of Zapdos' thoughts.

After resting, the Heroic Expedition Team and Jirachi went excitedly to the World Tree to see their future home.

After several months, the World Tree had undergone tremendous changes.

The bustling Explorer Team Guild, Tatsugiri's restaurant, Spinda's café, Vileplume's item shop, the orchard diligently cared for by Treecko and Arboliva...

Looking at these scenes, the Pokemon of the Heroic Expedition Team had eyes filled with light,

"This is exactly the Explorer Team Guild of its dreams!"

"Miu..."

Mew gazed blankly at the scene in front of it until Mewtwo sensed its presence and teleported over, bringing Mew back to its senses.

"Mew." Mewtwo nodded calmly towards Mew.

"Miu!" Mew cheerfully greeted Mewtwo.

Though they were traveling outside, they often used the Rotom Phone to chat with Mewtwo.

Under Mewtwo's lead, the Pokemon began to tour the current World Tree. Along the way, Mewtwo occasionally introduced everything to them.

Guild headquarters, farmland, mill, orchard, practice area, item shop, Pokemon Hospital...

After visiting every place.

The group of Pokemon went to the tavern run by Meowth and Pikachu to drink beverages.

Teddiursa and Mew surveyed the surroundings, feeling as though something was missing here.

"Hmm..."

After Teddiursa mentioned it, Mew also realized that it wasn't just it having this thought.

So the question arises, what exactly is missing?

"Hmm! It's D!"

Without D, it doesn't feel like home here...

If only D could come to the World Tree and live with them.

Mew and Teddiursa exchanged a look.

Then they used the Rotom Phone to call D.

Soon, the call connected, and D's voice came from the other end.

"Hello? What's up? Did something happen at the World Tree?"

"Hmm hmm!" Teddiursa shook its head repeatedly, indicating nothing happened, and then asked expectantly, "Hmm hmm?" (Will D come to the World Tree to live with everyone?)

D didn't expect Teddiursa to ask this question.

He thought for a moment and then replied, "Of course I will, but not now."

"Miu?" (Then when will it be?) Mew asked.

D: "Probably several decades from now!"

By then, they would all have grown old, and familiar people in the world would gradually pass away.

At that time, if he had children, he would pass the ranch to them, and then, together with Lono, would bring Koraidon and Bellibolt to live at the World Tree.

Even if the body ages, it doesn't matter; they can exist forever in the form of spirits.

In that sense, wouldn't they be considered alternative "Ghost-type Pokemon"?

As long as they don't participate in the cycle in the Spirit Realm, they can live forever at the World Tree.

This is D's current plan for the future.

In doing so, he wouldn't have to worry about the issue of time limits and could always be by Koraidon's side.

*

Meanwhile, in Levincia City.

Inside a mansion.

Lono's parents curiously looked at their daughter across from them.

"Our daughter even changed her hairstyle; it seems like she's really grown up this time!" said the man holding a newspaper, sighing.

However, the woman noticed something: "Are you in love?"

"Huh?!" The man beside her was surprised.

Lono laughed foolishly: "He's a really good person!"

The woman squinted her eyes: "Is it that person from last time?"

Lono didn't hide it from her parents and nodded in acknowledgment.

Seeing this, the couple didn't know what to say for a moment.

Thinking about how this was her daughter's first love, the woman couldn't help but say:

"I don't have much experience to share, but I do want to say something—a word from a famous person once said."

Lono looked at her mother.

Woman: "Love is a two-way street, requiring both sides to move towards each other for a lasting relationship. If one person only wants to stay in place while expecting the other to come to them, it's destined that they aren't a good match."

Lono was slightly stunned: "What do you mean?"

Woman: "It means that feelings are mutual. If you want a long-lasting relationship, your lives need to gradually intertwine, and sometimes you have to make changes for each other."

Lono: "...It doesn't seem that exaggerated, does it?"

At that moment, her Rotom Phone suddenly rang.

Upon opening it, she saw that D had sent her a message.

[D: Quickly bring your Pokemon to the ranch; I have a gift for you!]

A gift?

Lono's eyes lit up, and she immediately ran back to her room to chat over the phone.

In the living room, her parents looked at their daughter's back, full of helplessness.

At this moment, the man suddenly remembered something.

The daughter had once asked them to buy something from a small shop.

He took out his phone to search, and sure enough, he saw [D Ranch] written.

"That shop should be opened by that guy, right?" the man said, glaring.

"...What do you want to do?"

"Of course, I want to see what kind of person he is. It's our daughter's first love, and it wouldn't be good if she was cheated by a bad person," the man said confidently.

He decided to visit the ranch pretending to be a passerby.

The woman looked at her husband helplessly: "How do you plan on understanding him?"

The man replied naturally: "By using the Trainer's method!"

He would observe the opponent's character and personality through Pokemon battles.

Sometimes, Pokemon battles are indeed the best way to see a person's true nature.

Although the opponent is now a rancher, seeing Lono's attitude suggests that he must have also been a Trainer once.

If that person doesn't even have the courage to battle him, it proves he's a coward!

If he wants his approval, he must defeat him head-on!

Recalling his journey challenging gyms to collect badges, his Pokemon partners had already reached Gym Leader level in strength.

Now, he presumed his skills were still robust.

Next, let that guy witness the power of his Gym Leader level skills!

---

---

---

---

# 488: Chapter 485: Beating Up the Father-in-Law

As the New Year approached and the weather turned cold, the entire Paldea population stayed huddled at home, rarely venturing out.

That afternoon, after waking up from a nap, Lono realized that someone in the house was suddenly missing.

"Huh?" She was full of surprise: "Where's Dad?"

The woman sitting on the sofa knitting a scarf with Tarountula's bug silk looked a bit guilty:

"Him? A few of his old friends came to visit today, and they all went to the city for a meal."

"I've never heard that Dad has old friends?" Lono was even more puzzled.

The woman said, "Maybe they're people he met while traveling when he was younger?"

"Alright then."

Lono didn't pay much attention; she began attending to Bellibolt and the others for breakfast.

*

D, like the people of the Paldea Region, stayed home from morning till night, leisurely passing the time with his Pokémon, rarely going out.

However, to his surprise, even in this weather, Trainers still came to the ranch to challenge.

In the afternoon, just as D was at home watching Pokémon battles broadcasted live, he suddenly heard a knock on the door.

This surprised D greatly.

Why would anyone visit in this weather?

Could Mayor Thomas and his people have something urgent they need help with?

Thinking of this, D hurriedly went to open the door.

But when he opened the door, it wasn't Mayor Thomas, nor Mrs. Dantes.

It was a middle-aged man, approximately in his forties, dressed in gray winter clothes, wearing a scarf, and looking quite robust.

D was slightly stunned, then politely asked: "Whom are you looking for?"

"I'm looking for the owner of this place." The man looked at him and asked: "You are D, right?"

"...That's me." D thought of a possibility, appeared enlightened, and then invited him into the living room: "It's so cold outside, come in and have a cup of tea first!"

"Not bad manners," thought the man.

He followed the young man into the house and inconspicuously glanced around the room.

The fireplace in the room was burning strongly, the air was warm, the floor was clean, and Pokémon toys were scattered around.

A Cyclizar, a shiny Pawmot, two Indeevee, an Alcremie, and another Pokémon he had never seen before were lying in front of the fireplace.

As expected, many Pokémon lived here.

Looking at the Pokémon, each had smooth fur and looked very spirited, clearly well-cared for by their owner.

This indicated that the young man was a person who treated Pokémon well and with love.

The man nodded inwardly and then sat down on the sofa.

One of the Indeevee quickly brought him a steaming cup of tea.

Traveling through the snow and wind, with the cold enveloping him, a cup of hot tea downed, and he felt wholly comfortable.

"Are you also here for the Trials?" D asked.

Trials? The man kept a calm demeanor, but internally he was puzzled, what was this about Trials? Isn't this a ranch?

"That's right, I had heard about it before but never got a detailed understanding. Can you explain it to me?" The man said.

This was quite usual; occasionally some Trainers who had heard about the special training Trials at D Ranch would come to challenge them.

D didn't think much of it and began to introduce:

"Currently, the ranch hosts four Trials: the Grass Trial, the Thunder Trial, the Storm Trial, and the Metronome Trial."

"The Grass Trial involves battling my Gogoat, the Thunder Trial involves battling Pawmot, the Storm Trial involves battling Dragonite, and the Metronome Trial is essentially a test of luck, only Pokémon capable of using Metronome can participate."

Upon hearing this, the man suddenly realized that things seemed to be somewhat different from what he expected...

"Except for the Metronome Trial, the other Trials require collecting a certain number of Gym Badges to participate."

"The Grass Trial requires eight, the Thunder Trial and Storm Trial require four, which one would you like to challenge?"

"There are conditions?" The man was a bit surprised.

D nodded helplessly: "Because if there's too much of a strength gap with Pokémon, achieving a good effect is impossible."

The strength gap with Pokémon?

The man was a little unconvinced; back in the day, he was a genius Trainer who had gathered eight Gym Badges to challenge the Champion Level.

Since that's the case, then he would go directly to challenge the Grass Trial!

D nodded: "Alright, but you'll need to show your Gym Badges."

The man was a bit embarrassed: "I wasn't aware of this; the eight badges I previously collected were all left at home and not brought here."

D: "..."

This was the first time he encountered such a situation.

But seeing this man's age, he probably wasn't a new, inexperienced Trainer.

After a brief moment of thought, D chose to trust him.

"Alright then, since that's the case, I'll make an exception this time."

The snowstorm outside had just ceased.

D glanced at the weather and then led the man to the battle area in the ranch.

The man immediately noticed the three Gogoat lazing under a shed, enjoying berries.

Thinking of the goat milk sold in the shop, the man's eyes widened involuntarily.

"Are they the milk goats from your ranch?"

D was slightly stunned, then nodded: "Yes, they also serve in milk production; the goat milk sold in the ranch is all produced by these three."

The man frowned slightly, instinctively thinking that Pokémon engaged in production work weren't this young man's main battling force.

---

The milk-producing goats, how strong can they even be?

"Don't you have any stronger Pokemon? You used to be a traveling trainer, right? Bring out your best team and have a Pokemon battle with me!"

Hearing this, a big question mark appeared over D's head.

This guy sure talks big!

He diplomatically said, "Gogoat is one of my mainstays as well."

Besides those Legendary Pokemon, Gogoat ranks among the top in strength at the ranch.

"I'm quite strong, your rules don't apply to me, because I collected eight badges over thirty years ago." The man said, shaking his head.

This meant he had over thirty years of battle experience compared to ordinary trainers.

D: "..."

"To be honest, the stronger Pokemon are hidden trials at the ranch, and to challenge them, you first need to pass the basic three trials." He explained.

Upon hearing this, the man became interested.

"Then let me first see your strength!"

The two of them went to opposite ends of the battle field, and D didn't clear the snow off the ground, but used the snowy terrain as the battle field.

D sent out Gogoat, while the man sent out a Pokemon that looked a lot like a masked rider.

Lokix!

The battle officially began!

Lokix started with a Screech, then jumped high, pouncing toward Gogoat.

Gogoat looked calm, those determined eyes didn't show a hint of panic.

D already saw the flaw in that Lokix, he whispered: "Aerial Ace."

The next second, Gogoat's movements suddenly accelerated, rushing to the side.

Lokix immediately launched a pursuit, only to find out it was just a feint. When Lokix changed its landing spot to attack again, it was already too late!

After playing trickery with swift movements, a milky white stream of air surged from Gogoat's horns.

The airflow turned into a wind blade, slamming heavily into Lokix.

"How is this possible?!" Seeing this scene, the man was shocked, how could that Gogoat's speed be so fast?

Despite being shocked inside, he maintained his calm command: "Low Sweep!"

Lokix, knocked to the ground, calmly glanced backward, taking the opportunity to rise, spinning its right leg to hit Gogoat's legs.

"Very good, just like that!" The man was very excited seeing the move land.

Yet the next second, he saw an extremely terrifying scene.

Suddenly, a green light emanated from Gogoat, instantly turning into a hemispherical energy field that enveloped the entire battle field.

"What's that..." The man widened his eyes.

Lokix sensed the danger, also eyeing its surroundings warily.

"Gogoat, use that move!" D said.

"Moo."

Gogoat let out a steady cry, its body slightly bowed, the green light rapidly condensed in its mouth, forming a giant Grass Sacred Sword.

Man: !!!

What kind of move is that?

"Lokix, dodge it quickly!" The man sensed the danger, immediately shouting.

But it was already too late, Gogoat was holding the leaf blade in its mouth, cleaving straight at it.

Instantly, the green light engulfed Lokix within it.

In the massive explosion, Lokix was blasted away, landing on the snow with no fighting ability left.

The man stared in disbelief at that Gogoat.

Based on its performance just now, are these Gogoats really just ordinary milk goats?

The man began to have doubts about life.

"Very unfortunate, you did not pass the Grass Trial." D regretfully shook his head.

Hearing this, the man immediately snapped back to reality.

He glared at the youngster opposite with a heavy face, feeling like his pride was on the line.

A few days ago, he was planning to show this guy the might of his Gym Leader level skills.

"..."

After a brief silence, the man shook his head: "No, again! I have stronger Pokemon!"

Saying that, the man took out his next Poke Ball.

With a flash of white light, a handsome Armarouge appeared on the battle field.

Yet a minute later, history repeated itself with what happened to Lokix.

"Again!"

After thirty seconds, Kilowattrel, defeated!

"No way, I still have Pokemon!"

After twenty seconds, Espathra was unable to continue.

"I refuse to believe this!"

After fifteen seconds, Magneton couldn't recover.

"..."

After five seconds, Gastrodon was one-shot.

At this point, all six of the man's Pokemon were defeated, and Gogoat achieved an incredible streak of six.

In the end, even D felt a bit cruel.

"Um, maybe we should just call it a day, mister?"

His sister Indeedee came forward to heal those fallen Pokemon with goat milk.

The man, with a face like iron, glared, snorted, and left with his Pokemon.

Leaving only D standing there, bewildered.

A very strange man indeed...

"Moo?" Gogoat was equally puzzled.

D couldn't figure it out so he decided to drop it, he patted Gogoat's head and said:

"Great job, now go and have a good rest!"

*

"That kid, so rude!

Back home, the man plopped onto the couch.

His wife, seeing this, was a bit surprised: "Was his behavior really that bad?"

---

"..." The man had just been about to speak, but thinking it over carefully, he realized that aside from the Pokemon battle, the other guy had actually been quite polite, and he really did take good care of Pokemon.

Judging from how the Pokemon at that ranch acted around him, they all seemed to like him. Just from that alone, the man could tell the other party wasn't a bad person.

Seeing her husband's expression, the woman immediately understood.

She asked with a laugh, "Don't tell me you lost to him?"

Man: "..."

He gritted his teeth, furious. "That's why I'm saying that kid has no manners! He beat my six Pokemon with just one Pokemon!!!"

The woman couldn't help but snort with laughter. "You were the one who insisted on challenging him."

"And you're laughing!"

"Alright, alright!" The woman gently soothed her husband. "From the way you're acting, his character doesn't sound bad at all."

"Originally I thought, even if he wasn't some strong Trainer, it wouldn't matter—so long as he could take good care of our daughter, that would be enough."

"But looking at it now, not only can he take care of Lono, he's also a very outstanding Trainer. That's something we should be happy about!"

"Besides, you didn't reveal your identity when you went, did you?"

Man: "..."

Thinking about how he'd even boasted that he wanted to challenge that kid's stronger Pokemon, the man felt incredibly embarrassed.

It's over,

"Don't say another word, I need some time alone."

He lay down on the sofa, closed his eyes, and played dead.

Seeing her husband like this, the woman couldn't help but feel a bit curious about that young man named D.

She also wanted to go and see what kind of person he was.

But with the New Year almost here, she was afraid she wouldn't have the time.

"Forget it, I'll just wait until spring to pay him a visit."

The woman made up her mind.

When next spring came and the flowers were blooming, she would bring some gifts and pretend to be a lost tourist to visit that ranch.

*

Time slipped by quietly, and in the blink of an eye, the last day of League Calendar Year 200 arrived.

Early that morning, D Ranch started its New Year preparations.

This was D's third year in this world, and also the third year since he met Cyclizar and the others.

Once this New Year passed, it would be their fourth year together.

Thinking about it was a little emotional. Without him noticing, that once-abandoned ranch had completely changed its appearance.

And so many Pokemon had come here to live.

While getting the New Year's Eve dinner ready at home, D couldn't help but look around.

The Indeedee Sibling were decorating the house with streamers and lights, getting it ready for more of a New Year's atmosphere.

Ogerpon was leaning on the windowsill watching the snow, those starry eyes filled with the light of happiness.

Cyclizar was in front of the fireplace arranging its toys and scarves—those were all things D had bought for it. Cyclizar had put them together in their own storage box.

Koraidon kept "causing trouble" next to Cyclizar, looking like it was very jealous of Cyclizar having so many toys.

But it had a lot of toys of its own too… Wasn't this what they called wanting what's in someone else's bowl while eating from your own? D couldn't help silently complaining.

Pawmot was eating macadamias and chestnuts together with its parents, all prepared by D.

Since it was New Year's, so the whole family could be together, D had specially gone to the World Tree to bring Pawmot's parents over.

There were Eevee growls coming from upstairs; it had to be the Eevee playing around up there.

Dragonite and Dratini were sitting on the floor, happily munching on the fried little dried fish D had given them.

And Calyrex was still sitting on the sofa playing on its phone.

The once-famous King of Galar had completely turned into the King of Phones now.

Looking at this scene of Pokemon playing happily, D couldn't help but show a faint smile.

Just then, the town's mailman, Allen, arrived at the ranch.

"D, you've got mail! They're New Year's blessings from everyone in town!"

Allen was as energetic as ever.

"Thanks for the trouble!" D took the letters from his hands with a smile. After they exchanged New Year's greetings, Allen left to deliver mail to the other ranches.

Opening the envelopes, he found that most of the contents were New Year's blessings from the townsfolk.

There were also notices from some shops about new stock arriving—they said that next year they'd have new crops and new flower seeds, and that if D Ranch needed any, he could come by the shop to buy them.

---

---

---

---

# 489: Chapter 486: New Year's and Spring, a Fresh Crop Arrival!

D kept busy until nightfall before he finished preparing a large table of dishes.

Koraidon's favorite pan-seared steak, Dragonite's favorite steamed sea bass, Pawmot's favorite fried cashews...

During dinner, snowflakes began to drift outside again, but not as heavily as the past few days.

D was a bit worried the snow might collapse Gogoat's shelter, so he had Koraidon and the others eat first while he went outside to clear the snow off the shelter.

Night had fallen, the snowy land reflecting the nighttime, and even without a flashlight, the surrounding paths were clearly visible.

Gazing at the snowy landscape, D thought to himself that this was a good omen, as abundant snow brings a prosperous year!

Filled with anticipation for the new year, D turned around to head back, only to see Koraidon and Pawmot squatting under the eaves, looking towards him.

"Why did you come outside? It's so cold, quickly get back inside!" D pushed the Pokemon back into the house.

"Ah-ga-sii～"

"Alright, let's start the meal!" Returning to the table, D smiled warmly at everyone.

On the table were steaming hot dishes, in front were various Pokemon, outside the cold wind howled, yet his cabin felt warm as spring.

Even though he'd experienced this a few times already, D still felt filled with anticipation and enjoyment at this moment.

"Ga-oo!"

With his command, the countless Pokemon in front of him let out a cheer and began to enjoy the dinner D had carefully prepared for them.

D took out his Rotom Phone and snapped a photo, then posted it in the Poke Friend Circle.

[King Wing: Celebrating New Year with the Pokemon ^_^

Picture.jpg]

Soon, he received a few comments.

[Professor Sada: Koraidon looks so happy!]

[Cynthia: Happy New Year!]

[Professor Oak: Is that Pokemon the mythical Mew?!]

[Alder replying to Professor Oak: Haha, at D's place there are many rare Pokemon like Mew!]

[Professor Oak replying to Alder: What? More Pokemon as rare as Mew?]

[Professor Oak: D! When you have some time, come visit Pallet Town, and let me host you properly!]

D: "..."

Professor Oak's research obsession was acting up again.

For researchers like him, Pokemon such as Mew and Jirachi indeed have significant research value.

However, they might not have time recently!

Thinking it over, D replied to Professor Oak.

[King Wing: Sorry, Professor, the ranch here is quite busy, we might not have the chance to visit Pallet Town, but you can come to the Paldea Region, it's the same.]

However, Professor Oak also had no time available.

Apart from researching Pokemon, he also conducts lectures, introducing trainers to Pokemon ecology.

This left D quite regretful.

The two could only agree to visit each other when they have time in the future.

Then, D replied to Professor Sada and Cynthia, wishing them a Happy New Year.

In the Pokemon World, there's no Spring Festival Gala, but there is a special Pokemon Spring Festival program.

After dinner, D sat on the sofa with his Pokemon to watch TV.

To be precise, the Pokemon were watching TV while he chatted with Lono.

Lono said she just finished dinner too, and now she is at home with Bellibolt and the others, listening to the noisy fireworks outside.

[Lono: So noisy outside!]

D chuckled, then said: [Next year, come to the ranch here for New Year. It's quiet here, not much noise.]

[Lono: Sure! It's settled, then! Oh, by the way, I've recently begun learning to cook. Next time there's a chance, you should try my cooking!]

Seeing this message, D felt a bit surprised.

He remembered Lono saying before her cooking wasn't very good, and while living in Levincia City, she often dined outside with her Pokemon.

[King Wing: Why suddenly start learning to cook?]

[Lono: Want to give it a try. If I can learn, I can cook personally for Bellibolt and the others in the future!]

D made a video call.

Soon, the call was connected, Lono's face appeared on the screen.

Koraidon heard the voice and came closer.

Seeing Koraidon, Lono smiled and greeted it: "Happy New Year, Koraidon!"

"Ah-ga-sii～"

D was curious about Lono's past: "When you traveled outside before, did you buy food at restaurants to take away?"

"Traveling?" Lono shook her head: "I rarely went out! Back then, I was studying at Orange Academy, having breakfast, lunch, and dinner at the cafeteria just like everyone else. Although I would later go out to challenge Gyms, most of the time I took Pokemon taxis!"

"I see." D realized, "I thought it was like trainers in other regions. I encountered some trainers from other regions who didn't have to go to school, once they turned ten, they could receive their starter Pokemon from the local professor and embark on a journey."

"Maybe Paldea Academy has existed for a long time in the Paldea Region?" Lono speculated, "Enrollment at ten might be a tradition?"

"So how did you become a Gym Leader afterward?" D was curious.

Lono replied: "Well, because back then I didn't know what to do in the future either, so I just joined the Gym challenge like everyone else."

---

"Later, the chief invited me to join the Paldea League, and then I was assigned to work at the Levincia Gym! Hehe, actually, I quite like this job!"

"Oh, I see!" D nodded, "I thought you inherited your family's gym!"

Talking about this matter, Lono couldn't help but complain, "My parents just can't sit still. Every year they go traveling, saying the world is so big, they have so many places they haven't seen yet, often leaving me alone in Levincia City."

D envisioned the scene in his mind and couldn't help but laugh, no wonder they could raise such a quirky daughter like Lono, those parents must be interesting.

"What about D?" Lono suddenly asked, "I heard from the chief before that D only moved to the Paldea Region in the last few years, right? Where did you live before?"

"Me?" D was slightly taken aback, then replied, "I used to live with my parents in the Hoenn Region, but after they passed away due to illness, I left there and traveled around the world."

"It wasn't until three years ago that I returned to the Paldea Region to inherit the ranch left by my grandfather."

Before, their relationship wasn't that close, and Lono hadn't heard D mention his parents. Now hearing him say that, Lono couldn't help but feel a little heartache.

"Everything will get better," she comforted.

"Yeah!" D nodded gently, "But I've gotten used to it. Now with Koraidon and the others with me, and you, I feel pretty happy."

He wasn't lying to Lono.

Because before he crossed over to this world, his parents also passed away early.

But he was strong-hearted, didn't commit suicide, and later, although he became a corporate drone, D always maintained an optimistic attitude towards life.

After all, he still had Pokemon to accompany him, and the Pokemon games and anime series.

Hearing D's words, Lono nodded repeatedly, "Mm, I will always be by your side too!"
