```
D's heart softened, then he nodded lightly, "Mm, I'll remember that."

Then, the two of them started talking about other things, chatting until the midnight bell tolled.

Hearing that sound, D knew it was now the year 201 in the League calendar.

It was too late, and everything returned to silence in the night.

Lono, on the other side of the screen, yawned sleepily.

D glanced at the Pokemon beside him who had already fallen into dreamland, and said to Lono:

"If you're tired, go sleep!"

Lono also realized it was too late, she responded softly, "Mm, got it! Goodnight~"

"Goodnight."

The call ended, D turned off the TV, and started carrying the sleeping Pokemon back to their little nests one by one.

For larger Pokemon like Dragonite, D couldn't carry them, so he asked Calyrex to move them with Psychic powers.

During this, Koraidon was startled awake by Calyrex's Psychic powers, it opened its eyes wide, looking around in a daze, finally looking at D:

"Ah, gah, sss?"

"Time to go back to sleep." D patted its head.

Koraidon yawned, then obediently followed D back to the room.

D went to the bathroom to wash up. When he came out, he found that Koraidon was still not asleep.

D sat on the bed, patted its head, his thoughts shifted slightly, then he asked aloud:

"Koraidon, what do you think of Lono? Would it be okay for her to join the ranch and live with us?"

"Ah, gah, sss!" Hearing this, Koraidon was instantly wide awake, it excitedly nodded its big head.

"Hmm?" D was very surprised, "You like Lono a lot?"

"Ah, gah, sss!" Koraidon looked very happy.

"Is that so?" D smiled slightly, "Then it's settled!"

"Ah, gah, sss~"

"Alright, sleep now!" D rubbed Koraidon's head, then lay down on the bed.

He took a deep breath, looking up at the ceiling longingly.

Perhaps... maybe he could really have a complete family of his own in this world.

Running the ranch together with Lono, taking care of the Pokemon on the ranch.

Maybe they would have a child, just like all the children in this world, starting their journey as a Trainer at the age of ten.

Just like Mr. Elif next door at the Elif Cherry Garden, running a cherry garden with his wife and children, living a simple and happy life.

D closed his eyes and soon fell into a deep sleep.

He had a dream, dreaming of a wedding with Lono, Geeta acting as their officiant, and Koraidon and Cyclizar playing the role of flower children.

*

Winter passed, spring came, snow melted, and everything started to revive.

After the New Year ended, the Heroic Expedition Team resumed their journey.

But to D's surprise, Zapdos temporarily left the team.

According to Zapdos, it had some things to do and would come back once they were done.

D was puzzled, he asked Zapdos what it was, but Zapdos secretively told neither Mew nor Teddiursa, saying it was going to give them a surprise.

Before leaving, D gave Zapdos a Rotom Phone to keep in touch with Mew and Teddiursa.

Zapdos thanked him, then flapped its wings and flew away.

While Mew and Teddiursa watched its departing figure, they also embarked on their journey.

This year, they planned to travel to a place called the Sinnoh Region.

---

The Teddiursa, which previously heard from the Pokemon on the sea that there were many treasures there, immediately set its sights on the destination, eager to pick up the treasures.

Once the Heroic Expedition Team left, the World Tree returned to its usual calm.

The Pokemon there lived an orderly life.

Mewtwo continued guarding the World Tree, Ho-oh often traveled far away, Rayquaza mostly perched atop the canopy except when gnawing on meteorites in the cosmos, while Giratina remained a big influencer in the shadows of the World Tree.

And Groudon and Kyogre, having nothing better to do, spent all day arguing in their group chat.

D thought, as long as they weren't fighting, everything was fine, just let them vent their excess energy.

With the arrival of spring, the fields on the ranch began to be cultivated again.

This year, the seed shop in town had a batch of new seeds.

Such as cabbage, broccoli, lettuce, baby bok choy, gourds, and so on.

D, curious about the novelty, decided to buy some of each to try planting them at home, hoping perhaps the gourds would mutate and grow into seven-colored gourds, producing seven gourd children.

Seeing that he had bought so much, the seed shop owner Elo even gave him a few extra bags of seed.

Back at the ranch, D immediately began planting.

It was still Dugtrio responsible for tilling the soil, while D, with Koraidon and Cyclizar, planted the seeds in the fields, then had Dragonite use Rain Dance for watering, finally entrusting Gogoat to cultivate with Grass Energy.

D planned to finish planting this plot before beginning Raifort's commission to capture those four Ruinous Pokemon, using Reversing Cuisine to transform them into Pokemon that could bring auspicious signs to the ranch.

As for the ranch in the Alola Region...

No rush to go there now; it's primarily for winter vacations, and winter has already passed, so there's no need for a prolonged stay there.

Only after finishing all the tasks in the Paldea Region did D plan to go there and investigate, attempting to 'py' with the Tapu family to see if he could obtain Z Rings and Z Moves from them.

Not only the Grassium Z of Calyrex but also Eevee's exclusive Z; D had previously promised his partner Eevee to help it master Eevee's ultimate move: Extreme Evoboost.

And just as D was planning his next steps in his heart, a mysterious visitor suddenly arrived at the ranch.

Butterfree, basking in the sun on the roof, was the first to notice the person.

It immediately came to inform D.

As D, busy in the fields, raised his head, he saw a woman in a floral dress holding a bag by her side, displaying elegance and gentleness, standing at the ranch entrance, gazing towards this side.

D was slightly taken aback.

The next second, his gaze met the person's in midair.

Then, D heard the other person softly ask:

"Excuse me, what is this place?"

D: "?"

It seemed his confusion was too evident on his face, prompting the woman to look slightly embarrassed:

"I apologize deeply; I'm a traveler who came here specifically to explore the legend of Porto Marinada Town but got lost in the back hill. I'm now exhausted and hungry, could I borrow some water to drink? I can pay for it."

D suddenly realized.

In recent years, Porto Marinada Town has frequently attracted some tourists to visit Calyrex's Shrine in the back hill.

"Of course." D stopped his farm work and went to the faucet to wash his hands, then said to the woman, "This is D Ranch east of Porto Marinada Town, I'm the owner, D. No need to pay, please come in and take a rest!"

Upon hearing this, the woman smiled: "Thank you, sorry to trouble you～"

Not far away, Koraidon and Cyclizar stood in a line, with Koraidon looking at the woman with a face full of puzzlement.

"Ah, isn't that Lono's mom?"

```

# 490: Chapter 487: Precious Apricorn Balls, Zygarde 10% Form Appears!

The woman followed D into the living room.

She quietly observed the young man, her heart constantly nodding in approval.

Yes, indeed dignified, he seemed very composed, definitely a good man.

Her arrival attracted quite a few Pokemon who came running to watch.

The woman's gaze swept across those Pokemon, and as a former Trainer, she immediately recognized them.

Dratini, Cyclizar, a shiny Pawmo, Garganacl, Butterfree...

All these Pokemon were excellently bred, whether in coat color or in spirit, they were perfect.

Some of them even looked quite strong.

No wonder her husband had been so discouraged before, the woman thought.

D turned back to look at this lost visitor, offering her a seat on the sofa to rest, and then asked his sister, Indeedee, to bring the guest a cup of tea.

"Ayeee~"

Sister Indeedee nodded gently, soon bringing over a cup of tea.

"Thank you."

The woman took the cup and downed it in one go, looking very thirsty.

After finishing the tea, she let out a long breath: "Ah! Thank you so much!"

"You're welcome." D shook his head with a smile: "Are you a visitor from out of town?"

The woman nodded slightly: "That's right, I heard about the legends of the Legendary Pokemon in Porto Marinada Town, so I rushed over here from Levincia City to visit."

"But I didn't expect to get lost on the way, it took me a lot of effort to find a place where people live..."

Levincia City?

D immediately thought of Lono, he laughed and guided her to the destination:

"Just head west from this ranch, follow that path to the end, and you'll see Porto Marinada Town. If you want to visit the Abundant Shrine, you can head north from here, follow the mountain path up to reach the destination."

The woman listened intently: "Ah, thank you so much. By the way, I haven't asked your name yet."

"Name?" D introduced himself: "I'm D, D of D Ranch."

"D..." The woman repeated the name: "By the way, you look quite young, you probably aren't twenty-five yet?"

Hmm? D rarely encountered someone who didn't think he looked like an old man, and he felt a bit happy.

"That's right, I'm 22 this year, I'll be 23 in the second half." He nodded.

"Twenty-two, my daughter is about the same age!" The woman thought for a bit, then said with a smile.

"Daughter?" D was somewhat surprised.

The woman seemed to notice his thoughts, smiling as she said:

"Don't be fooled by my appearance, I'm actually forty! Speaking of which, you should call me auntie!"

"!!!"

D was quite shocked: "Forty?"

But the woman in front of him looked only about thirty?

People in the Pokemon World don't seem to age easily?

"That's right." The woman nodded gently, looking outside, seemingly very interested in the ranch: "Such pastoral scenery is hard to come by in a big city!"

The life here was so serene and leisurely, giving off a very comfortable feeling.

"Can I take a tour of your ranch?" the woman asked.

"Of course you can." D nodded in agreement.

Then, he gave her a tour of his ranch.

From the Gogoats that produce goat milk, to the fields filled with various crops, to the apple garden and Wooloo Ranch across the river.

Looking at such a large ranch, the woman couldn't help but exclaim:

"You're amazing, managing such a big ranch so well on your own!"

"It's not just me! Many Pokemon on the ranch also help out." D said somewhat bashfully.

"Your family? They don't live with you?" the woman asked again.

"Family?" D shook his head: "They've all passed away, I didn't originally live in this place, but moved here later."

"Ah? I'm very sorry!" The woman looked apologetic.

Such a poor child...

"It's okay, that's all in the past." D shook his head and said.

"Yes, that's right! One must look forward!" The woman agreed, nodding.

She glanced at the time, then said: "Well, thank you very much for your hospitality, I've recovered now, so I won't disturb you any longer!"

When she came earlier, she saw D working in the fields.

"It's no problem, I've also received help from others before." D said with a smile.

Afterwards, he again pointed the woman in the direction of Porto Marinada Town:

"Just keep going straight, and you'll see the town. There's no dangerous Pokemon along the way, so don't worry about being attacked by wild Pokemon, it's very safe here."

The woman smiled warmly, nodding slightly, and then handed over her bag:

"I bought this souvenir on the way, let it be my token of thanks for your hospitality!"

D instinctively shook his head, wanting to say there was no need.

However, after handing the gift to him, the woman turned and left, waving back at him with a warm smile: "See you next time!"

Upon hearing this, D was slightly taken aback, what did she mean?

The woman left.

D stood there for a while, then opened the bag to find a set of delicate Apricorn balls inside.

---

Love Ball, Friend Ball, Moon Ball, Heavy Ball, Fast Ball...

D was momentarily stunned.

Because these Apricorn Balls were a very precious gift!

Unlike regular Poke Balls, Apricorn Balls are handcrafted from apricots that grow on apricorn trees.

In the past, craftsmen who made Poke Balls would hollow out the apricots and insert special nets for capturing Pokemon to make the Poke Balls.

Most of these Poke Balls made from apricots have unique characteristics.

For example, the Love Ball is more likely to catch wild Pokemon of the opposite gender to your own Pokemon.

The Friend Ball has an incredibly comfortable interior, making Pokemon caught with it easier to become close with their Trainer, just as the name suggests, becoming friends.

The Moon Ball, on the other hand, makes it easy to catch Pokemon that can evolve using a Moon Stone.

Before the advent of modern Poke Balls, apricot-made Poke Balls were very common, and this technique is said to date back 400 to 700 years.

However, with technological development, these Poke Balls gradually got replaced by modern mass-produced Poke Balls due to their long production time and lower efficiency.

But craftsmen who continue to inherit this craft still make Poke Balls for outstanding Trainers, spending an entire day meticulously carving apricots into precious handcrafted Poke Balls.

For this reason, Apricorn Balls are far more valuable than the regular red and white Poke Balls and have more commemorative significance.

Trainers personally picking apricots and giving them to craftsmen to make special Poke Balls for their beloved Pokemon partners sounds romantic.

D never expected that just by offering that person a glass of water, he would receive such a precious thank-you gift.

This made him not know what to say for a moment.

*

After leaving the ranch, the woman went straight back to Levincia City.

She pushed open the door and saw her husband waiting for her there.

Upon hearing the commotion, the man looked up: "How was it?"

Having been together for many years, the couple had developed a tacit understanding; with just these three words, the woman understood what her husband meant.

He was asking what she thought of the boy.

"Him? He's a very sensible child!" the woman reminisced about the events at the ranch and said softly.

"Hmph." However, recalling his previous experience of being beaten one to six, the man's face turned sour.

Seeing this, the woman sighed: "Don't be like that, the kid has it tough too, his parents passed away early, leaving only him at home."

Hearing this, the man was momentarily stunned and turned to look at his wife.

"He single-handedly manages that ranch expertly and can care for so many Pokemon."

"Not only that, when I went there he was very polite in hosting me and offered his help. That child is a really good person!"

A mother-in-law looking at a son-in-law, the more she looks, the more she likes him.

The woman added: "If the two of them can progress smoothly, we can be at ease."

"Hmph." The man snorted again, but his tone was much softer than before: "Levincia City and Porto Marinada Town are so far apart, it won't be easy for Lono to return then."

"Oh come on, I think there's really nothing to worry about!" The woman chuckled and shook her head: "I saw a Dragonite there, and with Dragonite's speed, it takes less than thirty minutes to travel between the two places."

"Hmph."

Woman: "Cheapskate! I think you just can't bring yourself to admit it! Who was it who swore to show them your strength before?"

Man: "..."

*

That set of Apricorn Balls was eventually placed by D in a display cabinet for collection.

The time had come to February in the year 201 of the League calendar.

The land on the ranch had already been planted, and with the cultivation of Gogoat, those recently planted crops bloomed and bore fruit within a few breaths, reaching the harvest stage.

However, D did not immediately harvest them; he placed these fresh vegetables on the shelf of his ranch store.

If a customer made a purchase, he would pick them fresh and ship them via Dragonite Delivery, ensuring customers would eat the freshest vegetables possible.

After completing this task, D started preparing a plan to capture the four Ruinous Pokemon.

Raifort had already sent him the specific locations of those four Pokemon, and he only needed to go to those places to lift the seals, then capture them.

D looked at the map of the Paldea Region.

Currently, the nearest location to them is the Groundblight Shrine, where Ting-Lu is sealed, located north of Casseroya Lake, in a place called Bamber Forest.

[King Wing: I'm setting off.]

Before departing, D sent Raifort a message.

Raifort quickly replied.

[Raifort: Keep it up! It's up to you now!]

D gave an OK gesture and glanced at the time. It was now 8:30 in the morning, and the sun had just risen.

Setting off at this time, he might be able to return to the ranch by the afternoon.

"Today, I'm going out for a bit. I should be back by the afternoon, so I'll leave the Pokemon on the ranch to you during lunchtime." D said to the Indeedee Sibling.

"Indee~"

"Indee."

The siblings nodded in unison, indicating they would take good care of everything on the ranch.

D smiled slightly, then walked out the door.

In the ranch, a group of Pokemon was playing and running around on the grass.

It was springtime, and the air was pleasantly warm. The grass in the ranch was growing vigorously, and the blooming flowers in the flower fields attracted many butterflies.

Koraidon was lazily lying on the grass, feeling the breeze.

Meanwhile, Cyclizar was once again attracted by the butterflies, diving into a tall patch of grass to chase them.

Watching the scene, an image popped into D's mind:

A traveling Trainer walking through the grass, when suddenly Cyclizar jumps out, triggering a battle.

Alright, now he understood why in games, players always encounter Pokemon jumping out in the grass.

In the grass, Cyclizar focused entirely on the fluttering butterflies.

It stealthily raised its head, its eyes tightly following the butterfly's flight path.

At that moment, the butterfly flew out of the tall grass, preparing to land on a rock.

Now!

Seizing the opportunity, Cyclizar made a sudden lunge, pinning the butterfly underneath its claws.

However, in the next second, Cyclizar felt something struggling beneath its grasp.

The force and intensity of the struggle were not something a butterfly could manage!

"Gah-oh?"

Cyclizar confusedly moved its paw aside.

Immediately, on the rock, it saw a mysterious Pokemon: green, with one eye, and a blue diamond-shaped core on its belly.

Meanwhile, the butterfly had long flown away.

"Gah-oh?!"

Cyclizar was startled.

Likewise, the Pokemon appeared frightened, radiating a burst of green light.

Meanwhile, in D's room, the Zygarde Cell he had placed on the desk sunbathing suddenly emitted a green glow.

In the next second, that light flew outside.

The same phenomenon occurred across the ranch, with green lights flying out even from the back mountains. Who knows how many cells Zygarde had sent to observe.

While patting the head of Greavard, D noticed this scene.

He looked up in surprise, witnessing beams of green light flying across the sky, then converging onto Cyclizar.

D: "?"

He immediately stepped forward to check.

But when he saw the scene clearly, he was stunned.

Because amidst the green lights, a Pokemon resembling a Doberman gradually appeared!

This Pokemon had pale eyes, its entire body was a mix of green and black, and its neck had structures resembling a collar and leash.

Below, there was a blue diamond pattern!

Is that... Zygarde's 10% form?!

Was Squishy also sent to the ranch by Zygarde for observation?

No... staring at the blue core, D realized it wasn't Squishy, as Squishy's core was red.

This Pokemon with a blue core clearly represented the rational Z2!

And unlike Squishy with Bonnie, Z2 believed humans to be disruptors of order.

In the anime, it even advised Squishy to stay away from humans.

D looked at Z2; it was obvious that it hadn't just arrived but had been attracted here by the World Tree for some time.

So it had been hiding in the shadows, observing all along?

---

---

---

---

# 491: Chapter 488: Beautiful World

The situation here has caught the attention of many Pokemon in the ranch.

Soon, Z2, in its 10% form, was surrounded.

The surrounding Pokemon all cast curious glances at it.

Z2 was somewhat tense.

At this moment, it heard the human speak.

"When did you come here?"

D asked with interest.

Whether it was Kyogre, Groudon, Rayquaza, or Ho-oh, they were all attracted to the World Tree on the day it matured.

If he wasn't mistaken, Zygarde probably noticed the situation here at that time too.

Thus, it dispatched its cells and core to observe.

And as D predicted, this was indeed the case.

Perhaps due to its months of secret observation, the Z2 before him seemed quite familiar with him.

After a brief contemplation, Z2 spoke.

"Last summer." It said via Telepathy.

Upon hearing this, D thought silently to himself, so the World Tree matured at that time.

"Did you come to observe the situation?" he asked again.

Z2, remaining in its 10% Doberman form, looked at the human with its pale eyes and nodded its canine head.

At that time, it had been staying in unobtrusive places, silently observing this place and the humans living here.

Because it could sense a very rich natural aura here.

Originally, this land did not have cells present.

But later, Zygarde dispatched more cells after it to better observe the World Tree and this region's ecosystem.

"I see..." D nodded thoughtfully.

Yet, Z2 sensed a hint of peculiarity from his actions and words.

"You know me." It stated confidently.

"Of course I know you." D didn't hide it: "One of Zygarde's cores, a watcher of the ecosystem; if I'm not wrong, you were also drawn here by a natural aura, right?"

Z2 noted the human's choice of words.

"Also?"

Aside from it, had others been attracted here?

But upon careful thought, it made sense; such a significant occurrence would inevitably draw the attention of powerful entities.

"In human terms, they are probably Pokemon on the same level as you, aren't they?"

D considered for a moment and continued: "Oh well, for Little Soft's sake, I'll take you to visit that place!"

Based on his observations, neither Zygarde in this form nor Groudon could fly to reach the World Tree.

"Little Soft?" Z2 slightly paused.

D: "It fused into you just now."

Z2: "..."

Z2 was internally conflicted; it understood the human's implication.

But that was merely its cell, a part of its body without its own consciousness.

"Let's go!" D glanced at the time, feeling that he could first take Zygarde for a tour of the World Tree before heading to Groundblight Shrine.

Z2 didn't say much, and silently followed the human.

In its view, this human was different from others.

Other humans are disruptors who ruin ecological order, but this D was someone chosen by nature.

The human and the Pokemon traveled through the Reversed World and quickly arrived at the World Tree.

Upon stepping out of the passageway, Z2 widened its eyes, looking around.

Because it sensed an overpowering natural aura, akin to nature itself.

Staying here felt as comfortable as returning to a mother's embrace.

"There is such a place, even I didn't know..." Z2 murmured.

Above was a gigantic canopy blocking out the sun, and the sunlight filtered through the gaps, making the area appear dreamlike.

What shocked Z2 even more was that so many Pokemon lived here!

They planted Berries here, cultivated the land, living orderly lives!

"This is the interior of the World Tree; the natural aura you felt before comes from this tree." D introduced with a smile.

"So that's how it is..."

After a brief shock, Z2 quickly regained its composure.

"The ecosystem of this world was on the verge of collapse; on the land where I stayed, traces of negative energy cores had already appeared."

"Negative energy cores?" D looked at the nearby canine.

Z2 nodded and continued: "They are phenomena produced after the ecosystem is damaged."

"The land would grow many giant plants; humans and Pokemon cannot see them, only I can."

"Until now, I had been monitoring the ecosystem."

D nodded, remembering that in the anime, Ash's Greninja could see the negative energy cores and was left by Zygarde to help cut them down.

"But now, after this tree matured, all that negative energy vanished. I believe its released power repaired the damaged ecosystem." Z2 said softly.

This didn't surprise D too much.

After all, the description of the World Tree includes such effects; upon maturing, it causes changes throughout the entire world.

Regarding ecological restoration, that might be one such change.

"That's a good thing." D said.

Z2 didn't retort: "Whatever the case, I must express my gratitude to you."

---

Because according to its observations over the past few months, this World Tree was cultivated by the human in front of it.

Nature has chosen him.

It was the first time D heard a Legendary Pokemon thank him for something like this.

He couldn't help but take another couple of looks at Z2, is this the Ecological Supervisor?

Its thinking is indeed different from other Pokemon.

"Are you staying here?" D asked.

Just like Groudon and the others, living on the World Tree from now on.

However, Z2 refused.

"No, I still have more important things to do." It shook its head and said, "The ecosystem has only been temporarily restored, but as long as there are humans, one day the ecological order will be disrupted again. I must continue to fulfill my duty."

D: "... Isn't that a bit exaggerated?"

According to Z2, wouldn't humans become the source of all evil in this world?

"Humans are complex and diverse." Z2 shook its head and said, "You won't disrupt the ecological order, but there are always some humans who will destroy the existing order, causing the birth of negative energy cores."

Hearing this, D was momentarily speechless.

What Zygarde said was right; some people protect the ecosystem while others destroy it.

The most serious among them is the antagonist Lysandre from the Kalos Region.

Lysandre is quite a complex person.

In his youth, he often led Team Flare to aid the poor in the Kalos Region.

But after receiving help, those people took their actions for granted and even demanded more charity from Lysandre.

Because of this, Lysandre's ideas shifted, and he attempted to use the power of Legendary Pokemon to destroy the current world to create a beautiful utopian world.

However, these are all future matters.

At this time, the Kalos Region's storyline hasn't yet occurred.

Z2 did not linger long on the World Tree.

After understanding the source of that Natural Energy and the current state of this land, it left the World Tree and returned to the land.

However, Z2 did not leave the ranch.

Because now the order of the Paldea Region is supervised by it.

Watching beams of green light fly from Z2's body into the distance, D couldn't help but ask:

"To you, do these cells function like monitors?"

"Monitors?" Z2, having turned back into a blue core form, looked over puzzled.

"That is, being able to observe situations in other places through them." D explained.

Z2 nodded: "That's right."

"Then is your main part left in the Kalos Region?" D asked curiously.

This kind of Pokemon Zygarde is different from other Pokemon; it is composed of countless cells and cores assembled together.

And it changes into different forms depending on the number of cells.

The 10% form of Zygarde is like a Doberman, adept in close combat, attacking with sharp teeth.

The 50% form is generally Zygarde's main body.

It lives deep in the cave, monitoring if there are abnormalities in the Pokemon ecosystem through scattered cells and cores, taking on the guardianship role.

And when all cells and cores gather, Zygarde will transform into its complete 100% form.

This form of Zygarde resembles a Gundam, possessing overwhelming terrifying power, and it fully harnesses this power for its mission of defending the ecosystem, meant for battle.

Hearing D's words, Z2 nodded: "That's right, it was the main body that sent me and the other cells to this place."

The main body is now deep in a cave, monitoring changes in the ecosystem.

Upon hearing this, D thought to himself indeed.

He recalled the anime plot where Team Flare went to Terminus Cave to capture Zygarde, and he couldn't help but advise:

"I think you should warn the main body to act cautiously or relocate to another cave. I have a hunch that humans are planning to capture it."

Z2 was slightly stunned.

"How do you know this?" It asked.

"Because I used Future Sight."

Z2: "..."

And at this moment, D stood up: "Alright, let's end it here! I need to get busy with my own things. If you wish to stay on the ranch, feel free to stay! Koraidon, it's time for us to set off!"

"Kaaa-sha!" Koraidon, waiting on the side, immediately responded.

It glanced at Z2, then at D, who was turning to leave, and promptly chased after D.

Leaving only Z2, a lone Pokemon, standing on a rock.

It tried to sense its main body, but the distance was too far, making contact impossible.

*

This time, D brought with him six Pokemon: Koraidon, Cyclizar, Dragonite, Pawmot, Flutter Mane, and Ogerpon.

The distance between Groundblight Shrine and the ranch wasn't too far.

First, they crossed the vast Casseroya Lake, then climbed over a mountain, which led them to Koubari Path.

Under the guidance of the Rotom Phone's navigation and the location provided by Raifort, D found the location of Groundblight Shrine in just twenty minutes.

It was situated deep within a small island in a pond, flanked by waterfalls cascading down, with gentle streams forming a river that surrounded the island.

The island's terrain was complex, with valleys indented and protruding everywhere as far as the eye could see.

The shrine's location was extremely remote, nestled in a mountain hollow, with steep cliffs on either side, and four massive iron chains securely sealing the entrance to the shrine.

"I didn't expect Raifort's coordinates to be so accurate."

D stepped forward and tugged at the chains, but the thick chains didn't budge.

D stopped his actions and used the Rotom Phone to take a picture and send it to Raifort.

Raifort quickly replied.

[Raifort: You found the place?! Great, it seems my investigative direction wasn't wrong!]

[King Wings: So next, we need to find the stakes to pull out, right?]

[Raifort: That's right! If I'm not mistaken, those stakes are scattered on that mountain, with a total of eight. Once all are pulled out, the seal will automatically be lifted!]

[Raifort: Also, be sure not to use brute force to destroy the seal, or the power of the seal might cause a landslide on that mountain!]

D: "..."

He looked at the shrine in front of him, emitting a faint green light, thinking how there's so much ancient black technology in the Pokemon World.

The shrine in front was obviously not a regular shrine. If D remembered correctly, those stakes were also created using special powers.

In the game, once pulled out, the stakes would turn into fragments and disappear, and immediately, the corresponding chains would unlock automatically.

[King Wings: Alright, got it, I'll go look for the sealing stakes now.]

[Raifort: Good luck! Groundblight Shrine, the Ruinous Pokemon Ting-Lu should be sealed there. According to ancient records, Ting-Lu has the ability to cause earthquakes, so be careful! It's been sealed for so many years, its temper is sure to be awful.]

Putting away the phone, D mounted Koraidon's back and said to it: "Let's fly around this mountain first for a look!"

The mountain looked so large, finding those eight small stakes wouldn't be easy.

"Kaaa-sha!"

Koraidon responded and then spread its wings, flying around the mountain.

D's gaze was carefully observing the mountain below.

Below the mountain was Koubari Path, and the small road leading to the top was flanked by a lush pine forest, home to many wild Pokemon.

Deerling, Sawsbuck, Forretress, Heracross...

To the north was the Northern Paldea Sea, and to the south was Casseroya Lake, with no nearby towns, making it seem sparsely populated.

At this moment, Koraidon beneath him suddenly let out a warning growl.

"Kaaa-sha!"

D focused and spotted a faintly glowing blue stake beside a large rock.

He patted Koraidon's shoulder, and Koraidon understandingly landed beside it.

D curiously observed the stake, took out his Rotom Phone to snap a photo, and then reached out to pull it out forcefully.

The next second, the stake disintegrated into fragments and vanished.

"Seven more to go."

But relying solely on his own strength, who knows when he'd find them all.

So, D decisively took out three Poke Balls.

With three flashes of white light, the figures of Pawmot, Dragonite, and Flutter Mane appeared in front of him.

D showed them the photo he just took:

"Everyone, help me look for this thing nearby!"

The Pokemon began to act one after another.

---

---

---

---

# 492: Chapter 489: Ting-Lu, Get!

And D wasn't idling; he continued surveying the mountain carefully, riding on Koraidon.

The locations of the pegs were not obvious; many were hidden in inconspicuous places, and if not careful, they might be missed.

Koraidon flew in the sky, and the residing Pokémon were attracted by the sight, raising their heads to look up.

After spending about half an hour, D and Koraidon circled the mountain a few times before finally discovering the second peg.

When they returned to the starting point, the three Pokémon dispatched by D had already flown back.

"How is it? Any discoveries?" D asked.

"Pawmo!" Pawmo nodded repeatedly, having found one amidst the grass by a small river.

"Roar..." Dragonite nodded as well, having found one at the mountain top.

"Mane." Flutter Mane found two at once.

Seeing this, D was delighted; it was really effective!

Led by the three Pokémon, he went and pulled out the pegs they discovered.

Including the two he found earlier, a total of six pegs had been pulled out, with just two left.

Upon returning to the Groundblight Shrine side, D was startled to find only one of the four chains remaining.

As long as the last two pegs are pulled, the seal on the Groundblight Shrine can be released.

Next, D continued searching nearby with the Pokémon.

Time ticked away second by second, gradually reaching noon. Unfortunately, they still hadn't found the location of the last two pegs.

Helplessly, D had to halt the operation, returning to the front of the Groundblight Shrine, taking out the ingredients he brought over in the morning, and began preparing lunch.

During which, Raifort opened video communication to inquire about D's progress.

Raifort: "How's it going?"

D shook his head: "Searched the whole morning, but still can't locate the last two pegs. I plan to fill my stomach before continuing the search."

"The last two… indeed not that easy! If the principal hadn't sent people to keep an eye on me, I would have gone over to help investigate." Raifort sighed.

D: "...Do you have classes in the afternoon?"

Raifort nodded, answering truthfully: "Yes, two classes."

D speechless: "Then never mind, you stay in school for classes."

"Only can be this way."

After ending the call, D released Cyclizar and Ogerpon, letting them play there.

Meanwhile, he heated the pan with oil, placing the meat patties in the skillet to grill, golden oil sizzling, trailing an enticing aroma.

Next, D sliced the tomatoes, and once the meat was cooked, he used them to create a few special sandwiches.

Once everything was ready, D turned around, taking out Poffins and Pokeblocks from his backpack onto a plate.

Sandwiches, Pokeblocks, Poffins, and Berry Milk.

Thus completing a lunch set.

"Come over to eat!"

D called out, and the Pokémon playing on the grass immediately ran over.

They picked up the sandwiches, happily devouring them.

Just at this moment, as Ogerpon was about to start eating, it suddenly looked towards the distance: "Poni?"

"Hm? What is it?"

Following its gaze, D saw a Pokémon resembling a shrub tree with five yellow spots on its abdomen standing and watching from afar.

Is that... Sudowoodo?

At this moment, the Sudowoodo was drooling, eyes fixed on the Poffins and Pokeblocks D placed in the basket.

Is it a wild Pokémon attracted by the food aroma?

Suddenly noticing itself discovered by the human and the Pokémon, Sudowoodo showed a flustered expression, immediately standing still, covering its face with its hands, pretending to be a tree.

D: "..."

"Gaho!"

"Pawmo?"

At this moment, Cyclizar and Pawmo also looked up, gazing in two other directions.

Turning his head, D saw several other wild Pokémon attracted by the food aroma coming over.

A mature Sawsbuck led several Deerling, standing and watching from the grass.

Even attracting a wild Heracross.

And several wild Toedscool resembling jellyfish that could use tentacles to run on the ground.

Whoa! Attracted so many Pokémon at once!

D was quite surprised; he wasn't carrying any particularly enticing Pokeblocks?

Seeing no reaction from the human, Sudowoodo watched D's situation discreetly through its fingers.

The Sawsbuck calmly looked at the human, while the Deerlings behind it hopped agilely on the grass, seemingly curious about the human and the fragrant food.

Heracross focused on the sweet-smelling food, the aroma seemed sweeter than the nectar it had tasted.

Really wants to eat...

Heracross drooled.

Meanwhile, Toedscool couldn't contain its desire, racing over, intending to snatch food directly from the human and Pokémon!

Seeing their movement, D exclaimed in wonder.

Local Pokémon seem a bit unfriendly, huh?

Facing the bandit-like Toedscool, Ogerpon swiftly stood up.

---

Seeing this Pokemon blocking its way, the few Toedscool opened their mouths, spitting out blobs of mud.

Mud Shot!

Ogerpon couldn't dodge in time and was hit by the mud.

Looking at the dirt on its body, Ogerpon was very angry.

"Bonnie!"

It glanced back at D.

D nodded at it: "It's all yours."

"Bonnie."

Ogerpon was waiting for these words.

In the next moment, green light surged in its hand, creating a vine-covered club, and it swung the thorny club hard towards the bandit Toedscool.

The club slammed down, instantly hitting one Toedscool in the face, sending it flying backwards.

Then, Ogerpon rushed towards the other Toedscool.

However, witnessing this, the Toedscool were utterly shocked and fled in the opposite direction.

Heracross and Sawsbuck on the hillside were also startled.

Having driven away the food-thieving bandit Toedscool, Ogerpon finally put away the thorny club and happily returned to its spot to enjoy its lunch.

D: "..."

Ogerpon's appearance and strength indeed don't match at all!

It was really terrifying seeing it swing a big club with such a cute appearance.

But speaking of which, looking at the remaining wild Pokemon, D suddenly remembered something.

If anyone is familiar with the terrain and environment around here, it must be the Pokemon originally living here.

Many hands make light work; should he hire these Pokemon to help find the remaining two stakes?

He decided to do it, and luckily he still had some Poffins and Pokeblocks in his backpack.

D turned to look at those Pokemon and waved them over.

Seeing this, Sudowoodo was slightly surprised; was this human calling it over?

Sawsbuck was also a bit surprised and hesitated a bit, but the few Deerling beside it were happily bouncing over.

Heracross looked worriedly at the Pokemon that had just swung a club at people.

Would it get hit if it went over?

Yet, when it saw the human-friendly giving sweet food to the little Deerling, it couldn't sit still.

"Heya!"

Heracross immediately rushed down.

Sawsbuck and Sudowoodo also followed closely behind.

Looking at the group of wild Pokemon, D couldn't help but sigh inwardly: the Power of Viridian, which significantly enhances Pokemon affinity, is really convenient!

"Eat up!" D said with a smile.

Heracross ate the sweet Pokeblock with a blissful expression on its face.

Just as it thought, it's even sweeter than honey!

"By the way, there's something I want to ask you all." Once the wild Pokemon finished their Pokeblocks, D started speaking.

Hearing this, the Pokemon raised their heads, looking over with a bewildered expression.

"Heya?" Heracross asked.

D took out his Rotom Phone, found the photo of the stake he had previously taken, and asked:

"Have any of you seen this thing around here?"

The Pokemon stared intently. Living in the jungle, Sawsbuck and Deerling looked puzzled.

Heracross shook its head, indicating it hadn't seen it.

Sudowoodo, however, suddenly got an idea; it had seen this thing!

"Really?" D assured: "If anyone can help me find it, I'll give them a big bag of Pokeblocks as a reward."

Sudowoodo hadn't expected this surprise, nodding repeatedly: "Huso!"

Heracross's eyes also lit up; it could help find it too!

Three minutes later, the group of Pokemon scattered, eager to help this human find the glowing stake in exchange for Poffins.

Meanwhile, D followed Sudowoodo along the mountain path.

About ten minutes later, they arrived at a hidden cave.

The cave wasn't deep, and at the end of the cavern, there was a stake emitting a blue glow.

D went up and pulled it out; this leaves only the last one!

"I'll keep my promise and give you a bag of Pokeblocks; let's head back to the camp now!" D said.

"Huso!" Sudowoodo danced joyfully; this human was quite nice!

When they returned to the camp, Heracross and Sawsbuck hadn't come back yet.

D kept his promise and gave Sudowoodo a bag of Pokeblocks.

Looking at Sudowoodo's excited appearance, D wasn't sure what to say.

But speaking of which, Sudowoodo, despite its tree-like appearance, has a Rock type.

Not only that, but it's also very afraid of water.

"Interesting," D thought.

Heracross and Sawsbuck were still searching for the stake, and D didn't plan to be idle either.

After giving Sudowoodo the Pokeblocks, he continued to set out with Koraidon to look for it.

Koraidon was flying above, recalling the stake in the cave earlier, and D pondered for a moment before speaking to Koraidon:

"Lower the flying altitude; let's take a closer look below, paying extra attention to nearby caves or valleys."

"Agassi!"

Koraidon responded and immediately descended.

D's gaze carefully swept across the surface.

Grass, hollows, valleys, under the cliffs...

Time passed minute by minute, and after hours of thorough searching, D finally discovered the last stake in a very inconspicuous small cave halfway up a mountain.

---

When he pulled out the stake, a tremor suddenly echoed from afar.

The seal on Ting-Lu was broken!

"Let's go back!" D immediately said to Koraidon.

Koraidon acknowledged with a sound and promptly turned, flying towards the campsite.

Soon, they arrived there, just like D anticipated, as the final two stakes were pulled out, the last chain on the shrine disappeared!

In front of the shrine on the grassy field, the Heracross and Sawsbuck, who found nothing, also returned here.

Noticing the strange phenomena in front, several wild Pokemon turned their gaze over there.

Under their watchful eyes, D stepped forward and gently touched the large door radiating blue light.

In the next second, the shrine's door collapsed with a crash, causing dust and smoke to rise all around.

Immediately, a Pokemon leapt out from within.

"Guding ding!"

Ting-Lu, finally seeing the light of day after thousands of years of being sealed, was shrouded in a destructive aura.

A near-tangible malice emanated from its body.

It lowered its head, and the enormous cauldron slammed onto the ground with a crash.

In the next second, the earth began to shake violently, and a purple aura of disaster spread in all directions, with rocks rolling down the mountainside.

Seeing this, Heracross and the others appeared terrified and hid behind D.

"Is this the Treasures of Ruin? It indeed looks dangerous." D glanced at the strange surroundings.

Ting-Lu must be stopped; otherwise, it will bring terrifying calamities to this land, just like it did to the Paldea Ancient Kingdom thousands of years ago!

In this situation, a quick resolution is necessary. It's best to defeat Ting-Lu in a single blow!

"Koraidon, Collision Course!"

D steadied himself as the purple aura of disaster was about to envelop him, and he immediately issued the command.

"Ah gah ss!"

Koraidon promptly flew out.

Its crimson body leaped high into the air, transforming as it fiercely charged at Ting-Lu.

In the next moment, a violent explosion sounded from the front.

In an instant, all phenomena disappeared.

Looking at the fallen Ting-Lu, D took out a Poke Ball and threw it.

As the Poke Ball touched the incapacitated Ting-Lu, its body transformed into a red light and was captured within.

After three shakes, the red light on the Poke Ball's clasp dissipated.

This indicated that the capture was successful.

D stepped forward to pick up the Poke Ball containing Ting-Lu.

Next, it's time to use the Reversing Cuisine to transform the calamity shrouding it into a blessing.

Having accomplished the goal, D turned around and looked at the Heracross and other Pokemon behind him, then took out the remaining Pokeblocks from his bag.

"Consider these as a reward for helping me find the stakes. Thank you very much, we're leaving now."

Heracross accepted the Pokeblock, and with Sawsbuck, they blankly gazed at the human in front of them.

Even though they didn't manage to aid, they too received Pokeblocks?

"Goodbye!"

D mounted Koraidon, waved to the Pokemon below, and then left the Kutba Trail.

Back at the ranch, D initially intended to help recover Ting-Lu's strength after its incapacitation.

For this, D made thorough preparations, specifically inviting Calyrex to prevent Ting-Lu from going berserk.

However, unexpectedly, when he released Ting-Lu from the Poke Ball, the Ruinous Pokemon did not go berserk as before.

It stood calmly on the grass, appearing just like an ordinary deer.

D was slightly taken aback and then tentatively tried to communicate.

"Ting-Lu?"

Ting-Lu looked at him but did not respond.

This surprised D further, what on earth was going on?

After a brief moment of thought, he took out the Rotom Phone and dialed Raifort's video call to report the current situation to her.

"I have successfully broken the seal of the Groundblight Shrine and captured Ting-Lu."

"Wonderful! I knew you could do it!"

Raifort's tone was filled with surprise: "Did Ting-Lu not go berserk at that time?"

"I used a quick resolution tactic and defeated it before it could go berserk, but now Ting-Lu seems a bit strange..."

As he spoke, D turned the camera on the usually Ting-Lu.

"After returning to the ranch, it didn't cause any calamities and became very calm. When I call its name, it looks at me but does not respond."

"Became calm?" Raifort observed the Ting-Lu on the other side, feeling a bit caught off guard.

She carefully recalled the legends of the Treasures of Ruin.

According to legend, in the era of the distant Paldea Empire, a merchant brought four treasures: a cauldron, a sword, a scroll, and a jade, from the Eastern Lands.

He sold these treasures to the King, but that very night, the four treasures suddenly transformed into Pokemon and caused havoc, leading to the destruction of the royal city.

Ancient records state that it's said the true forms of the four treasures were Pokemon. Over the years, they absorbed many desires and curses from different places, and when handed to the King, his desires immediately activated them...

Consider D's nature, Raifort had a rough conjecture in her heart.

"I think I know the reason," she said.

"What is it?" D inquired.

Raifort felt a complex emotion: "It seems it transformed due to your inner self. The Treasures of Ruin were not inherently calamitous; they became what they are because of human desires."

"If I am not mistaken, you don't harbor greed towards them. You merely see them as Pokemon, unlike others who treat them as treasures."

---

---

---

---

# 493: Chapter 490: The Auspicious Ting-Lu of the Land, Mysterious Pokemon in the Bamboo Forest?

Raifort had a point.

After her explanation, D immediately understood the situation with Ting-Lu.

Now, should he still feed Ting-Lu with the Reversing series cuisine?

According to his research so far, there are three types of Reversing cuisines.

They are the [Heart] cuisine that reverses personality, the [Body] cuisine that reverses constitution, and the [Techniques] cuisine that reverses abilities, authority, and aura.

Ting-Lu has absorbed too many desires and curses, these human emotions have transformed into its power.

Its characteristic [Vessel of Ruin] and exclusive move [Calamitous Ruin] are proof of this.

In such a situation, if these abilities and emotions can be reversed, perhaps Ting-Lu can transform into an Auspicious Pokemon.

You just need to feed it [Reversing Cuisine·Techniques].

And the Reversed World is next door, D decided to give it a try and see the effects after reversing.

To prepare for the three Ruinous Pokemon that follow.

The call wasn't disconnected yet.

Raifort looked at Ting-Lu on the opposite screen with a complicated mood.

Indeed, is the Treasure of Ruin something people like her can't truly possess?

Raifort originally wanted to investigate the Treasures of Ruin in person and claim them for herself.

But now it seems that such a thing is beyond her power.

Because both ancient records and passed-down legends indicate, only a person with a pure heart can lift the shrine's seal.

Raifort remembered the content clearly.

At the end of the ancient text, it described:

The Treasures of Ruin will shine again because of the pure heart of the one who lifts the seal.

So be it!

Raifort sighed inwardly, now she felt very satisfied to just see these precious existences with her own eyes.

Thinking of this, Raifort smiled softly: "The treasures I've dreamed of, you can keep them for now! And the next three, I'll entrust them to you too!"

"Of course."

"Then let's leave it here for now! I have to go teach history. When you've collected all four Treasures of Ruin, remember to bring them by to show me!"

"Don't worry!" D had already promised Raifort this, so he naturally wouldn't go back on his word.

Only then did Raifort hang up the phone satisfactorily.

D headed to the Reversed World and asked Giratina for a bucket of water from there.

After some operations, he successfully crafted cuisine that could reverse Pokemon's authority and power.

[Reversed Red Tea·Techniques (s): A Legendary Cuisine made with water from the Reversed World, infused with the power of the Reversed World, possessing the ability to change the nature of substances.

Cooking Effect: Reversing the Universe, reversing a Pokemon's abilities, authority, and aura, effective only on Legendary Pokemon.

Evaluation: A magical cuisine, a Giratina product, guaranteed to be exquisite!]

D brought the cup of red tea to Ting-Lu.

"Ting-Lu, you can understand what I'm saying, right?"

Ting-Lu exhaled two jets of white air from its nostrils, looking solemnly and reverently at the human in front of it, not answering.

Just by looking into the eyes of this Pokemon, D knew it could understand him.

But why wasn't Ting-Lu responding?

After a brief silence, D continued to ask:

"This is the cuisine I made. It can change your essence, erase the evil thoughts and desires you've absorbed from humans, and return you to your original form. Want to give it a try?"

Hearing these words, Ting-Lu indeed set its gaze on the bowl of red tea.

After a while, Ting-Lu lifted its head again to look at the human in front of it.

How many years has it been?

In the distant past, Ting-Lu had turned into a giant cauldron and fell into slumber.

Never expecting to be taken as a treasure by humans, wandering from place to place, during which it was forced to absorb vast amounts of desires and curses.

Those evil thoughts caused its body and spirit to be influenced, corroded by malice, losing reason, and performing actions that went against its instincts, eventually leading to it being imprisoned by humans.

Even now, it retains a significant amount of cursed power.

Now being awakened again, initially, Ting-Lu thought this human would treat it like a treasure like the humans of the past.

But after observing for a while, it discovered that the human seemed to treat it as just a normal Pokemon?

No greed, no evil thoughts, nor any desire to possess it.

Such a person still exists in this world!

Ting-Lu was incredibly shocked.

D, who has already raised a bunch of Legendary Pokemon on the World Tree, is unaware of Ting-Lu's thoughts.

He had noticed that this Pokemon was contemplating, so he didn't speak and waited patiently for its response.

After a long time, the Ting-Lu in front of him finally spoke.

It opened its mouth and let out a peculiar growl: "Moo-moo?"

D was slightly taken aback.

Then, through the Power of Viridian, he understood Ting-Lu's thoughts.

Ting-Lu: "You say it can help this old one remove those curses and evil thoughts, is that true?"

"Of course it's true." D nodded, a bit surprised by how Ting-Lu's words had such a classical tone.

But then he thought of it; these Ruinous Pokemon came from the distant Eastern Lands, so it made sense.

"Would you like to give it a try?" D asked sincerely: "It can reverse the greed and malice, transforming them into the Power of Auspicious, and they'll never affect your body again."

---

Upon hearing these words, Ting-Lu's eyes lit up with a ray of light.

It could sense that this person was not lying.

If that is the case, then it shall give it a try!

With this thought, Ting-Lu lowered its head and used its tongue to roll up the bowl of red tea and drank it.

D stood by, observing its actions.

When Ting-Lu drank the last sip of red tea from the bowl, its body suddenly erupted with a golden brilliance.

The light instantly enveloped its body, and within the glow, Ting-Lu appeared extremely relaxed, shaking its head, causing the ancient and heavy giant cauldron on it to sway as well.

Soon, the golden light dissipated, and Ting-Lu's eyes became a degree brighter than before.

Its growl was light and excited: "Moo moo!" (I feel unprecedentedly relaxed now!)

"I?" D was somewhat unaccustomed to Ting-Lu's self-reference.

Ting-Lu looked at him: "Moo moo!" (Haha, I have existed in this world for a long time now! I haven't any place to go currently, since you've won over me, I shall stay here then!)

D: "..."

It truly is a naturally friendly Pokemon!

In that case, he must have gained Ting-Lu's approval, right?

Yet, compared to this matter, D was more concerned with Ting-Lu's current transformation.

He straightforwardly asked: "Do you feel any changes in yourself compared to before?"

"Moo moo?" (Changes?)

Ting-Lu carefully sensed for a moment, suddenly it felt some strange information appear in its mind!

Using Earth Power to enrich the soil, provide nutrients to the land, protect the tranquillity of the earth, prevent natural disasters like earthquakes...

What is going on?!

Ting-Lu was very puzzled: "Moo moo?"

It directly showcased its newly acquired ability to D.

Golden light surged from Ting-Lu's body, then enveloped the entire pasture field.

Instantly, the soil on this land was blessed with the Power of Auspicious and became more fertile.

"Just as I thought." Hearing Ting-Lu's new ability, D couldn't help but murmur in his heart.

He looked at the rather puzzled Ting-Lu and explained: "It seems the Power of Calamity has now transformed into the Power of Auspicious, from now on, you will no longer be controlled by it."

"Moo moo!" (Oh ho ho! Never thought I would encounter such a thing!)

Ting-Lu looked very happy indeed.

If that's the case, then it shall safeguard the peace of this land!

Meanwhile, D took the opportunity to inquire Ting-Lu about the other three Ruinous Pokemon.

"By the way, Ting-Lu, do you know Wo-Chien, Chi-Yu, and Chien-Pao?"

"Moo moo?" Upon hearing this, Ting-Lu, immersed in its new ability, immediately looked at D: "Moo moo?" (Could it be, you also want to use this power to dispel the Power of Calamity from their bodies?)

"Exactly so." D nodded: "To be honest, I have already obtained their current specific locations, before setting forth, I wish to understand them better."

"Moo moo." (I see.)

Ting-Lu nodded its heavy head.

Then, it began to introduce the three calamity beasts from its memory.

"Moo moo..." (I have not seen those three for a long time, the last time was several thousand years ago.)

At that time, all four were considered treasures and were continuously transported to various places, eventually sent to the hands of a human king.

Influenced by the greed of that king, all four lost their reason and were occupied by dark thoughts, madly destroying everything around.

"Moo moo." (Chi-Yu, that fellow was originally a very elegant and wise Pokemon, yet like me, due to absorbing too much sinister thought, it was entangled with too many flames of jealousy.)

"Moo moo." (Wo-Chien, that fellow, like me, has long existed in this world, originally a slow old creature, loved lying under the sun all day, but later was stained with too much hatred, completely changing its appearance.)

"Moo moo." (Chien-Pao, that fellow was originally a righteous and dignified Pokemon, but later its sword was broken due to absorbing too much hatred, since then, Chien-Pao lost its original nature.)

D listened silently, is this the original form of the Four Calamities?

Ting-Lu looked at him, requesting: "Moo Moo!" (If you're going to find them, take me along! Perhaps I can persuade them.)

"Alright, no problem." Since Ting-Lu wanted to go, D had no reason to refuse, and readily agreed.

As night fell, D returned to his room and began preparing dinner for the Pokemon.

After a night had passed, the next morning, after finishing his work on the ranch, D took a few Pokemon and Ting-Lu and set off again.

Today, they planned to head to the Paldea North Second District, a place called Fury Falls.

The Firescourge Shrine, which seals the ruinous Chi-Yu, is located there.

Koraidon flew quickly, and they reached their destination in just half an hour.

Below was a stretch of connected mountains, with a tranquil valley covered in light morning mist, along with the magnificent waterfall cascading down from the summit, creating a scene resembling a fairyland.

"No wonder it's one of the ten scenic spots of Paldea!"

Gazing at the largest of the waterfalls, D couldn't help but marvel aloud.

And Koraidon turned its head to look around, seemingly searching for something.

"Ah-gah-syy?"

"Koraidon, let's go down and have a look!" D patted Koraidon's head.

"Ah-gah-syy!"

Koraidon responded, turned its head and landed opposite the waterfall, where there was a lively bamboo forest area.

The towering and verdant bamboos created a rare bamboo forest scenery.

Seeing this environment, D couldn't help but think: A place like this must be popular among Pokemon like Pancham and Pangoro.

However, it's a pity that the Paldea Region doesn't have Pancham habitat.

This bamboo forest is home to normal Pokemon like Swablu, Deerling, and others.

D took out his phone and checked the location sent by Raifort on the map app.

According to the location display, the Firescourge Shrine is situated among these secluded mountains.

But the terrain is very complex here, with mountains surrounding valleys, and more than ten waterfalls and rivers of various sizes, making the specific location of the Firescourge Shrine unclear.

Raifort only knew that the Firescourge Shrine was located in this mountain range.

D had slightly more information than her, knowing that the Firescourge Shrine was hidden behind a waterfall in the "Water Curtain Cave."

The problem, however, is that there are so many waterfalls in this mountain range that finding the target wasn't easy.

Yet there's no choice, they could only search slowly.

D used his Rotom Phone to enlarge the nearby map, then rode on Koraidon to circle around the area, marking all visible waterfalls on the map.

Once marked, he began searching them one by one.

The first one, nothing.

The second one, nothing.

The third one, also nothing.

Drifting through the cascading waterfalls wasn't pleasant, but thanks to the special physiology of the Pokemon World, the waterfall's powerful impact didn't cause any major harm to D.

During this time, a few curious Swablu flew over, perched on the bamboo, and watched the human's actions with a curious gaze, as if looking at a fool.

"I should've brought Farfetch'd over for special training." Thinking about Farfetch'd's goal of cutting through the waterfall, D couldn't help but think.

He thought while marking a red cross on the waterfalls explored on the Rotom Phone.

"Hmm, none of the caves are behind the three waterfalls in this valley, Koraidon, let's check the mountain ahead!"

"Ah-gah-syy!"

Koraidon responded and immediately turned around, flying to another waterfall.

They didn't notice, among the bamboo behind them, a bipedal Pokemon with beige fur and a determined look was standing on a rock watching them.

Behind its head hung white fur, looking like it was wearing a white headband.

As a gust of wind blew, the two strands of the headband gently swayed in the wind.

---

---

---

---

# 494: Chapter 491: Waterfall Training, Cutting Through the Way of the Waterfall!

Paldea Region, North Second District, Fury Falls.

In the Bamboo Forest surrounded by streams, a large group of Pokemon is seriously listening to D speak.

Gastly, Jigglypuff, Amoonguss, Passimian, Swablu, Deerling, Hoppip, Vivillon, Venomoth...

"Everyone, listen up, if anyone can help me find the item in this picture, I can give them a big box of Pokeblocks and Poffins!"

D looked at the group of wild Pokemon attracted by the aroma and immediately decided to use his usual tactic, employing the Power of Viridian, to hire these Pokemon living near Fury Falls to help find the Firescourge Shrine.

The Power of Viridian greatly enhanced his affinity with wild Pokemon.

Coupled with the generally gentle nature of modern Pokemon, D quickly attracted a curious group of Pokemon when he took out the Poffins and Pokeblocks from his bag.

As expected, just as D anticipated, the greedy Pokemon's eyes lit up with joy upon hearing this.

One of the Passimians even let out a series of monkey calls, excitedly declaring that no one was more familiar with this place than it was!

It's best to entrust the search to it!

However, upon hearing this, an unhappy Amoonguss beside it clenched its fists.

That expression seemed to say: Such a good thing cannot be enjoyed by Passimian alone!

Unwilling to be outdone, Passimian hopped twice, looking as if it wouldn't yield to anyone.

As the two Pokemon were about to fight over this, D quickly said:

"You can search together, as long as you find it together, anyone can get the reward."

"Gastly～"

The Gastly in the group let out a happy Growl, as if saying it liked the idea.

Gastly happily floated away, enjoying this treasure hunt game.

The other Pokemon also turned and ran out in different directions.

Passimian and Amoonguss exchanged glances, neither yielding to the other, then turned away and dashed in opposite directions.

It seems like these two don't get along very well!

But to be honest, wild Pokemon are really interesting; even without humans, their interactions can be so fascinating.

This time, the number of Pokemon helpers was more than yesterday.

To receive their news promptly, D didn't wander around but stayed beneath Fury Falls.

However, looking at the spectacular waterfall before him, D couldn't help but think of Farfetch'd's waterfall slashing plan.

After a moment of contemplation, he said to the Dragonite next to him: "Dragonite."

"Hoo?" Dragonite turned its head to look.

"Can you go to the ranch and bring Farfetch'd here for me? I want to see the results of its previous special training," D said, smiling as he patted Dragonite's head.

"Hoo～" Dragonite gladly agreed.

Then, it flapped its wings and flew away.

As time ticked by, during the wait, D laid out a picnic cloth between the bamboo.

Then placed some food on it, sitting with Koraidon and others in the forest, waiting while watching the wind blow the bamboo leaves down.

At this moment, the first Pokemon D sent out to search for the Firescourge Shrine and the stakes returned.

It was the same Gastly as before.

It excitedly said: "Gastly～!"

"Found it?" D eagerly asked.

Gastly nodded repeatedly: "Gastly Gastly!"

Then, it turned, floating in front to lead the way.

Following behind, D indeed found a glowing stake at a mountainside spot.

Although the exact location of the Firescourge Shrine wasn't found yet, it's okay to pull out these stakes in advance.

"Great job, Gastly!" D praised.

This wild Gastly looked very happy, floating up and down with purple smoke billowing from it.

"Gastly!" This game is fun!

Gastly liked playing games; it used to live alone in this forest, feeling lonely with no one to play with.

Now, playing the treasure hunt game with someone, Gastly was very happy.

It eagerly conveyed to the human: "Gastly Gastly!"

D was surprised: "Are you saying that I should wait over there while you continue to help find a few more glowing stakes?"

"Gastly Gastly!" The purple mist on Gastly swirled continuously, affirming it!

Then, it turned and floated away ghostly.

Watching this scene, D suddenly found Gastly to be quite adorable.

Simple-minded and silly, slightly mischievous.

Did it take the commission for finding stakes and the Firescourge Shrine as a game?

Moreover, the evolution of Gastly, Gengar, is quite popular, D remembered many people liking this purple chubby character in his past life.

Many anime episodes featured "Haunted Houses," "Ghost Ships," and other "haunting" episodes,

with the haunting protagonist usually being ghost-type Pokemon like Gengar.

Interestingly, most Gengars prank people purely for fun, intentionally scaring them for certain purposes, unlike the wild Litwicks and Chandelures that actively attack humans.

"Well, I'll make sure to give this Gastly more Pokeblocks and gifts later!"

D made a decision in his mind.

Returning to the bamboo area, Dragonite had already brought Farfetch'd over.

---

Farfetch'd held its leek high, looking around the bamboo forest and the massive waterfall with high spirits.

"Farfetch'd, how about testing the fruits of your training over the past year here?" D asked, "Use your leek to cut through this waterfall!"

"Quack!"

Upon hearing this, Farfetch'd carried its leek and walked beneath the waterfall.

D's words piqued its interest. It also wanted to test the level of its sword technique.

"Then let's give it a try!" D encouraged.

"Quack!"

Farfetch'd walked beneath the waterfall.

It focused its mind and held its breath, carefully feeling the flow of the waterfall before it.

In the next moment, it gripped its leek tightly.

A flash of white light streaked by as the hefty leek slashed across the cascading waterfall.

The water flowing down from above hesitated slightly but soon resumed its flow.

"Hmm, although the power of this move looks quite impressive, it's clearly still a long way from slicing through the waterfall," D thought to himself.

After a brief contemplation, he remembered that Farfetch'd had learned Psyblade after eating the Berry Platter.

That's a move where telekinesis wraps around the blade to launch a swift attack with powerful spiritual power.

Perhaps with the help of psychic power, it might be able to cut through the waterfall...

D said, "Farfetch'd, use that move, Psyblade, and use your spiritual power to split the waterfall in front of you!"

"Quack!"

Farfetch'd also remembered that move.

A blue glow immediately appeared in its eyes.

An invisible psychic power enveloped its leek.

Farfetch'd focused its mind, then swung its leek powerfully.

A strong psychic power spread out instantly, blocking the falling water above, successfully splitting the waterfall in two.

The waterfall was cut, it worked!

Farfetch'd stared in disbelief, widening its eyes: "Quack?!"

"Whoosh!"

The energy of Psyblade gradually dissipated, and the waterfall returned to its original state.

"Congratulations, it seems you've done it," D nodded with satisfaction.

This was Farfetch'd's original dream when it came to the pasture, and now, it seemed D had helped it achieve it!

"Quack!"

Farfetch'd let out a joyful cry.

The surrounding Pokemon, seeing its success, felt a twinge of envy.

Farfetch'd's display sparked a training craze; Cyclizar and Pawmo, among others, told D they wanted to give it a try too!

"Hmm? You guys also want to cut through the waterfall?" D was taken aback and asked.

Both Pokemon nodded vigorously.

"Pamo!"

"Quack Ow!"

"But you're skilled in different areas compared to Farfetch'd?" D couldn't help but remind them.

Pawmo excels at using its speed and powerful electrical current to attack foes.

While Cyclizar's all-out speed is even faster than Pawmo's...

But seeing the eager looks on the two Pokemon's faces, D couldn't say much, and could only let them try it at the waterfall's base.

In the end, Pawmo and Cyclizar tried for a long time but couldn't cut through the waterfall like Farfetch'd.

D: "..."

Seeing the two Pokemon look dejected, D couldn't help but say:

"Don't be disheartened, each Pokemon has its areas of expertise! And I have good news for you, even if you can't cut through the waterfall, you can still use it for practice!"

"Quack Ow?" Upon hearing this, Cyclizar quickly looked over.

D nodded: "That's the Waterfall Training Method. In the human world, some martial artists and fighting masters train their bodies with Pokemon by using waterfalls."

"They sit beneath the waterfall and let the cascading water wash over them continuously as a form of training."

"If combined with proper training methods, this can make the body stronger and also hone the spirit and Guts, it's a very effective special training method."

"Want to give it a try?" D asked with a smile.

"Quack Ow!"

Three minutes later, Cyclizar, Pawmo, Dragonite, Ogerpon, and Flutter Mane all lined up on a big rock beneath the waterfall, closed their eyes, and started feeling the impact of the waterfall with focus.

Koraidon didn't go, because it had no effect on it anymore.

Ting-Lu stood aside, chuckling at the scene, and said: "Moo-moo!" (Ho-ho-ho! Such a bunch of hardworking little ones!)

However, just as it finished speaking, D saw Ogerpon start using its hands to prop up its coat, shielding itself from the water flow and lazily slacking off.

D: "..."

Upon realizing it was caught, Ogerpon didn't panic. It quickly stood up and ran out, happily hopping twice on the spot: "Bo-ni～"

"Alright, alright, if you don't want to train, just go over there and eat some Poffins!"

D didn't have many demands for the Pokemon; if they wanted to become stronger, he would help them with special training.

If they just wanted to live happily, then he would make delicious Poffins and Pokeblocks for them, allowing them to replenish their energy after playing.

"Bo-ni～"

Ogerpon happily ran around D and Koraidon.

And just at this moment, a Swablu chirped and flew over, landing on D's shoulder, and said to him: "Chirp-chirp!"

"Huh? You found a glowing stake too?" D quickly asked.

Swablu gently nodded its little head.

"Take me to see it!" D called over a few Pokemon who were training under the waterfall.

"Chirp-chirp!"

Swablu flapped its wings and started leading the way ahead.

And after they left, an off-white figure suddenly jumped down from the top of the cliff.

Shortly after, D indeed found a glowing stake in a patch of grass.

He stepped forward and pulled out the stake.

Now, the eight stakes sealing Chi-Yu were down to just six left!

What they needed to do next was return to Fury Falls and continue waiting.

However, when D arrived at Fury Falls, he caught a small shadow under the waterfall with the corner of his eye.

It was a small off-white Bear-type Pokemon.

At this moment, the Pokemon was sitting under Fury Falls, maintaining a cross-legged meditation posture, seemingly using this method to train.

"Gaghisshh!" Koraidon also noticed the Pokemon's action.

D: !!!

He immediately widened his eyes incredulously.

Wasn't this Bear-type Pokemon the first stage of the Galar Region's Legendary Pokemon, Kubfu?!

No, not right! How could there be a Kubfu residing near Fury Falls?

D looked at the Kubfu in disbelief.

Was it a wild Kubfu? Or did Trainers bring it here for training?

And just then, Kubfu sitting under the waterfall suddenly opened its eyes and looked towards the human not far away, seeming to sense something.

It's been discovered!

D saw that the Kubfu seemed startled by their arrival.

But Kubfu didn't run away; it continued sitting under the waterfall, training.

After a brief shock, D grew interested in this Legendary Pokemon from the Galar Region.

He was worried Kubfu would be scared and flee, so he didn't approach immediately, instead maintaining a respectful distance to observe.

Kubfu also opened its eyes and looked at the human over there.

Since their entrance into the Bamboo Forest, Kubfu had already noticed them.

Having originally lived here, Kubfu began secretly observing this human's actions.

Initially, nothing seemed amiss; Kubfu didn't find this human different from previous people visiting Fury Falls.

But later, it heard the human explaining training methods and directing his Pokemon to train their bodies under the waterfall.

Kubfu was very interested in this training method.

So, when it saw the human leave, it couldn't wait to go under the waterfall to try it.

And unexpectedly, it found this special training method very effective for it!

Using its organs capable of generating "Fighting Energy" and the unique breathing technique it had previously mastered, Kubfu carefully sensed the invisible force of the water impact, causing an extraordinary and mystical sensation within its body...

At this moment, its gaze met the human's mid-air.

Kubfu felt a bit flustered.

But the human merely stood there watching it.

D looked at the Kubfu whose fur was wetted by the rushing water, opened his mouth, and asked:

"Are you training too?"

---

---

---
