# 496: Chapter 493: Cyclizar vs Kubfu, The Auspicious Flames of Chi-Yu

Fury Falls, within the Bamboo Forest.

Looking at the group of Pokémon before him, D took out the pre-prepared Pokeblock and Poffin from his backpack.

"Thank you so much for your help. Here, these Poffin are gifts I prepared for you as a token of appreciation, please accept them!"

"Ohwoo!"

Passimian let out an excited cry, it stepped forward to receive the gift, full of excitement on its face.

Amoonguss also jumped forward and took the box of Pokeblock from D's hand.

The rest of the Pokémon stepped forward in turn to take their share of the gifts.

Jigglypuff, Gastly, Swablu, Hoppip, Vivillon...

Soon, only one Kubfu was left among the Pokémon.

Kubfu glanced at the other Pokémon, then looked up towards D in front of it.

A serious expression appeared on its small face.

"Beah!"

D had other plans in mind for this Kubfu.

He crouched down, aligning his gaze to Kubfu's height, and then asked:

"Kubfu, do you want to come with us?"

"Beah!"

Kubfu nodded its head. After spending several hours together, it felt this human and it were quite a match in temperament.

Seeing Kubfu also liking him, D was quite pleasantly surprised in his heart.

Yet at that moment, Kubfu looked at him with a very serious expression.

"Beah!"

D was slightly stunned: "You want to battle with me?"

Kubfu seriously nodded its head.

Seeing this, D immediately understood.

Indeed, this matches the tone of the Pokemon World!

Especially for a Pokémon like Kubfu with such a temperament.

He had to personally defeat Kubfu to prove he had the strength to become its Trainer, only then could he gain Kubfu's approval.

Looking at the serious Kubfu, D suddenly thought of something interesting and pretended to worry:

"What if I lose?"

Hearing this, even Kubfu was a bit stupefied.

It stood there dumbfounded for two seconds, showing a troubled expression.

"Be... Beah!"

Kubfu looked towards Koraidon, as if implying that Pokémon was strong, suggesting D could use that Pokémon to battle against it.

D couldn't help but laugh: "Alright, I was teasing you. Since you want to battle me, I should give it my all."

Kubfu suddenly breathed a sigh of relief.

"Cyclizar, I'm entrusting this battle to you!" D called out.

Deploying Koraidon against Kubfu wouldn't be fair, so it's just right for Cyclizar to step in and test the results of its training.

As D had expected.

Cyclizar, standing behind, showed a surprised expression upon hearing D call its name, but followed by a cry of excitement.

"Gahwoo!"

Cyclizar vs Kubfu!

Without much more words, once the two Pokémon were ready, the battle officially commenced.

Kubfu's eyes were firm, tightly clutching its right fist, and it commenced with Rock Smash charging towards Cyclizar.

"Draco Meteor!" D calmly commanded.

Cyclizar watched the approaching Kubfu, and a powerful burst of Dragon Type Energy erupted from its body.

That purple energy soared into the sky, gathering into meteors blazing with tail flames, crashing down!

The massive meteor halted Kubfu's advance.

The composed Bear-type Pokémon decisively shattered the meteor with a punch, yet in the next second, it was struck by another meteor.

The tremendous impact sent Kubfu flying, but it quickly stood back up, dodging Draco Meteor while swiftly maneuvering within it.

Just as it was about to exit the meteor's coverage, Kubfu suddenly leapt high, transforming its right hand into a chopping blade, slicing straight toward Cyclizar.

The next second, an astonishing scene occurred for Kubfu.

The Cyclizar below accelerated, dodging its attack!

"Beah?!" Such speed!

"Shift Change!" D's voice rang out.

The air sac on Cyclizar's front was instantly enveloped in a glow, rotating rapidly like a spinning tire.

Landed, Kubfu swiftly turned to launch a kick attack toward Cyclizar.

Cyclizar quickly crossed its arms in front to block it.

Kubfu seized the moment, completely abandoning defense, advancing into Cyclizar's space, and unleashed a rapid attack with its fists.

That was... Close Combat!

"Pa-pa-pa-pa!"

A barrage of fists rained down on Cyclizar, forcing it to retreat continuously.

"This Kubfu's strength is truly extraordinary!" D couldn't help but murmur inwardly.

Not yet evolved into Urshifu, but already refining its boxing to this extent, showing a semblance of a Grandmaster.

"Shift Change again!" D continued.

With the command, the tire-like air sac on Cyclizar's chest spun even faster instantaneously.

It swung its claws, unleashing a massive Dragon Claw to repel Kubfu.

Kubfu's figure staggered.

Seeing Kubfu exposing a flaw, D seized the moment and decisively gave the command:

"Now! Use Dragon Rush!"

The second Shift Change brought Cyclizar's speed to an extremely exaggerated level.

While releasing an intense and terrifying aura, it leapt up, rushing down towards Kubfu.

---

Such speed, so fast that Kubfu couldn't even react to dodge!

"Boom!"

A massive explosion was heard, and dust immediately filled the air. The gusts generated by the battle spread rapidly in all directions, blowing away a large swath of bamboo leaves.

As the light on the ground dissipated, Cyclizar slowly walked out.

Meanwhile, Kubfu was lying on the ground, clearly having lost the ability to fight.

D took a Poke Ball from his waist and tossed it toward Kubfu.

The Poke Ball hit the target successfully and automatically popped open, and Kubfu's body transformed into a red light and got captured inside.

The Poke Ball landed on the ground, gently shaking and continuously flashing with red light.

Once, twice, three times...

The red glow on the Poke Ball stopped flashing, replaced by a burst of white starlight.

This indicated that the capture was successful!

D excitedly went forward to pick up Kubfu's Poke Ball.

"Kubfu, come out!"

With a flash of white light, the injured Kubfu appeared once more in the bamboo forest.

"Please take care of me from now on, Kubfu!" D smiled and extended his hand.

Looking at the formidable human before him, Kubfu joyfully jumped up and gave him a high five.

"Bayah!"

D took out some Life Sheep Milk and handed it to Kubfu to help heal its injuries.

Curious, Kubfu took a sip and was amazed to find all the wounds on its body disappearing instantly!

"Bay, Bayah?!"

"Gah-oh~" Cyclizar came forward, smiling, to introduce the Life Sheep Milk to Kubfu.

Seeing the two Pokemon getting along so harmoniously, D also flashed a smile.

With Chi-Yu now captured, it was time for them to return to the ranch.

D returned Ogerpon and the others to their Poke Balls, then bade farewell to the Pokemon before him with Kubfu and Cyclizar.

"Everyone, goodbye!"

"Kukyuk!"

The group of wild Pokemon stood among the bamboo, seeing them off.

D held Kubfu as he rode on Koraidon, soaring into the sky.

Watching Fury Falls disappear below them until it could no longer be seen, D retracted his gaze and said to Koraidon:

"Let's go, home!"

"Bayah?" Sitting in D's arms, Kubfu looked expectant for the new life ahead.

"Home is where I live with Koraidon and the others. It's a place where everyone lives together, a ranch." D introduced with a smile.

"Bayah!" Kubfu began to look forward to it.

"Ah-gah-sii..."

Beneath him, Koraidon squinted, looking back.

It recalled past events, knowing full well that this seemingly harmless Teddy Bear Pokemon would emerge as its biggest rival in the future...

Of course, not in terms of strength.

Instead, because this guy had an adorable fur and appearance.

Damn it, why couldn't it grow fur? Koraidon thought sullenly.

It couldn't help but fantasize about having beautiful fur and D holding it and grooming it.

But unfortunately, from its observation, neither Cyclizar nor Koraidon had any fur.

Among the previous Pokemon, only Scream Tail, Flutter Mane, and Slither Wing had fur.

Koraidon had seen Slither Wing before, and in the past, it even fought with Slither Wing.

That was a particularly large Bug-type Pokemon, with thick, copious fur; no one knew how it grew it.

It's decided, it would talk with Flutter Mane later to ask how it grew such beautiful fur!

Koraidon had made up its mind.

*

Upon returning to the ranch, the first thing D did was make a cup of Reversed Red Tea using the Water of the Reversed World and then fed it to Chi-Yu.

Like Ting-Lu, Chi-Yu became quiet and no longer as active as before, devoted to melting rock and gravel to create magma seas after getting captured.

"Moo moo!" (Ho ho ho! Long time no see, Chi-Yu! Do you still remember me?)

Ting-Lu greeted Chi-Yu with a smile.

Chi-Yu calmly looked at Ting-Lu and the human in front: "Yu Yu?" (Of course I remember, but... how did you turn out like this?)

Chi-Yu's big eyes filled with confusion.

Ting-Lu explained: "Moo moo!" (Thanks to D, he helped purify the Power of Calamity from me, now I am a Pokemon that can bring auspiciousness!)

Chi-Yu: "?"

It turned its head to look at this human beside it.

Ting-Lu, seeming to have seen through its thoughts, smiled and said:

"Moo moo?" (You feel it too, right? There's no greed in him. We aren't affected, indicating that he just sees us as ordinary Pokemon, ho ho ho!)

Upon hearing this, Chi-Yu found it hard to believe for a moment.

D passed over the prepared Reversed Red Tea, placing it before Chi-Yu:

"Drink it, with this, the Power of Calamity on you can be purified."

Though reversed, essentially it was no different from purification.

After a moment of silence, Chi-Yu ultimately chose to trust Ting-Lu and this pure-hearted human.

It swam forward and gently drank from the cup.

And when it finished all the water in the cup, Chi-Yu suddenly felt an unprecedented strength surge in its body.

---

The golden light enveloped Chi-Yu's body.

A moment later, the golden light dispersed, and at first glance, Chi-Yu seemed unchanged, but upon closer inspection, D noticed that its flames had clearly become softer.

Completely devoid of the previous sense of threat!

Like Ting-Lu, after consuming the Reversing Cuisine, corresponding changes occurred on their bodies.

The feature of Ting-Lu was that the crack on the great cauldron on its head had been repaired.

As for Chi-Yu, it was the change in its flames.

So the question arises, what has Chi-Yu's Power of Calamity transformed into?

Thinking of this, D quickly asked aloud: "Chi-Yu, do you feel any different changes compared to before?"

Chi-Yu was slightly stunned, and at this moment, it discovered that a strange piece of information appeared in its mind.

Through flames, bring auspiciousness and blessings to people and Pokemon, making the surrounding temperature warmer. The flames possessed the power of blessing, and when someone prays to the flames, it can bring them good luck...

What is the meaning of this?!

Chi-Yu's eyes were filled with astonishment.

"How about it? Any changes?" D curiously asked.

Chi-Yu explained its changes to D.

After listening, D also felt a bit surprised.

An alternative Fire God?

Staring at Chi-Yu and Ting-Lu in front of him, D became increasingly curious about the powers Wo-Chien and Chien-Pao would have after the reversal.

If his guess was correct, Wo-Chien's ability was likely the Power of Auspicious, reviving deadwood and nourishing plants.

As for Chien-Pao... that was a bit harder to guess.

Would it be banishing evil and punishing wrong, or protecting against wind and snow?

Unfortunately, if it were abilities related to bounty, all four of these Ruinous Pokemon could be appointed as subordinates to Calyrex.

"Alright, that's it for today!" D said to the two Pokemon, "This ranch is my domain, you can safely stay here. Tomorrow, I'll go searching for Chien-Pao and Wo-Chien. Then you four can reunite!"

Next, he planned to give Kubfu a tour of the ranch.

In addition, he would also try to communicate with Kyogre, asking it to use the Sea God's abilities to help Kubfu comprehend the Hydro Vortex.

Kubfu was now with Cyclizar, and on the way to find them, D contacted Raifort using his phone, telling her that Chi-Yu and Ting-Lu had both arrived at the ranch.

Hearing this news, Raifort's voice was filled with excitement on the other end of the call.

"This is wonderful! At this rate, the four Treasures of Ruin can all be liberated soon!"

"Yes! At that time, I will take them to Mesagoza City."

"I'll be waiting for you all here." Raifort said.

Meanwhile, in the room.

Flutter Mane, as usual, sneaked into the fridge, checking D's food storage.

Koraidon wobbled over: "Ah-ga-sii!"

"Dream!"

Flutter Mane was startled, and upon turning around and realizing it was Koraidon, it sighed in relief.

Koraidon suddenly seeking it out left Flutter Mane quite puzzled.

"Dream?"

Koraidon explained: "Ah-ga-sii?" (Do you know what to do to grow fur?)

Grow fur? Flutter Mane was slightly stunned.

Immediately, it thought of something.

That was the Oran Berry it had secretly hidden before.

That Oran Berry had been forgotten in the corner, and when it was remembered and checked, it was covered in green fuzz, turning into a Venonat.

Koraidon must be referring to this incident, right?

Flutter Mane realized and then shared its understanding with Koraidon.

It explained that an Oran Berry left alone for a long time would grow green fuzz, and if a Pokemon was left alone for a long time, it might grow fur too.

A light bulb lit up over Koraidon's head.

"Ah-ga-sii!"

---

---

---

---

# 497: Chapter 494: Kyogre and Kubfu, Mysteries of the Sea!

"This is my ranch!"

D said as he led Kubfu around to tour his ranch.

Kubfu's little face was full of curiosity. Although it had seen humans before, it had rarely ventured into the human world.

"Beya?"

"A ranch?" D explained with a smile, "A ranch is a place where Pokemon are raised and live together with everyone. See the Gogoat over there? And the Miltank."

"They help us produce lots of fresh goat's milk and cow's milk every day. We can keep the milk for ourselves, or sell it."

"The money we earn from selling the milk can buy lots of food for everyone to enjoy, like your favorite bamboo shoots. If we have money, we can buy them directly from town."

Kubfu listened, somewhat understanding: "Beya..."

At this moment, its attention was drawn to the gigantic scallions in the garden.

In the Scallion Garden, the Farfetch'd that had once sliced a waterfall was sitting on the grass, polishing its scallion with its wings, exuding the aura of a strong being.

Following Kubfu's gaze, D saw the Farfetch'd.

"That's Farfetch'd. Its job in the ranch is to protect the scallions and the fields, preventing wild Pokemon from causing damage." He continued to introduce.

"Beya!"

Kubfu picked up a blade of grass from the ground, swinging it like a sword.

Its expression seemed to say that Farfetch'd slicing the waterfall was amazing!

D laughed, "Of course, Farfetch'd, like you, is a Pokemon dedicated to training. It undergoes special training every day, which is why it's become so powerful."

"Beya!"

Hearing this, Kubfu immediately ignited with determination. It too wanted to train hard and become a more powerful Pokemon!

D noticed Kubfu's thoughts and nodded,

"Alright, no rush. I'll help you come up with a more reasonable and efficient plan for your training. If you follow my method, you'll definitely see better results."

"Beya!" Kubfu nodded happily.

Kubfu really is such an adorable Pokemon!

D's heart filled with endless affection, and he couldn't resist patting Kubfu on the head.

He decided that he would build a Bamboo Forest for Kubfu in the ranch later!

Besides that, D remembered the set of Apricorns that a lost woman had given them.

He wanted to give the Friend Ball to Kubfu as a token of their friendship!

And as D petted it, Kubfu froze, suddenly becoming bashful.

No one had ever treated it like this before!

"You can stay at the ranch for now, and after I settle the things with Ting-Lu and them, I'll help you build a Bamboo Forest. Then you can settle down and live there peacefully," D said with a smile.

Build a Bamboo Forest? Kubfu looked around and found there wasn't a single piece of bamboo here.

Does that mean starting from bamboo shoots? That sounds so troublesome!

D's willingness to help it train already made Kubfu very happy. It didn't want to trouble D so much, so it shook its head repeatedly, "Beya!"

"Huh? You don't like it?" D was slightly surprised.

Kubfu, a bit embarrassed, replied, "Beya..."

D realized it didn't want to trouble him.

"It's no problem, really. Besides, there are Pokemon on the ranch that can make the bamboo grow instantly!"

D grinned, "And you don't have to be so distant. From now on, we're all family. If nothing unexpected happens, we'll be living together from now on."

"Beya..." Kubfu looked up, listening intently.

A moment later, a light shone in its eyes, and it nodded vigorously, "Beya!"

"That's more like it! Want to come up?" D patted his shoulder.

Used to living alone and being self-sufficient, Kubfu instinctively shook its head.

However, the next second, it felt itself being picked up.

"Beya!"

D placed Kubfu on his shoulder and took the opportunity to pat the little bear, "Don't be shy! Let's go; I'll continue taking you around the ranch!"

The duo continued forward.

Next, D and Kubfu visited the Berry Garden, Apple Garden, Chicken House, the Underground Cave of Garganacl, Wooloo Ranch, Pokemon Tavern, and Snorlax Park.

Soon, evening came.

To welcome Kubfu, Ting-Lu, and Chi-Yu, D prepared a grand dinner for them.

However, during dinner, D didn't see any sign of Koraidon.

He then realized he hadn't seen Koraidon all afternoon.

Something's not right!

He turned to his sister Indeedee and asked, "Have you seen Koraidon?"

Sister Indeedee pointed towards the direction of the wine cellar, "Aii..."

This afternoon, Koraidon had run into the wine cellar and hadn't come out since. No one knew what it was doing in there.

Wine cellar?

D was slightly puzzled, then he opened the door to the wine cellar and descended the wooden stairs.

The dimly lit wine cellar was filled with the aroma of fruit wine. With a click, the warm-toned lights instantly illuminated every corner of the room.

And there was Koraidon, crouched in the corner, doing something unknown.

Seeing this, D couldn't help but have a big question mark appear above his head.

"Koraidon? What are you doing in the wine cellar?"

Koraidon didn't seem unhappy. It responded energetically as it always did: "Agas!"

---

It's waiting here to grow fur!

Flutter Mane said so, saying that as long as it stays in a dark and damp corner like an Oran Berry, it will grow fuzz on it!

D: "..."

"Grow fur? Grow what fur? Come out and eat, if you don't come over, the food will be eaten up by other Pokemon."

"Ahhhh!"

Hearing this, Koraidon quickly ran out, having stayed for the whole afternoon, its stomach was already growling with hunger.

D closed the door to the wine cellar and followed Koraidon back to the living room.

Holding a roast meat sandwich with its wing claws, Flutter Mane took a big bite of the meat and saw Koraidon running out.

It remembered what happened in the afternoon and couldn't help but ask: "Meng?" (Did fur grow?)

"Ahhhh." Koraidon looked left and right and found that there wasn't a single fur on it: "Ahhhh..."

Looks like the plan failed.

Because if it remembered correctly, even when it was a Cyclizar, it had never seen a Koraidon grow fur.

This means Koraidon can't grow fur.

"Alright, let's eat!" D sat down at the dining table, took a deep breath, and looked at the group of Pokemon around him, feeling a sense of fulfillment in life.

Dinner ended in a harmonious atmosphere.

Ting-Lu and Chi-Yu returned to their Poke Balls to rest, while Kubfu was settled by D into a fluffy Pokemon nest.

"Alright, sleep here!" D patted Kubfu's head.

"Byaa~"

Kubfu responded softly, turning around to look around, curiously looking at the Pokemon nest beneath it.

Inside it was warm and cozy, a feeling it had never experienced before.

Kubfu felt very happy, curled up in the nest, and peacefully closed its eyes.

Koraidon squinted at the little bear-type Pokemon next door.

It thought to itself that this guy is really too naive, what's so amazing about just an ordinary Pokemon nest.

Even after evolving into Urshifu, it didn't want to let go.

Due to its size, Koraidon had never slept in a nest and couldn't understand this behavior.

After settling a few newly joined Pokemon in the ranch, D finally took out his Rotom Phone.

First, he chatted with Lono for a while, after saying good night to each other, they agreed to meet tomorrow.

D had a good understanding of the dates over at the Levincia Gym.

The Gym work in the Paldea Region is very humane, with each Gym Leader only needing to work six days a week and then getting a day of rest.

Today is Saturday, tomorrow Sunday, Levincia Gym doesn't have to accept challengers, that's why Lono has time to come to the ranch.

After closing the chat window with Lono, D opened the Legendary Beast chat group and @ Kyogre.

It was clear that Kyogre hadn't gone to sleep yet.

A few seconds after the message was sent, D received a reply from Kyogre.

[Kyogre: What do you want from me?]

[King of Wings: I have something I would like your help with.]

[Kyogre: What is it?]

[King of Wings: I just caught a Pokemon partner today, it's been training in the Mysteries of Water recently, like practicing under waterfalls and in river water, understanding the flow of water to gain insight.]

[King of Wings: I thought since you're the Sea God, you created so much seawater, maybe you could use your power to help it understand the Mysteries of Water.]

In the deep sea below the World Tree, in the Land of Dragons.

Kyogre calmly looked at the message from D.

Sea God... is that what humans call it?

That's not wrong, it is the embodiment of the sea, created the Primordial Sea, and holds the Hydro Vortex.

To some extent, it can indeed help that Pokemon train.

Kyogre felt no problem, repaying D for letting them stay in the World Tree.

During this time, it absorbed a lot of natural energy, its strength already back to its peak. Courtesy demands reciprocity, and it should give back to D.

Thinking of this, Kyogre was about to ask Rotom to reply "Ok".

But at this moment, Rotom in front of it told it via voice that its archenemy Groudon had sent a message.

[Groudon: Why only ask Kyogre and not me? I am much stronger than that guy!]

What's the point of the Mysteries of Water?

It can teach that Pokemon the Mysteries of the Earth, letting it feel the majestic power of the earth!

D: "..."

He wanted to, but the problem is, contacting Groudon is of no use!

Kyogre can help Kubfu comprehend the Mysteries of Water, evolving it into a Rapid Strike Urshifu.

However, in this area, Groudon can't offer much help.

Nevertheless, Groudon acted unrelenting, yelling and cursing at Kyogre in the group.

Eventually, the internet sensation Giratina also joined in to stir up the fun.

[Giratina: Here's an idea! Instead of quarreling in the group every day with nothing better to do, why not compete to see who can teach better, who can make that Pokemon grow more, and who's better? Decide the winner this way!]

[Groudon: Hmph, no problem from me!]

[Kyogre: If that's the case, then I have no problem either.]

D: "..."

He hadn't even agreed yet?

How come these Legendary Pokemon already had everything arranged for his Kubfu?

But then, on second thought, teachings from Legendary Pokemon are not often seen outside.

Though Groudon and Kyogre proposed this "war" to subdue each other.

---

But to achieve victory, those two will surely give it their all to teach Kubfu.

Undoubtedly, this is a rare, great opportunity for Kubfu.

Let's not mention Groudon for now, because D doesn't know what use it might have.

But Kyogre, the Sea God's Mysteries of Water, is indeed a substantial assistance for Kubfu.

Originally, Kubfu took inspiration from the river's sometimes rapid, sometimes calm waters, perfecting the Water Style and creating the move [Surging Strikes].

And now...

If Kubfu's source of inspiration were to shift from the usual river water to the Sea God's seawater, what kind of insights would it gain?

At a glance, seawater and river water don't seem all that different.

But in essence, they are vastly different; the capacity and expanse of seawater far surpass those of an ordinary river.

When the sea is calm, when the sea becomes waves, boundless and everlasting...

Thinking this, D posted a message in the Legendary Beast chat group.

[Wing King: Great, I'll take my Pokemon to the World Tree tomorrow afternoon when I have time.]

*

Early the next morning, Dragonite flew to Levincia City and picked up Lono to the ranch.

As soon as Lono landed, Greavard excitedly rushed to meet her.

"Woof woof!"

"Alright, alright!" Lono gently patted Greavard's head, then stood up and handed a bouquet of flowers to D: "Hehe, a gift for you!"

D was stunned on the spot, trying hard to remember if today was a holiday, but after thinking for a long time, he realized it was just an ordinary day.

"Why did you suddenly think of giving me flowers?" he asked.

Lono greeted Koraidon, then crouched down, hugging Greavard as she explained:

"I noticed a flower shop on the street when I just went out, the flowers there were blooming beautifully, so I bought a bouquet on the spur of the moment!"

Upon hearing this, D couldn't help but lower his head to look at the bouquet in his hand.

Some guys might never receive a bouquet in their lifetime, and the only place where they might is at a funeral.

This was the first time he received such a gift.

D was slightly moved, carrying the flowers into the house as he asked:

"Did the Lightning Steamed Bun I gave you last time work?"

During the previous New Year, Zapdos gifted him some feathers, and D made many Lightning Steamed Buns. They couldn't finish them, so he sent a few to Lono, letting her give them to Bellibolt and the others to eat.

"Yep, yep!" Lono nodded repeatedly, mentioning that incident, her face showed incredible expression: "Your psychic powers are amazing!"

D chuckled and said: "I used special ingredients for that."

"What do you plan to do today?" Lono asked.

D originally intended to continue capturing Tablets of Ruin and Sword of Ruin, but since Lono came today, he postponed his plan to tomorrow.

After a short contemplation, D thought of Kubfu, so he said:

"Yesterday I went to Fury Falls for some errands and captured a new Pokemon, I plan to build a Bamboo Forest for it on the ranch, and today I'm thinking of going to town to buy some bamboo shoots, do you want to come along?"

"Sure!" Lono nodded: "Hehe, just right for me to experience what a rancher's daily life is like!"

D smiled warmly.

He placed the bouquet safely, then told his sister Indeedee and the others, started his pickup truck, and headed to Porto Marinada Town with a few Pokemon joining the fun and Lono.

---

---

---

---

# 498: Chapter 495: Kubfu's Two Teachers

The car stereo played a melodious CD as D drove, carrying Lono and the Pokemon along this quaint countryside road, evoking the feeling of a family outing in spring.

Greavard sat excitedly in the back seat, panting with its tongue out, making a "heh heh" sound.

Cyclizar and Koraidon sat in the back compartment, feeling bored as they watched the scenery pass by.

"Life here is really so relaxing!" Lono exclaimed, enjoying the breeze. At this moment, she probably understood why D liked living in this place.

The quiet wilds, beautiful farm fields, enjoying the wind blowing across those fields with the Pokemon, running freely across the rhythmic grasslands...

Just thinking about it feels amazing!

"Right?" D said with a smile, "Houses in big cities feel like Pidove cages to me, with so many people living in the same building, it feels too stifling."

"And the living space isn't large enough for so many Pokemon to live together."

"Yeah!" Lono nodded, she immediately thought of the office workers in Levincia City.

Those people live such lives every day, walking hurriedly on the streets of Levincia City in full suits with briefcases, looking very busy.

As they chatted, the car arrived in Porto Marinada Town.

D parked the car by the side of the road and headed to the Elo Seed Store.

The shopkeeper Elo was moving goods at the door, just then, a young girl with an Espeon ran out of the store.

"Dad, we're heading out!"

Upon hearing the voice, Elo turned around, smiling and patting his daughter's head, "Alright, go play with Espeon! Remember to come back early!"

"Got it!" The girl replied and ran off.

Elo watched as his daughter's figure disappeared down the street. He was about to continue his work when he noticed D approaching.

"Good morning, D!" Elo greeted cheerfully, then noticed the girl beside D, and his eyes widened.

"Good morning, Mr. Elo." D replied politely, "Do you have bamboo seedlings in the store? I'd like to buy a batch to take back."

Elo snapped back to reality, nodding repeatedly, "Bamboo seedlings? Of course! How many would you like? I'll get them for you!"

D thought for a moment and replied, "Fifty will do."

"Please wait a moment!" Elo went back into the store, and soon, with the Helping Hand of assistant Pokemon, he brought out batch after batch of bamboo seedlings.

D, Koraidon, and Cyclizar stepped forward to help, loading the bamboo seedlings into the back of the pickup truck with him.

"Was that Bell just now?" D asked casually.

Bell, Elo's daughter, was once filled with fear of Pokemon and the outside world due to an attack, and she stayed home without going out.

Two years ago, D made a Courage Egg Fried Rice for her and suggested she raise a friendly Eevee.

Unexpectedly, the girl now has ventured out and become like a normal person.

"Yes!" Talking about his daughter, Elo's face showed a smile, "Thanks to your help, that girl has become much more cheerful, now she can go out on her own completely, and her Eevee has evolved into Espeon. Bell takes it to the beach to bask in the sun every day!"

"That's great." D nodded.

Having loaded all the bamboo seedlings onto the truck, he asked, "How much is it?"

Elo replied, "Oh, it totals to twenty-five thousand League coins!"

D paid the amount.

Elo said, "Bamboo is a very popular plant this year! Grown bamboo can be made into furniture, each piece selling for several thousand League coins. Bamboo shoots can be eaten or sold."

"No, I don't plan to sell them, just want to plant a bamboo forest at the ranch for the Pokemon to play." D shook his head with a smile.

He bid farewell to Elo, drove to the Porto Marinada Market to buy some groceries, and then returned to the ranch with Lono.

On the way back, Lono recalled how the townsfolk greeted D and felt very curious.

"It seems like everyone knows you!"

"Of course," D said proudly, "I'm quite popular in the town."

Currently, there's no one in Porto Marinada Town who doesn't know D Ranch.

Lono said, "Hmm, I can see that already!"

"Everyone is very kind." D recalled the time when he just arrived, "When I first came here, they all took good care of me."

For example, giving extra crop seeds or offering discounts when buying things.

D glanced at Lono and asked, "So, have you thought it through yet?"

Lono was puzzled, "What?"

"Managing the ranch with me, living here," D said.

Lono immediately understood.

She chuckled, "Once I've made a decision, I never give up. Don't underestimate me!"

Half an hour later, the two returned to the ranch.

Lono picked up a hoe and swung it forcefully over the land.

"Now, I'll show you how capable I, Lono, am!"

Watching the sod flying away, D was silent.

"..."

Lono blushed instantly, trying to defend herself, "That was a mistake!"

D couldn't help but laugh, "Alright, I've seen your capabilities, now let's leave the professional work to the professionals!"

With that, he blew a whistle.

---

Soon, the ground began to bulge as Dugtrio emerged from the earth and started their work.

Very quickly, holes were dug into the soil.

D brought Koraidon forward to plant the bamboo seedlings.

Lono watched for a while and then joined in.

Maybe the recent event had sparked her competitive spirit.

Lono firmly said, "Once I return to Levincia City, I'll pursue further studies. Next time, I'll show you the amazing skills of Lono!"

D: "..."

He replied helplessly, "Everyone has things they're good and not good at; let's leave the farming to me and Dugtrio."

After planting the bamboo seedlings, D had Koraidon call over Kubfu and Gogoat.

Soon, D saw Kubfu running over from the Scallion Garden.

Lono was also attracted to this Pokemon she had never seen before.

"What's this Pokemon?"

"Oh, it?" D introduced, "This is Kubfu, a Pokemon I brought back from Fury Falls yesterday. Perhaps you haven't heard of it, but in the Galar Region, Kubfu sightings are common."

After all, the previous Galar Champion Mustard's signature Pokemon is Kubfu's evolved form, Urshifu.

"Oh? A Pokemon from the Galar Region? It looks so cute!"

"Bya!"

Kubfu stood beside D, looking up at the human in front of it.

D introduced to it, "This is Lono, she will be the mistress of this place in the future!"

Upon hearing this, Kubfu glanced at Lono a few more times.

Lono waved at the Pokemon and greeted it, "Hello, Kubfu!"

Kubfu hesitated a bit, then quietly responded, "Bya..."

"Alright, time to get to work." D turned his attention to the three Gogoat and said, "Take care of the bamboo seedlings for us!"

"Meh!"

Such a task was a piece of cake for the Gogoat now.

With a slight effort of their minds, they released a burst of natural and rich Grass Energy from their bodies.

In the blink of an eye, this energy enveloped the entire Bamboo Forest, then poured into the bamboo seedlings.

In the next second, the seedlings began to grow visibly, getting taller and sprouting lush green bamboo leaves.

Soon, a lush Bamboo Forest appeared before them.

Having been with D for a long time, Lono was now used to scenes like this.

But for Kubfu, seeing this for the first time, it showed a surprised expression, gazing at the Bamboo Forest in disbelief, "Bya!"

Amazing, so amazing!

"This shouldn't be much different from the bamboo forest at Fury Falls, right?" D said with a smile, "From now on, this will be your new home!"

Upon hearing D's words, Kubfu quickly realized that this Bamboo Forest was specially built by D for it.

D is really good to it!

Kubfu, who had never been treated this way before, felt deeply moved.

But it's not very good at expressing its emotions, so facing D, Kubfu became shy and bashful.

Kubfu was embarrassed!

D saw through this little Bear-type Pokemon's thoughts, but he didn't say much, just smiled and patted its head.

*

In the afternoon, after sending Lono back to Levincia City, D returned to the ranch and found Kubfu in the Bamboo Forest.

"I've found two teachers for you; they agreed to help with your training," he said.

Upon hearing this, Kubfu paused.

"Bya?" (Teachers?)

D nodded and then picked up Kubfu, "Follow me! I'll take you to meet them!"

Kubfu didn't struggle; filled with curiosity, it entered a strange world under D's lead.

There were floating Sky Islands everywhere, captivating Kubfu's attention as it watched them wide-eyed.

Soon, they passed through this place and arrived at a region teeming with intense natural aura.

Green grass, dense forests, and various Pokemon...

Kubfu even saw many Pokemon living in those strange buildings like humans!

"Bya?!" Kubfu was very surprised.

Seeing D come over, a few Pokemon excitedly ran over to greet him.

"Ah, long time no see! Tatsugiri, and Treecko! How have you been lately?"

D reached out to stroke their heads, then said to Kubfu:

"I haven't introduced you yet; this is the World Tree, a place I've built especially for Pokemon."

"Those buildings ahead are the Explorer Team Guild, as you can see, it's a place where only Pokemon live."

"Bya..." Kubfu murmured.

"Alright, Tatsugiri, don't you need to work in the sushi shop? We've got some things to do; we'll come over and play with you later!" D said.

However, the Tatsugiri jumped with excitement.

They indicated that the sushi shop was currently managed by Venusaur; they now plan to team up with Dondozo to catch fish!

"Oh? That's great, I also need to visit that place, so we'll go together!"

"Me!"

The group came to the next level together, where D parted with the Tatsugiri on Dragonite Island.

The three Dondozo were already waiting there ahead of time, and when Tatsugiri arrived, they opened their big mouths eagerly.

---

Tatsugiri turned back and waved to D, then jumped into Dondozo's mouth to go fishing in the ocean with them.

It was afternoon, and the Dragonites hadn't returned yet; only some Dratini and Dragonair were on Dragonite Island.

D took out some Poffins and Pokeblocks from his bag and handed them to them, then led Kubfu to the seaside.

He sent a message to Kyogre on his phone.

Soon, a massive shadow emerged beneath the calm sea surface.

The next second, the shadow leapt out of the sea, sending a shower of water into the sky.

Amidst the spray, the massive figure of Primal Kyogre appeared.

Kubfu felt a terrifying aura from this Pokemon, as deep and unfathomable as the ocean itself.

"Beyah..."

"Don't be afraid." D gently patted Kubfu's little hand, "This is Kyogre, the Sea God, one of the two teachers I found for you. It masters the world's greatest Mysteries of Water, perhaps you can learn something from it."

Kubfu bravely lifted its head, facing the intimidating Pokemon before it.

Kyogre also lowered its body, gazing at this Pokemon with its golden pupils.

"From this day forward, I am your teacher. What you can learn from me depends entirely on you."

Besides battling with Groudon, Kyogre was also here to repay D for allowing them to settle on the World Tree.

Hence, it wished to help this Pokemon cultivate.

Upon hearing Kyogre's words, Kubfu shuddered, then nodded seriously with determination: "Beyah!"

Kyogre nodded slightly.

In the next instant, a vast blue wave surged from the ocean.

Kubfu felt a flash of blue light before the towering wave enveloped it.

Kubfu instinctively closed its eyes.

Yet it waited for ages without feeling the expected impact.

"Beyah?"

Kubfu opened its eyes, finding itself in a vast blue world.

This is... the ocean!

"Beyah!"

Kubfu instinctively held its breath to prevent drowning, but in the following second, it discovered something magical.

It seemed it could breathe underwater now?!

Realizing this, Kubfu quickly turned its head to look around, seeing D and Kyogre floating not far away.

D was also surprised by the fact that he could breathe underwater.

Is this Kyogre's power? Clearly, Kyogre had bestowed its protection upon them.

"No need to be nervous, feel it with your heart." D said to Kubfu.

On hearing this, Kubfu began to calm down.

Seeing this, Kyogre's will gently fluctuated.

The next second, the surrounding seawater suddenly started to churn violently.

Fearsome undercurrents and whirlpools surged madly around, rain suddenly poured from the sky, and the previously calm sea erupted into a massive wave.

The giant wave raged and roared, as if capable of destroying everything.

Kubfu's body was swept by the undercurrent, lifted by the giant wave high into the air.

"Beyah!"

Experiencing such an event for the first time, Kubfu uttered a panicked cry.

However, soon the giant wave vanished, and the storm ceased.

Everything returned to its original state.

The blue ocean exuded a serene and gentle aura, appearing so beautiful, so gentle.

In that haze, one felt an urge to drown in it.

Kubfu panted heavily, quickly turning its head to look around at the peaceful sea, as if everything that had just happened was merely an illusion,

Yet the strong impact on its body told it that it was no illusion; the giant wave and storm had indeed been real.

"Beyah..."

What exactly is going on?

And then, Kyogre's ethereal voice came from nearby.

"Did you feel it? This is the ocean, sometimes gentle and calm, sometimes fierce and violent, it can embrace all things and also destroy everything."

Kubfu was slightly stunned.

D's words echoed in its ears, Kubfu closed its eyes, sitting cross-legged in the blue water, starting to feel the form and meaning of the sea.

Watching this, Kyogre was somewhat surprised.

It hadn't expected this Pokemon to enter a state so quickly, is it comprehending?

D also nodded in satisfaction.

Today, he planned to let Kubfu have a simple try first, and introduce it to the two different forms of Urshifu and their respective skills in detail after returning at night.

Kubfu sat there for two hours.

When it was over, D did not take it away from the World Tree, but instead continued down to the volcanic region where Groudon resided.

Primal Groudon stood within the magma, gazing down at D and Kubfu with its golden eyes.

"I can feel its aura on you, you probably just came from it, right?"

Kubfu was a little confused: "Beyah?"

"It's fine, no need to worry." D spoke up: "Focus, Groudon!"

Groudon: "Hmph, what did that guy teach you? Never mind, no need to care about its teachings, come on, learn with me. What I teach is better than what it teaches!"

"Beyah..."

Hearing this, Kubfu couldn't help but feel a strange sensation.

It suddenly realized that its two teachers seemed to not get along too well...

---

# 499: Chapter 496: The Reason to Become Stronger

Groudon's subsequent actions completely validated Kubfu's thoughts.

Suddenly, it stomped its right foot on the ground, and the strange patterns on its body emitted a crimson glow, resembling flowing magma.

An incomparably mighty energy was released from Groudon, surging into the earth.

The next second, a gigantic "Stone Sword" burst out from the ground, rushing at high speed straight towards Kubfu's position!

"Beyah!"

Facing that terrifying scene, Kubfu's heart rang with alarm bells.

If it were a normal Pokemon, it would probably abandon the fight and flee due to this frightening aura.

But Pokemon like Kubfu are born with an indomitable spirit.

They develop their willpower through training, never cowering from an opponent's attack.

As the massive Stone Sword was about to strike, Kubfu focused its mind, fixed its gaze on the target, and then leapt to the side with force.

Dodged!

However, the Stone Sword, as if equipped with an auto-navigation system, changed direction and continued its pursuit as soon as it saw Kubfu evade.

"Boom boom boom!"

The sound of the earth cracking continuously echoed, and in the blink of an eye, the gigantic "Stone Sword" formed a circle, trapping Kubfu inside.

The air was filled with scorching sulfuric fumes, and Earth Vein Energy hadn't dispersed from Precipice Blades, continuously emitting rolling heatwaves, making the surrounding space appear blurred.

Primal Reversion Groudon opened its golden eyes, letting out an ancient roar towards the sky, releasing its pressure and terrifying power with abandon.

A moment later, Groudon seemed to calm down.

It looked quite appreciative towards the tiny Kubfu.

Unexpectedly, this little fellow showed no fear facing its pressure, maintaining a calm posture to evade.

Nice!

"Little one, am I mightier than that other guy?" Groudon asked.

All it had just done seemed to be a display of its mighty power to Kubfu, to overshadow Kyogre.

D: "..."

Indeed, that's very Groudon.

Kubfu also understood the specific meaning behind this Pokemon's recent actions.

It couldn't help but break out in a sweat and turned back to look at D: "Beyah..."

These two Pokemon don't seem to get along, do they?

D comforted, "That's their business, has nothing to do with us. You just need to focus on learning the skills of both of them."

Upon hearing this, Groudon glanced at D, seemingly wanting to say something.

But it suddenly thought of something, decisively shut its mouth.

Relying on others isn't a good feeling...

Hmm? D noticed Groudon peeking at him: "What's wrong, Groudon?"

Groudon immediately shook its enormous head.

If it spoke its mind, given this human's character, it would definitely be told to leave...

If it left, it couldn't return.

Probably nowhere in this world is more comfortable than here.

Groudon imagined its archenemy Kyogre basking in luxury on the World Tree, enjoying absorbing Natural Energy, while it was alone in a remote volcano crying.

"..."

Groudon decisively chose to play dumb.

Its focus returned to Kubfu, then it said to it:

"Battle me!"

Kubfu looked bewildered: "Beyah?!"

Fighting Groudon? Really?

Groudon: "Afraid?"

Upon hearing this, Kubfu immediately shook its head, its gaze gradually becoming determined.

"Beyah!"

"Very well."

The next second, Groudon unleashed a strong burst of Earth Vein Energy from its body, and the familiar Earth Sword thrust out again.

Kubfu began to sprint at high speed, dodging Groudon's attacks while charging towards it.

As Kubfu was about to approach, Groudon narrowed its eyes slightly.

The next second, a colossal Precipice Blade thrust out from beneath Kubfu's feet, sending it flying into the air.

Kubfu instantly lost its ability to battle.

D furrowed his brow, quickly ran up to check Kubfu's condition, then used the Power of Viridian to heal its injuries.

The green glow surged through D's palm, pure Natural Energy frantically converging into Kubfu's body.

In no time, Kubfu gradually awoke.

"Beyah..."

Compared to Kyogre's special training, Groudon's training was truly simple and crude, increasing experience and honing skills through battling it.

For Pokemon like Kubfu, this method is undoubtedly the most suitable.

But the issue is Groudon is too powerful.

With Kubfu's current strength, there's no chance of winning.

After a moment's contemplation, D said to Groudon: "Groudon, try a different method!"

However, as soon as he finished speaking, he heard Kubfu's voice behind him: "Beyah!"

D was slightly stunned, then turned to ask: "You want to continue challenging Groudon?"

"Beyah!" Kubfu steadfastly nodded.

D lowered his head, making eye contact with Kubfu.

"...Alright." D compromised.

In some ways, this is also the Pokemon's own choice.

Just like Ash's Charizard, which opted to stay in Charizard Valley to strengthen its power through constant battles with other Charizards there.

---

"I understand." D nodded and said, "In that case, I'll stay here with you!"

With the Power of Viridian and Gogoat's Life Sheep Milk, plus the presence of Ho-oh, no matter how severe Kubfu's injuries, it can recover immediately.

"Baa!" Seeing the Trainer willing to respect it, Kubfu happily nodded.

The martial artist instinct within its body made it eager to battle strong opponents!

Kubfu charged out once again.

D stood behind, watching this scene. Kyogre taught Kubfu theory, helping it grasp the Mysteries of Water.

Groudon, on the other hand, honed Kubfu through practical combat, allowing it to apply the theories it had learned.

In a sense, Groudon and Kyogre, these sworn enemies, are truly the most perfect partners in this world.

It's because of their existence that the land and ocean have evolved into their current forms, each being an indispensable part.

Without Groudon, this world would become the sea of doom.

Without Kyogre, this world would turn into an endless inferno.

Kubfu fell time and again, and rose time and again.

D continuously used the Power of Viridian and Life Sheep Milk to help it regain strength.

Eventually, even Groudon's expression changed.

It looked at Kubfu with a seriousness that wasn't there at first.

Without a doubt, this Legendary Pokemon was moved by Kubfu's determination.

Groudon acknowledged Kubfu.

*

In the evening, after a tough afternoon of training, Kubfu was eating the food that D had prepared for it with big bites.

Its diligent demeanor shocked all the Pokemon in the ranch.

Alcremie, Cyclizar, Dragonite, Indeedee, and the Eevee gathered around the table, all eyes on Kubfu, who was eating heartily.

"Baa?"

Kubfu noticed everyone's gaze, immediately raised its head to look at this group of Pokemon, and showed an expression of confusion.

"Eevee?" (You've been battling all afternoon, aren't you tired?)

The Eevee asked curiously.

Kubfu thought carefully, then happily replied, "Baa!" (Not tired! Just really hungry! D's cooking smells great! I could eat two more bowls!)

Dragonite was shocked; this Pokemon could eat even more than it!

"Awoo..." (What an appetite...)

Kubfu earnestly replied, "Baa!" (Only by eating well can the body become stronger, and strength increase!)

Even Flutter Mane was startled by Kubfu's appetite.

It wasn't in the mood to float here, and quickly ran to the fridge to check the food inside, worried that there wouldn't be enough to keep it from going hungry.

Alcremie was also shocked.

This Pokemon puzzled it deeply; Alcremie couldn't help but ask what was unclear to it.

"Maa?" (Why do you work so hard in special training?)

"Baa?" Kubfu was drawn to this question.

It looked at Alcremie, seriously thought for a moment before responding, "Baa!" (Because this way I can become a more powerful Pokemon!)

Alcremie was even more confused, "Maa?" (Why do you want to become a more powerful Pokemon?)

"Baa?"

Kubfu scratched its head; it suddenly realized it didn't quite know the answer to this question either...

Why become stronger?

Since it had memory, it began accumulating years of hard training and honing its moves.

After pondering, Kubfu answered, "Baa!" (Because I'll become very strong this way!)

Alcremie: "..."

"Alright, alright." D then laughed and said, "Every Pokemon in this world pursues different goals."

"For example, some Pokemon enjoy living with their Trainers, staying by their side, eating with them every day, walking together, working together, sleeping together."

"Others want to become stronger Pokemon, so they travel with Human Trainers, challenge Gyms, and reach the peak of the world."

"None of this requires any special reason, just because they like it. Kubfu works hard in training because it enjoys the feeling of becoming stronger, so it's not afraid of hardship or fatigue."

"This kind of Pokemon is really charming!" D couldn't help but marvel as he watched Kubfu eating heartily.

Serious Pokemon are the cutest!

Kubfu happily nodded its little head, "Baa!"

Alcremie understood half and half.

After a while, it also thought of what it likes the most, "Maa!" (I don't want to become stronger; I want to live with D!)

Pawmo nodded along, "Bahmo!" (Me too!)

Cyclizar also said, "Raaow!"

"A-ga-sii!"

"Bonny~"

Seeing everyone's antics, Kubfu looked at these Pokemon, then turned to D.

"Baa!" (I want to live with D too!)

"Hmm, yes, everyone lives together," D said with a smile.

He glanced at Flutter Mane peeking into the fridge and couldn't help but remark:

"Flutter Mane, don't worry, the food hasn't been eaten up by Kubfu. There's plenty in the storeroom besides the fridge!"

"Moe?"

Flutter Mane withdrew its head from the fridge.

And only then did Kubfu realize it was because it had eaten too much, causing the Pokemon named Flutter Mane to worry that all the food might be gone!

"Baa!"

Kubfu sheepishly stopped its actions.

It seemed like... it didn't eat much?

D: "..."

"Eat up, I promise there's plenty!" D assured, patting his chest.

To ease Flutter Mane's mind, he stood up and guided Flutter Mane to the home's storeroom.

There were special foods and drinks D had prepared before.

Sausages, cheese, bacon, wheat bread, pickled radishes, salted fish, dried berries, brewed wine...

"See? So don't worry about the food running out," D said to Flutter Mane.

"Moe!"

Flutter Mane nodded brightly, feeling totally reassured.

D closed the storeroom door and led Flutter Mane back to the dining room.

Kubfu looked at Flutter Mane apologetically.

Flutter Mane noticed its gaze too, and remembering the storeroom filled with food, Flutter Mane said, "Moe!" (Eat up!)

After a whole afternoon of special training, even though the wounds on its body were healed by the Power of Viridian, Kubfu was left dirty and covered in dust.

After dinner, D took Kubfu for a bath.

He filled the tub with warm water, placed Kubfu inside, soaked its fur, and then applied shower gel.

Finally, he rinsed off all the foam, resulting in a freshly clean Kubfu.

"All done!" D patted Kubfu's head: "Head over to the fireplace, Slugma will help dry your fur."

"Baa~"

Kubfu was a little embarrassed, but it couldn't refuse D's bath offer.

Upon hearing this, Kubfu quickly scampered away.

Meanwhile, D disposed of the dirty water, and when he walked out of the bathroom and entered the room, he discovered Koraidon there pretending to be serious.

D: "?"

Seeing Koraidon's demeanor, D suddenly remembered that Koraidon had overheard him at dinner claiming that serious Pokemon are the most charming.

Although Koraidon looked silly, D humorously played along like he was entertaining a child:

"Wow, which Pokemon is this? Why are you so handsome today?"

Having never heard such words from D before, Koraidon immediately became embarrassed.

"A-ga-sii!" It shyly replied.

D couldn't help but laugh: "Alright, alright, play here for a moment while I go to the tavern and help Gholdengo and brother Indeedee clean up, then I'll be back."

"A-ga-sii!"

Koraidon instantly indicated that it wanted to come along.

"Okay, let's go together then."

One human and one Pokemon arrived at the tavern, where most of the guests had already left.

D helped brother Indeedee and Gholdengo tidy up the tableware used by the Pokemon guests, and once everything was done, it was eleven at night.

Kubfu's fur had been dried by Slugma.

It was sitting in its little nest waiting for D to come back.

Everything for the day was finally finished, and it was time for bed rest!

The Eevees were already asleep.

D undressed and headed to bed, wishing Kubfu, Koraidon, and the other Pokemon goodnight before turning off the lights.

Tomorrow, he'll be heading off to Cabo Poco Town to Grasswither Shrine to release Wo-Chien's imprison.

---

---

---

---

# 500: Chapter 497: Tablets of Ruin, Flying Pikachu

The Grasswither Shrin is located in the southeastern part of the Paldea Region. According to the marker on the map Raifort provided, it seems to be somewhere near a cliff.

D didn't know the exact spot, so he could only investigate once he got there.

If it really didn't work out, he'd just rely on his personal charm and rally the wild Pokemon living around that area to help him search.

D had already decided in his heart that he'd rest today.

The next morning, after finishing the work at the ranch, he got ready to ride Koraidon and head for South Province (Area One) of Paldea.

Before leaving, D brought some gifts and sent Kubfu up to the World Tree, to the stretch of ocean where Kyogre was.

"Beh ah?"

Seeing D suddenly stop in his tracks, Kubfu tilted its head up at him in confusion.

In that moment, D suddenly felt like a parent dropping their kid off at kindergarten.

He shook his head, throwing that strange thought out of his mind, then reached out to rub Kubfu's head and said to it:

"I really want to stay here and keep training with you, but I still have some very important things to do. Just focus on your training here, and I'll come pick you up tonight to go home."

Kubfu blinked its eyes, then obediently nodded.

"Beh ah!"

"You can do it!" D cheered Kubfu on.

Right at that moment, Primal Kyogre sensed their arrival and surfaced from the sea.

To have Groudon and Kyogre take good care of Kubfu, D walked forward and handed over some Magical Cuisine he'd brought today to Kyogre, completely treating it as Kubfu's tuition fee.

"I'm leaving Kubfu in your care, Kyogre," D said.

Compared to Groudon, Kyogre was still calm and full of reason.

It lowered its head to look at the human in front of it, its gaze dropping to the bag in his hand.

Right now, a sweet fragrance was constantly drifting out through the gaps of the bag.

D took the Pokeblocks and Poffins out of his pack and tossed them into Kyogre's mouth like a trainer feeding an orca.

Unexpectedly, Kyogre didn't refuse; instead, it opened its mouth and accepted his feeding.

Kyogre obediently eating like this looked nothing like the Sea God, the Legendary Pokemon that could summon downpours and tsunamis to drown the world.

Watching this scene, D grew a bit bolder and reached out to gently pat Kyogre's head.

Kyogre: "?"

"It's nothing," D said, acting like nothing had happened as he stepped back, then turned to Kubfu and said, "I'm heading out. Remember to train hard. I'll come pick you up tonight."

"Beh ah!"

Hearing this, a feeling it had never experienced before suddenly welled up inside Kubfu's heart.

D said he'd come pick it up and take it home tonight—what an amazing feeling!

From now on, it was a Pokemon with a family too!

"Beh ah~"

After dropping Kubfu off, D left the Land of Dragons.

However, he didn't leave the World Tree, instead making another trip to the volcanic domain where Groudon lived and delivering the Pokeblocks and Poffins to Groudon.

"These are handmade by me. Just treat them as a gift to thank you for being willing to teach Kubfu," D said.

Groudon froze for a moment, a look of astonishment appearing in its eyes.

It could actually receive a gift?!

It might not care about these gifts themselves, but they were from D!

And who was D? The master of this tree, Rayquaza's representative, the one who single-handedly stopped its battle with Kyogre.

And now, such a powerful human was actually giving it a gift...

This was simply too shocking for the Pokemon!

Groudon wanted to say something more, but D had already turned and left.

Leaving the World Tree, D returned to the ranch, mounted Koraidon, and said goodbye to the Pokemon:

"We're heading out now. We'll probably be back in the afternoon. Stay put in the ranch and don't run off, got it?"

"Boni~"

Led by Ogerpon, the group of Pokemon answered happily.

D nodded, then took out his Rotom Phone to navigate. After confirming the destination, he rode Koraidon and headed straight south.

The fields and towns below whipped by rapidly behind them.

After an hour of flying, D saw the Paldea South Coast up ahead.

Compared to the northern area, the southern part of the Paldea Region was mostly plains. Quiet and peaceful towns like Cabo Poco Town and Los Platos Town, gentle and reserved, were nestled on this stretch of flatland.

D was very interested in the town of Cabo Poco.

Because in the games, that's the town players depart from for their journey, and it's also where Nemona's house is.

And it seemed that Professor Sada and Professor Turo had a Lighthouse Research Institute somewhere nearby as well.

Thinking of this, D had Koraidon make a little detour and flew over to Cabo Poco Town.

The real-life Cabo Poco Town wasn't like in the games where there were only two houses.

Although it was small, if you added up all the scattered homes, there were still a few dozen households.

With so many residences, D couldn't tell where Nemona's house was, but he did find the Lighthouse Research Institute.

Because there was only one lighthouse in this place, it was obvious that had to be Professor Sada and Professor Turo's research institute.

Besides that, D also saw Bay Cave, the place in the games where the player encounters Koraidon.

Just like in the games, many Houndour and Houndoom lived there, living as a pack in the cave and guarding their territory.

---

D watched the scene with great interest and asked the Koraidon beneath him:

"Oh right, Koraidon, how did you just happen to fly into my ranch back then? If you'd landed here instead, you might've met a kind-hearted boy or girl, you know!"

Koraidon's eyes went distant: "Aa-ga-sii..."

"Forget it, there's no point talking about that now." D reached out and rubbed Koraidon's head—he couldn't bear the thought of letting Koraidon leave him.

Koraidon strongly agreed: "Aa-ga-sii!"

Exactly!

"Alright, alright, I've finished watching. Let's get going."

After checking off Cabo Poco Town, D rode Koraidon away from there, heading for the location of the Grasswither Shrin.

"Cliff... cliff..."

If he remembered correctly, the exact location of the Grasswither Shrin was somewhere at the base of a cliff.

D locked his gaze on that line of cliffs, then had Koraidon descend.

The moment they landed, his Rotom Phone started ringing.

Once he picked up, Raifort's face appeared on the screen.

Raifort looked at him expectantly: "So? How many of the Treasures of Ruin have you caught now?"

D answered honestly: "Two. Chi-Yu and Ting-Lu have already started a new life in my ranch. Right now I'm on the south coast of the Paldea Region, getting ready to investigate the Grasswither Shrin here."

"I see. Then you've got to keep it up!"

Just then, Koraidon, walking ahead, seemed to have discovered something and suddenly let out a warning cry.

"Aa-ga-sii!"

"What is it?"

D hurriedly caught up.

They followed along the edge of a lake, moving forward until they stopped at the cliff at the far end of the lake.

Koraidon lowered its head and looked down: "Aa-ga-sii!"

D followed its gaze downward.

The next second, he saw a glowing shrine at the base of the cliff.

The vegetation all around was growing exuberantly, the grass lush and fertile, with clusters of tiny wildflowers blooming in the grass.

Strangely enough, however, in the vicinity of that shrine, all the grass had withered and turned yellow, leaving the surrounding land barren.

D was slightly stunned.

That was...

The Rotom Phone at this moment also pointed its camera downwards.

On the other end, Raifort naturally saw the scene near the Grasswither Shrin as well.

"Rotom, get a bit closer and take a look." Raifort said.

"Koraidon, let's go down." D brought Rotom and Koraidon all the way to the bottom.

Rotom floated in close and carefully recorded the situation at the Grasswither Shrin.

With just one glance, D and Raifort noticed something unusual about the Grasswither Shrin.

The four chains that had originally imprisoned the Grasswither Shrin were now down to only two!

That meant four Sealing Stakes had already been pulled out!

Who did it? A passing Trainer, or some wild Pokemon?

Raifort glanced around at the surroundings and pondered aloud:

"With the stakes pulled, the power of the Imprison grows weaker as well. Looks like Wo-Chien's power has already seeped through the Imprison and is affecting the environment around it."

"According to ancient records, Wo-Chien was originally just an ordinary Wooden Scroll. Later, someone recorded the King's misdeeds onto it."

"But that person was punished by the King, and his hatred wrapped itself around the Wooden Scroll, turning it into Wo-Chien."

"And if I'm not mistaken, Wo-Chien possesses the Power of Calamity that lets it absorb the energy of vegetation, instantly drying up surrounding forests and causing poor harvests in the fields."

At this point, Raifort suddenly showed a guilty expression: "If you catch it, it might bring trouble to your ranch..."

"Don't worry, I've got a way." D said.

The Immortal has his own clever tricks.

As long as he fed Wo-Chien some Reversing Cuisine, the Reversing Power within would completely flip Wo-Chien's power.

Absorption would turn into release, turning an instant forest wither into instant seedling growth, turning failing crops into bountiful harvests.

That way, the ranch would gain yet another snail of abundance.

Hearing this, Raifort paused for a moment, then relaxed and said: "You're right. Maybe your intentions will calm Wo-Chien down. In that case, I'll leave you to it. I'll be waiting for your good news!"

D: "..."

It has to be said, this world really is an idealistic world.

After hanging up the phone, D began working on pulling out the last four stakes of the Grasswither Shrin.

He rode Koraidon back up to the cliff and then looked around.

The Pokemon living around here were mostly some fairly common Normal-type Pokemon.

Things like Fletchling, Vivillon, Lechonk, Skwovet, Sunkern, and the like.

After a brief moment of thought, D decided to stick with the same method as the last two times.

He would use the Power of Viridian to communicate with these Pokemon, then ask them to help search for the stakes.

First, he took out some lure-type Pokeblocks.

The rich, mellow aroma drifted out with the air, and in no time at all, it attracted a large group of wild Pokemon.

They looked curiously at the human in front of them. Sensing the friendly, approachable aura on him, not a single one of these Pokemon ran away!

"Ahem." D gave a light cough twice, then said, "Everyone, I'm D. There's something I'd like to ask for your help with!"

Next, D showed them a photo of the stakes and told these Pokemon that as long as they could help him find the glowing stakes, he would give them a pile of delicious Pokeblocks and Poffins.

For these wild Pokemon, this was just about the most suitable kind of request.

All they had to do was walk around helping look for something and they'd get those tasty treats; it was simply too good a deal!

So this group of Pokemon agreed without hesitation.

They scattered and ran off in all directions, each going to look for stakes.

D, on the other hand, glanced around, planning to find a spot with an open view to set up camp and wait for these wild Pokemon to come back.

Very soon, his gaze fixed on a certain cliff. Standing there and looking out, what entered his eyes was the boundless Paldea Sea.

"Koraidon, let's go over there and wait for them!" D said.

"Aa-ga-sii!"

Koraidon nodded in agreement right away.

However, before they got close, Koraidon seemed to have noticed something and turned its gaze in a certain direction.

"Aa-ga-sii?"

"Hm? Did you spot something?" D was a bit puzzled.

He followed Koraidon's line of sight, and the next second, he spotted a Pikachu on the broken cliff by the seaside ahead.

When he saw clearly what that Pikachu was doing, D froze on the spot.

Because that Pikachu was actually flying!

It kept swishing its lightning-shaped tail, slowly flying through the air.

Pikachu... can fly?

How was it doing that? D's face was full of shock.

But just then, that Pikachu suddenly lost its balance and fell from the sky.

It landed on the grass, rolled a few times, then got back up with wounds all over its body.

Just when D thought it would stop and rest, that Pikachu suddenly burst with a surge of yellow electricity!

It Discharged while sprinting quickly along the ground, using the recoil from the energy to launch itself into the air.

The Pikachu that had successfully taken to the sky kept swinging its tail, expertly controlling the airflow, looking just like it was flying!

Watching this with his own eyes, D quickly figured out the principle behind it.

That Pikachu first used the dash-type move Volt Tackle to run in spinning loops, thereby creating a tornado-shaped vortex of energy.

Then it used its strong tail like a spring, bouncing repeatedly within the vortex to raise its height until it flew into the air, and finally used its tail swings to balance the airflow, making flight possible.

However, it wasn't long before that Pikachu fell again.

It seemed it still hadn't fully mastered that combo move.

D instinctively glanced around but didn't see any other human nearby.

That meant this Pikachu wasn't a Trainer's Pokemon but a wild Pokemon.

And that combo move of its was something it had invented all on its own!

Realizing this, D was even more shocked inside.

This Pikachu's comprehension and talent were really something else!

But just then, that wild Pikachu noticed him and Koraidon.

Vigilance immediately showed on its face, and a spark of electricity flickered across its cheeks.

"Pika pi!"

The next second, that Pikachu, covered in wounds, turned and ran.

It was very fast; in the blink of an eye, it vanished into the nearby grass.

---

---

---
